1:1 In the name of Allah, the Rahman, the Raheem
1:2 Praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of the worlds
1:3 The Rahman, the Raheem
1:4 Sovereign of the Day of Judgment
1:5 You alone we worship, and You alone we ask for help
1:6 Guide us to the straight path
1:7 The path of those whom You have favored, not of those upon whom wrath has fallen, nor of those who go astray
2:1 Alif, Lam, Mim.
2:2 That is the Kitab: no doubt in it; guidance for the muttaqin.
2:3 Those who believe in the ghayb and keep up the prayer and spend from what We provided them,
2:4 and those who believe in what was sent down to you and what was sent down before you, and of the Last they are certain —
2:5 those are on guidance from their Rabb, and those are the ones who attain success.
2:6 Indeed those who disbelieve — it is equal to them whether you warn them or do not warn them — they will not believe.
2:7 Allah has set a seal upon their hearts and upon their hearing, and over their sight is a covering; and for them is a tremendous punishment.
2:8 And among the people are those who say, "We believe in Allah and in the Last Day," yet they are not muminin.
2:9 They try to deceive Allah and those who believe, but they deceive none but themselves, yet they do not perceive.
2:10 In their hearts is a sickness, and Allah increased them in sickness; and for them is a painful punishment for what they used to deny.
2:11 And when it is said to them, "Do not corrupt in the earth," they say, "We are only ones who set things right."
2:12 Truly, they are the corrupters, but they do not perceive.
2:13 And when it is said to them, "Believe as the people believed," they say, "Shall we believe as the fools believed?" Truly, they are the fools, but they do not know.
2:14 And when they meet those who believe, they say, "We believe," but when they are alone with their satans, they say, "We are with you; we were only mocking."
2:15 Allah mocks them and grants them extension in their transgression, wandering blind.
2:16 Those are the ones who bought straying in exchange for guidance, and their trade gained nothing, and they were not guided.
2:17 Their likeness is the likeness of one who kindled a fire; when it lit up what was around him, Allah took away their light and left them in darkness, unseeing.
2:18 Deaf, dumb, unseeing — so they do not return.
2:19 Or like a torrent from the heaven, in it darkness and thunder and lightning: they put their fingers in their ears against the thunderstrikes, wary of death. And Allah is muhit over the kafirin.
2:20 The lightning nearly snatches their sight: whenever it lights the way for them they walk in it, and when darkness falls on them they stand still. Had Allah willed, Allah would have taken away their hearing and their sight. Indeed Allah is over all things able.
2:21 O people, worship your Rabb, who created you and those before you, so that you may be reverent.
2:22 The one who made the earth a spread for you and the heaven a building, and sent down from the heaven water, and brought forth with it fruits as provision for you — so do not set up equals to Allah while you know.
2:23 And if you are in doubt about what We sent down upon Our servant, then bring a chapter like it, and call your witnesses apart from Allah, if you are sadiq.
2:24 But if you do not — and you will not — then beware the Fire whose fuel is people and stones, prepared for the kafirin.
2:25 And give glad tidings to those who believe and do the good deeds: that for them are gardens beneath which rivers flow. Whenever they are provided from it with a provision of fruit, they say, "This is what we were provided before," and they are given the like of it. And for them in it are purified zawj, and they abide in it forever.
2:26 Indeed Allah is not ashamed to strike a likeness — even of a gnat or what is above it. As for those who believe, they know it is the truth from their Rabb; but as for those who disbelieve, they say, "What did Allah intend by this likeness?" By it Allah lets many stray and guides many, and by it none are led astray except the fasiqun.
2:27 Those who unbind Allah's pledge after its pact, and sever what Allah commanded to be joined, and corrupt in the earth — those are the khasirin.
2:28 How can you disbelieve in Allah when you were dead and Allah gave you life, then Allah causes you to die, then Allah brings you to life, then to Allah you will be returned?
2:29 It is Allah who created for you all that is in the earth, then turned to the heaven and leveled them into 7 heavens — and Allah is knowing of all things.
2:30 And when your Rabb said to the malakat, "I am placing in the earth a successor," they said, "Will You place in it one who will corrupt in it and shed blood, while we glorify with praise of You and sanctify You?" Allah said, "I know what you do not know."
2:31 And Allah taught Adam the names, all of them, then presented them to the malakat and said, "Tell Me the names of these, if you are sadiq."
2:32 They said, "Glory be to You! We have no knowledge except what You taught us. You are the Knowing, the Wise."
2:33 Allah said, "Adam, tell them their names." And when he told them their names, Allah said, "Did I not tell you that I know the ghayb of the heavens and the earth, and I know what you manifest and what you were hiding?"
2:34 And when We said to the malakat, "Prostrate before Adam," they prostrated, except Iblis: he refused and was arrogant, and was among the kafirin.
2:35 And We said, "Adam, dwell, you and your spouse, in the garden, and eat from it freely wherever you wish; but do not approach this tree, or you would be among the wrongdoers."
2:36 Then the satan made them slip from it and brought them out of what they were in. And We said, "Descend, some of you an enemy to some; and for you in the earth is a settlement and enjoyment until a time."
2:37 Then Adam received from his Rabb some words, and Allah turned to him in forgiveness. Indeed, Allah is the Turning, the Raheem.
2:38 We said, "Descend from it, all of you. Then if guidance comes to you from Me, whoever follows My guidance — no fear upon them, nor will they grieve."
2:39 And those who disbelieve and deny Our ayat — those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
2:40 Children of Israel, remember My favor that I favored upon you, and fulfill My pledge — I will fulfill your pledge — and of Me be in awe.
2:41 And believe in what I sent down, confirming what is with you, and do not be the first kafir in it, and do not buy with My ayat a small price; and of Me be reverent.
2:42 And do not clothe truth with falsehood and hide the truth while you know.
2:43 And keep up the prayer and give the alms and bow with those who bow.
2:44 Do you command people to goodness and forget yourselves while you recite the Kitab? Will you not understand?
2:45 And seek help in steadfastness and prayer; and it is indeed hard, except on the humbled,
2:46 those who consider that they will meet their Rabb and that to Allah they will return.
2:47 Children of Israel, remember My favor that I favored upon you, and that I favored you above the worlds.
2:48 And beware a day when no self will avail another self anything, and no intercession will be accepted from it, and no compensation will be taken from it, and they will not be helped.
2:49 And when We saved you from the family of Fir'awn, who afflicted you with the evil of punishment, slaughtering your sons and letting your nisa live — and in that was a great test from your Rabb.
2:50 And when We split the sea for you and saved you, and drowned the family of Fir'awn while you watched.
2:51 And when We appointed with Musa 40 nights, then you took the calf after him, and you were wrongdoers.
2:52 Then We passed over you after that, so that you might be grateful.
2:53 And when We gave Musa the Kitab and the criterion, so that you might be guided.
2:54 And when Musa said to his people, "My people, you have wronged yourselves by taking the calf, so turn in repentance to your Maker and kill your selves; that is better for you with your Maker." So Allah turned to you in forgiveness. Indeed, Allah is the Turning, the Raheem.
2:55 And when you said, "Musa, we will not believe you until we see Allah openly," so the thunderstrike seized you while you watched.
2:56 Then We raised you after your death, so that you might be grateful.
2:57 And We shaded over you the cloud, and sent down upon you the unstinting favor and the solace: "Eat of the good of what We provided you." And they did not wrong Us, but they were wronging themselves.
2:58 And when We said, "Enter this town and eat from it freely wherever you wish, and enter the gate bowing and say, 'Remit' — We will forgive you your errors, and We will increase the muhsinin."
2:59 But those who wronged substituted a word other than what was said to them, so We sent down upon those who wronged an affliction from the heaven for what they used to yafsuquna.
2:60 And when Musa asked for drink for his people, We said, "Strike the stone with your staff," and 12 springs burst forth from it. Every people already knew their drinking place. "Eat and drink from Allah's provision, and do not ravage in the earth as corrupters."
2:61 And when you said, "Musa, we cannot endure one food; call your Rabb for us to bring forth for us of what the earth grows — its herbs, its gourds, its garlic, its lentils, and its onions." He said, "Would you substitute what is lower for what is better? Descend to Riverland; you will have what you asked for." And abasement and want were struck upon them, and they met with wrath from Allah. That is because they used to disbelieve in Allah's ayat and kill the nabis without right. That is because they disobeyed and used to transgress.
2:62 Indeed those who believe, and the Yahud, and the Nazarenes, and the Sabians — whoever believes in Allah and the Last Day and does good, their reward is with their Rabb, and no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
2:63 And when We took your pact and raised the Mount above you: "Take what We gave you with strength, and remember what is in it, so that you may be reverent."
2:64 Then you turned away after that; had it not been for Allah's favor upon you and Allah's mercy, you would have been among the khasirin.
2:65 And you already knew those of you who transgressed in the sabbath; We said to them, "Be apes, despised."
2:66 And We made it a warning to what was before it and what was after it, and an admonition for the muttaqin.
2:67 And when Musa said to his people, "Allah commands you to slaughter a cow." They said, "Do you take us in mockery?" He said, "I seek refuge with Allah from being among the ignorant."
2:68 They said, "Call your Rabb for us to make clear to us what she is." He said, "Allah says she is a cow neither worn out nor young, middling between that; so do what you are commanded."
2:69 They said, "Call your Rabb for us to make clear to us her color." He said, "Allah says she is a yellow cow, intense in color, gladdening the beholders."
2:70 They said, "Call your Rabb for us to make clear to us what she is, for the cows look alike to us, and if Allah wills we will be guided."
2:71 He said, "Allah says she is a cow not subdued, that does not stir the earth nor water the tillage, sound, with no blemish in her." They said, "Now you have come with the truth," and they slaughtered her, though they barely did it.
2:72 And when you killed a person and disputed over it — and Allah brings out what you were hiding.
2:73 So We said, "Strike him with part of it." Thus Allah brings the dead to life and shows you Allah's ayat, so that you may understand.
2:74 Then your hearts hardened after that, so they are like stones, or harder; yet from some stones rivers burst forth, and some of them split so water comes out of them, and some of them descend in awe of Allah. And Allah is not unmindful of what you do.
2:75 Do you hope that they will believe for you when a party of them used to hear Allah's word and then distort it after they had understood it, while they knew?
2:76 And when they meet those who believe, they say, "We believe," but when some of them are alone with others, they say, "Do you tell them what Allah has opened for you, so that they may argue with you by it before your Rabb? Do you not understand?"
2:77 Do they not know that Allah knows what they hide and what they openly declare?
2:78 And among them are unlettered ones who do not know the Kitab except as wishes, and they do nothing but conjecture.
2:79 Woe to those who write the Kitab with their own hands and then say, "This is from Allah," to buy with it a small price. Woe to them for what their hands have written, and woe to them for what they earn.
2:80 And they said, "The Fire will not touch us except for counted days." Say, "Have you taken a pledge with Allah? Then Allah will never break Allah's pledge. Or do you say of Allah what you do not know?"
2:81 Yes — whoever earns an evil and their sin ahatat them, those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
2:82 And those who believe and do the good deeds — those are the companions of the garden; they abide in it forever.
2:83 And when We took the pact of the Children of Israel: worship none but Allah, and goodness to parents and to the near kin, and to orphans and the needy, and speak good to the people, and keep up the prayer and give the alms. Then you turned away — except a few of you — and you are turning aside.
2:84 And when We took your pact: do not shed your blood nor drive yourselves out from your homes; then you affirmed, and you were witnesses.
2:85 Yet here you are, killing yourselves and driving out a party of you from their homes, backing one another against them in sin and aggression; and if they come to you as captives, you ransom them, though their driving out was forbidden to you. Do you believe in part of the Kitab and disbelieve in part? Then what is the recompense for whoever does that among you except disgrace in the nearer life? And on the Day of Rising they will be returned to the hardest punishment. And Allah is not unmindful of what you do.
2:86 Those are the ones who bought the nearer life for the Last; so the punishment will not be lightened for them, nor will they be helped.
2:87 And We gave Musa the Kitab and followed him up with the Rasuls afterward, and We gave Isa son of Maryam clear proofs and strengthened him with ruh al-qudus. Then is it that whenever a Rasul comes to you with what your nafs do not desire, you are arrogant: a party you deny, and a party you kill?
2:88 And they said, "Our hearts are wrapped." No — Allah has cursed them for their disbelief, so little do they believe.
2:89 And when a Kitab came to them from Allah confirming what was with them — while before they used to seek triumph over those who disbelieved — then when there came to them what they recognized, they disbelieved in it. So Allah's curse is upon the kafirin.
2:90 Miserable is what they bought their nafs with: that they disbelieve in what Allah sent down, grudging that Allah should send down of Allah's favor upon whomever Allah wills of Allah's servants. So they met wrath upon wrath, and for the kafirin is a humiliating punishment.
2:91 And when it is said to them, "Believe in what Allah sent down," they say, "We believe in what was sent down on us," and they disbelieve in what is beyond it — though it is the truth confirming what is with them. Say, "Then why did you kill the nabis of Allah before, if you are muminin?"
2:92 And Musa came to you with clear proofs, then you took the calf after him, and you were wrongdoers.
2:93 And when We took your pact and raised the Mount above you: "Take what We gave you with strength, and listen." They said, "We hear and we disobey," and the calf was drunk into their hearts for their disbelief. Say, "Miserable is what your faith commands you, if you are muminin."
2:94 Say, "If the Last Abode with Allah is yours exclusively, apart from the people, then wish for death, if you are sadiq."
2:95 And they will never wish it, for what their hands sent forth. And Allah is knowing of the wrongdoers.
2:96 And you will surely find them the most ardently wanting of people for life, more than the associators: each one of them would like to be given a thousand years of life, and it would not move them away from the punishment to be given life so long. And Allah is all-seeing of what they do.
2:97 Say, "Whoever is an enemy to Jibril — indeed, it is he who sent it down upon your heart by Allah's leave, confirming what was before it, and guidance and glad tidings for the muminin."
2:98 Whoever is an enemy to Allah, and to Allah's malakat, and to Allah's rusul, and to Jibril and Mikail — then indeed Allah is an enemy to the kafirin.
2:99 And We sent down upon you clear ayat, and none disbelieve in them except the fasiqun.
2:100 Is it that whenever they pledged a pledge, a party of them cast it away? No — most of them do not believe.
2:101 And when a Rasul from Allah came to them confirming what was with them, a party of those given the Kitab cast the Kitab of Allah behind their backs, as if they did not know.
2:102 And they followed what the satans recited over Sulayman's kingdom — and Sulayman did not disbelieve, but the satans disbelieved, teaching people sihr, and what was sent down upon the two malakat at Babel, Harut and Marut. And they taught no one without first saying, "We are only a test, so do not disbelieve." Yet from the two they learned what they use to divide between a person and their spouse. And by it they harm no one except by Allah's leave. And they learn what harms them and does not benefit them. And they have surely known that whoever buys it has no share in the Last. And miserable is what they bought their nafs with, if only they knew.
2:103 If only they had believed and been reverent — a recompense from Allah would have been better, if only they knew.
2:104 O you who believe, do not say "keep us," but say "look upon us," and listen. And for the kafirin is a painful punishment.
2:105 Neither those who disbelieved among the people of the Kitab nor the associators wish that any good be sent down upon you from your Rabb. But Allah singles out for Allah's mercy whomever Allah wills, and Allah is possessor of the tremendous favor.
2:106 Whatever aya We abrogate or cause to be forgotten, We bring better than it or its like. Do you not know that Allah is over all things able?
2:107 Do you not know that to Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth, and that you have, apart from Allah, no protecting friend and no helper?
2:108 Or do you wish to ask your Rasul as Musa was asked before? And whoever exchanges disbelief for belief has strayed from the even way.
2:109 Many of the people of the Kitab wish they could return you to disbelief after your belief, out of envy from themselves, after the truth was made clear to them. So pass over and pardon, until Allah brings Allah's command. Indeed Allah is over all things able.
2:110 And keep up the prayer and give the alms. Whatever good you send ahead for yourselves, you will find it with Allah. Indeed Allah is all-seeing of what you do.
2:111 And they said, "None will enter the garden except who is a Jew or a Nazarene." Those are their wishes. Say, "Bring your proof, if you are sadiq."
2:112 Rather, whoever surrenders their face to Allah while doing good — their reward is with their Rabb, and no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
2:113 The Yahud said, "The Nazarenes stand on nothing," and the Nazarenes said, "The Yahud stand on nothing," while they recite the Kitab. Likewise said those who do not know, the like of their saying. So Allah will judge between them on the Day of Rising in what they used to differ.
2:114 And who is more zalimun than one who bars the places of prostration of Allah, that Allah's name be remembered in them, and strives for their ruin? Those — it was not for them to enter them except in fear. For them in the nearer life is disgrace, and for them in the Last is a tremendous punishment.
2:115 And to Allah belong the east and the west; so wherever you turn, there is the face of Allah. Indeed Allah is all-ample, knowing.
2:116 And they said, "Allah has taken a child." Glory be to Allah! No — to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth; all are devoutly obedient to Allah.
2:117 Originator of the heavens and the earth — and when Allah decides a matter, Allah only says to it, "Be," and it is.
2:118 And those who do not know said, "If only Allah would speak to us, or an aya would come to us." Likewise said those before them, the like of their saying. Their hearts are alike. We have made the ayat clear for a people who are certain.
2:119 Indeed We sent you with the truth, as a bringer of glad tidings and a warner, and you will not be asked about the companions of the blazing fire.
2:120 And the Yahud will never be pleased with you, nor the Nazarenes, until you follow their way. Say, "The guidance of Allah — that is the guidance." And if you follow their desires after the knowledge that came to you, you will have from Allah no protecting friend and no helper.
2:121 Those to whom We gave the Kitab recite it as it should be recited — those believe in it. And whoever disbelieves in it, those are the khasirin.
2:122 Children of Israel, remember My favor that I favored upon you, and that I favored you above the worlds.
2:123 And beware a day when no self will avail another self anything, and no compensation will be accepted from it, and no intercession will benefit it, and they will not be helped.
2:124 And when Ibrahim's Rabb tested him with words and he completed them, Allah said, "I am making you an imam for the people." He said, "And from my offspring?" Allah said, "My pledge does not reach the wrongdoers."
2:125 And when We made the House a resort for people and a sanctuary: "And take from the standing-place of Ibrahim a place of prayer." And We charged Ibrahim and Ismail: "Purify My House for those who go around it, and those who cleave to it, and those who bow and prostrate."
2:126 And when Ibrahim said, "Rabb, make this a secure town and provide its people with fruits — whoever among them believes in Allah and the Last Day." Allah said, "And whoever disbelieves, I will give them enjoyment a little, then force them to the punishment of the Fire — and miserable is the arrival."
2:127 And when Ibrahim, with Ismail, was raising up the foundations of the House: "Our Rabb, accept from us; indeed You are the Hearing, the Knowing."
2:128 "Our Rabb, make us submissive to You, and from our offspring a community submissive to You, and show us our rites, and turn to us in forgiveness. Indeed You are the Turning, the Raheem."
2:129 "Our Rabb, and raise up among them a Rasul from them who will recite to them Your ayat and teach them the Kitab and the wisdom and purify them. Indeed You are the Mighty, the Wise."
2:130 And who would turn away from the way of Ibrahim except one who fools themselves? And We chose him in the nearer life, and in the Last he is among the good ones.
2:131 When Allah said to him, "Surrender," he said, "I surrender to the Rabb of the worlds."
2:132 And Ibrahim enjoined it upon his sons, and Yaqub: "My sons, Allah has chosen the way for you, so do not die except as ones submissive."
2:133 Or were you witnesses when death attended Yaqub, when he said to his sons, "What will you worship after me?" They said, "We will worship your ilah and the ilah of your fathers, Ibrahim and Ismail and Ishaq — one ilah — and to Allah we are submissive."
2:134 That is a community that has passed on: theirs is what they earned, and yours is what you earn, and you will not be asked about what they used to do.
2:135 And they said, "Be Yahud or Nazarenes and you will be guided." Say, "No — the way of Ibrahim, the upright, and he was not among the associators."
2:136 Say, "We believe in Allah and what was sent down to us, and what was sent down to Ibrahim and Ismail and Ishaq and Yaqub and the tribes, and what was given to Musa and Isa, and what was given to the nabis from their Rabb. We make no division between any of them, and to Allah we are submissive."
2:137 So if they believe in the like of what you believe in, they are guided; but if they turn away, they are only in opposition, and Allah will shield you from them. And Allah is the Hearing, the Knowing.
2:138 The coloring of Allah — and who is better than Allah at coloring? And to Allah we are worshipers.
2:139 Say, "Do you argue with us about Allah when Allah is our Rabb and your Rabb? Ours are our deeds and yours are your deeds, and to Allah we are devoted."
2:140 Or do you say that Ibrahim and Ismail and Ishaq and Yaqub and the tribes were Yahud or Nazarenes? Say, "Are you more knowing, or Allah?" And who is more zalimun than one who hides a testimony in their possession from Allah? And Allah is not unmindful of what you do.
2:141 That is a community that has passed on: theirs is what they earned, and yours is what you earn, and you will not be asked about what they used to do.
2:142 The fools among the people will say, "What turned them from their direction of prayer that they were upon?" Say, "To Allah belong the east and the west; Allah guides whomever Allah wills to a straight path."
2:143 And thus We made you a middle community, so that you may be witnesses over the people and the Rasul may be a witness over you. And We did not make the direction of prayer that you were upon except to know who follows the Rasul from who turns back on their heels — and it was indeed hard except on those Allah guided. And Allah was not to lose your faith. Indeed Allah is to people gentle-merciful, merciful.
2:144 We see you turning your face about in the heaven, so We will turn you to a direction of prayer you will be pleased with: turn your face toward the Sacred Mosque; and wherever you are, turn your faces toward it. And those given the Kitab surely know that it is the truth from their Rabb, and Allah is not unmindful of what they do.
2:145 And even if you brought those given the Kitab every aya, they would not follow your direction of prayer, and you are not to follow their direction of prayer, and some of them are not followers of each other's direction of prayer. And if you followed their desires after the knowledge that came to you, you would surely be among the wrongdoers.
2:146 Those to whom We gave the Kitab recognize it as they recognize their own sons. Yet a party of them surely hide the truth while they know.
2:147 The truth is from your Rabb, so do not be among the doubters.
2:148 And for every direction there is one who turns to it; so race ahead to the good things. Wherever you are, Allah will bring you all together. Indeed Allah is over all things able.
2:149 And from wherever you set out, turn your face toward the Sacred Mosque — and it is indeed the truth from your Rabb. And Allah is not unmindful of what you do.
2:150 And from wherever you set out, turn your face toward the Sacred Mosque; and wherever you are, turn your faces toward it, so that there be no argument from the people against you — except from those of them who wronged. So do not fear them, but fear Me, and so that I may complete My favor upon you and so that you may be guided.
2:151 Just as We sent among you a Rasul from you, who recites to you Our ayat and purifies you and teaches you the Kitab and the wisdom, and teaches you what you did not know.
2:152 So remember Me — I will remember you — and be grateful to Me, and do not disbelieve.
2:153 O you who believe, seek help in steadfastness and prayer; indeed Allah is with the steadfast.
2:154 And do not say of those killed in Allah's way, "Dead." No — they are alive, but you do not perceive.
2:155 And We will surely test you with something of fear and hunger, and loss of wealth and lives and fruits. And give glad tidings to the steadfast,
2:156 those who, when a calamity strikes them, say, "Indeed we belong to Allah, and indeed to Allah we are returning."
2:157 Those — upon them are prayers from their Rabb and mercy, and those are the guided.
2:158 Indeed al-Ṣafā and al-Marwah are among Allah's rites, so whoever makes pilgrimage to the House or performs the visitation, there is no blame on them to go around the two. And whoever volunteers good, then Allah is appreciative, knowing.
2:159 Indeed those who hide what We sent down of clear proofs and guidance after We made it clear to the people in the Kitab — those Allah curses, and the cursers curse them.
2:160 Except those who repent and set right and make clear — those I turn to in forgiveness, and I am the Turning, the Raheem.
2:161 Indeed those who disbelieved and died while they were kafirin — upon them is the curse of Allah and the malakat and the people, all together,
2:162 abiding in it; the punishment will not be lightened for them, nor will they be looked upon.
2:163 Your ilah is one ilah: there is no ilah but Allah, the Rahman, the Raheem.
2:164 Indeed in the creation of the heavens and the earth, and the alternation of night and day, and the ships that run in the sea with what benefits people, and what Allah sent down from the heaven of water and gave life with it to the earth after its death, and scattered in it of every creature, and the directing of the winds, and the clouds subjected between the heaven and the earth — are ayat for a people who understand.
2:165 And among the people are those who take equals apart from Allah, loving them as Allah is loved. But those who believe are stronger in love for Allah. And if only those who wronged could see, when they see the punishment, that all strength belongs to Allah, and that Allah is severe in punishment.
2:166 When those who were followed disavow those who followed them, and they see the punishment, and the means are cut off from them,
2:167 and those who followed say, "If only we had a return, we would disavow them as they disavowed us." Thus Allah shows them their deeds as regrets upon them, and they will not be coming out of the Fire.
2:168 O people, eat of what is in the earth, lawful and good, and do not follow the footsteps of the satan; indeed, to you he is an open enemy.
2:169 He commands you only evil and obscenity, and that you say of Allah what you do not know.
2:170 And when it is said to them, "Follow what Allah sent down," they say, "No, we follow what we found our fathers upon." Even if their fathers understood nothing and were not guided?
2:171 And the likeness of those who disbelieve is the likeness of one who calls out to what hears nothing but a cry and a shout: deaf, dumb, unseeing — so they do not understand.
2:172 O you who believe, eat of the good things We provided you, and be grateful to Allah, if it is Allah you worship.
2:173 Forbidden to you are only carrion, and blood, and the flesh of the pig, and what has been dedicated to other than Allah. But whoever is compelled, without craving or transgressing, there is no sin upon them. Indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:174 Indeed those who hide what Allah sent down of the Kitab and buy with it a small price — those eat into their bellies nothing but fire. And Allah will not speak to them on the Day of Rising, nor purify them, and for them is a painful punishment.
2:175 Those are the ones who bought straying for guidance, and punishment for forgiveness. So how sabir they are for the Fire!
2:176 That is because Allah sent down the Kitab with the truth, and those who differed about the Kitab are in far opposition.
2:177 Goodness is not that you turn your faces toward the east or the west. Rather, goodness is of one who believes in Allah and the Last Day and the malakat and the Kitab and the nabis, and gives wealth, despite love of it, to near kin and orphans and the needy and the wayfarer and the askers, and for the freeing of necks; and keeps up the prayer and gives the alms; and those who fulfill their pledge when they pledge, and the steadfast in hardship and harm and the time of hardship — those are the ones who are true, and those are the muttaqin.
2:178 O you who believe, requital is written upon you for the slain: the free for the free, the slave for the slave, the female for the female. Whoever is pardoned anything by their brother, then follow up with kindness and deliver to him with goodness. That is a lightening from your Rabb and a mercy. Whoever transgresses after that, for them is a painful punishment.
2:179 And in requital for you is life, O people of understanding, so that you may be reverent.
2:180 It is written upon you, when death attends any of you, if they leave behind good: a bequest for the parents and the near kin with kindness — a duty upon the muttaqin.
2:181 Whoever alters it after hearing it, its sin is only upon those who alter it. Indeed Allah is hearing, knowing.
2:182 Whoever fears from the testator a tilt or a sin and sets things right between them — there is no sin upon them. Indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:183 O you who believe, fasting is written upon you as it was written upon those before you, so that you may be reverent.
2:184 Counted days; but whoever among you is sick or on a journey — a number of other days. And upon those who can bear it, a ransom of feeding a needy person. Whoever volunteers good, it is better for them. And that you fast is better for you, if only you knew.
2:185 The month of Ramadan, in which the Recitation was sent down, as guidance for people and clear proofs of the guidance and the criterion. So whoever of you witnesses the month, let them fast it; and whoever is sick or on a journey — a number of other days. Allah intends for you ease and does not intend for you hardship, and so that you complete the number and magnify Allah for what Allah guided you to, and so that you may be grateful.
2:186 And when My servants ask you about Me — indeed I am near. I answer the call of the caller when they call Me. So let them answer Me and believe in Me, so that they may be guided aright.
2:187 Permitted to you on the night of the fast is intimacy with your nisa: they are a garment for you, and you are a garment for them. Allah knew that you betrayed yourselves, so Allah turned to you in forgiveness and passed over you. So now be intimate with them and seek what Allah wrote for you; and eat and drink until the white thread becomes distinct to you from the black thread of dawn. Then complete the fast until nightfall, and do not be intimate with them while you are cleaving to the mosques. Those are Allah's hadud, so do not approach them. Thus Allah makes Allah's ayat clear to people, so that they may be reverent.
2:188 And do not consume your wealth among yourselves in falsehood, and offer it to the judges so as to consume a portion of people's wealth sinfully while you know.
2:189 They ask you about the crescents. Say, "They are appointed times for the people and for the pilgrimage." And goodness is not that you come to the houses from their backs; rather, goodness is of the reverent. So come to the houses by their doors, and be reverent to Allah, so that you may attain success.
2:190 And fight in Allah's way those who fight you, but do not transgress. Indeed Allah does not love the transgressors.
2:191 And kill them wherever you overtake them, and drive them out from where they drove you out; for transgression is worse than killing. And do not fight them at the Sacred Mosque until they fight you in it; but if they fight you, then kill them. Such is the recompense of the kafirin.
2:192 But if they cease, then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:193 And fight them until there is no more transgression and the way is for Allah entirely. But if they cease, then there is no aggression except against the wrongdoers.
2:194 The sacred month is for the sacred month, and the sacred things are requital. Whoever transgresses against you, transgress against them in the like of what they transgressed against you. And be muttaqin to Allah, and know that Allah is with the muttaqin.
2:195 And spend in Allah's way, and do not throw yourselves with your own hands into destruction. And do good; indeed Allah loves the muhsinin.
2:196 And complete the pilgrimage and the visitation for Allah. But if you are confined, then whatever is easy of the offering. And do not shave your heads until the offering reaches its place. Whoever among you is sick or has harm from their head — then a ransom of fasting or charity or a rite. Then when you are secure, whoever enjoys the visitation until the pilgrimage — whatever is easy of the offering. Whoever cannot find it, then a fast of 3 days during the pilgrimage and 7 when you return; that is 10 complete. That is for whoever does not have their people present at the Sacred Mosque. And be reverent to Allah, and know that Allah is severe in punishment.
2:197 The pilgrimage is in well-known months; whoever charges themselves with the pilgrimage in them — no intimacy, and no fusuq, and no dispute in the pilgrimage. And whatever good you do, Allah knows it. And take provision, for the best provision is reverence. And be muttaqin to Me, O people of understanding.
2:198 There is no blame on you to seek bounty from your Rabb. And when you pour down from Arafat, remember Allah at the sacred landmark, and remember Allah as Allah guided you, though before that you were among the strayers.
2:199 Then pour forth from where the people pour forth, and seek Allah's forgiveness. Indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:200 And when you have completed your rites, remember Allah as you remembered your fathers — or with stronger remembrance. Among the people are those who say, "Our Rabb, give us in the nearer life," and for them in the Last there is no share.
2:201 And among them are those who say, "Our Rabb, give us in the nearer life a good, and in the Last a good, and shield us from the punishment of the Fire."
2:202 Those — for them is a portion of what they earned, and Allah is swift in reckoning.
2:203 And remember Allah in counted days. Whoever hastens on in two days — no sin upon them; and whoever delays — no sin upon them, for the reverent. And be reverent to Allah, and know that to Allah you will be gathered.
2:204 And among the people is the one whose speech impresses you in the nearer life, and who calls Allah to witness what is in their heart, while they are the fiercest of contenders.
2:205 And when they turn away, they strive in the earth to corrupt it and destroy the tillage and the offspring. And Allah does not love corruption.
2:206 And when it is said to them, "Be reverent to Allah," arrogance in sin seizes them. So Hell is enough for them — and miserable is the resting place.
2:207 And among the people is one who sells their nafs, seeking Allah's good pleasure, and Allah is gentle-merciful to the servants.
2:208 O you who believe, enter into submission, all together, and do not follow the footsteps of the satan; indeed, to you he is an open enemy.
2:209 But if you slip after the clear proofs have come to you, then know that Allah is mighty, wise.
2:210 Do they wait for anything except that Allah come to them in canopies of cloud, and the malakat, and the matter be decided? And to Allah all matters are returned.
2:211 Ask the Children of Israel how many a clear aya We gave them. And whoever alters Allah's favor after it came to them — indeed Allah is severe in punishment.
2:212 Made attractive for those who disbelieved is the nearer life, and they mock those who believe. But those who were reverent will be above them on the Day of Rising. And Allah provides whomever Allah wills without reckoning.
2:213 People were one community; then Allah raised up the nabis as bringers of glad tidings and warners, and sent down with them the Kitab with the truth, to judge between people in what they differed about it. And none differed about it except those given it, after the clear proofs came to them, out of envy between them. So Allah guided those who believed to the truth of what they differed about it, by Allah's leave. And Allah guides whomever Allah wills to a straight path.
2:214 Or did you suppose you would enter the garden when there had not yet come to you the like of those who passed before you? Hardship and harm touched them, and they were shaken until the Rasul and those who believed with him said, "When is Allah's help?" Truly, Allah's help is near.
2:215 They ask you what to spend. Say, "Whatever good you spend is for parents and near kin and orphans and the needy and the wayfarer. And whatever good you do, indeed Allah is knowing of it."
2:216 Fighting is written upon you though it is hateful to you. And perhaps you hate a thing while it is good for you, and perhaps you love a thing while it is evil for you. And Allah knows, while you do not know.
2:217 They ask you about fighting in the sacred month. Say, "Fighting in it is grave, and barring from Allah's way and disbelief in Allah and the Sacred Mosque, and driving out its people from it, is graver with Allah. And transgression is graver than killing." They will not cease fighting you until they turn you back from your way, if they can. Whoever of you turns back from their way and dies while committing kufr — those, their deeds are void in the nearer life and the Last, and those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
2:218 Indeed those who believed, and those who emigrated and strove in Allah's way — those hope for Allah's mercy. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:219 They ask you about wine and gaming. Say, "In both is grave sin, and benefit for people, but their sin is greater than their benefit." And they ask you what to spend. Say, "The surplus." Thus Allah makes the ayat clear to you, so that you may reflect
2:220 in the nearer life and the Last. And they ask you about the orphans. Say, "Setting right for them is best." And if you mix with them, they are your brothers. And Allah knows the corrupter from the setter-right. Had Allah willed, Allah could have put you in hardship. Indeed Allah is mighty, wise.
2:221 And do not marry the associating nisa until they believe; a believing bondimra'a is better than an imra'a who associates, even if she impresses you. And do not marry the associating men until they believe; a believing slave is better than an associator, even if he impresses you. Those call to the Fire, and Allah calls to the garden and forgiveness, by Allah's leave. And Allah makes the ayat clear to people, so that they may remember.
2:222 And they ask you about the monthly course. Say, "It is harm, so keep away from the nisa during the monthly course, and do not approach them until they are purified. When they have purified themselves, then come to them from where Allah commanded you." Indeed Allah loves the ones who turn back and loves the ones who purify themselves.
2:223 Your nisa are tillage for you, so come to your tillage as you wish, and send ahead for yourselves. And be reverent to Allah, and know that you will meet Allah. And give glad tidings to the muminin.
2:224 And do not make Allah an excuse in your oaths against being good, and reverent, and making things right between the people. And Allah is hearing, knowing.
2:225 Allah does not take you to task for idle talk in your oaths, but takes you to task for what your hearts earned. And Allah is forgiving, forbearing.
2:226 For those who vow off their nisa, a wait of 4 months; then if they turn back, indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
2:227 And if they resolve on divorce, then indeed Allah is hearing, knowing.
2:228 The divorced nisa wait by themselves for 3 monthly courses, and it is not lawful for them to hide what Allah created in their wombs, if they believe in Allah and the Last Day. And their husbands are more entitled to take them back in that, if they want setting right. And for them is the like of what is upon them, with kindness, and for men over them is a degree. And Allah is mighty, wise.
2:229 Divorce is twice; then either kind holding or kind release. And it is not lawful for you to take anything of what you gave them, unless the two fear that they cannot uphold Allah's hadud. If you fear that the two cannot uphold Allah's hadud, there is no blame on the two in what she ransoms herself with. Those are Allah's hadud, so do not transgress them. And whoever transgresses Allah's hadud — those are the wrongdoers.
2:230 So if he divorces her, she is not lawful for him after that until she marries a spouse other than him. And if he divorces her, there is no blame on the two that they turn back to each other, if they think that they can uphold Allah's hadud. And those are Allah's hadud, which Allah makes clear for a people who know.
2:231 And when you divorce nisa and they reach their term, then either hold them with kindness or release them with kindness, and do not hold them in harm to transgress. Whoever does that has wronged themselves. And do not take Allah's ayat in mockery, and remember Allah's favor upon you and what was sent down upon you of the Kitab and the wisdom, with which Allah admonishes you. And be reverent to Allah, and know that Allah is knowing of all things.
2:232 And when you divorce nisa and they reach their term, do not hinder them from marrying their husbands when the two are pleased with each other in kindness. That is admonished with, for whoever among you believes in Allah and the Last Day. That is purer for you and cleaner. And Allah knows, while you do not know.
2:233 The mothers nurse their children two full years, for whoever wants to complete the nursing. And upon the one to whom the child is born is their provision and clothing, with kindness. No self is charged beyond its capacity. No harm shall be done to a mother by her child, nor to a child by its mother. And upon the heir is the like of that. And if the two want weaning, by mutual consent and consultation between them, there is no blame on the two. And if you want to seek nursing for your children, there is no blame on you when you deliver what you gave, with kindness. And be reverent to Allah, and know that Allah is all-seeing of what you do.
2:234 And those of you who are taken in death and leave behind zawj — the nisa wait by themselves 4 months and 10. When they reach their term, there is no blame on you in what they do with themselves with kindness. And Allah is aware of what you do.
2:235 There is no blame on you in what you offer of proposal to nisa, or what you conceal in yourselves. Allah knows that you will remember them. But do not promise them secretly, except that you say a kind word. And do not resolve on the knot of marriage until the waiting-term reaches its term. And know that Allah knows what is in yourselves, so beware of Allah. And know that Allah is forgiving, forbearing.
2:236 There is no blame on you if you divorce nisa when you have not touched them or appointed for them an appointed portion. And provide for them — upon the ample according to their means, and upon the constrained according to their means — a provision with kindness, a duty upon the muhsinin.
2:237 And if you divorce them before you have touched them, but you have appointed for them an appointed portion, then half of what you appointed — unless they waive it, or the one in whose hand is the knot of marriage waives it. And that you waive is nearer to reverence. And do not forget the grace between you. Indeed Allah is all-seeing of what you do.
2:238 Guard the prayers, and the middle prayer, and stand up devoutly for Allah.
2:239 And if you fear, then on foot or riding; then when you are secure, remember Allah as Allah taught you what you did not know.
2:240 And those of you who are taken in death and leave behind zawj — a bequest for their zawj, provision for a year without driving out. But if they leave, there is no blame on you in what they do with themselves of kindness. And Allah is mighty, wise.
2:241 And for the divorced nisa is provision with kindness — a duty upon the muttaqin.
2:242 Thus Allah makes Allah's ayat clear to you, so that you may understand.
2:243 Have you not seen those who went out from their homes by the thousands, wary of death? Allah said to them, "Die," then brought them to life. Indeed Allah is possessor of favor over people, but most people are not grateful.
2:244 And fight in Allah's way, and know that Allah is hearing, knowing.
2:245 Who is it that will lend Allah a goodly loan, so that Allah may multiply it for them many fold? And Allah draws in and spreads out, and to Allah you will be returned.
2:246 Have you not seen the chiefs of the Children of Israel after Musa, when they said to a Nabi of theirs, "Raise up for us a king; we will fight in Allah's way." He said, "Perhaps, if fighting is written upon you, you would not fight?" They said, "Why should we not fight in Allah's way when we have been driven out from our homes and our children?" But when fighting was written upon them, they turned away, except a few of them. And Allah is knowing of the wrongdoers.
2:247 And their Nabi said to them, "Allah has raised up Talut as a king for you." They said, "How can kingship be his over us when we have more right to kingship than him, and he has not been given ample wealth?" He said, "Allah has chosen him over you and increased him abundantly in knowledge and in body. And Allah gives Allah's kingdom to whomever Allah wills, and Allah is all-ample, knowing."
2:248 And their Nabi said to them, "The aya of his kingship is that the chest will come to you, in which is sakina from your Rabb and a remnant of what the family of Musa and the family of Harun left behind; the malakat will carry it. Indeed in that is an aya for you, if you are muminin."
2:249 And when Talut set out with the hosts, he said, "Allah is testing you with a river: whoever drinks from it is not of me, and whoever does not taste it is of me — except one who scoops a scoop in their hand." But they drank from it, except a few of them. And when he crossed it, he and those who believed with him, they said, "We have no strength today against Jalut and his hosts." Those who expected to meet Allah said, "How many a small band has overcome a great band, by Allah's leave! And Allah is with the steadfast."
2:250 And when they came forth against Jalut and his hosts, they said, "Our Rabb, pour steadfastness upon us, steady our feet, and help us against the committing kufr people."
2:251 So they routed them, by Allah's leave, and Dawud killed Jalut. And Allah gave him the kingdom and the wisdom, and taught him of what Allah wills. And if Allah did not push back people by means of others, the earth would be corrupted; but Allah is possessor of favor over the worlds.
2:252 Those are Allah's ayat, which We recite to you with the truth, and you are indeed among the mursalin.
2:253 Those are the Rasuls: We favored some of them over others. Among them are those Allah spoke to, and Allah raised some of them by degrees. And We gave Isa son of Maryam clear proofs and strengthened him with ruh al-qudus. And had Allah willed, those after them would not have fought each other after the clear proofs came to them; but they differed, and among them were those who believed and those who disbelieved. And had Allah willed, they would not have fought each other, but Allah does what Allah wills.
2:254 O you who believe, spend of what We provided you before a day comes in which there is no trade, and no bosom friendship, and no intercession. And the kafirin — they are the wrongdoers.
2:255 Allah — there is no ilah but Allah, the Living, the Upholder. Neither slumber nor sleep overtakes Allah. To Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. Who is it that can intercede with Allah except by Allah's leave? Allah knows what is before them and what is behind them, and they yuhituna nothing of Allah's knowledge except what Allah wills. the kursi of Allah yuhitu the heavens and the earth, and preserving them does not burden Allah. And Allah is the Most High, the Most Great.
2:256 There is no compulsion in the way: the course has become clear from the misguidance. So whoever disbelieves in the false arbiter and believes in Allah has grasped the firmest handhold, with no breaking of it. And Allah is hearing, knowing.
2:257 Allah is the protecting friend of those who believe, bringing them out from darkness into light. And those who disbelieved — their protecting friends are the false arbiters, bringing them out from light into darkness. Those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
2:258 Have you not seen the one who argued with Ibrahim about his Rabb, because Allah gave him the kingdom? When Ibrahim said, "My Rabb is the one who gives life and causes death," he said, "I give life and cause death." Ibrahim said, "Indeed Allah brings the sun from the east, so bring it from the west." So the kafir was stunned. And Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.
2:259 Or like the one who passed by a town while it had collapsed on its raised frames. He said, "How will Allah give life to this after its death?" So Allah made him die a hundred years, then raised him. Allah said, "How long did you tarry?" He said, "I tarried a day or part of a day." Allah said, "No, you tarried a hundred years. So look at your food and your drink — it has not changed. And look at your donkey. And so that We may make you an aya for the people, look at the bones, how We raise them up and then clothe them with flesh." And when it became clear to him, he said, "I know that Allah is over all things able."
2:260 And when Ibrahim said, "Rabb, show me how You give life to the dead." Allah said, "Have you not believed?" He said, "Yes, but so that my heart may be settled." Allah said, "Then take 4 birds and incline them to yourself, then place on every hill a portion of them, then call them — they will come to you swiftly. And know that Allah is mighty, wise."
2:261 The likeness of those who spend their wealth in Allah's way is the likeness of a grain that grew 7 ears; in every ear 100 grains. And Allah multiplies for whomever Allah wills. And Allah is all-ample, knowing.
2:262 Those who spend their wealth in Allah's way, then do not follow up what they spent with favor nor harm — their reward is with their Rabb, and no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
2:263 A kind word and forgiveness are better than charity followed by harm. And Allah is free of need, forbearing.
2:264 O you who believe, do not void your charities with favor and harm, like the one who spends their wealth to be seen of the people and does not believe in Allah and the Last Day. His likeness is the likeness of a smooth rock with dust upon it: a downpour strikes it and leaves it bare. They have no power over anything of what they earned. And Allah does not guide the committing kufr people.
2:265 And the likeness of those who spend their wealth seeking Allah's good pleasure and as a steadying from themselves is the likeness of a garden on high ground: a downpour strikes it, and it brings forth its fruit twofold; and if no downpour strikes it, then dew. And Allah is all-seeing of what you do.
2:266 Would any of you like to have a garden of palms and grapes, with rivers flowing beneath it, for them in it every fruit — and old age strikes them, and they have weak offspring, and a whirlwind with fire in it strikes it, and it burns? Thus Allah makes the ayat clear to you, so that you may reflect.
2:267 O you who believe, spend of the good of what you earned and of what We brought forth for you from the earth, and do not aim for the vile of it to spend, when you would not take it except that you shut your eyes to it. And know that Allah is free of need, praiseworthy.
2:268 The satan promises you need and commands you to obscenity, and Allah promises you forgiveness from that and favor. And Allah is all-ample, knowing.
2:269 The wisdom is given to whomever Allah wills, and whoever is given the wisdom has been given much good. And none remember except people of understanding.
2:270 And whatever expense you spent or vow you vowed — indeed Allah knows it. And the wrongdoers have no helpers.
2:271 If you manifest the charities, how good they are; and if you hide them and give them to the needy, it is better for you, and it will wipe away from you your evil deeds. And Allah is aware of what you do.
2:272 Guiding them is not upon you, but Allah guides whomever Allah wills. And whatever good you spend is for yourselves: you spend only seeking the face of Allah. And whatever good you spend will be repaid to you in full, and you will not be wronged.
2:273 For the needy who are confined in Allah's way, unable to go about in the earth: the ignorant think them rich because of their self-restraint. You know them by their marks — they do not ask people importunately. And whatever good you spend, indeed Allah is knowing of it.
2:274 Those who spend their wealth by night and day, secretly and openly — their reward is with their Rabb, and no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
2:275 Those who devour usury do not stand except as one stands whom the satan throws into convulsion by the touch. That is because they say, "Trade is just like usury." But Allah has made trade lawful and made usury forbidden. Whoever receives an admonition from their Rabb and desists — theirs is what is past, and their matter is with Allah. And whoever returns — those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
2:276 Allah blots out usury and grows charities. And Allah loves not every committing kufr sinner.
2:277 Indeed those who believe and do the good deeds and keep up the prayer and give the alms — their reward is with their Rabb, and no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
2:278 O you who believe, be reverent to Allah and leave what remains of the usury, if you are muminin.
2:279 But if you do not, then take notice of war from Allah and from Allah's Rasul. And if you repent, then yours are the heads of your wealth: you wrong not, and you are not wronged.
2:280 And if one is in hardship, then a waiting until ease. And that you give charity is better for you, if only you knew.
2:281 And beware a day in which you will be returned to Allah; then every self will be paid in full what it earned, and they will not be wronged.
2:282 O you who believe, when you contract a debt to a stated term, then write it down. And let a scribe write it down between you with justice. Let no scribe refuse to write as Allah taught them, so let them write. And let the one upon whom is the right dictate, and let them be reverent to Allah their Rabb, and let them not diminish from it anything. And if the one upon whom is the right is foolish or weak or unable to dictate themselves, then let their guardian dictate with justice. And call two witnesses from your men; but if there are not two men, then a man and two nisa from those you approve of as witnesses, so that if one of the two errs, the one may remind the other. And let the witnesses not refuse when they are called. And do not weary of writing it, small or great, to its term. That is more equitable with Allah, and more upright for testimony, and nearer that you not doubt — unless it be a present trade you circulate among yourselves; then there is no blame on you not to write it. And call witnesses when you trade with one another. And let no scribe be harmed, nor any witness. And if you do that, then it is fusuq on your part. And be reverent to Allah, and Allah teaches you. And Allah is knowing of all things.
2:283 And if you are on a journey and find no scribe, then a pledge taken in hand. And if some of you trust others, then let the one entrusted deliver the amanat and be reverent to Allah their Rabb. And do not hide the testimony. And whoever hides it — then it is a sin of the heart. And Allah is knowing of what you do.
2:284 To Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. Whether you manifest what is in yourselves or hide it, Allah will call you to account for it. Then Allah forgives whomever Allah wills and punishes whomever Allah wills. And Allah is over all things able.
2:285 The Rasul believed in what was sent down to him from his Rabb, and the muminin: each believed in Allah and Allah's malakat and Allah's Kutub and Allah's rusul. We make no division between any of His Rusul. And they said, "We hear and we obey. Your forgiveness, our Rabb — and to You is the arrival."
2:286 Allah does not charge a self beyond its capacity: for it what it earned, and upon it what it acquired. Our Rabb, do not take us to task if we forget or err. Our Rabb, and do not lay upon us a burden as You laid it upon those before us. Our Rabb, and do not lay upon us what we have no strength for. And pass over us, and forgive us, and have mercy on us. You are our protecting friend, so help us against the committing kufr people.
3:1 Alif. Lam. Mim.
3:2 Allah — there is no ilah but Allah, the Living, the Self-Sustaining.
3:3 Allah sent down upon you the Kitab with the truth, confirming what was before Allah's hands, and sent down the Tawrah and the Injil.
3:4 Earlier, as guidance for people, and sent down the Criterion. Truly those who rejected the ayat of Allah — for them is a severe punishment, and Allah is Mighty, Rabb of requital.
3:5 Truly nothing is hidden from Allah in the earth nor in the heaven.
3:6 It is Allah who forms you in the wombs as Allah wills. There is no ilah but Allah, the Mighty, the Wise.
3:7 It is Allah who sent down upon you the Kitab; of it are ayat decisive — those are the mother of the Kitab — and others resembling. As for those in whose hearts is swerving, they follow what resembles of it, seeking dissension and seeking its interpretation; and none knows its interpretation but Allah. And those firm-rooted in knowledge say: We believe in it, all of it is from our Rabb. And none remembers but people of understanding.
3:8 Our Rabb, do not make our hearts swerve after You have guided us, and grant us mercy from Your presence; truly You are the Bestower.
3:9 Our Rabb, it is You who will gather people for a day of which there is no doubt; truly Allah does not break the appointment.
3:10 Truly neither the wealth nor the children of those who rejected will avail them anything against Allah, and those are fuel for the fire.
3:11 Like the way of Fir'awn's people and those before them: they denied our ayat, so Allah seized them for their offenses, and Allah is severe in punishment.
3:12 Say to those who reject: You shall be overcome and gathered into hell, and an evil couch it is.
3:13 There has already been an aya for you in the two bands that met: a band fighting in Allah's path and another rejecting, seeing them twice their number as the eye sees. And Allah strengthens with Allah's help whom Allah wills. Truly in that is a lesson for people of vision.
3:14 Adorned for people is the love of desires: nisa, sons, heaped-up hoards of gold and silver, branded horses, cattle, and tillage. That is the enjoyment of the worldly life, and with Allah is the goodly return.
3:15 Say: Shall I inform you of what is better than that? For those who keep their duty, with their Rabb are gardens with rivers flowing beneath, abiding therein forever, and purified zawj, and good pleasure from Allah. And Allah sees the servants.
3:16 Those who say: Our Rabb, we have believed, so forgive us our offenses and shield us from the punishment of the fire.
3:17 The sabirin, the sadiqin, the qanitin, the munfiqin, and those who seek forgiveness at the dawn hours.
3:18 Allah bears witness that there is no ilah but Allah — and the malakat, and people of knowledge, upholding equity. There is no ilah but Allah, the Mighty, the Wise.
3:19 Truly the religion with Allah is surrender. And those who were given the Kitab did not differ except after knowledge came to them, out of rivalry among themselves. And whoever rejects the ayat of Allah — then Allah is swift in reckoning.
3:20 So if they argue with you, say: I have surrendered my face to Allah, and so has whoever follows me. And say to those who were given the Kitab and to the unlettered: Have you surrendered? So if they have surrendered, they are guided; and if they turn away, then upon you is only the delivering. And Allah sees the servants.
3:21 Truly those who reject the ayat of Allah and kill the nabis without right and kill those among people who command equity — give them tidings of a painful punishment.
3:22 Those are the ones whose deeds are void in the worldly life and the world to come, and they have no helpers.
3:23 Have you not seen those who were given a portion of the Kitab? They are called to the Kitab of Allah that it may judge between them; then a party of them turns away, and they are averse.
3:24 That is because they say: The fire will not touch us except for counted days. And what they used to invent has deceived them in their religion.
3:25 So how will it be when We gather them for a day of which there is no doubt, and every nafs is paid in full what it earned, and they are not wronged?
3:26 Say: O Allah, Owner of kingship, You give kingship to whom You will and strip kingship from whom You will; You strengthen whom You will and abase whom You will. In Your hand is all good. Truly You have power over everything.
3:27 You make the night enter into the day and make the day enter into the night; You bring forth the living from the dead and bring forth the dead from the living; and You provide for whom You will without reckoning.
3:28 The muminin must not take the rejecters as allies apart from the muminin. Whoever does that has nothing with Allah — unless it be that you protect yourselves against them out of protection. And Allah warns you of Allah. And to Allah is the arrival.
3:29 Say: Whether you hide what is in your breasts or manifest it, Allah knows it. And Allah knows what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and Allah has power over everything.
3:30 On the day every nafs finds present what it did of good, and what it did of evil it will wish there were a great distance between itself and that. And Allah warns you of Allah. And Allah is gentle in mercy toward the servants.
3:31 Say: If you love Allah, then follow me; Allah will love you and forgive you your offenses. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
3:32 Say: Obey Allah and the Rasul. But if they turn away, then Allah does not love the rejecters.
3:33 Truly Allah chose Adam and Nuh and the family of Ibrahim and the family of Imran above the worlds.
3:34 Offspring, some of it from some; and Allah hears, knows.
3:35 When the imra'a of Imran said: My Rabb, I have vowed to You what is in my belly, devoted; so accept it from me. Truly You are the Hearing, the Knowing.
3:36 So when she gave birth to her she said: My Rabb, I have given birth to her, a female — and Allah knew best what she had given birth to, and the male is not like the female — and I have named her Maryam, and I seek Your refuge for her and her offspring from the stoned satan.
3:37 So her Rabb accepted her with a good acceptance, and made her grow a good growth, and Zakaria undertook her care. Whenever Zakaria entered the sanctuary upon her he found provision with her. He said: O Maryam, from where is this to you? She said: It is from Allah. Truly Allah provides for whom Allah wills without reckoning.
3:38 There Zakaria called upon his Rabb. He said: My Rabb, grant me from Your presence goodly offspring; truly You hear the call.
3:39 So the malakat called to him while he stood praying in the sanctuary: Allah gives you good tidings of Yahya, confirming a word from Allah, and a master, chaste, and a Nabi of the salihin.
3:40 He said: My Rabb, how shall I have a boy when old age has reached me and my imra'a is barren? Allah said: Thus — Allah does what Allah wills.
3:41 He said: My Rabb, appoint for me an aya. Allah said: Your aya is that you shall not speak to the people for 3 days except by gesture. And remember your Rabb much, and glorify in the evening and the morning.
3:42 And when the malakat said: O Maryam, truly Allah has chosen you and purified you and chosen you above the nisa of the worlds.
3:43 O Maryam, be devout to your Rabb and bow and bow down with those who bow.
3:44 That is from the tidings of the ghayb We reveal to you. And you were not with them when they cast their quills as to which of them should undertake Maryam, and you were not with them when they contended.
3:45 When the malakat said: O Maryam, truly Allah gives you good tidings of a word from Allah whose name is the Messiah, Isa, son of Maryam, honored in the worldly life and the world to come, and among those brought near.
3:46 And Allah will speak to the people in the cradle and in maturity, and is one of the salihin.
3:47 She said: My Rabb, how shall I have a child when no human has touched me? Allah said: Thus — Allah creates what Allah wills. When Allah decrees a matter, Allah only says to it "Be," and it is.
3:48 And Allah will teach him the Kitab and the wisdom and the Tawrah and the Injil.
3:49 And a Rasul to the children of Israel: I have come to you with an aya from your Rabb — that I create for you from clay the form of a bird, then I breathe into it and it becomes a bird by My leave; and I heal the blind from birth and the leprous, and I bring the dead to life by My leave; and I inform you of what you eat and what you store up in your houses. Truly in that is an aya for you, if you are muminin.
3:50 And confirming what was before me of the Tawrah, and to make lawful for you some of what was forbidden to you; and I have come to you with an aya from your Rabb. So keep your duty to Allah and obey me.
3:51 Truly Allah is my Rabb and your Rabb, so serve Allah. This is a straight path.
3:52 So when Isa perceived rejection from them he said: Who will be my helpers to Allah? The disciples said: We are Allah's helpers. We believe in Allah; and bear witness that we are those who surrender.
3:53 Our Rabb, we believe in what You sent down and we follow the Rasul, so write us down among the witnesses.
3:54 And they planned, and Allah planned; and Allah is the best of planners.
3:55 When Allah said: O Isa, I am taking you to Myself and raising you to Me and purifying you of those who rejected, and setting those who follow you above those who rejected until the day of rising. Then to Me is your return, and I shall judge between you concerning what you differed about.
3:56 So as for those who rejected, I shall punish them with a severe punishment in the worldly life and the world to come, and they have no helpers.
3:57 But as for those who believed and did the right deeds, I shall pay them their rewards in full. And Allah does not love the wrongdoers.
3:58 This We recite to you of the ayat and the wise reminder.
3:59 Truly the likeness of Isa with Allah is as the likeness of Adam: Allah created him from dust, then said to him "Be," and he was.
3:60 The truth is from your Rabb, so do not be of the doubters.
3:61 Whoever argues with you about it after the knowledge that has come to you, then say: Come, let us call our sons and your sons, our nisa and your nisa, our nafs and your nafs; then let us pray humbly, and we shall make the curse of Allah upon the kazibin.
3:62 Truly this is the true narrative, and there is no ilah but Allah; and truly Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
3:63 So if they turn away — then Allah knows the corrupters.
3:64 Say: O people of the Kitab, come to a word equal between us and you: that we serve none but Allah, and ascribe nothing as partner to Allah, and that some of us do not take others as rabbs apart from Allah. So if they turn away, say: Bear witness that we are those who surrender.
3:65 O people of the Kitab, why do you argue about Ibrahim when the Tawrah and the Injil were not sent down except after him? Do you not understand?
3:66 Here you are, arguing about what you have knowledge of — so why do you argue about what you have no knowledge of? And Allah knows, and you do not know.
3:67 Ibrahim was not a Jew nor Nasara, but he was upright, surrendered, and not among those who ascribe partners.
3:68 Truly the people closest to Ibrahim are those who followed him, and this Nabi, and those who believe; and Allah is the ally of the muminin.
3:69 A faction of the people of the Kitab would love to lead you astray, but they lead astray none but themselves, and they do not perceive.
3:70 O people of the Kitab, why do you reject the ayat of Allah while you witness?
3:71 O people of the Kitab, why do you clothe truth with falsehood and hide the truth while you know?
3:72 And a faction of the people of the Kitab say: Believe in what was sent down on those who believe at the beginning of the day, and reject at its end — perhaps they may return.
3:73 And believe only those who follow your religion. Say: Truly the guidance is Allah's guidance — that anyone should be given the like of what you were given, or that they should argue with you before your Rabb. Say: Truly the bounty is in Allah's hand; Allah gives it to whom Allah wills, and Allah is all-ample, knowing.
3:74 Allah singles out for Allah's mercy whom Allah wills; and Allah is the Owner of tremendous bounty.
3:75 Among the people of the Kitab is the one who, if you entrust him with a hoard, pays it back to you; and among them is the one who, if you entrust him with a single coin, does not pay it back to you unless you keep standing over him. That is because they say: There is no charge upon us regarding the unlettered. And they speak falsehood against Allah while they know.
3:76 Rather, whoever fulfills his pledge and keeps his duty — then Allah loves the dutiful.
3:77 Truly those who sell Allah's pledge and their oaths for a small price — those have no portion in the world to come, and Allah will not speak to them nor look at them on the day of rising, nor purify them, and for them is a painful punishment.
3:78 And indeed among them is a party who twist their tongues with the Kitab so that you may reckon it part of the Kitab, though it is not part of the Kitab; and they say: It is from Allah, when it is not from Allah; and they speak falsehood against Allah while they know.
3:79 It was not for any human that Allah should give him the Kitab, the judgment, and the Nabihood, then he should say to the people: Be servants to me apart from Allah; but rather: Be teachers, devout, by what you know of the Kitab and by what you study.
3:80 And he would not command you to take the malakat and the nabis as rabbs. Would he command you to rejection after you have surrendered?
3:81 And when Allah took the pledge of the nabis: Whatever I give you of Kutub and wisdom, then a Rasul comes to you confirming what is with you, you shall surely believe in him and surely help him. Allah said: Do you affirm and take My bond upon that? They said: We affirm. Allah said: Then bear witness, and I am with you among the witnesses.
3:82 Then whoever turns away after that — those are the fasiqun.
3:83 Is it other than Allah's religion that they seek, when to Allah has surrendered whatever is in the heavens and the earth, willingly or unwillingly, and to Allah they are returned?
3:84 Say: We believe in Allah and what was sent down on us, and what was sent down on Ibrahim and Ismail and Ishaq and Yaqub and the tribes, and what was given to Musa and Isa and the nabis from their Rabb. We make no division between any of them, and to Allah we are those who surrender.
3:85 Whoever seeks other than surrender as religion, it will not be accepted from him, and in the world to come he is among the khasirin.
3:86 How shall Allah guide a people who rejected after their faith, and bore witness that the Rasul is true, and clear proofs came to them? And Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.
3:87 Those — their reward is that upon them rests the curse of Allah, the malakat, and all people together.
3:88 Abiding therein; the punishment will not be lightened for them, nor will they be looked at.
3:89 Except those who repented after that and made right — then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
3:90 Truly those who rejected after their faith, then increased in rejection — their repentance will not be accepted, and those are the ones astray.
3:91 Truly those who rejected and died while they were rejecters — not one of them will be accepted even if he offered a fill of the earth in gold as ransom. Those have a painful punishment, and they have no helpers.
3:92 You will never reach goodness until you spend of what you love; and whatever you spend of anything, then Allah knows it.
3:93 All food was lawful to the children of Israel, except what Israel forbade to himself before the Tawrah was sent down. Say: Bring the Tawrah and recite it, if you are sadiq.
3:94 So whoever invents falsehood against Allah after that — those are the wrongdoers.
3:95 Say: Allah speaks truth, so follow the creed of Ibrahim, upright; and he was not among those who ascribe partners.
3:96 Truly the first house placed for people is the one at Bakka, blessed and a guidance for the worlds.
3:97 In it are clear ayat — the station of Ibrahim — and whoever enters it is secure. And owed to Allah from people is the pilgrimage to the house, for whoever can find a way to it. And whoever rejects — truly Allah has no need of the worlds.
3:98 Say: O people of the Kitab, why do you reject the ayat of Allah, when Allah is witness to what you do?
3:99 Say: O people of the Kitab, why do you bar from Allah's path those who believe, seeking to make it crooked, while you are witnesses? And Allah is not unmindful of what you do.
3:100 O you who believe, if you obey a party of those who were given the Kitab, they will turn you back after your faith into rejecters.
3:101 And how could you reject when the ayat of Allah are recited to you and among you is Allah's Rasul? Whoever holds fast to Allah is guided to a straight path.
3:102 O you who believe, keep your duty to Allah with the duty due to Allah, and do not die except as those who surrender.
3:103 And hold fast all together to the rope of Allah and do not divide. And remember Allah's favor upon you: when you were enemies, and Allah joined your hearts, so by Allah's favor you became brothers; and you were on the brink of a pit of the fire and Allah rescued you from it. Thus Allah makes the ayat clear to you, that perhaps you may be guided.
3:104 And let there be among you a community calling to the good and commanding what is recognized and forbidding what is rejected; and those are the muflihin.
3:105 And do not be like those who divided and differed after clear proofs came to them; and those have a tremendous punishment.
3:106 On the day when faces are whitened and faces are blackened. As for those whose faces are blackened: Did you reject after your faith? Then taste the punishment for what you were rejecting.
3:107 But as for those whose faces are whitened — they are in Allah's mercy, abiding therein forever.
3:108 Those are the ayat of Allah We recite to you in truth; and Allah does not intend wrong for the worlds.
3:109 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth, and to Allah all affairs are returned.
3:110 You were the best community brought forth for people: you command what is recognized, forbid what is rejected, and believe in Allah. And if the people of the Kitab had believed, it would have been better for them. Among them are muminin, but most of them are fasiqun.
3:111 They will never harm you except with hurt; and if they fight you, they will turn their backs to you, then they will not be helped.
3:112 Abasement has been struck upon them wherever they are found, except through a rope from Allah and a rope from people; and they have met with wrath from Allah, and poverty has been struck upon them.
