That is because they rejected the ayat of Allah and killed the nabis without right. That is because they disobeyed and used to transgress.
3:113 They are not all alike. Among the people of the Kitab is a community standing upright, reciting the ayat of Allah through the night hours while they bow down.
3:114 They believe in Allah and the last day, and command what is recognized and forbid what is rejected, and hasten to good deeds; and those are among the salihin.
3:115 And whatever they do of good, they will not be disbelieved it; and Allah knows the dutiful.
3:116 Truly neither the wealth nor the children of those who rejected will avail them anything against Allah, and those are the companions of the fire, abiding therein forever.
3:117 The likeness of what they spend in this worldly life is as a likeness of a wind in which is a biting chill; it struck the tillage of a people who wronged themselves and destroyed it. And Allah did not wrong them, but they wronged themselves.
3:118 O you who believe, do not take as intimates other than yourselves; they will not stint you corruption. They love what distresses you. Hatred has already shown from their mouths, and what their breasts hide is greater. The ayat have been made clear to you — if you use your understanding.
3:119 There you are, loving them, while they do not love you, and you believe in the whole Kitab. When they meet you they say: We believe; but when they are alone they bite at you the fingertips from rage. Say: Die in your rage! Truly Allah knows what the breasts hide.
3:120 If a good thing touches you, it grieves them; and if a misfortune befalls you, they rejoice at it. And if you are sabir and keep your duty, their scheme will not harm you anything. Truly Allah is muhit over what they do.
3:121 And when you set out from your family at morning, settling the muminin in their places for the battle — and Allah hears, knows.
3:122 When two parties of you resolved to lose heart, and Allah was their ally; and upon Allah let the muminin rely.
3:123 Allah has already helped you at Badr when you were abased. So keep your duty to Allah, that perhaps you may give thanks.
3:124 When you said to the muminin: Will it not suffice you that your Rabb supply you with 3,000 malakat sent down?
3:125 Yes — if you are sabir and keep your duty, and they come at you suddenly like this, your Rabb will supply you with 5,000 malakat marked.
3:126 And Allah made it nothing but good tidings for you, so that your hearts might be settled by it; and help is none but from Allah, the Mighty, the Wise.
3:127 That Allah may cut off a fringe of those who reject, or overwhelm them so they turn back disappointed.
3:128 You have no say in the matter: whether Allah turns to them or punishes them — for they are wrongdoers.
3:129 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth: Allah forgives whom Allah wills and punishes whom Allah wills. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
3:130 O you who believe, do not devour usury, doubled and multiplied; and keep your duty to Allah, that perhaps you may succeed.
3:131 And keep your duty to the fire prepared for the rejecters.
3:132 And obey Allah and the Rasul, that perhaps you may be shown mercy.
3:133 And hasten to forgiveness from your Rabb and a garden whose width is the heavens and the earth, prepared for the dutiful.
3:134 Those who spend in ease and hardship, and hold in their rage, and pass over the people; and Allah loves the muhsinin.
3:135 And those who, when they commit an obscenity or wrong themselves, remember Allah and seek forgiveness for their offenses — and who forgives the offenses but Allah? — and do not persist in what they did while they know.
3:136 Those — their reward is forgiveness from their Rabb and gardens with rivers flowing beneath, abiding therein forever. And an excellent reward for the workers.
3:137 Ways have passed before you; so journey in the earth and look how was the end of the mukazzibin.
3:138 This is a clarification for people, and a guidance and admonition for the dutiful.
3:139 And do not weaken nor grieve; you are the uppermost, if you are muminin.
3:140 If a wound touches you, a wound the like of it has already touched the people; and such days We alternate among the people — and so that Allah may know those who believe, and take witnesses from you. And Allah does not love the wrongdoers.
3:141 And so that Allah may purify those who believe and erase the rejecters.
3:142 Or did you reckon you would enter the garden while Allah has not yet known those who strove among you and known the sabirin?
3:143 You used to wish for death before you met it; so you have seen it while you were looking on.
3:144 Muhammad is nothing but a Rasul; rusul have passed before him. So if he dies or is killed, will you turn back on your heels? Whoever turns back on his heels will not harm Allah anything, and Allah will repay the thankful.
3:145 No nafs can die except by Allah's leave — a fixed term prescribed. And whoever desires the reward of this world, We give him of it; and whoever desires the reward of the world to come, We give him of it; and We will repay the thankful.
3:146 And how many a Nabi was killed, with whom were many rabbiyyin; and they did not weaken for what befell them in Allah's path, and did not weaken and did not submit. And Allah loves the sabirin.
3:147 And their word was only that they said: Our Rabb, forgive us our offenses and our extravagance in our affair, and make firm our feet and help us against the rejecting people.
3:148 So Allah gave them the reward of the world and the goodly reward of the world to come; and Allah loves the muhsinin.
3:149 O you who believe, if you obey those who reject, they will turn you back on your heels and you will turn back as losers.
3:150 No — Allah is your ally; and Allah is the best of helpers.
3:151 We shall cast terror into the hearts of those who reject, for what they ascribed to Allah of partners for which Allah sent down no warrant; and their destination is the fire — and an evil lodging for the wrongdoers.
3:152 Allah has indeed been true to you in the promise when you were crushing them by Allah's leave, until when you lost heart and quarreled about the affair and disobeyed after Allah showed you what you love. Among you are those who desire the world, and among you are those who desire the world to come. Then Allah turned you away from them to test you; and Allah has indeed passed over you, and Allah is bounteous to the muminin.
3:153 When you were climbing and not turning to anyone, while the Rasul was calling you from behind you, so Allah rewarded you with grief upon grief, that you might not grieve for what escaped you nor for what befell you. And Allah is well aware of what you do.
3:154 Then after the grief Allah sent down upon you security — a slumber overcoming a party of you, while a party had their nafs trouble them, thinking of Allah other than the truth — the thought of the pagan way. They say: Is there any part in the affair for us? Say: Truly the whole affair belongs to Allah. They hide in themselves what they do not disclose to you, saying: If we had any part in the affair, we would not have been killed here. Say: If you had been in your houses, those for whom killing was decreed would have marched out to their resting-places. And so that Allah may test what is in your breasts and purify what is in your hearts; and Allah knows what the breasts hide.
3:155 Truly those of you who turned away on the day the two hosts met — it was only the satan who made them stumble for some of what they earned; and Allah has indeed passed over them. Truly Allah is forgiving, forbearing.
3:156 O you who believe, do not be like those who rejected and said of their brothers when they journeyed in the land or were raiding: If they had been with us, they would not have died and would not have been killed — so that Allah may make that a regret in their hearts. And Allah gives life and causes death; and Allah sees what you do.
3:157 And if you are killed in Allah's path or die — forgiveness from Allah and mercy are better than what they amass.
3:158 And whether you die or are killed, to Allah you shall be gathered.
3:159 So by mercy from Allah you were gentle to them; and had you been harsh, hard of heart, they would have scattered from around you. So pardon them and seek forgiveness for them and consult them in the affair; then when you resolve, rely upon Allah. Truly Allah loves those who rely.
3:160 If Allah helps you, none can overcome you; and if Allah forsakes you, who is it that can help you after that? And upon Allah let the muminin rely.
3:161 And it is not for a Nabi to embezzle; and whoever embezzles shall bring what he embezzled on the day of rising. Then every nafs is paid in full what it earned, and they will not be wronged.
3:162 So is one who follows the good pleasure of Allah like one who meets with wrath from Allah, whose destination is hell? And an evil arrival it is.
3:163 They are degrees in Allah's sight, and Allah sees what they do.
3:164 Allah has indeed bestowed a favor upon the muminin when Allah raised up among them a Rasul from themselves, reciting Allah's ayat to them and purifying them and teaching them the Kitab and the wisdom, though before that they were in manifest error.
3:165 Or when a misfortune befell you — you had struck a misfortune the like of it — you said: From where is this? Say: It is from yourselves. Truly Allah has power over everything.
3:166 And whatever befell you on the day the two hosts met was by Allah's leave, and so that Allah may know the muminin.
3:167 And so that Allah may know the munafiqin: and it was said to them: Come, fight in Allah's path or repel. They said: If we knew fighting, we would have followed you. They were closer to rejection that day than to faith. They say with their mouths what is not in their hearts, and Allah knows best what they hide.
3:168 Those who said of their brothers, while they sat back: If they had obeyed us, they would not have been killed. Say: Then ward off death from yourselves, if you are sadiq.
3:169 And do not reckon those killed in Allah's path as dead; rather, they are alive with their Rabb, provided for.
3:170 Rejoicing in what Allah has given them of bounty, and rejoicing for those who have not yet joined them from behind them: no fear is upon them, nor do they grieve.
3:171 Rejoicing at a blessing from Allah and bounty, and that Allah does not waste the reward of the muminin.
3:172 Those who answered Allah and the Rasul after the wound struck them — for those of them who did good and kept their duty is a tremendous reward.
3:173 Those to whom people said: The people have gathered against you, so fear them; but it only increased them in faith, and they said: Allah is sufficient for us, and an excellent guardian is Allah.
3:174 So they returned with a blessing from Allah and bounty; no harm touched them, and they followed the good pleasure of Allah; and Allah is the Owner of tremendous bounty.
3:175 That is only the satan frightening his allies. So do not fear them, and fear Me — if you are muminin.
3:176 And do not grieve for those who hasten into rejection; they will not harm Allah anything. Allah intends to give them no portion in the world to come, and for them is a tremendous punishment.
3:177 Truly those who purchased rejection in exchange for faith will not harm Allah anything, and for them is a painful punishment.
3:178 And let not the rejecters reckon that Our respite to them is good for themselves; We only respite them so that they may increase in sin, and for them is a humiliating punishment.
3:179 It is not for Allah to leave the muminin upon what you are upon until Allah distinguishes the vile from the good; nor is Allah to give you access to the ghayb. But Allah chooses from among the Rasuls whom Allah wills. So believe in Allah and Allah's rusul; and if you believe and keep your duty, then for you is a tremendous reward.
3:180 And let not those who stint reckon that what Allah has given them of bounty that they stint is good for them; no, it is evil for them. They shall be collared with what they stinted on the day of rising. And to Allah belongs the inheritance of the heavens and the earth, and Allah is well aware of what you do.
3:181 Allah has indeed heard the saying of those who say: Allah is poor and we are rich. We shall write down what they say and their killing of the nabis without right, and We shall say: Taste the punishment of the burning.
3:182 That is for what your hands have sent ahead, and that Allah is not zalimun to the servants.
3:183 Those who said: Allah has pledged to us that we should not believe any Rasul until one brings us an offering which the fire consumes. Say: Rusul came to you before me with clear proofs and with what you speak of — so why did you kill them, if you are sadiq?
3:184 But if they deny you — so rusul were denied before you, who came with clear proofs and the Kitab and the enlightening Kitab.
3:185 Every nafs tastes death; and only on the day of rising are you paid your rewards in full. Whoever is drawn far from the fire and admitted to the garden has indeed won. And this worldly life is nothing but the enjoyment of deception.
3:186 You shall surely be tested in your wealth and your nafs, and you shall surely hear much hurt from those who were given the Kitab before you and from those who ascribe partners. And if you are sabir and keep your duty — truly that is among the resolute affairs.
3:187 And when Allah took the pledge of those who were given the Kitab: You shall make it clear to the people and not hide it. But they cast it behind their backs and sold it for a small price. And an evil purchase it is.
3:188 Do not reckon those who rejoice in what they have brought and love to be praised for what they did not do — do not reckon them secure from the punishment; and for them is a painful punishment.
3:189 And to Allah belongs the kingship of the heavens and the earth, and Allah has power over everything.
3:190 Truly in the creation of the heavens and the earth and the alternation of night and day are ayat for people of understanding.
3:191 Those who remember Allah standing and sitting and on their sides, and reflect upon the creation of the heavens and the earth: Our Rabb, You did not create this in vain; glory be to You; so protect us from the punishment of the fire.
3:192 Our Rabb, whoever You admit into the fire — You have disgraced them; and the wrongdoers have no helpers.
3:193 Our Rabb, we heard a caller calling to faith: Believe in your Rabb — and we believed. Our Rabb, so forgive us our offenses and wipe away our evil deeds, and take us to Yourself with the abrar.
3:194 Our Rabb, and give us what You promised us through Your rusul, and do not disgrace us on the day of rising. Truly You do not break the promise.
3:195 So their Rabb answered them: I do not waste the work of any worker among you, whether male or female — some of you are from others. As for those who emigrated and were driven out of their homes and were hurt in My path, and fought and were killed — I shall surely wipe away their evil deeds from them and surely admit them into gardens with rivers flowing beneath, a reward from Allah; and with Allah is the excellent reward.
3:196 Let not the moving about of the rejecters in the towns deceive you.
3:197 A little enjoyment, then their destination is hell — and an evil couch it is.
3:198 But for those who keep their duty to their Rabb are gardens with rivers flowing beneath, abiding therein forever — hospitality from Allah; and what is with Allah is better for the salihin.
3:199 And indeed among the people of the Kitab are those who believe in Allah and what was sent down to you and what was sent down to them, humbled before Allah. They do not sell the ayat of Allah for a small price. Those have their reward with their Rabb; truly Allah is swift in reckoning.
3:200 O you who believe, be of the sabirin and vie in patience and hold together, and keep your duty to Allah, that perhaps you may succeed.
4:1 O humankind, be mindful of your Rabb who created you from one nafs, and created from it its mate, and scattered from the two many men and nisa. And be mindful of Allah, in whose name you demand from one another, and of the ties of kinship. Truly Allah has been a watcher over you.
4:2 And give the orphans their wealth, and do not substitute the vile for the good, and do not devour their wealth into your wealth. Truly that has been a tremendous sin.
4:3 And if you fear that you cannot act equitably toward the orphans, then marry the nisa who seem good to you, 2, 3, or 4. But if you fear that you will not be just, then one only, or what your right hands own. That is nearer, that you not act unjustly.
4:4 And give the nisa their due portions gladly; but if they are pleased to give you back some of it willingly, then consume it with enjoyment and wholesome ease.
4:5 And do not give the foolish your wealth which Allah has made a mainstay for you. Provide for them from it, clothe them, and speak to them kind words.
4:6 Test the orphans until, when they reach marriageable age, if you perceive right course in them, hand over to them their wealth. Do not devour it extravagantly and hastily for fear they will grow up. Whoever is rich, let him abstain; whoever is needy, let him consume what is fitting. And when you hand over to them their wealth, call witnesses over them. And sufficient is Allah as reckoner.
4:7 For men is an allotted share of what parents and near kin leave behind, and for nisa is an allotted share of what parents and near kin leave behind, whether it is little or much — an ordained share.
4:8 And when the division is at hand and the near kin, the orphans, and the needy are present, provide for them from it and speak to them kind words.
4:9 And let those have awe who, if they were to leave behind them weak offspring, would fear for them. So let them be mindful of Allah and speak a straight saying.
4:10 Truly those who devour the wealth of orphans wrongfully only devour fire into their bellies, and they shall enter a blaze.
4:11 Allah charges you concerning your children: for the male, the share of 2 females. If they are nisa, more than 2, then for them two-thirds of what he left; and if she is one, then for her the half. And for his parents, for each one of the two a sixth of what he left, if he has a child. If he has no child and his parents inherit him, then for his mother a third. And if he has brothers, then for his mother a sixth — after any charge to be enjoined or a debt. Your parents and your children: you know not which of them is nearer to you in benefit. An ordinance from Allah. Truly Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:12 And for you is half of what your zawj leave, if they have no child. But if they have a child, then for you a fourth of what they leave — after any charge they enjoin or a debt. And for them a fourth of what you leave, if you have no child; but if you have a child, then for them an eighth of what you leave — after any charge you enjoin or a debt. And if a man or an imra'a die leaving neither parent nor child, and he has a brother or sister, then for each one of the two a sixth; but if they are more than that, then they are partners in the third — after any charge to be enjoined or a debt, without harm. A charge from Allah. And Allah is knowing, forbearing.
4:13 Those are the hadud set by Allah. Whoever obeys Allah and the Rasul, Allah will admit him into gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. And that is the tremendous triumph.
4:14 And whoever disobeys Allah and the Rasul and oversteps hadud set by Allah, Allah will admit him into a fire, abiding therein forever, and for him is a humiliating punishment.
4:15 As for those of your nisa who come to an obscenity, call 4 of you to witness against them. If they testify, then confine them to the houses until death takes them, or Allah makes for them a way.
4:16 And the two who commit it among you, then do them harm. But if they repent and set right, then turn away from them. Truly Allah has been ever-turning, merciful.
4:17 Turning to Allah is only for those who do evil in ignorance, then turn soon after. Those — Allah turns to them. And Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:18 But turning is not for those who do evils until, when death presents itself to one of them, he says, "I turn now" — nor for those who die while committing kufr. Those — We have prepared for them a painful punishment.
4:19 O muminin, it is not lawful for you to inherit nisa against their will, and do not hold them back to take away part of what you gave them, unless they come with a clear obscenity. And live with them in kindness. If you are averse to them, perhaps you are averse to something in which Allah places much good.
4:20 And if you wish to replace one spouse in place of another, and you have given one of them a great sum, then do not take anything from it. Would you take it by slander and manifest sin?
4:21 How could you take it when some of you have gone in to others, and they have taken from you a solemn pact?
4:22 And do not marry nisa whom your fathers married — except what has already passed. Truly that was an obscenity and a loathing, and an evil way.
4:23 Forbidden to you are your mothers, your daughters, your sisters, your paternal aunts, your maternal aunts, your brother's daughters, your sister's daughters, your mothers who suckled you, your suckling sisters, your mothers-in-law, your stepdaughters who are in your keeping from the nisa you have gone in to — but if you have not gone in to them, there is no blame on you — and the nisa of your sons who are from your loins; and that you gather together two sisters, except what has already passed. Truly Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:24 And the fortified among nisa, except what your right hands own — that Allah has decreed upon you. And permitted to you, beyond that, is that you seek with your wealth as muhsinin, not as musifhin. Then whatever enjoyment you take from them, give them their ordained wages; and there is no blame on you in what you mutually agree upon after the ordinance. Truly Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:25 Whoever among you lacks the means to marry the believing fortified nisa, then from what your right hands own of your believing young nisa — and Allah knows best your faith, some of you from others. So marry them with the leave of their families, and give them their wages in kindness, fortified, not licentious, nor taking secret lovers. Then if they are fortified and come with an obscenity, upon them is half of the punishment upon the fortified nisa. That is for whoever among you fears hardship. And that you be of the sabirin is better for you. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
4:26 Allah wills to make clear to you and to guide you to the ways of those before you, and to turn to you. And Allah is knowing, wise.
4:27 And Allah wills to turn to you, but those who follow their desires wish you to deviate with a great deviation.
4:28 Allah wills to lighten the burden upon you, for humankind was created weak.
4:29 O muminin, do not devour your wealth among yourselves falsely, unless it be trade by mutual agreement among you. And do not kill one another. Truly Allah has been merciful to you.
4:30 Whoever does that in aggression and wrong, We shall make him enter a fire. And that is easy for Allah.
4:31 If you avoid the great sins of what you are forbidden, We will remit from you your evils and admit you into a noble entrance.
4:32 And do not wish for that by which Allah has favored some of you over others. For men is a share of what they earn, and for nisa is a share of what they earn. And ask Allah of bounty. Truly Allah has been knowing of everything.
4:33 And for each We have appointed heirs of what parents and near kin leave behind, and those whom your right hands have pledged — so give them their share. Truly Allah has been witness over everything.
4:34 Men are maintainers of nisa by what Allah has favored some of them over others, and by what they spend of their wealth. So the salihin nisa are devout, guarding in absence what Allah has guarded. As for those whose defiance you fear, admonish them, forsake them in the beds, and strike them. If they obey you, seek no way against them. Truly Allah has been high, great.
4:35 And if you fear a split between the two, send forth an arbiter from his family and an arbiter from her family. If the two intend reconciliation, Allah will bring accord between them. Truly Allah has been knowing, aware.
4:36 Serve Allah and associate nothing with Allah. And to parents do goodness, and to near kin, the orphans, the needy, the near neighbor, the neighbor beside you, the companion beside you, the wayfarer, and what your right hands own. Truly Allah loves not whoever is conceited, vaunting.
4:37 Those who stint and bid people to stint, and hide what Allah has given them of bounty — We have prepared for the unmuminin a humiliating punishment.
4:38 And those who spend their wealth to be seen by people, and do not believe in Allah or the Last Day. Whoever has Shaytan as a companion — how evil a companion.
4:39 What would it harm them to believe in Allah and the Last Day, and to spend of what Allah has provided them? And Allah has been knowing of them.
4:40 Truly Allah does not wrong the weight of a mote. And if it be a good deed, Allah doubles it and gives from Allah's presence a great reward.
4:41 So how will it be when We bring from every community a witness, and We bring you as a witness against these?
4:42 On that day, those who disbelieved and disobeyed the Rasul will wish the earth were leveled over them, and they will hide no hadith from Allah.
4:43 O muminin, do not approach the prayer while in a stupor, until you know what you are saying, nor in a state of impurity — unless merely passing along a way — until you wash. And if you are ill, or on a journey, or one of you comes from the privy, or you have touched nisa, and found no water, then aim toward good surface-soil and wipe your faces and your hands. Truly Allah has been ever-passing-over, forgiving.
4:44 Have you not seen those given a share of the Kitab buying error and wishing that you lose the way?
4:45 Allah knows best your enemies. And sufficient is Allah as a protecting-friend, and sufficient is Allah as a helper.
4:46 Some of the Yahud shift words from their places and say, "We hear and we disobey," and "Hear, without hearing," and "Be our keeper" — a twisting with their tongues and a stabbing at the faith. If only they had said, "We hear and we obey," and "Hear," and "Look upon us," it would have been better for them and more upright. But Allah has cursed them for their unbelief, so they do not believe except a few.
4:47 O you who were given the Kitab, believe in what We have sent down, confirming what is with you, before We blot out faces and turn them to their backs, or curse them as We cursed the people of the Sabbath. And the command of Allah has ever been done.
4:48 Truly Allah does not forgive that one associate with Allah, and forgives what is below that for whom Allah wills. Whoever associates with Allah has fabricated a tremendous sin.
4:49 Have you not seen those who purify themselves? Rather, it is Allah who purifies whom Allah wills, and they are not wronged so much as a fiber.
4:50 See how they fabricate lies upon Allah — and sufficient is that as a manifest sin.
4:51 Have you not seen those given a share of the Kitab believing in idols and the false-arbiter, and saying of those who disbelieved, "These are better guided than the muminin" in way?
4:52 Those are the ones Allah has cursed. And whomever Allah curses, you will never find a helper for him.
4:53 Or do they have a share in the kingdom? If so, they would give people not so much as a speck.
4:54 Or do they envy people for what Allah has given them of bounty? We have given the family of Ibrahim the Kitab and wisdom, and We have given them a great kingdom.
4:55 Among them are some who believed in it, and among them are some who turned away from it. And sufficient is Jahannam as a blaze.
4:56 Truly those who disbelieve in Our ayat, We shall make them enter a fire. Whenever their skins are roasted through, We will replace them with other skins, so they may taste the punishment. Truly Allah has been mighty, wise.
4:57 And those who believe and do salih deeds, We shall admit them into gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. For them therein are purified mates, and We shall admit them into deep shade.
4:58 Truly Allah commands you to deliver the amanats to their rightful people, and when you judge among people, that you judge with justice. How excellent is that whereby Allah admonishes you. Truly Allah has been hearing, seeing.
4:59 O muminin, obey Allah and obey the Rasul and those in authority among you. If you dispute over anything, refer it to Allah and the Rasul, if you believe in Allah and the Last Day. That is better and a better outcome.
4:60 Have you not seen those who claim that they believe in what was sent down to you and what was sent down before you, yet wish to seek judgment from the false-arbiter when they were commanded to disbelieve in it? And Shaytan wishes to lead them far astray.
4:61 And when it is said to them, "Come to what Allah has sent down and to the Rasul," you see the munafiqin turning from you in sheer aversion.
4:62 So how is it that when a calamity befalls them for what their hands have put forth, they come to you swearing by Allah, "We intended only goodness and right guidance"?
4:63 Those are the ones whose hearts Allah knows what is in. So turn away from them, admonish them, and say to them about themselves a word that strikes home.
4:64 We did not send any Rasul except to be obeyed, by Allah's leave. If only, when they wronged themselves, they had come to you and sought forgiveness from Allah, and the Rasul had sought forgiveness for them, they would have found Allah ever-turning, merciful.
4:65 But no, by your Rabb, they will not believe until they make you judge in what arises between them, then find no constraint within themselves at what you decreed, and submit in full submission.
4:66 If We had decreed upon them, "Kill yourselves," or "Leave your homes," they would not have done it, except a few of them. If only they had done what they were admonished to, it would have been better for them and firmer in steadfastness.
4:67 And then We would surely have given them from Our presence a great reward.
4:68 And We would have guided them to a straight path.
4:69 Whoever obeys Allah and the Rasul, those are with the ones upon whom Allah has bestowed favor — among the nabis, the sadiqin, the shahids, and the salihin. And excellent are those as companions.
4:70 That is bounty from Allah, and sufficient is Allah as knower.
4:71 O muminin, take your precaution, then go forth in detachments or go forth all together.
4:72 Among you is one who lags behind; then if a calamity befalls you, he says, "Allah has favored me, that I was not with them as a witness."
4:73 And if a bounty from Allah befalls you, he will surely say — as if there had been no affection between you and him — "If only I had been with them, I would have won a tremendous triumph."
4:74 So let those fight in the way of Allah who sell the worldly life for the Last. Whoever fights in the way of Allah, whether killed or overcoming, We shall surely give him a great reward.
4:75 What is wrong with you that you do not fight in the way of Allah and for the weak among men, nisa, and children who say, "Our Rabb, bring us out of this town whose people are wrongdoers, and appoint for us from Your presence a protector, and appoint for us from Your presence a helper"?
4:76 Those who believe fight in the way of Allah, and those who disbelieve fight in the way of the false-arbiter. So fight the allies of Shaytan. Truly the scheme of Shaytan has been weak.
4:77 Have you not seen those to whom it was said, "Restrain your hands, establish the prayer, and give the purifying dues"? Then when fighting was decreed upon them, a group of them feared people with a fear of Allah — or greater still. And they said, "Our Rabb, why have You decreed fighting upon us? If only You had postponed us to a near term." Say: The enjoyment of the worldly life is little, and the Last is better for whoever is reverent — and you will not be wronged a fiber.
4:78 Wherever you may be, death will overtake you, even if you are in lofty towers. If good befalls them, they say, "This is from Allah"; if evil befalls them, they say, "This is from you." Say: All is from Allah. So what is the matter with these people that they scarcely understand a hadith?
4:79 Whatever good befalls you is from Allah, and whatever evil befalls you is from yourself. And We have sent you to humankind as a Rasul, and sufficient is Allah as witness.
4:80 Whoever obeys the Rasul has obeyed Allah. And whoever turns away — We have not sent you as a keeper over them.
4:81 They say, "Obedience." But when they leave your presence, a band of them spends the night plotting other than what you say. And Allah records what they plot by night. So turn away from them and trust in Allah. And sufficient is Allah as disposer.
4:82 Do they not ponder the Recitation? If it had been from other than Allah, they would have found in it much contradiction.
4:83 And when a matter of security or fear comes to them, they broadcast it. If only they had referred it to the Rasul and to those in authority among them, those who interpret it among them would have known it. Were it not for Allah's bounty upon you and mercy, you would have followed Shaytan, except a few.
4:84 So fight in the way of Allah; you are charged only with yourself. And urge on the muminin. Perhaps Allah will restrain the might of those who disbelieve. And Allah is stronger in might and stronger in exemplary punishment.
4:85 Whoever intercedes with a good intercession shall have a share of it, and whoever intercedes with an evil intercession shall have a portion of its burden. And Allah has been over everything all-powerful.
4:86 And when you are greeted with a greeting, greet back with better than it or return it. Truly Allah has been over everything a reckoner.
4:87 Allah — there is no ilah but Allah. Allah will surely gather you to the Day of Standing, about which there is no doubt. And who is more sadiq than Allah in hadith?
4:88 So what is the matter with you that you have become two bands over the munafiqin? Allah has cast them back for what they earned. Do you wish to guide one whom Allah has led astray? And whomever Allah leads astray, you will never find a way for him.
4:89 They wish that you disbelieve as they disbelieved, so that you would be equal. So do not take allies from them until they emigrate in the way of Allah. But if they turn away, seize them and kill them wherever you find them, and do not take from them a protecting-friend or a helper.
4:90 Except those who join a people between whom and you there is a pact, or who come to you with hearts straitened from fighting you or fighting their own people. If Allah had willed, Allah would have set them over you, and they would have fought you. So if they hold off from you and do not fight you, and cast peace toward you, then Allah has made no way for you against them.
4:91 You will find others who wish to be secure from you and to be secure from their own people; whenever they are returned to sedition, they are cast down in it. So if they do not hold off from you and cast peace toward you and restrain their hands, then seize them and kill them wherever you come upon them. Those — We have made for you against them a clear warrant.
4:92 It is not for a mumin to kill a mumin except by error. Whoever kills a mumin by error, then the freeing of a believing neck, and compensation handed over to his family — unless they forgo it. If he belonged to a people enemy to you and he is a mumin, then the freeing of a believing neck. And if he belonged to a people between whom and you there is a pact, then compensation handed over to his family and the freeing of a believing neck. Whoever does not find means, then fasting two consecutive months — a turning from Allah. And Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:93 Whoever kills a mumin deliberately, his recompense is Jahannam, abiding therein, and the wrath of Allah upon him, and Allah's curse, and Allah has prepared for him a tremendous punishment.
4:94 O muminin, when you set out in the way of Allah, ascertain, and do not say to one who casts peace toward you, "You are not a mumin," seeking the display of worldly life — with Allah are many gains. Thus you were before, then Allah favored you, so ascertain. Truly Allah has been aware of what you do.
4:95 Not equal are those of the muminin who remain behind — other than those held back by harm — and those who strive in the way of Allah with their wealth and their lives. By bounty from Allah, the strivers with their wealth and their lives are above the ones remaining behind by a degree; and to each Allah has promised the best. And by bounty from Allah the strivers are above those remaining behind with a great reward.
4:96 Degrees from Allah, and forgiveness, and mercy. And Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:97 Truly those whom the malakat take in death while they wrong themselves — they say, "In what state were you?" They say, "We were weak in the land." They say, "Was not the land of Allah spacious, that you might have emigrated in it?" Those — their destination is Jahannam, and evil is the arrival.
4:98 Except the weak among men, nisa, and children who can find no means and are not guided to a way.
4:99 Those — perhaps Allah will pass over them. And Allah has been ever-passing-over, forgiving.
4:100 Whoever emigrates in the way of Allah will find in the land many a refuge and spaciousness. And whoever leaves his home emigrating toward Allah and the Rasul, then death overtakes him — his reward has fallen upon Allah. And Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:101 And when you travel through the land, there is no blame on you in shortening the prayer, if you fear that those who disbelieve may assail you. Truly the unmuminin have been for you a clear enemy.
4:102 And when you are among them and establish the prayer for them, let a party of them stand with you and let them take their weapons. Then when they have bowed down, let them be behind you, and let another party come who have not prayed, and let them pray with you, and let them take their precaution and their weapons. Those who disbelieve wish that you be unmindful of your weapons and your gear, so that they may rush upon you in one rush. There is no blame on you if harm from rain or illness befalls you in laying down your weapons — but take your precaution. Truly Allah has prepared for the unmuminin a humiliating punishment.
4:103 Then when you have completed the prayer, remember Allah standing and sitting and on your sides. Then when you are secure, establish the prayer. Truly the prayer has been upon the muminin a timed ordinance.
4:104 And do not weaken in seeking the people. If you suffer, they suffer as you suffer, while you hope from Allah what they do not hope. And Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:105 Truly We have sent down the Kitab to you in truth, that you may judge among people by what Allah has shown you. And do not be an advocate for the khā'inīn.
4:106 And seek forgiveness from Allah. Truly Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:107 And do not dispute on behalf of those who betray themselves. Truly Allah loves not whoever is a betraying sinner.
4:108 They hide from people but do not hide from Allah, though Allah is with them when they pass the night in what does not please of speech. And Allah has been muhit over what they do.
4:109 There you are — you disputed on their behalf in worldly life. But who will dispute with Allah on their behalf on the Day of Standing? Or who will be a guardian over them?
4:110 Whoever does evil or wrongs himself, then seeks forgiveness from Allah, will find Allah forgiving, merciful.
4:111 Whoever earns a sin only earns it against himself. And Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:112 Whoever earns an error or a sin, then casts it upon an innocent one, has borne a stunning-slander and a manifest sin.
4:113 Were it not for Allah's bounty upon you and mercy, a band of them would have resolved to lead you astray. But they lead astray only themselves, and they cannot harm you in anything. And Allah sent down upon you the Kitab and wisdom, and taught you what you did not know. And the bounty of Allah upon you has been tremendous.
4:114 There is no good in much of their secret talk, except one who bids charity or kindness or reconciliation between people. And whoever does that, seeking the pleasure of Allah, We shall give him a great reward.
4:115 Whoever opposes the Rasul after guidance has become clear to him, and follows other than the way of the muminin, We will turn him to what he turned to, and make him enter Jahannam — and evil is the arrival.
4:116 Truly Allah does not forgive that one associate with Allah, and forgives what is below that for whom Allah wills. Whoever associates with Allah has gone far astray.
4:117 They call upon, other than Allah, only females; and they call upon only a defiant Shaytan.
4:118 Allah cursed him, and he said, "I will surely take from Your servants an ordained portion."
4:119 "I will surely mislead them, and surely grant them their wishes, and surely command them, so they will slit the ears of cattle; and I will surely command them, so they will change the creation of Allah." Whoever takes Shaytan as an ally other than Allah has lost a manifest loss.
4:120 He promises them and grants them wishes, but Shaytan promises them only deception.
4:121 Those — their destination is Jahannam, and they will find no escape from it.
4:122 And those who believe and do salih deeds, We shall admit them into gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. Allah's promise is truth. And who is more sadiq than Allah in saying?
4:123 It is not by your wishes, nor by the wishes of the people of the Kitab. Whoever does evil is recompensed for it, and he will not find for himself, other than Allah, a protector or a helper.
4:124 And whoever does salih deeds, whether male or female, and is a mumin — those will enter the Garden, and they will not be wronged a speck.
4:125 And who is better in way than one who submits his face to Allah while being a doer of good, and follows the creed of Ibrahim, upright? And Allah took Ibrahim as a bosom-friend.
4:126 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. And Allah has been muhit over everything.
4:127 They ask you for a ruling concerning the nisa. Say: Allah gives you a ruling concerning them, and concerning what is recited to you in the Kitab — the orphan nisa to whom you do not give what is decreed for them while you are eager to marry them; and the weak among children; and that you stand in equity for the orphans. And whatever good you do, truly Allah has been knowing of it.
4:128 And if an imra'a fears from her ba'l estrangement or turning-away, there is no blame on the two if they reconcile between themselves a reconciliation. And reconciliation is best, though nafs are summoned to stinginess. And if you do good and are reverent, truly Allah has been aware of what you do.
4:129 And you will never be able to do justice between nisa, however ardently you desire it. So do not lean with the whole leaning, leaving her like one suspended. And if you reconcile and are reverent, truly Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:130 And if the two part, Allah will enrich each from Allah's abundance. And Allah has been all-ample, wise.
4:131 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. We have charged those given the Kitab before you, and you, that you be mindful of Allah. And if you disbelieve — truly to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. And Allah has been free of need, praiseworthy.
4:132 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth. And sufficient is Allah as disposer.
4:133 If Allah wills, Allah can remove you, O humankind, and bring others. And Allah has been powerful over that.
4:134 Whoever desires the reward of the worldly life — with Allah is the reward of the worldly life and of the Last. And Allah has been hearing, seeing.
4:135 O muminin, be upholders in equity, witnesses for Allah, even against yourselves, or parents and near kin — whether one is rich or needy, Allah is worthier of the two. So do not follow desire, lest you swerve from justice. And if you twist aside or turn away — truly Allah has been aware of what you do.
4:136 O muminin, believe in Allah and the Rasul and the Kitab sent down upon Allah's Rasul, and the Kitab sent down before. Whoever disbelieves in Allah and the malakat and the Kutub and the Rasuls and the Last Day has gone far astray.
4:137 Truly those who believe, then disbelieve, then believe, then disbelieve, then increase in disbelief — it is not for Allah to forgive them, nor to guide them a way.
4:138 Give tidings to the munafiqin that for them is a painful punishment.
4:139 Those who take the unmuminin as allies other than the muminin — do they seek might with them? Truly all might belongs to Allah.
4:140 And it has been sent down to you in the Kitab that when you hear the ayat of Allah being disbelieved and mocked, do not sit with them until they engage in other hadith — or you would be like them. Truly Allah will gather the munafiqin and the unmuminin into Jahannam, all together.
4:141 Those who lie in wait against you: if a triumph from Allah comes to you, they say, "Were we not with you?" And if the unmuminin have a share, they say, "Did we not gain advantage over you and shield you from the muminin?" So Allah will judge between you on the Day of Standing, and Allah will never make for the unmuminin a way over the muminin.
4:142 Truly the munafiqin deceive Allah, and it is Allah who outwits them. And when they stand to the prayer they stand sluggish, showing off to people, and they do not remember Allah except a little.
4:143 Wavering between that — belonging neither to these nor to those. And whomever Allah leads astray, you will never find a way for him.
4:144 O muminin, do not take the unmuminin as allies other than the muminin. Do you wish to make for Allah against yourselves a clear warrant?
4:145 Truly the munafiqin are in the lowest reach of the fire, and you will never find a helper for them.
4:146 Except those who turned, set right, held fast to Allah, and devoted their way purely to Allah — those are with the muminin, and Allah will give the muminin a great reward.
4:147 What would Allah do with your punishment, if you give thanks and believe? And Allah has been appreciative, knowing.
4:148 Allah loves not open loudness with evil of speech, except by one who has been wronged. And Allah has been hearing, knowing.
4:149 If you manifest good or conceal it, or pass over evil — truly Allah has been ever-passing-over, powerful.
4:150 Truly those who disbelieve in Allah and the Rasuls, and wish to separate between Allah and the Rasuls, and say, "We believe in some and disbelieve in some," wishing to take a way between that.
4:151 Those are the unmuminin in truth, and We have prepared for the unmuminin a humiliating punishment.
4:152 And those who believe in Allah and the Rasuls and make no separation between any of them — those Allah will surely give their rewards. And Allah has been forgiving, merciful.
4:153 The people of the Kitab ask you to send down upon them a Kitab from the heaven. They asked Musa for greater than that; they said, "Show us Allah openly." So the thunderstrike seized them for their wrong. Then they took the calf after the clear proofs had come to them — yet We passed over that, and We gave Musa a clear warrant.
4:154 And We raised the Mount above them at their pact, and We said to them, "Enter the gate bowing down," and We said to them, "Do not transgress in the Sabbath," and We took from them a hard pact.
4:155 For their breaking of the pact, and their disbelief in the ayat of Allah, and their killing of the nabis without right, and their saying, "Our hearts are wrapped" — rather, Allah has stamped them over for their disbelief, so they do not believe except a few.
4:156 And for their disbelief, and their uttering against Maryam a great slander.
4:157 And their saying, "We killed the Anointed, Isa son of Maryam, the Rasul of Allah" — but they did not kill him, and they did not crucify him; rather, it was made unclear to them. Indeed, those who differ over it are in doubt of it; they have no knowledge of it except following conjecture, and they did not kill him with certainty.
4:158 Rather, Allah raised him up to Allah. And Allah has been mighty, wise.
4:159 And there is none of the people of the Kitab but will surely believe in him before his death, and on the Day of Standing he will be a witness over them.
4:160 For the wrongdoing of the Yahud, We forbade them good things that had been made lawful to them, and for their turning away from the way of Allah, much.
4:161 And their taking of usury when they had been forbidden it, and their devouring of people's wealth falsely. And We have prepared for the unmuminin among them a painful punishment.
4:162 But those deeply rooted in knowledge among them, and the muminin, believe in what was sent down to you and what was sent down before you — the establishers of the prayer, the givers of the purifying dues, the muminin in Allah and the Last Day. Those We will surely give a great reward.
4:163 Truly We revealed to you as We revealed to Nuh and the nabis after him, and We revealed to Ibrahim and Ismail and Ishaq and Yaqub and the Tribes, and Isa and Ayyub and Yunus and Harun and Sulayman, and We gave Dawud a the Kitab.
4:164 And rusul We have narrated to you before, and rusul We have not narrated to you. And Allah spoke to Musa directly.
4:165 Rusul bearing glad tidings and warnings, so that humankind would have no proof against Allah after the Rasuls. And Allah has been mighty, wise.
4:166 But Allah bears witness to what was sent down to you — Allah sent it down with Allah's knowledge — and the malakat bear witness. And sufficient is Allah as witness.
4:167 Truly those who disbelieved and turned away from the way of Allah have gone far astray.
4:168 Truly those who disbelieved and wronged — it is not for Allah to forgive them nor to guide them a path.
4:169 Except the path of Jahannam, abiding therein forever. And that is easy for Allah.
4:170 O humankind, the Rasul has come to you with truth from your Rabb, so believe — it is better for you. And if you disbelieve, then truly to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth. And Allah has been knowing, wise.
4:171 O people of the Kitab, do not exaggerate in your way, and do not say of Allah anything but the truth. The Anointed, Isa son of Maryam, is only the Rasul of Allah, and the word of Allah cast to Maryam, and a spirit from Allah. So believe in Allah and the Rasuls, and do not say "Three." Desist — it is better for you. Truly Allah is one ilah; exalted is Allah above having a child. To Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth, and sufficient is Allah as disposer.
4:172 The Anointed would never disdain being a servant of Allah, nor the brought-near malakat. And whoever disdains devotion to Allah and is arrogant — Allah will gather them all to Allah.
4:173 Then as for those who believe and do salih deeds, Allah will pay them their rewards in full and increase them from Allah's bounty. And as for those who disdained and grew arrogant, Allah will punish them with a painful punishment, and they will not find for themselves, other than Allah, a protector or a helper.
4:174 O humankind, a clear proof has come to you from your Rabb, and We have sent down to you a clear light.
4:175 Then as for those who believe in Allah and hold fast to Allah, Allah will admit them into mercy from Allah, and bounty, and guide them to Allah along a straight path.
4:176 They ask you for a ruling. Say: Allah gives you a ruling concerning one who dies leaving neither parent nor child: if a man perishes having no child but having a sister, then for her is half of what he left; and he inherits from her if she has no child. If there are two sisters, then for them is two-thirds of what he left. And if there are brothers, men and nisa, then for the male is the share of two females. Allah makes clear to you, lest you go astray, and Allah is knowing of everything.
5:1 O you who believe, fulfill the pacts. Made lawful for you is the grazing livestock, except what is recited to you: hunting game is not permitted while you are in the sacred state. Allah decides what Allah wills.
5:2 O you who believe, do not violate the rites of Allah, nor the sacred month, nor the offerings, nor the garlanded ones, nor those bound for the sacred house, seeking bounty from their Rabb and acceptance. And when you have left the sacred state, hunt. And let not the hatred of a people — because they barred you from the sacred Mosque — move you to transgress. And help one another to goodness and mindfulness, and do not help one another to sin and transgression. And be mindful of Allah; Allah is severe in punishment.
5:3 Forbidden to you are carrion, and blood, and the flesh of the pig, and what is dedicated to other than Allah, and the strangled, and the bludgeoned, and the fallen, and the gored, and what predators have eaten — except what you slaughter — and what is slaughtered upon the erected stones, and your apportioning by arrows. That is fisq. Today those who disbelieve have despaired of your religion, so do not fear them, but fear Me. Today I have completed your religion for you and completed My blessing upon you, and accepted Islam for you as religion. Whoever is compelled by hunger, not inclining to sin, then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
5:4 They ask you what is made lawful for them. Say: the good things are made lawful for you, and the hunting beasts you train, teaching them from what Allah taught you. So eat of what they catch for you, and mention the name of Allah over it, and be mindful of Allah. Allah is swift in reckoning.
5:5 Today the good things are made lawful for you, and the food of those given the Kitab is lawful for you, and your food is lawful for them. And the chaste nisa of the muminin, and the chaste nisa of those given the Kitab before you, when you give them their dowries, in wedlock, not in license, nor taking secret lovers. And whoever disbelieves in faith — void is that one's work, and in the last world that one is among the khasirin.
5:6 O you who believe, when you rise to the prayer, wash your faces and your hands up to the elbows, and wipe your heads and your feet up to the ankles. And if you are unwashed, purify yourselves. And if you are sick, or on a journey, or one of you comes from the privy, or you have touched nisa and find no water, then aim toward clean soil and wipe your faces and hands with it. Allah does not will to place any constriction upon you, but wills to purify you and to complete blessing upon you, so that you may give thanks.
5:7 And remember the blessing of Allah upon you, and the covenant with which Allah bound you when you said: we hear and we obey. And be mindful of Allah. Allah knows what the breasts hold.
5:8 O you who believe, be upholders for Allah as witnesses with equity; and let not the hatred of a people move you away from justice. Be just — that is nearer to mindfulness. And be mindful of Allah. Allah is aware of what you do.
5:9 Allah promised those who believe and do good works: for them forgiveness and a great reward.
5:10 And those who disbelieve and deny Our ayat — those are the companions of the blaze.
5:11 O you who believe, remember the blessing of Allah upon you, when a people resolved to stretch their hands against you, and Allah held their hands back from you. And be mindful of Allah; and in Allah let the muminin place their trust.
5:12 And Allah took the covenant of the children of Israel, and We raised among them 12 leaders. And Allah said: I am with you — if you uphold the prayer, and give the alms, and believe in My rusul and aid them, and lend Allah a goodly loan, I will surely blot out your evil deeds for you and enter you into gardens beneath which rivers flow. But whoever of you disbelieves after that has strayed from the even path.
5:13 Then, for their breaking of the pact, they were cursed, and We made their hearts hard. They shift the word from its places, and they forgot a portion of what they were reminded of. You will not cease to perceive khiyāna from them, except a few of them — so pass over them and pardon. Allah loves the muhsinin.
5:14 And of those who said: we are Nasara, We took their covenant, but they forgot a portion of what they were reminded of; so We cast among them enmity and hatred until the Day of Rising. And Allah will tell them what they used to do.
5:15 O People of the Kitab, Our Rasul has come to you, making clear to you much of what you used to conceal of the Kitab, and passing over much. A light has come to you from Allah, and a clear writ.
5:16 By it Allah guides whoever follows the acceptance into the paths of peace, and brings them out of the darkness into the light by leave, and guides them to a straight path.
5:17 Truly disbelieved those who say: Allah is the Messiah, son of Maryam. Say: who can own anything against Allah, if Allah wills to destroy the Messiah son of Maryam and his mother and everyone on earth altogether? To Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is between them. Allah creates what Allah wills, and Allah is over everything able.
5:18 And the Yahud and the Nasara said: we are the children of Allah and the beloved ones. Say: then why does Allah punish you for your offenses? Rather, you are humans among creation. Allah forgives whom Allah wills and punishes whom Allah wills, and to Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is between them, and to Allah is the arrival.
5:19 O People of the Kitab, Our Rasul has come to you, making clear to you after an interval in the succession of rusul, lest you say: no bringer of good news and no warner came to us. A bringer of good news and a warner has come to you, and Allah is over everything able.
5:20 And when Musa said to his people: O my people, remember the blessing of Allah upon you, when Allah made nabis among you and made you kings, and gave you what was given to no one of the worlds.
5:21 O my people, enter the holy land that Allah has ordained for you, and do not turn back on your heels, lest you turn back as losers.
5:22 They said: O Musa, in it is a people of overpowering tyrants; we will never enter it until they leave it. If they leave it, then we will surely enter.
5:23 Two men of those who feared, whom Allah had blessed, said: go in upon them by the gate; once you have entered it, you will be the overcomers. And in Allah place your trust, if you are muminin.
5:24 They said: O Musa, we will never enter it so long as they are in it. Go, you and your Rabb, and fight — the two of you. We will sit right here.
5:25 Musa said: Rabb, I possess only myself and my brother — so decide between us and the fasiqin.
5:26 Allah said: then it is forbidden to them for 40 years, and they will wander the land. So do not grieve over the fasiqin.
5:27 And recite to them the true account of the two sons of Adam: when they offered an offering, and it was accepted from one of them and not accepted from the other. The first said: I will surely kill you. The other said: Allah accepts only from the muttaqin.
5:28 If you stretch out your hand against me to kill me, I will not stretch out my hand against you to kill you. I fear Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
5:29 I want you to bear my sin and your own sin, and so be among the companions of the fire — and that is the recompense of the wrongdoers.
5:30 Then his nafs inclined him toward killing his brother, and he killed him, and became one of the khasirin.
5:31 Then Allah sent a raven searching the land, to show him how to hide his brother's nakedness. He said: woe is me! Am I unable to be like this raven and hide my brother's nakedness? And he became one of the regretful.
5:32 Because of that We wrote for the children of Israel: whoever kills a nafs — other than for a nafs, or for corruption in the land — it is as if killed all people; and whoever gives it life, it is as if gave life to all people. And Our rusul came to them with clear proofs; yet many of them after that are exceeders in the land.
5:33 The recompense of those who war against Allah and against Allah's Rasul and strive in the land for corruption is only this: that they be killed, or crucified, or that their hands and feet be cut off on opposite sides, or that they be exiled from the land. That for them is disgrace in this world, and for them in the last world is a great punishment.
5:34 Except those who repent before you overpower them — and know that Allah is forgiving, merciful.
5:35 O you who believe, be mindful of Allah and seek the way to Allah, and strive in the path, so that you may attain success.
5:36 Surely those who disbelieve — if they had all that is in the earth and the like of it with it, they would offer it as ransom from the punishment of the Day of Rising. It would not be accepted from them, and for them is a painful punishment.
5:37 They will want to come out of the fire, but they will not come out of it, and for them is a lasting punishment.
5:38 And the male thief and the female thief: cut off their hands, a recompense for what they earned, a warning from Allah. And Allah is mighty, wise.
5:39 Whoever repents after the wrong and makes amends — Allah turns to that one. Allah is forgiving, merciful.
5:40 Do you not know that to Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth? Allah punishes whom Allah wills and forgives whom Allah wills, and Allah is over everything able.
5:41 O Rasul, let not grieve you those who hasten in disbelief — from those who say with their mouths: we believe, while their hearts do not believe, and from those who are Yahud: listeners to falsehood, listeners for other people who never come to you. They shift the word from its places, saying: if you are given this, take it; if you are not given it, beware. And whoever Allah wills to test — you will own nothing for that one against Allah. Those are the ones whose hearts Allah did not will to purify. For them in this world is disgrace, and for them in the last world is a great punishment.
5:42 Listeners to falsehood, devourers of ill-gotten gain. If they come to you, judge between them or turn away from them. If you turn away from them, they cannot harm you at all; and if you judge, judge between them with equity. Allah loves the equitable.
5:43 But how can they turn to you for judgment when they have the Tawrah, in which is Allah's judgment — then they turn away after that? Those are not muminin.
5:44 We sent down the Tawrah: in it are guidance and light. By it the nabis who had submitted judged for those who are Yahud, and the rabbis and the learned ones, by what they were entrusted of Allah's writ and over which they were witnesses. So do not fear people, but fear Me, and do not trade My ayat for a small price. Whoever does not judge by what Allah sent down — those are the kafirin.
5:45 And We wrote for them in it: a nafs for a nafs, an eye for an eye, a nose for a nose, an ear for an ear, a tooth for a tooth, and for wounds — requital. Whoever gives it up in charity, it is expiation for that one. And whoever does not judge by what Allah sent down — those are the zalimun.
5:46 And We tracked in their footsteps with Isa son of Maryam, confirming what was before him of the Tawrah; and We gave him the Injil, in which are guidance and light, confirming what was before it of the Tawrah, and a guidance and admonition for the muttaqin.
5:47 And let the people of the Injil judge by what Allah sent down in it. Whoever does not judge by what Allah sent down — those are the fasiqun.
5:48 And We sent down to you the Kitab in truth, confirming what is before it of the Kitab and a guardian over it. So judge between them by what Allah sent down, and do not follow their desires away from the truth that has come to you. For each of you We made a law and a way. Had Allah willed, Allah would have made you one community, but to test you in what was given you. So race ahead to the good things. To Allah is your return, all of you, and Allah will tell you what you differed in.
5:49 And judge between them by what Allah sent down, and do not follow their desires, and beware of them, that they not test you away from some of what Allah sent down to you. If they turn away, then know that Allah only wills to strike them for some of their offenses. And many of the people are fasiqun.
5:50 Is it the judgment of the pagan age they seek? And who is better than Allah as judge for a people who are certain?
5:51 O you who believe, do not take the Yahud and the Nasara as allies. Some of them are allies of each other. Whoever of you turns to them — that one is one of them. Allah does not guide the zalimun people.
5:52 So you will see those in whose hearts is sickness hastening to them, saying: we fear a turn of fortune may strike us. Perhaps Allah will bring triumph, or a command from Allah — and then they will regret, at dawn, what they hid in themselves.
5:53 And those who believe will say: are these the ones who swore by Allah their utmost oaths that they were with you? Void are their works, and they have become losers.
5:54 O you who believe, whoever among you turns back from religion — Allah will bring a people whom Allah loves and who love Allah: humble toward the muminin, mighty over the kafirin, striving in the path of Allah and fearing no blamer's blame. That is Allah's bounty, given to whom Allah wills, and Allah is all-ample, all-knowing.
5:55 Your ally is only Allah and the Rasul and those who believe: those who uphold the prayer and give the alms, and they bow.
5:56 And whoever turns to Allah, and to the Rasul, and to those who believe — then the party of Allah are the victorious.
5:57 O you who believe, do not take as allies those who took your religion in mockery and play, from among those given the Kitab before you and the kafirin. And be mindful of Allah, if you are muminin.
5:58 And when you are called to the prayer, they take it in mockery and play. That is because they are a people who do not understand.
5:59 Say: O People of the Kitab, do you requite us for anything except that we believe in Allah and in what was sent down to us and what was sent down before — and that most of you are fasiqun?
5:60 Say: shall I inform you of worse than that, as recompense with Allah? Those whom Allah cursed and raged upon, and made among them monkeys and pigs, and those who serve the false arbiter. Those are worse in place, and further astray from the even path.
5:61 And when they come to you they say: we believe — though they entered with disbelief and left with it. And Allah knows best what they were hiding.
5:62 And you see many of them hastening into sin and transgression, and devouring ill-gotten gain. Evil is what they used to do.
5:63 Why do the rabbis and the learned ones not forbid them their sinful speech and their devouring of ill-gotten gain? Evil is what they used to make.
5:64 And the Yahud said: the hand of Allah is fettered. Fettered are their own hands, and they are cursed for what they said. Rather, both hands are spread wide, spending as Allah wills. And what your Rabb sent down to you only increases many of them in transgression and disbelief. And We cast among them enmity and hatred until the Day of Rising. Every time they kindle a fire for war, Allah puts it out. And they strive in the land for corruption, and Allah does not love the corrupters.
5:65 If the People of the Kitab had believed and been mindful, We would have blotted out their evil deeds for them and entered them into gardens of bliss.
5:66 If they had upheld the Tawrah and the Injil and what was sent down to them from their Rabb, they would have eaten from above them and from beneath their feet. Among them is a middle-course community, but many of them — evil is what they do.
5:67 O Rasul, deliver what was sent down to you from your Rabb; and if you do not, then you have not delivered the message. And Allah will protect you from the people. Allah does not guide the committing kufr people.
5:68 Say: O People of the Kitab, you stand on nothing until you uphold the Tawrah and the Injil and what was sent down to you from your Rabb. And what your Rabb sent down to you only increases many of them in transgression and disbelief. So do not grieve over the committing kufr people.
5:69 Surely those who believe, and those who are Yahud, and the Sabians, and the Nasara — whoever believes in Allah and the Last Day and does good work: no fear upon them, nor do they grieve.
5:70 We took the covenant of the children of Israel and sent rusul to them. Whenever a Rasul came to them with what their nafs did not desire, a party of them denied and a party killed.
5:71 They reckoned there would be no trial, so they became blind and deaf. Then Allah turned to them; then many of them became blind and deaf again. And Allah is seeing of what they do.
5:72 Truly disbelieved those who say: Allah is the Messiah son of Maryam. And the Messiah said: O children of Israel, worship Allah, my Rabb and your Rabb. Whoever associates others with Allah — Allah has forbidden the garden to that one, shelter is the fire, and there are no helpers for the zalimun.
5:73 Truly disbelieved those who say: Allah is a third of three. There is no ilah but one ilah. If they do not cease from what they say, a painful punishment will touch those of them who disbelieve.
5:74 Will they not turn to Allah and seek forgiveness? And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
5:75 The Messiah son of Maryam is only a Rasul; rusul passed on before him, and his mother was a sadiq imra'a. Both of them used to eat food. Look how We make the ayat clear to them; then look how they are turned away.
5:76 Say: do you worship besides Allah what owns for you neither harm nor benefit? And Allah is the all-hearing, the all-knowing.
5:77 Say: O People of the Kitab, do not exceed in your religion beyond the truth, and do not follow the desires of a people who strayed before and led many astray, and strayed from the even path.
5:78 Cursed were those who disbelieved from the children of Israel, by the tongue of Dawud and Isa son of Maryam. That is because they disobeyed and used to transgress.
5:79 They would not restrain one another from the rejected things they did. Evil was what they used to do.
5:80 You see many of them turning to those who disbelieve. Evil is what their nafs sent ahead for them — that Allah's wrath is upon them, and in the punishment they abide.
5:81 If they believed in Allah and the Nabi and what was sent down to him, they would not have taken them as allies — but many of them are fasiqun.
5:82 You will surely find the most vehement of the people in enmity toward those who believe to be the Yahud and those who associate others; and you will surely find the nearest of them in affection to those who believe to be those who say: we are Nasara. That is because among them are priests and monks, and because they do not wax proud.
5:83 And when they hear what was sent down to the Rasul, you see their eyes overflow with tears from what they recognize of the truth. They say: our Rabb, we believe — so write us down with the witnesses.
5:84 And why should we not believe in Allah and in what has come to us of the truth, and hope that our Rabb will enter us with the good people?
5:85 So Allah recompensed them for what they said: gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein. And that is the recompense of the muhsinin.
5:86 And those who disbelieve and deny Our ayat — those are the companions of the blaze.
5:87 O you who believe, do not forbid the good things that Allah made lawful for you, and do not transgress. Allah does not love the transgressors.
5:88 And eat of what Allah provided you, lawful and good, and be mindful of Allah, in whom you believe.
5:89 Allah does not take you to task for the idle talk in your oaths, but takes you to task for what your oaths have bound. So its expiation is feeding 10 needy ones with the middle of what you feed your families, or clothing them, or freeing one in bondage. Whoever cannot find — then a fast of 3 days. That is the expiation of your oaths when you swear. And guard your oaths. Thus Allah makes the ayat clear to you, so that you may give thanks.
5:90 O you who believe, wine, and gaming, and the erected stones, and the arrows are filth from the work of Shaytan — so avoid it, so that you may attain success.
5:91 Shaytan only wants to cast between you enmity and hatred through the wine and the gaming, and to turn you away from the remembrance of Allah and from the prayer. Will you not cease?
5:92 And obey Allah and obey the Rasul, and beware. If you turn away, then know that upon Our Rasul is only the clear delivery.
5:93 No blame rests on those who believe and do good works for what they have tasted, when they are mindful and believe and do good works; then are mindful and believe; then are mindful and do good. And Allah loves the muhsinin.
5:94 O you who believe, Allah will test you with something of game that your hands and spears reach, so that Allah may know who fears Allah in the ghayb. Whoever transgresses after that — for that one is a painful punishment.
5:95 O you who believe, do not kill game while you are in the sacred state. Whoever of you kills it deliberately — the recompense is the like of what was killed of cattle, judged by two just ones among you, as an offering reaching the Ka'bah; or an expiation: feeding needy ones, or the equal of that as fasting, so that the heavy cost of the deed may be tasted. Allah has passed over what went before. Whoever returns — Allah will requite that one. And Allah is mighty, possessor of requital.
5:96 Made lawful for you is the game of the sea and its food, as provision for you and for the travelers. Forbidden to you is the game of the land while you remain in the sacred state. And be mindful of Allah, to whom you will be gathered.
5:97 Allah has made the Ka'bah, the sacred house, a standing-place for people, and the sacred month, and the offerings, and the garlanded ones. That is so that you may know that Allah knows what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and that Allah knows everything.
5:98 Know that Allah is severe in punishment, and that Allah is forgiving, merciful.
5:99 Upon the Rasul is only the delivery. And Allah knows what you make manifest and what you hide.
5:100 Say: the vile and the good are not equal, even if the mass of the vile impresses you. So be mindful of Allah, O people of understanding, so that you may attain success.
5:101 O you who believe, do not ask about things which, if shown to you, would displease you. And if you ask about them when the recitation is sent down, they will be shown to you. Allah has passed over them, and Allah is forgiving, forbearing.
5:102 A people before you asked about them, then became kafirin in them.
5:103 Allah did not make a Bahira, nor a Sa'iba, nor a Wasila, nor a Hami — but those who disbelieve fabricate lies about Allah, and most of them do not understand.
5:104 And when it is said to them: come to what Allah sent down and to the Rasul, they say: sufficient for us is what we found our fathers upon. What — even if their fathers knew nothing and were not guided?
5:105 O you who believe, upon you rests your own selves. Those who stray cannot harm you if you are guided. To Allah is your return, all of you, and Allah will tell you what you used to do.
5:106 O you who believe, the testimony among you: when death comes to one of you at the time of the bequest — two just ones of you, or two others from other than you, if you are traveling in the land and the blow of death strikes you. Detain the two after the prayer, and let them both swear by Allah — if you are in doubt: we will not take any price for it, even if it is one near of kin, and we will not hide the testimony of Allah — for then we would be among the sinful.
5:107 Then if it is discovered that the two merited guilt, two others shall stand in their place from those entitled against the first two, and they shall both swear by Allah: our testimony is truer than their testimony, and we have not transgressed — for then we would be among the wrongdoers.
5:108 That is nearer — that they bring the testimony in full, or that they fear oaths being returned after their oaths. And be mindful of Allah and listen. Allah does not guide the fasiqin.
5:109 The day Allah gathers the Rasuls and says: what answer were you given? They say: we have no knowledge. You alone are the knower of the unseen.
5:110 When Allah said: O Isa son of Maryam, remember My blessing upon you and upon your mother — when I strengthened you with ruh al-qudus, you spoke to people in the cradle and in maturity; and when I taught you the Kitab, and wisdom, and the Tawrah, and the Injil; and when you create from clay like the shape of a bird, by My leave, then breathe into it and it becomes a bird by My leave; and you heal the blind and the leper by My leave; and when you bring out the dead by My leave; and when I restrained the children of Israel from you when you came to them with clear proofs — yet those of them who disbelieved said: this is nothing but clear sihr.
5:111 And when I inspired the disciples: believe in Me and in My Rasul. They said: we believe — and bear witness that we are in submission.
5:112 When the disciples said: O Isa son of Maryam, can your Rabb send down to us a spread-table from the heaven? Isa said: be mindful of Allah, if you are muminin.
5:113 They said: we want to eat of it and our hearts be settled, and to know that You have been true to us, and to be witnesses over it.
5:114 Isa son of Maryam said: O Allah, our Rabb, send down to us a spread-table from the heaven, to be a festival for our first and our last, and an aya from You, and provide us — and You are the best of providers.
5:115 Allah said: I am sending it down to you. Whoever of you disbelieves after — I will punish that one with a punishment with which I punish no one of the worlds.
5:116 And when Allah said: O Isa son of Maryam, did you say to the people: take me and my mother as two alihah besides Allah? Isa said: glory be to You — it is not for me to say what is not mine by truth. If I had said it, You would have known it. You know what is in my nafs, and I do not know what is in Your nafs. You alone are the knower of the unseen.
5:117 I said to them only what You enjoined upon me: worship Allah, my Rabb and your Rabb. And I was a witness over them as long as I remained among them; and when You took me, You were the watcher over them, and You are witness over everything.
5:118 If You punish them, they are Your servants; and if You forgive them, then You are the mighty, the wise.
