5:119 Allah said: this is the day the sadiqin benefit by their sadiqness. For them are gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. Allah is pleased with them and they are pleased with Allah. That is the great triumph.
5:120 To Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is in them, and Allah is over everything able.
6:1 Praise belongs to Allah, who created the heavens and the earth and made the darkness and the light; then those who disbelieve set up equals to their Rabb.
6:2 It is Allah who created you from clay, then decreed a term; and a fixed term is with Allah; yet you doubt.
6:3 And Allah is in the heavens and in the earth, knows your secret and your open-loud, and knows what you earn.
6:4 And no aya comes to them from the ayat of their Rabb but they turn away from it.
6:5 They have already denied the truth when it came to them; but soon there will come to them news of what they used to mock.
6:6 Have they not seen how many generations We destroyed before them, whom We had established in the earth in what We have not established you, and sent the heaven down upon them pouring, and made rivers flow beneath them? Yet We destroyed them for their offenses, and raised up after them other generations.
6:7 Had We sent down to you a writing on paper which they touched with their hands, those who disbelieve would have said: This is nothing but clear sihr.
6:8 And they said: Why has no malakat been sent down to him? And had We sent down a malakat, the matter would have been decided, and then they would be given no respite.
6:9 And had We made the malakat a man, We would have cast over them the very doubt they cast.
6:10 Rusul were mocked before you; so those who mocked them were encircled by what they used to mock.
6:11 Say: Journey in the land, then look how was the end of the mukazzibin.
6:12 Say: To whom belongs what is in the heavens and the earth? Say: To Allah. Allah has written mercy upon the self, to gather you surely to the Day of Rising, no doubt in it. Those who lose themselves, they do not believe.
6:13 And to Allah belongs what rests in the night and the day, and Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
6:14 Say: Shall I take as protector other than Allah, the originator of the heavens and the earth, who feeds and is not fed? Say: I am commanded to be the first of those who submit, and: never be of those who join partners.
6:15 Say: I fear, if I disobey my Rabb, the punishment of a great day.
6:16 Whoever is averted from it that day, then Allah has had mercy on them; and that is the clear winning.
6:17 And if Allah touches you with harm, none can lift it but Allah; and if Allah touches you with good, then Allah is over all things powerful.
6:18 And Allah is the overpowering above the servants; and Allah is the Wise, the Aware.
6:19 Say: What thing is greatest in testimony? Say: Allah, a witness between me and you. And this Quran was revealed to me, to warn you with it and whomever it reaches. Do you indeed testify that with Allah there are other alihah? Say: I do not testify. Say: Allah is only one ilah, and I am clear of what you join as partners.
6:20 Those to whom We gave the Kitab know it as they know their own children. Those who lose themselves, they do not believe.
6:21 And who is more zalimun than one who fabricates against Allah a lie, or denies Allah's ayat? Indeed the zalimun do not attain success.
6:22 And the day We gather them all, then We say to those who joined partners: Where are your partners whom you used to claim?
6:23 Then their only plea will be to say: By Allah our Rabb, we were not of those who joined partners.
6:24 See how they lied against themselves, and what they used to fabricate strayed from them.
6:25 Among them are those who listen to you, and We have placed coverings over their hearts so they do not understand it, and heaviness in their ears. And if they see every aya, they will not believe in it, until when they come to you disputing with you, those who disbelieve say: This is nothing but tales of the ancients.
6:26 And they forbid it and keep away from it; yet they ruin none but themselves, and they do not perceive.
6:27 If only you could see when they stand over the Fire and say: If only we could return, and not deny the ayat of our Rabb, and be among the muminin.
6:28 Rather, what they used to conceal before has become manifest to them. And even if they were returned, they would go back to what they were forbidden; and they are truly liars.
6:29 And they said: There is nothing but our life of this world, and we will not be raised.
6:30 If only you could see when they stand before their Rabb: Is this not the truth? They say: Yes, by our Rabb. It is said: Taste the punishment for what you used to disbelieve.
6:31 Those who denied the meeting with Allah have already lost, until when the Hour comes upon them suddenly, they say: Our regret, over what we fell short in it, while they carry their loads on their backs. How evil is what they bear.
6:32 And the life of this world is nothing but play and diversion; and the final home is better for those who are mindful. Will you not then reason?
6:33 We know well that what they say grieves you. Yet it is not you they deny; rather the zalimun deny the ayat of Allah.
6:34 Rusul before you were denied, yet they were sabir over what they denied and were harmed, until Our help came to them. And none can change the words of Allah. And there has already come to you news of the mursalin.
6:35 And if their turning away is hard upon you, then, if you can, seek a tunnel in the earth or a ladder in the heaven to bring them an aya. Had Allah willed, Allah would have gathered them upon the guidance; so never be of the ignorant.
6:36 Only those who listen respond. And the dead, Allah will raise them, then to Allah they return.
6:37 And they said: Why has no aya from his Rabb been sent down to him? Say: Allah is able to send down an aya, but most of them do not know.
6:38 There is no creature on the earth, nor a bird flying on its two wings, but are communities like you. We have neglected nothing in the Kitabten record. Then to their Rabb they will be gathered.
6:39 Those who deny Our ayat are deaf and dumb in darkness. Whomever Allah wills, Allah leaves astray; and whomever Allah wills, Allah sets upon a straight path.
6:40 Say: Tell me, if the punishment of Allah came to you, or the Hour came upon you, would you call on other than Allah, if you are sadiq?
6:41 Rather, it is Allah you call upon; and Allah lifts away what you call upon, if Allah wills, and you forget what you join as partners.
6:42 We sent to nations before you, and We seized them with hardship and harm, so that they might plead humbly.
6:43 Then why, when Our hardship came to them, did they not plead humbly? But their hearts hardened, and the devil made fair to them what they used to do.
6:44 Then when they forgot what they were reminded of, We opened upon them the doors of everything, until when they rejoiced in what they were given, We seized them suddenly, and there they were, despair-struck.
6:45 So the remnant of the people who did wrong was cut off. And all praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
6:46 Say: Tell me, if Allah took away your hearing and your sight and sealed your hearts, which ilah other than Allah could restore it to you? See how We vary the ayat; yet they turn away.
6:47 Say: Tell me, if the punishment of Allah came to you suddenly or openly, would any be destroyed but the zalimun people?
6:48 We send the mursalin only as bearers of good news and warners. So whoever believes and sets things right, no fear upon them, nor will they grieve.
6:49 And those who deny Our ayat, the punishment will touch them for what they used to yafsuquna.
6:50 Say: I do not say to you that I possess the storehouses of Allah, nor do I know the ghayb, nor do I say to you that I am a malakat. I follow only what is revealed to me. Say: Are the blind and the seeing equal? Will you not reflect?
6:51 And warn with it those who fear that they will be gathered to their Rabb; they have no protector besides Allah, and no intercessor, so that they may be mindful.
6:52 And do not drive away those who call upon their Rabb at morning and evening, seeking the face of Allah. Nothing of their account is upon you, and nothing of your account is upon them; so do not drive them away, or you would be of the zalimun.
6:53 And thus We test some of them by others, so that they say: Are these the ones Allah has favored among us? Is not Allah most knowing of the shakirin?
6:54 And when those who believe in Our ayat come to you, say: Peace be upon you. Your Rabb has written mercy upon the self, that whoever among you does wrong in ignorance, then turns back after it and sets right, then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
6:55 And thus We spell out the ayat, and so the way of the guilty becomes clear.
6:56 Say: I am forbidden to serve those you call upon besides Allah. Say: I will not follow your desires; then I would be astray, and not of the guided.
6:57 Say: I am upon clear proof from my Rabb, and you have denied it. I do not have what you hurry toward. The decision belongs only to Allah; Allah speaks the truth and is the best of deciders.
6:58 Say: If I had what you hurry toward, the matter between me and you would already be decided. And Allah knows best the zalimun.
6:59 And with Allah are the keys of the ghayb; none knows them but Allah. And Allah knows what is in the land and the sea; and no leaf falls but Allah knows it, and no grain in the darkness of the earth, nothing fresh nor dry, but it is in a clear record.
6:60 And Allah is the one who takes you in death by night, and knows what you do by day, then raises you in it, so that a fixed term may be fulfilled. Then to Allah is your return; then Allah will inform you of what you used to do.
6:61 And Allah is the overpowering above the servants, and sends guardians over you, until when death comes to one of you, Our rusul take him, and they never fall short.
6:62 Then they are returned to Allah, their true protector. Indeed the judgment belongs to Allah alone, and Allah is the swiftest of reckoners.
6:63 Say: Who rescues you from the darkness of the land and the sea? You call upon Allah in humble pleading and in secret: If You rescue us from this, we will surely be among the shakirin.
6:64 Say: Allah rescues you from it and from every distress; yet you join partners.
6:65 Say: Allah is able to send upon you punishment from above you or from beneath your feet, or to divide you into parties and let some of you taste the violence of others. See how We vary the ayat, so that they may understand.
6:66 And your people denied it, while it is the truth. Say: I am not a guardian over you.
6:67 Every report has its appointed place, and soon you will know.
6:68 And when you see those who wade idly into Our ayat, turn away from them until they wade into some other hadith. And if the devil makes you forget, do not sit, after the reminder, with the zalimun people.
6:69 And nothing of their account is upon those who are mindful; but a reminder, so that they too may be mindful.
6:70 And leave those who took their religion as play and diversion, and whom the life of this world deceived. And remind with it, lest a nafs be plunged in ruin for what it has earned, having, besides Allah, no protector and no intercessor; and though it offer every compensation, none will be taken from it. Those are the ones plunged in ruin for what they earned; for them is a drink of scalding water and a painful punishment, for what they used to disbelieve.
6:71 Say: Shall we call upon, besides Allah, what can neither benefit nor harm us, and be turned back on our heels after Allah has guided us, like one whom the devils lure in the land, bewildered, who has companions calling to guidance, Come to us? Say: The guidance of Allah is the true guidance. And we are commanded to submit to the Rabb of the worlds.
6:72 And to establish the prayer and be mindful of Allah; and it is to Allah you will be gathered.
6:73 And Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth in truth. And the day Allah says Be, and it is. Allah's word is the truth, and to Allah belongs the kingdom the day the trumpet is blown. Knower of the ghayb and the witnessed; and Allah is the Wise, the Aware.
6:74 And when Ibrahim said to his father Azar: Do you take asnam as alihah? I see you and your people in clear error.
6:75 And thus We showed Ibrahim the kingdom of the heavens and the earth, so that he might be of those certain.
6:76 So when night fell over him, he saw a star and said: This is my Rabb. But when it set, he said: I do not love the setting ones.
6:77 Then when he saw the moon rising, he said: This is my Rabb. But when it set, he said: If my Rabb does not guide me, I will surely be of the dallin people.
6:78 Then when he saw the sun rising, he said: This is my Rabb; this is greater. But when it set, he said: O my people, I am clear of what you join as partners.
6:79 Indeed I have set my face, upright, to the one who originated the heavens and the earth, and I am not of those who join partners.
6:80 And his people disputed with him. He said: Do you dispute with me about Allah, when Allah has guided me? I do not fear what you join with Allah, unless my Rabb wills something. My Rabb's knowledge encompasses all things. Will you not then remember?
6:81 And how should I fear what you have joined, when you do not fear joining with Allah what Allah has sent down no warrant for upon you? So which of the two parties has more right to security, if you know?
6:82 Those who believe and do not clothe their belief with injustice, those have security, and they are the guided.
6:83 And that was Our proof, which We gave Ibrahim against his people. We raise in degree whomever We will. Indeed your Rabb is wise, knowing.
6:84 And We granted him Ishaq and Ya'qub, each We guided. And Nuh We guided before. And among his descendants: Dawud, Sulaiman, Ayyub, Yusuf, Musa and Harun. And thus We repay the muhsinin.
6:85 And Zakaria, Yahya, Isa and Ilyas, each was of the salihin.
6:86 And Ismail, Al-Yasa', Yunus and Lut, and each We favored over the worlds.
6:87 And among their fathers, and their descendants, and their brothers, We chose them and guided them to a straight path.
6:88 That is the guidance of Allah; by it Allah guides whomever Allah wills of the servants. And had they joined partners, all they used to do would be voided from them.
6:89 Those are the ones to whom We gave the Kitab, and the judgment, and the Nabihood. So if these disbelieve in it, We have already entrusted it to a people who do not disbelieve in it.
6:90 Those are the ones whom Allah guided; so follow their guidance. Say: I ask of you no wage for it. It is nothing but a reminder for the worlds.
6:91 They did not value Allah with the valuation due, when they said: Allah has sent down nothing to a human being. Say: Who sent down the Kitab that Musa brought, a light and guidance for the people, which you make into papers you show openly and conceal much of? And you were taught what you did not know, neither you nor your fathers. Say: Allah. Then leave them in their idle wading, playing.
6:92 And this is a blessed Kitab We have sent down, confirming what is before it, and to warn the mother of towns and those around it. And those who believe in the hereafter believe in it, and they keep watch over their prayers.
6:93 And who is more zalimun than one who fabricates a lie against Allah, or says: It was revealed to me, when nothing was revealed to them, or says: I will send down the like of what Allah sent down? If only you could see when the zalimun are in the throes of death, and the malakat stretch out their hands: Bring out your nafs. Today you are repaid the humiliating punishment for what you used to say about Allah other than the truth, and you were arrogant toward Allah's ayat.
6:94 You have come to Us one by one, as We created you the first time, and you have left behind your backs what We bestowed on you. And We do not see with you your intercessors whom you claimed were partners to you. The bond between you has been cut off, and what you used to claim has strayed from you.
6:95 Indeed Allah splits the grain and the kernel, bringing forth the living from the dead, and bringing forth the dead from the living. That is Allah; so how are you turned away?
6:96 The splitter of the daybreak, and maker of the night as rest, and the sun and the moon as a reckoning. That is the ordaining of the Mighty, the Knowing.
6:97 And Allah is the one who made for you the stars, so that you may be guided by them through the darkness of the land and the sea. We have spelled out the ayat for people who know.
6:98 And Allah is the one who raised you from a single nafs; then a lodging and a depository. We have spelled out the ayat for people who understand.
6:99 And Allah is the one who sends down water from the heaven, and with it We bring forth growth of every kind; We bring forth from it greenery, from which We bring forth grain stacked in layers, and from the palm, from its spathe, hanging clusters near at hand, and gardens of grapes, and the olive and the pomegranate, alike and unlike. Look at its fruit when it bears fruit and ripens. Indeed in that are ayat for people who believe.
6:100 Yet they made for Allah partners, the jinn, though Allah created them. And they fabricated for Allah sons and daughters without knowledge. Glory be to Allah, and exalted above what they describe.
6:101 Originator of the heavens and the earth, how could there be a child when there is no companion? And Allah created all things, and Allah is knowing of all things.
6:102 That is Allah, your Rabb; there is no ilah but Allah. Creator of all things, so serve Allah. And Allah is guardian over all things.
6:103 Sights do not overtake Allah, yet Allah overtakes the sights; and Allah is the subtle, the aware.
6:104 Clear insights have come to you from your Rabb. So whoever sees, it is for the self; and whoever is unseeing, it is upon it. And I am not a keeper over you.
6:105 And thus We vary the ayat, so that they say: You have merely studied; and so that We may make it clear to people who know.
6:106 Follow what has been revealed to you from your Rabb; there is no ilah but Allah. And turn away from those who join partners.
6:107 Had Allah willed, they would not have joined partners. And We have not made you a keeper over them, nor are you a guardian over them.
6:108 And do not insult those they call upon besides Allah, lest they insult Allah in enmity without knowledge. Thus We have made fair to every nation their deeds; then to their Rabb is their return, and Allah will inform them of what they used to do.
6:109 And they swore by Allah with their utmost oaths that if an aya came to them they would surely believe in it. Say: The ayat are only with Allah. And what will make you perceive that when they come, they will not believe?
6:110 And We turn their fu'ad and their sights, as they did not believe in it the first time, and We leave them in their transgression, wandering blindly.
6:111 And even if We sent the malakat down to them, and the dead spoke to them, and We mustered all things before them, they would not believe unless Allah wills; yet most of them are ignorant.
6:112 And thus We have made for every Nabi an enemy, devils of humankind and jinn, inspiring one another with varnished speech, by way of deception. And had your Rabb willed, they would not have done it. So leave them and what they fabricate.
6:113 And so the hearts of those who do not believe in the hereafter may incline to it and be pleased with it, and may keep committing what they are committing.
6:114 Shall I seek a judge other than Allah, when Allah is the one who sent down to you the Kitab spelled out? And those to whom We gave the Kitab know that it is sent down from your Rabb in truth. So never be of the doubters.
6:115 And the word of your Rabb is complete, in truth and justice; none can change Allah's words. And Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
6:116 And if you obey most of those on the earth, they will lead you astray from Allah's path. They follow only conjecture, and they do nothing but guess.
6:117 Indeed your Rabb knows best who strays from the path, and knows best the guided.
6:118 So eat of that over which the name of Allah has been mentioned, if you are muminin in Allah's ayat.
6:119 And what is it with you that you do not eat of that over which the name of Allah has been mentioned, when Allah has spelled out for you what is forbidden to you, unless you are compelled to it? Indeed many lead astray by their desires, without knowledge. Indeed your Rabb knows best the transgressors.
6:120 And leave the outward and the inward of sin. Indeed those who earn sin will be repaid for what they used to commit.
6:121 And do not eat of that over which the name of Allah has not been mentioned, for indeed it is fisq. And indeed the devils inspire their allies to dispute with you. And if you obey them, you are indeed those who join partners.
6:122 Is one who was dead, and We gave life, and made a light by which to walk among people, like one whose likeness is in darkness, with no way out of it? Thus has been made fair to the kafirin what they used to do.
6:123 And thus We have made in every town the greatest of its mujrimin, so that they scheme there. Yet they scheme only against themselves, and they do not perceive.
6:124 And when an aya comes to them, they say: We will not believe until we are given the like of what was given to the rusul of Allah. Allah knows best where to place the message. Humiliation before Allah and a severe punishment will strike those who commit crimes, for what they used to scheme.
6:125 So whomever Allah wills to guide, Allah opens the chest to submission; and whomever Allah wills to leave astray, Allah makes the chest tight and constricted, as if climbing up into the heaven. Thus does Allah place filth upon those who do not believe.
6:126 And this is the straight path of your Rabb. We have spelled out the ayat for people who remember.
6:127 Theirs is the home of peace, with their Rabb; and Allah is their protector for what they used to do.
6:128 And the day Allah gathers them all: O assembly of jinn, you have taken much among humankind. And their allies from humankind say: Our Rabb, we took enjoyment in one another, and we reached our term which You appointed for us. Allah says: The Fire is your lodging, to abide in it forever, except what Allah wills. Indeed your Rabb is wise, knowing.
6:129 And thus We make some of the zalimun prevail over others, for what they used to earn.
6:130 O assembly of jinn and humankind, did rusul not come to you from among you, recounting My ayat to you and warning you of the meeting of this day of yours? They say: We bear witness against ourselves. The life of this world deceived them, and they bore witness against themselves that they were kafirin.
6:131 That is because your Rabb would never destroy towns in wrong while their people were unmindful.
6:132 And for each is a degree according to what they did; and your Rabb is not unmindful of what they do.
6:133 And your Rabb is the Self-Sufficient, possessor of mercy. If Allah wills, Allah can remove you and bring successors after you, just as Allah raised you from the offspring of other people.
6:134 Indeed what you are promised is coming, and you cannot escape.
6:135 Say: O my people, work according to your standing; I too am working. Soon you will know to whom the final home belongs. Indeed the zalimun do not attain success.
6:136 And they assign to Allah a portion of what Allah spread forth of tillage and livestock, and say by their claim: This is for Allah, and this is for our partners. But what is for their partners never reaches Allah, and what is for Allah reaches their partners. Evil is what they judge.
6:137 And thus has the joining of partners made fair to many the killing of their children, in order to plunge them and to confuse their religion for them. And had Allah willed, they would not have done it. So leave them and what they fabricate.
6:138 And they said: These livestock and tillage are restricted, none may eat them but whom we will, by their claim. And livestock whose backs are forbidden, and livestock over which they do not mention the name of Allah, fabricating against Allah. Allah will repay them for what they used to fabricate.
6:139 And they said: What is in the bellies of these livestock is exclusively for our males and forbidden to our nisa. But if it is born dead, then they share in it. Allah will repay them for their describing. Indeed Allah is wise, knowing.
6:140 Losers are those who killed their children in folly, without knowledge, and forbade what Allah provided them, fabricating against Allah. They have gone astray, and they were not guided.
6:141 And Allah is the one who raised gardens, trellised and untrellised, and the palm and crops of varied produce, and the olive and pomegranate, alike and unlike. Eat of its fruit when it bears, and give its due on the day of its harvest, and do not exceed. Indeed Allah does not love the exceeders.
6:142 And among the livestock are carriers and ones spread out for you. Eat of what Allah has provided you, and do not follow the footsteps of the devil; indeed the devil is a clear enemy to you.
6:143 8 pairs: 2 of sheep, and 2 of goats. Say: Has Allah forbidden the 2 males, or the 2 females, or what the wombs of the 2 females enclose? Inform me with knowledge, if you are sadiq.
6:144 And two of camels and two of cattle. Say: Has Allah forbidden the two males, or the two females, or what the wombs of the two females enclose? Or were you witnesses when Allah charged you with this? So who is more zalimun than one who fabricates a lie against Allah to lead people astray without knowledge? Indeed Allah does not guide the zalimun people.
6:145 Say: I find in what has been revealed to me nothing forbidden to one who partakes of it, except it be carrion, or poured-out blood, or the flesh of swine, for that is filth, or an obscenity dedicated to other than Allah. But whoever is compelled, neither craving nor fisq, then your Rabb is forgiving, merciful.
6:146 And upon those who took the rival way We forbade every hoofed creature; and of cattle and sheep We forbade them their fats, except what their backs carry, or the entrails, or what is mixed with bone. That is how We repaid them for their exceeding. And indeed We are sadiq.
6:147 So if they deny you, say: Your Rabb is possessor of vast mercy, yet the force will not be turned back from the mujrim people.
6:148 Those who joined partners will say: If Allah had willed, we would not have joined partners, nor our fathers, nor would we have forbidden anything. Thus those before them denied, until they tasted Our force. Say: Do you have any knowledge you could bring out for us? You follow only conjecture, and you do nothing but guess.
6:149 Say: To Allah belongs the decisive proof. So had Allah willed, Allah would have guided you all.
6:150 Say: Bring your witnesses who testify that Allah has forbidden this. Then if they testify, do not testify with them. And do not follow the desires of those who deny Our ayat, and those who do not believe in the hereafter, and set up equals to their Rabb.
6:151 Say: Come, I will recite what your Rabb has forbidden you: that you join nothing with Allah; and goodness to parents; and do not kill your children from want, We provide for you and for them; and do not approach obscenitys, outward and inward; and do not kill the nafs which Allah has forbidden except by right. That is what Allah has charged you with, so that you may reason.
6:152 And do not approach the orphan's wealth except in the best manner, until the orphan reaches full strength. And give full measure and weight with equity. We charge no nafs beyond its capacity. And when you speak, be just, even if it is a near relative. And fulfill the pledge of Allah. That is what Allah has charged you with, so that you may remember.
6:153 And that this is My straight path, so follow it; and do not follow the paths, for they would scatter you from the path. That is what Allah has charged you with, so that you may be mindful.
6:154 Then We gave Musa the Kitab, complete for the one who does good, a detailing of all things, and a guidance and a mercy, so that they might believe in the meeting with their Rabb.
6:155 And this is a blessed Kitab We have sent down; so follow it and be mindful, so that you may be shown mercy.
6:156 Lest you say: The Kitab was only sent down to two parties before us, and we were indeed unmindful of their study.
6:157 Or lest you say: If only the Kitab had been sent down to us, we would have been better guided than they. A clear proof has come to you from your Rabb, and a guidance and a mercy. So who is more zalimun than one who denies the ayat of Allah and turns away from them? We will repay those who turn away from Our ayat with the evil of punishment, for what they used to turn away from.
6:158 Do they wait for anything but that the malakat come to them, or your Rabb comes, or some of your Rabb's ayat come? On the day some of your Rabb's ayat come, no nafs will benefit from its faith that had not believed before or earned good in its faith. Say: Wait, indeed we too are waiting.
6:159 Indeed those who split their religion and became parties, you have nothing to do with them. Their affair is only with Allah; then Allah will inform them of what they used to do.
6:160 Whoever brings a good deed will have 10 times its like; and whoever brings an evil deed will be repaid only its like; and they will not be wronged.
6:161 Say: Indeed my Rabb has guided me to a straight path, an upright religion, the way of Ibrahim, upright; and he was not of those who join partners.
6:162 Say: Indeed my prayer and my rites and my living and my dying are for Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
6:163 No partner has Allah; and with that I have been commanded, and I am the first of those who submit.
6:164 Say: Shall I seek a Rabb other than Allah, when Allah is Rabb of all things? And no nafs earns except against itself; and no laden nafs bears the load of another. Then to your Rabb is your return, and Allah will inform you of what you used to differ about.
6:165 And Allah is the one who made you successors of the earth, and raised some of you above others in degrees, to test you in what Allah has given you. Indeed your Rabb is swift in requital, and indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
7:1 Alif Lam Mim Sad
7:2 A writing sent down to you — so let there be no constriction in your breast about it — that you may warn with it, and a reminder for the muminin.
7:3 Follow what has been sent down to you from your Rabb, and do not follow protectors besides Allah. Little do you remember.
7:4 How many a town We destroyed: Our might came upon it by night, or while it rested at midday.
7:5 Their cry, when Our might came upon them, was only to say: "Indeed we were wrongdoers."
7:6 We shall surely question those to whom the Rasuls were sent, and We shall surely question the mursalin.
7:7 Then We shall relate to them with knowledge; and We were not absent.
7:8 The weighing on that day is the truth. Those whose scales weigh heavy — those are the ones who attain success.
7:9 And those whose scales weigh light — those are the ones who lost themselves, for they were wronging Our ayat.
7:10 We have established you in the earth and made for you livings in it. Little do you thank.
7:11 We created you, then We formed you; then We said to the malakat: "Prostrate before Adam." So they prostrated, except Iblis — he was not of those who prostrate.
7:12 Allah said: "What prevented you from prostrating when I commanded you?" He said: "I am better than him; You created me from fire and created him from clay."
7:13 Allah said: "Descend from it! It is not for you to show pride in it. Get out — you are among the belittled."
7:14 He said: "Give me respite until the day they are raised."
7:15 Allah said: "You are among the respited."
7:16 He said: "Because You have misled me, I will sit in wait for them on Your straight path."
7:17 Then I will come upon them from before them and from behind them, and from their right hands and from their left hands; and You will not find most of them thankful."
7:18 Allah said: "Get out from it, rejected and driven away. Whoever of them follows you — I will surely fill Gehenna with all of you."
7:19 "Adam, dwell, you and your spouse, in the Garden, and eat from wherever you wish; but do not approach this tree, lest you be among the wrongdoers."
7:20 So Shaytan whispered to them, to show them what had been concealed from them of their shame; and he said: "Your Rabb only forbade you this tree lest you become malakat, or become of the immortal."
7:21 And he swore to them: "Indeed I am to you both one of the nasihin."
7:22 So he led them both astray by deception. When they tasted the tree, their shame became manifest to them, and they began to stitch over themselves leaves of the Garden. Their Rabb called to them: "Did I not forbid you that tree, and say to you: Shaytan is to you both an open enemy?"
7:23 They said: "Our Rabb, we wronged ourselves; and if You do not forgive us and have mercy on us, we shall surely be among the khasirin."
7:24 Allah said: "Descend, some of you to others as enemies; and for you in the earth is a resting-place and provision until a time."
7:25 Allah said: "In it you live, and in it you die, and from it you are brought forth."
7:26 Children of Adam, We have sent down upon you a garment that covers your shame, and an adornment; and the garment of mindfulness — that is better. That is of Allah's ayat, so that they may remember.
7:27 Children of Adam, do not let Shaytan tempt you, as he brought your parents out of the Garden, stripping from them their garment to show them their shame. He sees you, he and his tribe, from where you do not see them. We have made the satans protectors of those who do not believe.
7:28 When they commit an obscenity they say: "We found our fathers upon it, and Allah commanded us to it." Say: "Allah does not command obscenity. Do you say of Allah what you do not know?"
7:29 Say: "My Rabb has commanded equity. Set your faces at every place of prostration, and call on Allah, devoting the religion sincerely to Allah. As Allah began you, so you return."
7:30 A party Allah guided, and a party the straying befell rightly. They took the satans as protectors instead of Allah, and they suppose that they are guided.
7:31 Children of Adam, take your adornment at every place of prostration, and eat and drink, and do not exceed. Allah does not love the exceeders.
7:32 Say: "Who has forbidden the adornment of Allah that Allah brought forth for servants, and the wholesome things of provision?" Say: "They are for those who believed in the life of this world, exclusive on the Day of Resurrection." Thus We detail the ayat for a people who know.
7:33 Say: "My Rabb has only forbidden obscenitys, their open and their hidden, and sin, and wrongful aggression without right, and that you associate with Allah that for which Allah sent down no warrant, and that you say of Allah what you do not know."
7:34 Every nation has a term; and when its term comes, they cannot delay an hour, nor can they advance it.
7:35 Children of Adam, when rusul from among you come to you relating My ayat, whoever keeps his duty and sets right — no fear is upon them, nor do they grieve.
7:36 Those who deny Our ayat and turn away from them in pride — those are the companions of the Fire. In it they abide.
7:37 Who is more zalimun than one who fabricates against Allah a lie, or disbelieves Allah's ayat? Those will reach their allotted portion of the Kitab, until Our malakat come to them to take them, saying: "Where is that which you used to call upon besides Allah?" They say: "They have strayed from us," and they bear witness against themselves that they were kafirin.
7:38 Allah says: "Enter into nations that passed away before you of jinn and humankind into the Fire." Every time a nation enters, it curses its sister, until when they have all overtaken one another in it, the last of them say to the first of them: "Our Rabb, these led us astray, so give them a double punishment of the Fire." Allah says: "For each is double — but you do not know."
7:39 The first of them say to the last of them: "You have no superiority over us, so taste the punishment for what you earned."
7:40 Those who deny Our ayat and are arrogant toward them — the gates of the heaven will not be opened for them, nor will they enter the Garden until a camel passes through the eye of a needle. Thus We requite the mujrimin.
7:41 For them is a couch of Gehenma, and coverings above them. Thus We requite the wrongdoers.
7:42 Those who believed and did salih deeds — We charge no nafs beyond its capacity. Those are the companions of the Garden; in it they abide.
7:43 We will pull out whatever rancor is in their breasts; rivers flow beneath them. They say: "Praise belongs to Allah, who guided us to this; and we would not have been guided had Allah not guided us. The rusul of our Rabb came with the truth." And they are called: "That is the Garden you have inherited for what you did."
7:44 The companions of the Garden call to the companions of the Fire: "We have found what our Rabb promised us true. Have you found what your Rabb promised true?" They say: "Yes." Then a crier proclaims between them: "The curse of Allah is upon the wrongdoers,"
7:45 "who turn away from the way of Allah and seek to make it crooked, and of the Hereafter they are kafirin."
7:46 Between them is a veil, and on the heights are men who know each by their marks. They call to the companions of the Garden: "Peace upon you." They have not entered it, yet they hope.
7:47 And when their eyes are turned toward the companions of the Fire, they say: "Our Rabb, do not place us with the wrongdoing people."
7:48 The companions of the Heights call to men whom they know by their marks, saying: "Your gatherings availed you nothing, nor your pride."
7:49 "Are these the ones you swore Allah would not reach with mercy?" "Enter the Garden; no fear is upon you, nor do you grieve."
7:50 The companions of the Fire call to the companions of the Garden: "Pour down upon us some water, or some of what Allah provided you." They say: "Allah has forbidden both to the kafirin,"
7:51 "who took their religion as diversion and play, and whom the life of this world deceived." Today We forget them as they forgot the meeting of this day of theirs, and they used to deny Our ayat.
7:52 We brought them a Kitab that We detailed with knowledge — guidance and mercy for a people who believe.
7:53 Do they look for anything but its interpretation? On the day its interpretation comes, those who forgot it before will say: "The rusul of our Rabb came with the truth. Do we have any intercessors to intercede for us, or can we be returned so that we may do other than what we did?" They lost themselves, and what they fabricated has strayed away from them.
7:54 Your Rabb is Allah, who created the heavens and the earth in six days, then set over the raised frame, covering the day with the night. It seeks it swiftly, and the sun, the moon and the stars are subjected by Allah's command. Beware — to Allah belong creation and command. Blessed be Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
7:55 Call on your Rabb humbly and secretly. Allah does not love the transgressors.
7:56 Do not work corruption in the earth after its setting right, and call on Allah in fear and in hope. The mercy of Allah is near to those who do good.
7:57 It is Allah who sends the winds as good tidings ahead of Allah's mercy, until when they bear heavy clouds, We drive them to a dead land and send down the water with it, and bring forth with it every fruit. Thus We bring forth the dead, so that you may remember.
7:58 The good land brings forth its vegetation by the leave of its Rabb; but the vile brings forth nothing except scant and vile growth. Thus We turn the ayat about for a people who give thanks.
7:59 We sent Nuh to his people, and he said: "O my people, serve Allah — you have no ilah other than Allah. I fear for you the punishment of a tremendous day."
7:60 The chiefs of his people said: "We see you in clear error."
7:61 He said: "O my people, there is no error in me, but I am a Rasul from the Rabb of the worlds."
7:62 "I deliver to you the messages of my Rabb, and I am a sincere adviser to you, and I know from Allah what you do not know."
7:63 "Do you marvel that a reminder from your Rabb has come to you upon a man from among you, to warn you and that you may keep your duty, and that you may receive mercy?"
7:64 They denied him, so We saved him and those with him in the ship, and We drowned those who denied Our ayat. They were a people unseeing.
7:65 And to Ad, their brother Hud: "O my people, serve Allah — you have no ilah other than Allah. Will you not keep your duty?"
7:66 The chiefs who disbelieved among his people said: "We see you in folly, and we think you are one of the kazibin."
7:67 He said: "O my people, there is no folly in me, but I am a Rasul from the Rabb of the worlds."
7:68 "I deliver to you the message of my Rabb, and I am a sincere, faithful adviser to you."
7:69 "Do you marvel that a reminder from your Rabb has come to you upon a man from among you, to warn you? Remember when Allah made you successors after the people of Nuh, and increased you abundantly in creation. Remember Allah's bounties, so that you may attain success."
7:70 They said: "Have you come to us that we should serve Allah alone, and leave what our fathers served? Bring us what you promise us, if you are of the sadiqin."
7:71 He said: "Ruin and wrath from your Rabb have befallen you. Do you dispute with me over names that you and your fathers named, for which Allah sent down no warrant? Wait then — I am with you among those waiting."
7:72 So We saved him and those with him by a mercy from Us, and We cut off the remnant of those who denied Our ayat; and they were not muminin.
7:73 And to Thamud, their brother Salih: "O my people, serve Allah — you have no ilah other than Allah. Clear proof has come to you from your Rabb: this is Allah's naqah, an aya for you. Leave her to eat in Allah's land, and do not harm her, lest a painful punishment take you."
7:74 "Remember when Allah made you successors after Ad, and settled you in the land: you take palaces from its plains, and hew houses from the mountains. Remember Allah's bounties, and do not ravage in the earth as corrupters."
7:75 The chiefs who showed pride among his people said to those they had weakened, to those who believed among them: "Do you know that Salih is a Rasul from his Rabb?" They said: "Indeed we believe in what he was sent with."
7:76 Those who showed pride said: "Indeed we disbelieve in what you believed in."
7:77 So they hamstrung the naqah, and insolently defied their Rabb's command, and said: "Salih, bring us what you promise us, if you are of the mursalin."
7:78 So the earthquake seized them, and they became, in their home, sprawled.
7:79 He turned away from them and said: "O my people, I have delivered to you the message of my Rabb and advised you sincerely, but you do not love sincere advisers."
7:80 And Lut, when he said to his people: "Do you commit the obscenity that no one in the worlds has done before you?"
7:81 "You approach the males of the worlds and abandon what your Rabb made for you of mates. Indeed you are a people transgressing."
7:82 The answer of his people was only to say: "Drive them out of your town — they are people who keep themselves pure."
7:83 So We saved him and his family, except his imra'a — she was among the ghābirīn.
7:84 And We rained upon them a rain. See how the end of the mujrimin was.
7:85 And to Midian, their brother Shu'ayb: "O my people, serve Allah — you have no ilah other than Allah. Clear proof has come to you from your Rabb. Give full measure and weight, do not diminish people their goods, and do not work corruption in the earth after its setting right. That is better for you, if you are muminin."
7:86 "Do not sit in wait on every path, threatening, and turning away from the way of Allah those who believe in Allah, and seeking to make it crooked. Remember when you were few, and Allah multiplied you. See how the end of the corrupters was."
7:87 "If a party of you has believed in what I was sent with, and a party has not believed, be of the sabirin until Allah judges between us; and Allah is the best of judges."
7:88 The chiefs who showed pride among his people said: "We will surely drive you out, Shu'ayb, and those who believed with you, from our town, or you shall return to our way." He said: "Even if we are unwilling?"
7:89 "We would have fabricated against Allah a lie if we returned to your way after Allah saved us from it. It is not for us to return into it unless Allah our Rabb wills. Our Rabb encompasses all things in knowledge. Upon Allah we have placed our trust. Our Rabb, decide between us and our people with the truth — and You are the best of deciders."
7:90 The chiefs who disbelieved among his people said: "If you follow Shu'ayb, you are surely losers."
7:91 So the earthquake seized them, and they became, in their home, sprawled.
7:92 Those who denied Shu'ayb became as if they had never flourished in it. Those who denied Shu'ayb — it was they who were the khasirin.
7:93 He turned away from them and said: "O my people, I have delivered to you the messages of my Rabb and advised you sincerely. How shall I grieve for a people who disbelieved?"
7:94 We sent no Nabi to any town but We seized its people with hardship and harm, so that they might plead humbly.
7:95 Then We substituted in place of the bad the good, until they multiplied and said: "Hardship and ease touched our fathers before." So We seized them suddenly, while they were unaware.
7:96 If the people of the towns had believed and kept their duty, We would have opened upon them blessings from the heaven and the earth. But they denied, so We seized them for what they earned.
7:97 Do the people of the towns feel secure that Our might will come to them by night while they sleep?
7:98 Or do the people of the towns feel secure that Our might will come to them in the forenoon while they play?
7:99 Do they feel secure from Allah's plan? None feels secure from Allah's plan except the losing people.
7:100 Is it not clear to those who inherit the earth after its people, that if We willed We could strike them for their offenses, and stamp upon their hearts so that they do not hear?
7:101 Those are the towns whose tidings We relate to you. Their rusul came to them with clear proofs, but they could not believe in what they had disbelieved before. Thus does Allah stamp upon the hearts of those who disbelieve.
7:102 We found most of them without pact, and We found most of them fasiqin.
7:103 Then We raised after them Musa, with Our ayat, to Fir'awn and his chiefs, but they wronged with them. See how the end of the corrupters was.
7:104 And Musa said: "Fir'awn, I am a Rasul from the Rabb of the worlds,"
7:105 "obligated to say nothing of Allah except the truth. I have come to you with clear proof from your Rabb, so send with me the Children of Israel."
7:106 He said: "If you have come with an aya, then bring it, if you are of the sadiqin."
7:107 So he threw down his staff, and behold — it was a clear serpent.
7:108 And he drew out his hand, and behold — it was white to the onlookers.
7:109 The chiefs of Fir'awn's people said: "This is a master sorcerer,"
7:110 "who wants to drive you out of your land. What do you command?"
7:111 They said: "Put him off, and his brother, and send into the cities summoners"
7:112 "to bring you every cunning sorcerer."
7:113 The sorcerers came to Fir'awn. They said: "We shall surely have a reward if we are the ones who prevail?"
7:114 He said: "Yes — and you shall be among those brought near."
7:115 They said: "Musa, either you cast, or we shall be the ones to cast."
7:116 He said: "Cast." So when they cast, they bewitched the people's eyes and struck terror into them, and brought mighty sorcery.
7:117 And We revealed to Musa: "Throw your staff." And behold — it swallowed what they fabricated.
7:118 So the truth came to pass, and what they were doing proved false.
7:119 Thus they were overcome there, and turned back, humbled.
7:120 And the sorcerers fell down prostrating.
7:121 They said: "We believe in the Rabb of the worlds,"
7:122 "the Rabb of Musa and Harun."
7:123 Fir'awn said: "You believed in him before I gave you leave. This is a scheme you schemed in the city, to drive its people out. You shall soon know."
7:124 "I will surely cut off your hands and your feet on opposite sides, then I will surely crucify you all together."
7:125 They said: "Indeed to our Rabb we shall return."
7:126 "Why do you requite us except that we believed in the ayat of our Rabb when they came to us? Our Rabb, pour upon us patience, and take us to Yourself as those who submit."
7:127 The chiefs of Fir'awn's people said: "Will you leave Musa and his people to work corruption in the land, and leave you and your ilahs?" He said: "We shall kill their sons and spare their nisa, and we are triumphant over them."
7:128 Musa said to his people: "Seek help from Allah and be of the sabirin. The earth belongs to Allah — Allah gives it as inheritance to whom Allah wills of the servants; and the final outcome is for the muttaqin."
7:129 They said: "We were harmed before you came to us, and after you came to us." He said: "Perhaps your Rabb will destroy your enemy and make you successors in the land, to see how you act."
7:130 We seized the family of Fir'awn with years, and diminished fruits, so that they might remember.
7:131 When good came to them they said: "This is ours." And if evil befell them, they took it as an omen through Musa and those with him. Beware — their omen was only with Allah; but most of them do not know.
7:132 They said: "Whatever aya you bring us to bewitch us with it — we shall not believe in you."
7:133 So We sent upon them the flood, and the locusts, and the lice, and the frogs, and the blood — detailed ayat. But they showed pride, and were a mujrim people.
7:134 And when the affliction fell upon them, they said: "Musa, call on your Rabb for us by the pledge that is with you. If you remove from us the affliction, we will surely believe you, and we will surely send with you the Children of Israel."
7:135 Then when We removed from them the affliction, to an appointed term that they reached, at once they broke their pledge.
7:136 So We requited them, and drowned them in the sea, for they denied Our ayat and were heedless of them.
7:137 And We made the people who had been weakened inherit the eastern parts of the land and its western parts, which We had blessed. And the fairest word of your Rabb was completed for the Children of Israel, for their patience. And We destroyed what Fir'awn and his people made, and what they raised.
7:138 And We brought the Children of Israel across the sea, and they came upon a people cleaving to asnam they had. They said: "Musa, make for us a ilah as they have alihah." He said: "You are an ignorant people."
7:139 "These are ruined in what they are in, and void is what they were doing."
7:140 He said: "Shall I seek for you a ilah other than Allah, when Allah has favored you above the worlds?"
7:141 And when We saved you from the family of Fir'awn, who were afflicting you with the worst of punishment, killing your sons and sparing your nisa. In that was a tremendous trial from your Rabb.
7:142 We appointed Musa 30 nights, and completed them with 10, so the appointed time of his Rabb was completed at 40 nights. And Musa said to his brother Harun: "Take my place among my people, set things right, and do not follow the way of the corrupters."
7:143 And when Musa came to Our appointed time, and his Rabb spoke to him, he said: "My Rabb, show me, that I may look upon You." Allah said: "You will never see Me; but look at the mountain — if it remains firm in its place, then you will see Me." So when his Rabb unveiled glory to the mountain, Allah leveled it to dust, and Musa fell down thunderstruck. When he recovered, he said: "Glory be to You — I turn to You in repentance, and I am the first of the muminin."
7:144 Allah said: "Musa, I have chosen you above people by My messages and by My word. So take what I have given you, and be among the thankful."
7:145 And We wrote for him on the tablets an admonition and a detailing of everything: "Take them firmly, and command your people to take the best of them. I will show you the abode of the fasiqin."
7:146 I will turn away from My ayat those who show pride in the earth without right. If they see every aya, they will not believe in it; and if they see the way of right guidance, they will not take it as a way; and if they see the way of error, they will take it as a way. That is because they denied Our ayat and were heedless of them.
7:147 Those who deny Our ayat and the meeting of the Hereafter — their deeds are void. Are they requited except for what they did?
7:148 And the people of Musa took, after him, from their ornaments a calf — a body with a lowing sound. Did they not see that it did not speak to them, nor guide them to a way? They took it, and were wrongdoers.
7:149 And when the truth fell into their hands and they saw that they had gone astray, they said: "If our Rabb does not have mercy on us and forgive us, we shall surely be among the khasirin."
7:150 And when Musa returned to his people, angry and grieving, he said: "How evil is what you did after me! Did you hasten your Rabb's command?" And he threw down the tablets, and seized his brother by the head, dragging him. He said: "Son of my mother, the people overpowered me and nearly killed me. Do not let the enemy gloat over me, and do not count me among the wrongdoing people."
7:151 He said: "My Rabb, forgive me and my brother, and bring us into Your mercy — and You are the most merciful of the merciful."
7:152 Those who took the calf — wrath from their Rabb will reach them, and abasement in the life of this world. Thus We requite the fabricators.
7:153 And those who did evil deeds, then repented after them and believed — indeed your Rabb thereafter is forgiving, merciful.
7:154 And when the anger stilled upon Musa, he took up the tablets; and in their transcript was guidance and mercy for those who hold their Rabb in awe.
7:155 And Musa chose from his people 70 men for Our appointed time. So when the earthquake seized them, he said: "My Rabb, had You willed, You could have destroyed them before, and me too. Will You destroy us for what the foolish ones among us did? It is only Your trial, by which You lead astray whom You will and guide whom You will. You are our protecting friend, so forgive us and have mercy on us — and You are the best of forgivers."
7:156 "And write for us good in this world and in the Hereafter; indeed we have turned to You." Allah said: "My punishment — I strike with it whom I will; and My mercy has encompassed all things. So I will write it for those who keep their duty, and give the due, and those who believe in Our ayat,"
7:157 "those who follow the Rasul, the unlettered Nabi, whom they find written with them in the Tawrah and the Injil. He commands them the recognized, and forbids them the rejected, and makes wholesome things lawful for them, and forbids them the vile, and lifts from them their burdens and the fetters that were upon them. Those who believe in him, and support him, and help him, and follow the light sent down with him — those are the ones who attain success."
7:158 Say: "O people, I am Allah's Rasul to you all — to whom belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth. There is no ilah but Allah; Allah gives life and death. So believe in Allah and the Rasul, the unlettered Nabi, who believes in Allah and Allah's word, and follow him, so that you may be guided."
7:159 Among the people of Musa is a community guided by the truth, and by it they do justice.
7:160 We divided them into 12 tribes, as communities. And We revealed to Musa, when his people asked him for drink: "Strike the rock with your staff." So 12 springs gushed forth from it; each people knew its drinking place. We shaded them with the cloud canopy, and sent down upon them manna and quails: "Eat of the good things We provided you." They did not wrong Us, but they were wronging themselves.
7:161 And when it was said to them: "Dwell in this town, and eat from it wherever you wish, and say 'Pardon,' and enter the gate bowing — We will forgive you your misdeeds; We will increase the muhsinin."
7:162 But those who wronged among them substituted a word other than what was said to them. So We sent down upon them an affliction from the heaven, for the wrong they were doing.
7:163 And ask them about the town that was present at the sea, when they were yafsuquna the Sabbath: their huitan came to them openly on their Sabbath day, and on the day they did not keep Sabbath they did not come. Thus We tried them for their defiance.
7:164 And when a community among them said: "Why do you admonish a people Allah is about to destroy, or punish with a severe punishment?" They said: "An excuse to your Rabb, and that perhaps they may keep their duty."
7:165 So when they forgot what they had been reminded of, We saved those who forbade the evil, and We seized those who wronged with a painful punishment, for their yafsuquna.
7:166 So when they insolently defied what they had been forbidden, We said to them: "Be apes, rejected."
7:167 And when your Rabb declared that those would surely be raised against them until the Day of Resurrection, people who would inflict upon them the worst of punishment. Your Rabb is swift in requital — and indeed is forgiving, merciful.
7:168 And We dispersed them through the lands as communities: among them are salih, and among them are otherwise. We tried them with good and with evil, so that they might return.
7:169 Successors came after them who inherited the Kitab, taking the goods of this lower world and saying: "We will be forgiven." And if goods like them come to them, they take them. Was not the pact of the Kitab taken upon them, that they should say of Allah nothing but the truth, and they studied what is in it? The abode of the Hereafter is better for those who keep their duty. Will you not reason?
7:170 And those who hold fast to the Kitab, and establish the prayer — indeed We do not lose the wage of those who set things right.
7:171 And when We wrenched the mountain above them as if it were a canopy, and they thought it would fall upon them: "Take what We have given you with strength, and remember what is in it, so that you may keep your duty."
7:172 And when your Rabb took from the children of Adam, from their backs, their offspring, and made them testify concerning themselves: "Am I not your Rabb?" They said: "Yes, we testify" — lest you say on the Day of Resurrection: "We were unaware of this,"
7:173 or lest you say: "It was only our fathers who associated before, and we were descendants after them. Will You destroy us for what the void-doers did?"
7:174 Thus We detail the ayat, and perhaps they will return.
7:175 Relate to them the account of the one to whom We gave Our ayat, and he stripped away from them, so Shaytan followed him, and he became one of the misled.
7:176 Had We willed, We would have raised him by them; but he clung to the earth and followed his desire. His likeness is the likeness of a dog: if you burden it, it pants, and if you leave it, it pants. That is the likeness of a people who deny Our ayat. So relate the narratives, so that they may reflect.
7:177 Evil indeed is the likeness of a people who denied Our ayat while they were wronging themselves.
7:178 Whomever Allah guides — that is the one guided; and whomever Allah leads astray, those are the khasirin.
7:179 We have spread forth for Gehenna many of the jinn and humankind. They have hearts with which they do not understand, and eyes with which they do not see, and ears with which they do not hear. Those are like cattle — rather, they are more astray. Those are the unmindful.
7:180 To Allah belong the most beautiful names, so call on Allah thereby. And leave those who deviate in the names — they will be requited for what they did.
7:181 Among those We created is a community guided by the truth, and by it they do justice.
7:182 Those who deny Our ayat — We will draw them on gradually from where they do not know.
7:183 I grant them respite. My scheme is firm.
7:184 Have they not reflected: their companion has no madness — he is only a clear warner.
7:185 Have they not looked into the dominion of the heavens and the earth, and whatever Allah created of anything, and that perhaps their term has drawn near? In what hadith after it will they believe?
7:186 Whomever Allah leads astray — there is no guide for that one; and Allah leaves them blundering in their transgression.
7:187 They ask you about the Hour: "When will it anchor?" Say: "Its knowledge is only with my Rabb. None unveils it at its time but Allah. It weighs heavily in the heavens and the earth. It comes to you only suddenly." They ask you as though you were intimate with it. Say: "Its knowledge is only with Allah — but most people do not know."
7:188 Say: "I have no power over benefit or harm for myself, except what Allah wills. Had I known the ghayb, I would have multiplied good for myself, and no evil would have touched me. I am only a warner and a bearer of glad tidings for a people who believe."
7:189 It is Allah who created you from a single nafs, and made from it its spouse, that he might settle to her. So when he covered her, she bore a light burden and passed with it; and when it grew heavy, they both called Allah, their Rabb: "If You give us a sound one, we shall surely be among the thankful."
7:190 So when Allah gave them a sound one, they assigned partners to Allah in what Allah had given them. Exalted is Allah above what they associate.
7:191 Do they associate what creates nothing, when they themselves create?
7:192 They cannot help them, nor can they help themselves.
7:193 If you call them to guidance, they do not follow you. It is the same to you whether you call them, or you are silent.
7:194 Those you call upon besides Allah are servants like you — so call them, and let them answer you, if you are sadiq.
7:195 Do they have legs to walk with? Or hands to seize with? Or eyes to see with? Or ears to hear with? Say: "Call on your partners, then scheme against me, and give me no respite."
7:196 Indeed my protecting friend is Allah, who sent down the Kitab; and Allah takes charge of the salihin.
7:197 Those you call upon besides Allah cannot help you, nor can they help themselves.
7:198 If you call them to guidance, they do not hear; and you see them looking at you, while they do not see.
7:199 Take the surplus, and command the recognized, and turn away from the ignorant.
7:200 If a sting from Shaytan stings you, seek refuge with Allah. Allah is hearing, knowing.
7:201 Those who keep their duty — when a visitation from Shaytan touches them, they remember, and at once they see clearly.
7:202 But their brothers draw them on into error, and do not fall short.
7:203 And when you do not bring them an aya, they say: "Why have you not chosen one?" Say: "I follow only what is revealed to me from my Rabb. These are insights from your Rabb, and guidance and mercy for a people who believe."
7:204 And when the Recitation is recited, listen to it and be silent, so that you may receive mercy.
7:205 And remember your Rabb within yourself, humbly and in awe, without loudness in speech, morning and evening; and do not be among the unmindful.
7:206 Those who are with your Rabb are not too proud to serve Allah, and they glorify Allah, and to Allah they prostrate.
8:1 They ask you about the gains. Say: The gains are for Allah and the Rasul. So be mindful of Allah and set right what is between you, and obey Allah and Allah's Rasul, if you are muminin.
8:2 The muminin are only those who, when Allah is mentioned, their hearts tremble with awe, and when Allah's ayat are recited to them they increase them in belief, and upon their Rabb they rely.
8:3 Those who keep up the prayer and spend from what We have provided them.
8:4 Those are the muminin in truth. For them are degrees with their Rabb, and forgiveness, and a generous provision.
8:5 Just as your Rabb brought you out from your home with the truth, while a faction of the muminin were unwilling.
8:6 They dispute with you about the truth after it became clear, as if they were being driven to death while they watched.
8:7 When Allah promised you one of the two parties, that it would be yours, and you wished that the one without the thorn would be yours. And Allah wills to establish the truth with words, and to cut off the remnant of the kafirin.
8:8 To establish the truth and nullify the falsehood, even if the guilty disliked it.
8:9 When you cried for help to your Rabb, and the answer came to you: I am supplying you with 1,000 malakat following one another.
8:10 And Allah made it only as glad tidings, and so that your hearts would be settled by it. And help is only from Allah. Truly Allah is mighty, wise.
8:11 When drowsiness covered you, a security from it, and sent down upon you water from the heaven, to purify you with it and to take away from you the affliction of the devil, and to bind your hearts and to steady your feet with it.
8:12 When your Rabb inspired the malakat: I am with you, so steady those who believe. I will cast terror into the hearts of those who disbelieve. So strike above the necks, and strike from them every fingertip.
8:13 That is because they opposed Allah and Allah's Rasul. And whoever opposes Allah and Allah's Rasul — truly Allah is severe in punishment.
8:14 That is yours, so taste it. And the kafirin have the punishment of the fire.
8:15 O you who believe, when you meet those who disbelieve advancing, do not turn your backs to them.
8:16 And whoever turns his back to them that day, unless shifting for battle or joining a band, has met with wrath from Allah, and his shelter is hell — and evil is the arrival.
8:17 So you did not kill them, but Allah killed them. And you did not hurl when you hurled, but Allah hurled. And to test the muminin from it with a good test. Truly Allah is all-hearing, all-knowing.
8:18 That is yours, and that Allah is the weakener of the scheme of the kafirin.
8:19 If you seek triumph, the triumph has already come to you. And if you desist, it is better for you. And if you return, We will return, and your band will never avail you anything, even if numerous. And Allah is with the muminin.
8:20 O you who believe, obey Allah and Allah's Rasul, and do not turn away from him while you hear.
8:21 And do not be like those who said we heard, while they do not hear.
8:22 Truly the worst of creatures with Allah are the deaf, the dumb, those who do not reason.
8:23 And if Allah knew any good in them, Allah would have made them hear. And if Allah were to make them hear, they would turn away, turning aside.
8:24 O you who believe, respond to Allah and to the Rasul when the call comes to you for what gives you life. And know that Allah comes between a person and his heart, and that to Allah you will be gathered.
8:25 And be mindful of a trial that will not strike only those who wronged among you exclusively. And know that Allah is severe in punishment.
8:26 And remember when you were few, weakened in the land, fearing that the people would snatch you; then Allah sheltered you and strengthened you with Allah's help, and provided you from the good things, so that you may be thankful.
8:27 O you who believe, do not betray Allah and the Rasul, and do not betray your amanats while you know.
8:28 And know that your wealth and your children are only a trial, and that with Allah is a great reward.
8:29 O you who believe, if you are mindful of Allah, Allah will make for you a criterion, and wipe away your bad deeds for you, and forgive you. And Allah is the possessor of great bounty.
8:30 And when those who disbelieve scheme against you to hold you fast, or kill you, or expel you — and they scheme, and Allah schemes, and Allah is the best of planners.
8:31 And when Our ayat are recited to them, they say: We have heard; if we wished we could say the like of this. This is only tales of the ancients.
8:32 And when they said: O Allah, if this is the truth from You, then rain down upon us stones from the heaven, or bring us a painful punishment.
8:33 And Allah was not to punish them while you are among them, and Allah was not to punish them while they seek forgiveness.
8:34 And why should Allah not punish them, when they bar from the sacred mosque and were not its keepers? Its keepers are only the muttaqin. But most of them do not know.
8:35 And their prayer at the House was only whistling and clapping. So taste the punishment for what you used to disbelieve.
8:36 Truly those who disbelieve spend their wealth to bar from the way of Allah. They will spend it, then it will be regret upon them, then they will be overcome. And those who disbelieve — to hell they will be gathered.
8:37 So that Allah distinguishes the vile from the good, and makes the vile some upon some, then piles it all together, and puts it in hell. Those are the khasirin.
8:38 Say to those who disbelieve: if they desist, what has passed will be forgiven them. And if they return, the way of the ancients has already passed.
8:39 And fight them until there is no trial, and the religion is all for Allah. Then if they desist, truly Allah is all-seeing of what they do.
8:40 And if they turn away, know that Allah is your protector — an excellent protector, and an excellent helper.
8:41 And know that whatever you take as gain, a fifth of it is for Allah and for the Rasul and for the near kin, and the orphans, and the poor, and the son of the way, if you believe in Allah and what We sent down upon Our servant on the day of the criterion, the day the two hosts met. And Allah is able over all things.
8:42 When you were on the near shore and they were on the far shore, and the caravan was below you. And if you had agreed on an appointment, you would have differed about the appointment. But so that Allah decrees a matter that was to be done: so that whoever perishes perishes by clear proof, and whoever lives lives by clear proof. And truly Allah is all-hearing, all-knowing.
8:43 When Allah showed them to you in your dream as few. And if shown to you as many, you would have faltered and contended about the matter. But Allah granted safety. Truly Allah is knowing of what is in the breasts.
8:44 And when Allah showed them to you, when you met, as few in your eyes, and made you few in their eyes, so that Allah decrees a matter that was to be done. And to Allah all matters are returned.
8:45 O you who believe, when you meet a band, stand firm and remember Allah much, so that you may succeed.
8:46 And obey Allah and Allah's Rasul, and do not contend with one another, lest you falter and lose your wind. And be of the sabirin — truly Allah is with the sabirin.
8:47 And do not be like those who went out from their homes exultantly and for show before the people, and bar from the way of Allah. And Allah is muhit over what they do.
8:48 And when the devil adorned their deeds for them and said: No one among the people will overcome you today, and I am a protector for you. But when the two bands saw each other, he turned back on his heels and said: I am free of you; I see what you do not see; I fear Allah. And Allah is severe in punishment.
8:49 When the munafiqin say, and those in whose hearts is sickness: These people were deceived by their religion. And whoever relies upon Allah — truly Allah is mighty, wise.
8:50 And if you could see, when the malakat take away those who disbelieve, striking their faces and their backs: Taste the punishment of the burning.
8:51 That is for what your hands sent ahead, and that Allah is never a wrongdoer to the servants.
8:52 Like the practice of Fir'awn's clan and those before them: they disbelieved the ayat of Allah, so Allah seized them for their sins. Truly Allah is strong, severe in punishment.
8:53 That is because Allah would never change a blessing Allah bestowed upon a people until they change what is in themselves. And that Allah is all-hearing, all-knowing.
8:54 Like the practice of Fir'awn's clan and those before them: they denied the ayat of their Rabb, so We destroyed them for their sins, and We drowned Fir'awn's clan. And all were wrongdoers.
8:55 Truly the worst of creatures with Allah are those who disbelieve — so they will not believe.
8:56 Those of them with whom you made a pledge, then they break their pledge every time, and they are not mindful.
8:57 So if you overtake them in war, scatter by them those behind them, so that they may take heed.
8:58 And if you fear khiyāna from a people, cast away to them on equal terms. Truly Allah does not love the betrayers.
8:59 And let not those who disbelieve think they have outstripped. Truly they cannot escape.
8:60 And prepare against them whatever you are able of strength, and of tethered horses, to strike dread by them into the enemy of Allah and your enemy, and others besides them whom you do not know — Allah knows them. And whatever you spend of anything in the way of Allah will be repaid to you, and you will not be wronged.
8:61 And if they incline to peace, then incline to it, and rely upon Allah. Truly Allah is the all-hearing, the all-knowing.
8:62 And if they intend to deceive you, Allah is sufficient for you. Allah is the one who strengthened you with help and with the muminin.
8:63 And Allah united their hearts: if you had spent all that is in the earth, you would not have united their hearts. But Allah united them. Truly Allah is mighty, wise.
8:64 O Nabi, Allah is sufficient for you, and whoever among the muminin followed you.
8:65 O Nabi, urge the muminin to fight. If there are 20 steadfast among you, they will overcome 200. And if there are 100 among you, they will overcome 1,000 of those who disbelieve, because they are a people who do not understand.
8:66 Now Allah has lightened for you and knows that there is weakness in you. So if there are 100 steadfast among you, they will overcome 200. And if there are 1,000 among you, they will overcome 2,000, by Allah's leave. And Allah is with the steadfast.
8:67 It is not for a Nabi to have captives until firmly subduing in the land. You want the goods of the worldly life, and Allah wills the Hereafter. And Allah is mighty, wise.
8:68 Were it not for a decree from Allah that came before, a great punishment would have touched you for what you took.
8:69 So eat of what you gained, lawful and good, and be mindful of Allah. Truly Allah is forgiving, merciful.
8:70 O Nabi, say to those among the captives in your hands: If Allah knows good in your hearts, Allah will give you better than what was taken from you, and will forgive you. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
8:71 And if they intend to betray you, they already betrayed Allah before. So Allah has given you firm hold over them. And Allah is knowing, wise.
8:72 Truly those who believe and emigrate and strive with their wealth and themselves in the way of Allah, and those who sheltered and helped — those are allies of one another. And those who believe and did not emigrate: you have no alliance with them in anything until they emigrate. And if they ask your help in the religion, help is due from you, except against a people between you and whom there is a pact. And Allah is all-seeing of what you do.
8:73 And those who disbelieve — some of them are allies of one another. If you do not do so, there will be trial in the land and great corruption.
8:74 And those who believe and emigrate and strive in the way of Allah, and those who sheltered and helped — those are the muminin in truth. For them is forgiveness and a generous provision.
8:75 And those who believed afterward and emigrated and strove with you — those are of you. And blood-kin: some have closer claim than others in the decree of Allah. Truly Allah is knowing of all things.
9:1 A disavowal from Allah and Allah's Rasul to those of the mushrikin with whom you made a pledge.
9:2 Move freely in the land for 4 months, and know that you cannot frustrate Allah, and that Allah disgraces the kafirin.
9:3 And a proclamation from Allah and Allah's Rasul to the people, on the day of the greater pilgrimage, that Allah disavows the mushrikin, and so does Allah's Rasul. If you repent, that is better for you; but if you turn away, know that you cannot frustrate Allah — and give tidings to the kafirin of a painful punishment.
9:4 Except those of the mushrikin with whom you made a pledge, and who then did not diminish anything toward you and backed no one against you: fulfill for them their pledge to its full term. Allah loves the muttaqin.
9:5 Then, when the sacred months have passed, slay the mushrikin wherever you find them, seize them, confine them, and lie in wait for them at every watch-post. But if they repent, establish the prayer, and give the purifying-dues, then leave them their way. Allah is forgiving, merciful.
9:6 And if one of the mushrikin seeks your protection, grant him protection so that he may hear the word of Allah; then deliver him to his place of safety. That is because they are a people who do not know.
9:7 How can the mushrikin have a pledge with Allah and with Allah's Rasul — except those with whom you pledged at the Sacred Mosque? So long as they remain straight with you, remain straight with them. Allah loves the muttaqin.
9:8 How? If they gain the upper hand over you, they observe toward you no kinship and no protection-bond. They please you with their mouths while their hearts refuse — and most of them are fasiqun.
9:9 They exchanged the ayat of Allah for a paltry price and turned others away from the way of Allah. Evil indeed is what they used to do.
9:10 They observe toward a mumin no kinship and no protection-bond — and those are the aggressors.
9:11 But if they repent, establish the prayer, and give the purifying-dues, then they are your brothers in the faith. And We set out the ayat in detail for a people who know.
9:12 But if they break their oaths after their pledge and attack your religion, then slay the leaders of disbelief — they have no oaths to bind them — so that perhaps they may desist.
9:13 Will you not slay a people who broke their oaths and resolved to drive out the Rasul — and it was they who began against you the first time? Do you fear them? Allah is more worthy of being feared, if you are muminin.
9:14 Slay them — Allah will punish them at your hands, disgrace them, give you victory over them, and heal the hearts of a believing people.
9:15 And remove the rage from their hearts. And Allah turns in mercy toward whomever Allah wills — and Allah is knowing, wise.
9:16 Or did you reckon that you would be left alone, before Allah knows those of you who strive and take no intimate protector besides Allah, Allah's Rasul, and the muminin? And Allah is fully aware of what you do.
9:17 It is not for the mushrikin to tend the mosque of Allah while witnessing against themselves with disbelief. Those — their deeds are void, and in the fire they abide forever.
