9:18 Only those tend the mosque of Allah who believe in Allah and the Last Day, establish the prayer, give the purifying-dues, and fear none but Allah. Those — perhaps they will be among the guided.
9:19 Do you count the watering of pilgrims and the tending of the Sacred Mosque as equal to one who believes in Allah and the Last Day and strives in the way of Allah? They are not equal with Allah — and Allah does not guide the zalimun people.
9:20 Those who believe and emigrate and strive in the way of Allah with their wealth and their lives — greater in degree are they with Allah, and those are the triumphant.
9:21 Their Rabb gives them glad tidings of mercy from Allah, and approval, and gardens for them in which is lasting bliss.
9:22 Abiding therein forever. Indeed, with Allah is a great reward.
9:23 O you who believe, do not take your fathers and your brothers as allies if they prefer disbelief over belief — and whoever of you takes them as allies, those are the zalimun.
9:24 Say: If your fathers, your sons, your brothers, your zawj, your clans, wealth you have amassed, a trade you fear may slump, and dwellings you are pleased with — are dearer to you than Allah, Allah's Rasul, and striving in the way of Allah, then wait until Allah brings about the command of Allah. And Allah does not guide the fasiqin.
9:25 Allah has given you victory in many sites — and on the day of Hunayn, when your great number amazed you but availed you nothing, and the earth, for all its breadth, was straitened for you; then you turned away, fleeing.
9:26 Then Allah sent down sakina upon Allah's Rasul and upon the muminin, and sent down hosts you did not see, and punished those who disbelieved. And that is the recompense of the kafirin.
9:27 Then Allah turns in mercy, after that, toward whomever Allah wills — and Allah is forgiving, merciful.
9:28 O you who believe, the mushrikin are nothing but unclean, so let them not approach the Sacred Mosque after this year of theirs. And if you fear poverty, Allah will enrich you from the bounty of Allah, if Allah wills. Allah is knowing, wise.
9:29 Fight those who do not believe in Allah or the Last Day, do not hold sacred what Allah and Allah's Rasul have declared inviolable, and do not profess the religion of truth — among those given the Kitab — until they give the levy out of hand, humbled.
9:30 The Yahud say: "Uzair is the son of Allah," and the Nasara say: "The Messiah is the son of Allah." That is their saying with their mouths, matching the saying of those who disbelieved before. May Allah destroy them! How are they turned away?
9:31 They have taken their learned ones and their monks as rabbs besides Allah, and the Messiah son of Mary — though they were commanded only to worship one ilah; there is no ilah but Allah. Glorified is Allah above what they associate.
9:32 They want to extinguish the light of Allah with their mouths — but Allah refuses except to complete the light, even though the kafirin are averse.
9:33 It is Allah who has sent the Rasul with the guidance and the religion of truth, to make it prevail over all religion — even though the mushrikin are averse.
9:34 O you who believe, many of the learned ones and the monks indeed devour people's wealth by falsehood and turn others away from the way of Allah — and those who hoard gold and silver and do not spend it in the way of Allah: give them tidings of a painful punishment.
9:35 On the day it is heated in the fire of Jahannam, and their foreheads, sides, and backs are branded with it: "This is what you hoarded for yourselves — so taste what you used to hoard."
9:36 Indeed, the count of months with Allah is 12 months, in the Kitab of Allah, the day Allah created the heavens and the earth; of them, 4 are inviolable. That is the upright religion — so do not wrong yourselves in them, and fight the mushrikin all together, just as they fight you all together, and know that Allah is with the muttaqin.
9:37 Indeed, the postponement is but an increase in disbelief, by which those who disbelieve are led astray: they declare it lawful one year and inviolable another year, to match the count of what Allah made inviolable — thus making lawful what Allah made inviolable. Their evil deeds are made fair to them, and Allah does not guide the committing kufr people.
9:38 O you who believe, what is the matter with you that, when it is said to you, "Go forth in the way of Allah," you hang back heavily toward the earth? Are you content with the nearer life over the Last? Yet the enjoyment of the nearer life compared to the Last is nothing but little.
9:39 If you do not go forth, Allah will punish you with a painful punishment and will substitute another people in your place — and you will not harm Allah in anything. Allah is powerful over all things.
9:40 If you do not help that one — Allah has already helped, when those who disbelieve drove the Rasul out, the second of two, when the two were in the cave, when the Rasul said to the companion: "Do not grieve — Allah is with us." Then Allah sent down sakina upon the Rasul and strengthened the Rasul with hosts you did not see, and made the word of those who disbelieve the lowest, while the word of Allah is the highest. And Allah is mighty, wise.
9:41 Go forth, light and heavy, and strive with your wealth and your nafs in the way of Allah. That is better for you, if only you knew.
9:42 Had it been a near gain and a moderate journey, they would have followed you — but the distance was too far for them. And they will swear by Allah: "If we could, we would have gone out with you." They destroy themselves — and Allah knows that they are liars.
9:43 Allah has pardoned you. Why did you give them leave, before it became clear to you which of them were sadiq, and you knew the kazibin?
9:44 Those who believe in Allah and the Last Day do not ask your leave to strive with their wealth and their nafs — and Allah is knowing of the muttaqin.
9:45 Only those ask your leave who do not believe in Allah and the Last Day, whose hearts are in doubt — and in their doubt they waver.
9:46 Had they wanted to go out, they would have prepared equipment for it — but Allah disliked their setting out, so held them back, and it was said: "Sit with those who sit."
9:47 Had they gone out among you, they would have added nothing for you but corruption, and they would have plunged into your midst seeking discord for you — and among you are listeners to them. And Allah is knowing of the zalimun.
9:48 They had sought discord before, and overturned affairs for you — until the truth came and the command of Allah prevailed, while they were unwilling.
9:49 And among them is one who says: "Give me leave, and do not put me to trial." Surely into trial they have fallen — and indeed, Jahannam is muhit over the kafirin.
9:50 If good befalls you, it grieves them; and if a calamity befalls you, they say: "We took our affair in hand beforehand" — and they turn away, rejoicing.
9:51 Say: "Nothing will befall us except what Allah has decreed for us. Allah is our protector — and upon Allah let the muminin place their trust."
9:52 Say: "Do you wait for us anything but one of the two fair ends? We, for our part, wait for you — that Allah strike you with a punishment from the divine presence or at our hands. So wait — we are waiting with you."
9:53 Say: "Spend, willingly or unwillingly — it will never be accepted from you. You have been fasiqin."
9:54 And nothing withholds that their spending be accepted from them except that they disbelieve in Allah and in Allah's Rasul, and they come to the prayer only sluggishly, and they spend only unwillingly.
9:55 Let neither their wealth nor their children amaze you — Allah only wants, by means of them, to punish them in the nearer life, and that their nafs perish while they are kafirin.
9:56 They swear by Allah that they are indeed of you — but they are not of you; rather, they are a people who sow division.
9:57 If they could find a refuge, or caves, or any way in, they would turn to it, rushing headlong.
9:58 And among them are those who find fault with you concerning the alms: if they are given from them they are pleased, but if they are not given from them, at once they are angry.
9:59 If only they had been content with what Allah and Allah's Rasul gave them, and had said: "Allah is enough for us — Allah will give us from the bounty, and so will the Rasul. To Allah we turn eagerly."
9:60 The alms are only for the poor, the destitute, those working on them, those whose hearts are to be joined, for freeing necks, for those burdened with liability, in the way of Allah, and for the wayfarer — an ordinance from Allah. And Allah is knowing, wise.
9:61 And among them are those who harm the Nabi and say: "That one is all ears." Say: "An ear of good for you — one who believes in Allah and believes for the muminin, and a mercy for those of you who believe." And those who harm the Rasul of Allah — for them is a painful punishment.
9:62 They swear to you by Allah to please you — but Allah and Allah's Rasul are more worthy to be pleased by them, if they are muminin.
9:63 Do they not know that whoever yuhadidi Allah and Allah's Rasul — for such is the fire of Jahannam, abiding therein forever? That is the great disgrace.
9:64 The munafiqin dread that a chapter be sent down upon them informing them of what is in their hearts. Say: "Go on mocking — Allah will bring out what you dread."
9:65 And if you ask them, they will surely say: "We were only wading in idle talk and playing." Say: "Was it Allah, and the ayat of Allah, and the Rasul of Allah, that you were mocking?"
9:66 Make no excuses — you have disbelieved after your belief. If We pass over a party of you, We punish a party, because they were mujrimin.
9:67 The munafiqin, men and nisa, are of one another: they command the rejected, forbid the recognized, and draw in their hands. They forgot Allah, so Allah forgot them — indeed, the munafiqin are the fasiqun.
9:68 Allah has promised the munafiqin, men and nisa, and the kafirin, the fire of Jahannam, abiding therein forever — it is enough for them. And Allah has cursed them, and for them is a lasting punishment.
9:69 Like those before you — they were stronger than you in strength and greater in wealth and children; they enjoyed their life-terms, and you have enjoyed your life-terms as those before you enjoyed theirs, and you waded in idle talk as they waded. Those — their deeds are void in the nearer life and the Last, and those are the khasirin.
9:70 Has the news not come to them of those before them — the people of Nuh, and Ad, and Thamud, and the people of Ibrahim, and the people of Midian, and the Overturned ones? Their rusul came to them with clear proofs; and it was not for Allah to wrong them, but it was themselves they wronged.
9:71 And the muminin, men and nisa, are allies of one another: they command the recognized, forbid the rejected, establish the prayer, give the purifying-dues, and obey Allah and Allah's Rasul. Those — Allah will have mercy on them. Allah is mighty, wise.
9:72 Allah has promised the muminin, men and nisa, gardens through which rivers flow, abiding therein forever, and pleasant dwellings in the gardens of Eden — and approval from Allah is greater. That is the great triumph.
9:73 O Nabi, strive against the kafirin and the munafiqin, and be hard upon them. Their refuge is Jahannam — and wretched is the destination.
9:74 They swear by Allah that they did not say — yet they said the word of disbelief, and disbelieved after their submission, and resolved what they could not attain. And they resented nothing except that Allah and Allah's Rasul enriched them from the bounty. So if they repent, it will be better for them; but if they turn away, Allah will punish them with a painful punishment in the nearer life and the Last — and for them in the earth is neither protector nor helper.
9:75 And among them are those who pledged to Allah: "If Allah gives us of the bounty, we will surely give alms and be among the salihin."
9:76 But when Allah gave them of the bounty, they stinted with it, turned away, and they were averse.
9:77 So hypocrisy rebounded upon them in their hearts, until the day they meet it — because they failed Allah in what they promised, and because they used to lie.
9:78 Do they not know that Allah knows their secrets and their private counsels, and that Allah is the knower of the unseen?
9:79 Those who find fault with the willing givers among the muminin in the alms, and with those who find nothing but their utmost — and so they mock them: Allah has mocked them, and for them is a painful punishment.
9:80 Ask forgiveness for them or do not ask forgiveness for them — if you ask forgiveness for them 70 times, Allah will never forgive them. That is because they disbelieved in Allah and Allah's Rasul, and Allah does not guide the fasiqin.
9:81 Those left behind rejoiced in their sitting-place behind the Rasul of Allah, and disliked striving with their wealth and their nafs in the way of Allah, and said: "Do not go forth in the heat." Say: "The fire of Jahannam is more severe in heat" — if only they understood.
9:82 So let them laugh a little and weep much — as recompense for what they used to earn.
9:83 Then, if Allah returns you to a party of them and they ask your leave to go out, say: "You shall never go out with me, and never fight an enemy with me. You were content with sitting the first time — so sit with those who stay behind."
9:84 And never pray over any of them who dies, nor stand at that one's grave — they disbelieved in Allah and Allah's Rasul and died while they were fasiqun.
9:85 Let neither their wealth nor their children amaze you — Allah only wants to punish them thereby in the nearer life, and that their nafs perish while they are kafirin.
9:86 And when a chapter is sent down: "Believe in Allah and strive with Allah's Rasul," the ones of means among them ask your leave and say: "Leave us — let us be with those who sit."
9:87 They were content to be with the stay-behinds, and their hearts were sealed — so they do not understand.
9:88 But the Rasul and those who believe with that one strive with their wealth and their nafs — and those: for them are the good things, and those are the muflihin.
9:89 Allah has prepared for them gardens through which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. That is the great triumph.
9:90 And those making excuses among the desert Arabs came to be given leave, while those who lied against Allah and Allah's Rasul sat back. A painful punishment will strike those of them who disbelieve.
9:91 There is no blame upon the weak, nor upon the sick, nor upon those who find nothing to spend — if they are sincere toward Allah and Allah's Rasul. There is no way against the muhsinin — and Allah is forgiving, merciful.
9:92 Nor upon those who come to you for mounts, and you say: "I find nothing to mount you upon" — and they turn away, their eyes pouring with tears, out of grief that they find nothing to spend.
9:93 The blame is only upon those who ask your leave while they are rich. They were content to be with the stay-behinds, and Allah sealed their hearts — so they do not know.
9:94 They will make excuses to you when you return to them. Say: "Make no excuses — we will never believe you. Allah has informed us of your news. And Allah and Allah's Rasul will see your deeds; then you will be returned to the knower of the ghayb and the witnessed, and will be told what you used to do."
9:95 They will swear to you by Allah when you return to them, so that you may turn away from them. So turn away from them — they are filth, and their refuge is Jahannam, as recompense for what they used to earn.
9:96 They swear to you so that you may be pleased with them — but even if you are pleased with them, Allah is not pleased with the fasiqin.
9:97 The desert Arabs are more severe in disbelief and hypocrisy, and more apt not to know the hadud of what Allah has sent down upon Allah's Rasul. And Allah is knowing, wise.
9:98 And among the desert Arabs are those who take what they spend as a penalty and wait for turns of fortune against you. The turn of evil shall be upon them — and Allah is all-hearing, knowing.
9:99 And among the desert Arabs are those who believe in Allah and the Last Day, and take what they spend as means of nearness with Allah and as prayers of the Rasul. Indeed, it is a nearness for them — Allah will enter them into the mercy. Allah is forgiving, merciful.
9:100 And the sabiqun, the first of the muhajirin and the ansar, and those who followed them with good — Allah is pleased with them, and they are pleased with Allah — and Allah has prepared for them gardens through which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. That is the great triumph.
9:101 And among those around you of the desert Arabs are munafiqin, and among the people of the city — obstinate in hypocrisy. You do not know them; Allah knows them. Allah will punish them twice; then they will be returned to a great punishment.
9:102 And others have acknowledged their sins: they mixed a salih deed and another evil. Perhaps Allah will turn in mercy toward them — Allah is forgiving, merciful.
9:103 Take from their wealth an alms to purify them and cleanse them thereby, and pray over them. Indeed, your prayer is a sakan for them — and Allah is all-hearing, knowing.
9:104 Do they not know that it is Allah who accepts repentance from the servants, and takes the alms, and that Allah is the ever-relenting, the merciful?
9:105 And say: "Act — Allah will see your deeds, and so will Allah's Rasul and the muminin. And you will be returned to the knower of the ghayb and the witnessed, and will be told what you used to do."
9:106 And others are deferred for the affair of Allah — either Allah will punish them or turn in mercy toward them. And Allah is knowing, wise.
9:107 And those who have taken a mosque in harm, in disbelief, in dividing the muminin, and as an ambush for whoever warred against Allah and Allah's Rasul before — and they will surely swear: "We intended nothing but good." And Allah witnesses that they are liars.
9:108 Never stand in it. A mosque founded upon reverence from the first day is more worthy that you stand in it — in it are men who love to purify themselves, and Allah loves the purified.
9:109 Then is one who founded that one's building upon reverence of Allah and good-pleasure better, or one who founded that one's building on the edge of a crumbling cliff — so it tumbles with that one into the fire of Jahannam? And Allah does not guide the zalimun people.
9:110 Their building, which they built, will not cease to be a doubt in their hearts unless their hearts are severed. And Allah is knowing, wise.
9:111 Indeed, Allah has purchased from the muminin their nafs and their wealth — that for them is the Garden. They fight in the way of Allah: they slay and are slain — a promise true upon Allah, in the Tawrah, the Injil, and the Quran. And who is truer to the covenant than Allah? So rejoice in the deal you have made — and that is the great triumph.
9:112 The repentant, the worshippers, the praisers, the journeyers, the bowing, the prostrating, those who command the recognized and forbid the rejected, and the keepers of the hadud of Allah — and give tidings to the muminin.
9:113 It is not for the Nabi and those who believe to ask forgiveness for the mushrikin — even if they were nearest kin — after it has become clear to them that they are companions of the Blaze.
9:114 And the seeking of forgiveness by Ibrahim for his father was only about a promise he had promised him. But when it became clear to him that his father was an enemy of Allah, he disavowed him. Indeed, Ibrahim was most tender-hearted, forbearing.
9:115 And it is not for Allah to lead a people astray after guiding them, until it is made clear to them what they should be mindful of. Allah is knowing of all things.
9:116 Indeed, to Allah belongs the dominion of the heavens and the earth: Allah gives life and gives death — and you have, besides Allah, no protector and no helper.
9:117 Allah has turned in mercy toward the Nabi, the muhajirin, and the helpers who followed the Nabi in the hour of hardship, after the hearts of a party of them had nearly swerved; then Allah turned in mercy toward them — indeed, toward them Allah is gentle in mercy, merciful.
9:118 And upon the 3 who were left behind — until, when the earth was straitened for them for all its breadth, and their own selves were straitened for them, and they supposed there was no refuge from Allah except to Allah — then Allah turned in mercy toward them, that they might repent. Indeed, Allah is the ever-relenting, the merciful.
9:119 O you who believe, revere Allah and be with the sadiqin.
9:120 It was not for the people of the city and those around them of the desert Arabs to stay behind the Rasul of Allah, nor to prefer their own selves over that one's self. That is because neither thirst, nor toil, nor hunger befalls them in the way of Allah, nor do they tread any treading-place that enrages the kafirin, nor reach from an enemy any reach, but it is decreed for them thereby as a salih deed. Allah does not lose the reward of the muhsinin.
9:121 Nor do they spend any spending, small or large, nor cross any valley, but it is decreed for them — that Allah may recompense them the best of what they used to do.
9:122 And it is not for the muminin to go forth all together. So why should not a party from every division of them go forth, to understand the religion and to warn their people when they return to them, so that perhaps they may beware?
9:123 O you who believe, fight those of the kafirin who are near to you, and let them find hardness in you — and know that Allah is with the muttaqin.
9:124 And whenever a chapter is sent down, there are some of them who say: "Which of you has this increased in faith?" As for those who believe, it increases them in faith — and they rejoice.
9:125 But as for those in whose hearts is disease, it increases them in filth upon their filth — and they die while they are kafirin.
9:126 Do they not see that they are tried once or twice every year — yet they do not repent and do not remember?
9:127 And whenever a chapter is sent down, they look one to another: "Does anyone see you?" Then they turn away. Allah has turned their hearts away — because they are a people who do not understand.
9:128 A Rasul has come to you from among yourselves — grievous to that one is whatever hardship you suffer, ardently desiring good for you, gentle in mercy and merciful toward the muminin.
9:129 But if they turn away, say: "Allah is enough for me — there is no ilah but Allah. Upon Allah I have placed my trust, and Allah is the Rabb of the great arsh."
10:1 Alif Lam Ra. Those are the ayat of the wise Kitab.
10:2 Is it a marvel for mankind that We revealed to a man among them: "Warn mankind, and give good news to those who believe that for them is a footing of truth with their Rabb"? The kafirin say: "This is clear sorcery."
10:3 Indeed your Rabb is Allah, who created the heavens and the earth in six days, then settled upon the high seat; Allah directs the affair. None intercedes except by leave from Allah. That is Allah, your Rabb — so worship Allah. Will you not remember?
10:4 To Allah is your return, all together; the promise of Allah is true. Indeed Allah begins the creation, then brings it back, to repay those who believe and do the salihin deeds with equity; and those who disbelieve — for them is a drink of scalding water and a painful punishment, for what they used to disbelieve.
10:5 It is Allah who made the sun a shining light and the moon a light, and set its measures, that you may know the count of years and the reckoning. Allah created that only in truth. Allah sets out the ayat in detail for a people who know.
10:6 Indeed in the alternation of night and day, and in what Allah created in the heavens and the earth, are ayat for a people who are mindful.
10:7 Indeed those who do not hope for meeting Us, and are content with the life of this world and settled in it, and those who are unmindful of Our ayat —
10:8 their shelter is the Fire, for what they used to earn.
10:9 Indeed those who believe and do the salihin deeds — their Rabb guides them by their faith; rivers flow beneath them in the gardens of bliss.
10:10 Their call in them is: "Glory be to You, O Allah", and their greeting in them is "Peace", and the last of their call is: "Praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of all beings."
10:11 Were Allah to hasten for mankind the evil as they hasten the good, their term would already have been decreed for them. So We leave those who do not hope for meeting Us to wander blind in their transgression.
10:12 And when harm touches a human, he calls Us — on his side, or sitting, or standing. Then when We uncover the harm from him, he passes on as though he never called Us to the harm that touched him. Thus is made fair for the exceeders what they used to do.
10:13 And We destroyed the generations before you when they did wrong, and their rusul came to them with clear proofs, yet they would not believe. Thus We repay the mujrim people.
10:14 Then We made you successors in the earth after them, to see how you do.
10:15 And when Our ayat are recited to them, clear, those who do not hope for meeting Us say: "Bring a reading other than this, or change it." Say: "It is not for me to change it of my own accord; I only follow what is revealed to me. Indeed I fear, if I disobey my Rabb, the punishment of an immense day."
10:16 Say: "Had Allah willed, I would not have recited it to you, nor would Allah have made it known to you. I tarried among you a lifetime before it. Will you not reason?"
10:17 So who is more wrongful than one who fabricates against Allah a lie, or denies the ayat of Allah? Indeed the mujrimin do not attain success.
10:18 And they worship besides Allah what can neither harm them nor benefit them, and they say: "These are our intercessors with Allah." Say: "Do you tell Allah of what Allah does not know in the heavens or the earth?" Glory be to Allah, and exalted is Allah above what they associate.
10:19 Mankind was but a single community; then they differed. And had not a word gone before from your Rabb, judgment would have been passed between them over what they differ about.
10:20 And they say: "Why is an aya not sent down to him from his Rabb?" Say: "The unseen belongs to Allah alone — so wait; I am with you among the waiting."
10:21 And when We let mankind taste mercy after hardship that touched them, at once they plot against Our ayat. Say: "Allah is swifter in planning." Indeed Our envoys write down what you plot.
10:22 It is Allah who moves you on the dry land and the sea, until when you are in the ships and they sail with them on a fair wind, and they rejoice in it — a storm wind comes upon it, and waves come at them from every side, and they think they are uhit. Then they call Allah, devoting the religion to Allah alone: "If You rescue us from this, we shall surely be among the thankful."
10:23 But when Allah rescues them, at once they transgress in the earth without right. O mankind, your transgression is only against yourselves — an enjoyment of the life of this world. Then to Us is your return, and We will tell you what you used to do.
10:24 The likeness of the life of this world is only like water We send down from the heaven: the plants of the earth mingle with it, from what mankind and the livestock eat, until when the earth has taken its adornment and is beautified, and its people think they have power over it, Our command comes to it by night or by day, and We make it a harvested stubble, as if it had not flourished the day before. Thus We set out the ayat in detail for a people who reflect.
10:25 And Allah calls to the home of peace, and guides whomever Allah wills to a straight path.
10:26 For those who do good is the finest reward, and more; neither gloom nor abasement will overwhelm their faces. Those are the companions of the garden; they abide in it forever.
10:27 And those who earn evil deeds — the repayment of an evil deed is its like, and abasement will overwhelm them. They have none to shield them from Allah. As if their faces are covered by pieces of dark night. Those are the companions of the Fire; they abide in it forever.
10:28 And the day We gather them all, then We say to those who associated: "Take your places, you and your associates." Then We part them, and their associates say: "You were not worshipping us."
10:29 "Allah is sufficient as witness between us and you — we were indeed unaware of your worship."
10:30 There each nafs is tested for what it sent before, and they are returned to Allah, their true protector; and lost to them is what they used to fabricate.
10:31 Say: "Who provides for you from the heaven and the earth? Who holds the hearing and the sight? Who brings out the living from the dead and brings out the dead from the living? Who directs the affair?" They will say: "Allah." Say: "Will you not then be mindful?"
10:32 That then is Allah, your true Rabb. So what is there after the truth but wandering? How then are you turned away?
10:33 Thus the word of your Rabb comes true upon those who fasuqu, that they will not believe.
10:34 Say: "Is there any of your associates who begins the creation then brings it back?" Say: "Allah begins the creation then brings it back. So how are you turned away?"
10:35 Say: "Is there any of your associates who guides to the truth?" Say: "Allah guides to the truth." Is one who guides to the truth worthier to be followed, or one who cannot guide unless guided? What is the matter with you? How do you judge?
10:36 And most of them follow only conjecture. Indeed conjecture avails nothing against the truth. Indeed Allah knows what they do.
10:37 And this reading could not have been fabricated apart from Allah; rather, it is a confirmation of what came before it, and a detailing of the Kitab, in which there is no doubt, from the Rabb of all beings.
10:38 Or do they say: "He fabricated it"? Say: "Then bring a chapter like it, and call whomever you can apart from Allah, if you are sadiq."
10:39 No — they denied what they did not yuhitu in knowledge, and whose meaning had not yet come to them. Thus denied those before them. So see how the end of the wrongdoers was.
10:40 And among them are those who believe in it, and among them are those who do not believe in it. And your Rabb knows best the corrupters.
10:41 And if they deny you, say: "For me my deeds, and for you your deeds. You are cleared of what I do, and I am cleared of what you do."
10:42 And among them are those who listen to you. But can you make the deaf hear, even though they do not reason?
10:43 And among them are those who look at you. But can you guide the unseeing, even though they do not see?
10:44 Indeed Allah wrongs mankind nothing, but mankind wrong themselves.
10:45 And the day We gather them, it will be as though they had tarried but an hour of the day, recognizing one another. Those are the khasirin who denied the meeting with Allah, and they were not guided.
10:46 And whether We show you some of what We promise them, or We take you back, to Us is their return; then Allah is witness over what they do.
10:47 And every community has a Rasul. And when their Rasul comes, judgment is passed between them with equity, and they are not wronged.
10:48 And they say: "When is this promise, if you are sadiq?"
10:49 Say: "I possess for myself neither harm nor benefit except what Allah wills. Every community has a term; when its term comes, they cannot delay it an hour, nor can they advance it."
10:50 Say: "Tell me — if the punishment of Allah comes upon you by night or by day, what is it that the mujrimin seek to hasten?"
10:51 Then, when it befalls, will you believe in it? Now — when you had been seeking to hasten it?
10:52 Then it will be said to those who did wrong: "Taste the punishment of eternity. Are you repaid anything but what you used to earn?"
10:53 And they ask you to tell them: "Is it true?" Say: "Yes, by my Rabb, it is true indeed — and you cannot escape."
10:54 And if every nafs that did wrong had all that is in the earth, it would offer it as ransom. And they will conceal regret when they see the punishment, and judgment will be passed between them with equity, and they will not be wronged.
10:55 Indeed, to Allah belongs what is in the heavens and the earth. Indeed, the promise of Allah is true — but most of them do not know.
10:56 It is Allah who gives life and causes death, and to Allah you return.
10:57 O mankind, admonition has come to you from your Rabb, and a healing for what is in the breasts, and a guidance and a mercy for the muminin.
10:58 Say: "By the favor of Allah and by the mercy of Allah — in that let them rejoice. It is better than what they amass."
10:59 Say: "Have you considered what Allah has sent down to you of provision, and you have made some of it forbidden and some lawful?" Say: "Has Allah permitted you, or do you fabricate against Allah?"
10:60 And what do those who fabricate lies against Allah expect on the Day of Resurrection? Indeed Allah is full of favor toward mankind, but most of them do not give thanks.
10:61 And you are in no affair, nor do you recite any reading of it, nor do any deed, but We are witness over you as you pour into it. And nothing escapes your Rabb of the weight of a mote in the earth or in the heaven, nor smaller than that nor larger, but it is in a clear record.
10:62 Indeed, the allies of Allah — no fear is upon them, nor do they grieve.
10:63 Those who believe and have been mindful.
10:64 For them is the good news in the life of this world and in the other life. No change is there to the words of Allah. That is the supreme win.
10:65 And let not their speech grieve you. Indeed all might belongs to Allah. Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
10:66 Indeed, to Allah belongs whoever is in the heavens and whoever is on the earth. And those who call upon apart from Allah follow no associates — they follow only conjecture, and they do nothing but guess.
10:67 It is Allah who made the night for you to rest in, and the day giving sight. Indeed in that are ayat for a people who listen.
10:68 They say: "Allah has taken a child." Glory be to Allah! Allah is the Self-Sufficient — to Allah belongs what is in the heavens and what is in the earth. Do you have any warrant for this? Do you say of Allah what you do not know?
10:69 Say: "Indeed those who fabricate lies against Allah do not attain success."
10:70 An enjoyment in this world; then to Us is their return; then We make them taste the severe punishment for what they used to disbelieve.
10:71 And recite to them the story of Nuh: when he said to his people: "O my people, if my staying among you and my reminding you of the ayat of Allah is too much for you, then upon Allah I have relied. So resolve your affair, you and your associates together, and let not your affair be a clouded thing for you; then carry out your decision concerning me, and give me no respite."
10:72 "But if you turn away, I have not asked you for any reward — my reward is only upon Allah — and I am commanded to be among those who submit."
10:73 But they denied him, so We rescued him and those with him in the ship, and We made them successors, and We drowned those who denied Our ayat. So see how the end of those who were warned was.
10:74 Then We sent after him rusul to their people, and they came to them with clear proofs, but they would not believe in what they had denied before. Thus We seal upon the hearts of the transgressors.
10:75 Then We sent after them Musa and Harun to Fir'awn and his chiefs with Our ayat, but they were arrogant — and they were a mujrim people.
10:76 And when the truth came to them from Us, they said: "This is clear sorcery."
10:77 Musa said: "Do you say of the truth, when it has come to you: is this sorcery? Yet the sorcerers do not attain success."
10:78 They said: "Have you come to us to turn us from what we found our fathers upon, and that you two may have the greatness in the land? And we are not muminin in you."
10:79 And Fir'awn said: "Bring me every knowing sorcerer."
10:80 And when the sorcerers came, Musa said to them: "Cast what you are casting."
10:81 And when they cast, Musa said: "What you have brought is sorcery; Allah will void it. Indeed Allah does not set right the work of the corrupters."
10:82 And Allah confirms the truth by the words, even if the mujrimin dislike it.
10:83 And none believed Musa except offspring of his people, for fear of Fir'awn and his chiefs that they would persecute them. And indeed Fir'awn was lofty in the land, and indeed he was among the exceeders.
10:84 And Musa said: "O my people, if you have believed in Allah, then upon Allah rely, if you are submitters."
10:85 They said: "Upon Allah we rely. Our Rabb, do not make us a trial for the wrongdoing people."
10:86 "And rescue us by the mercy of Allah from the committing kufr people."
10:87 And We revealed to Musa and his brother: "Settle your people in homes in the Riverland, and make your homes a direction, and establish the prayer, and give good news to the muminin."
10:88 And Musa said: "Our Rabb, You have given Fir'awn and his chiefs adornment and wealth in the life of this world. Our Rabb, so that they lead astray from Your path — our Rabb, blot out their wealth and harden their hearts, so that they do not believe until they see the painful punishment."
10:89 Allah said: "Your call is answered; so stay straight and do not follow the path of those who do not know."
10:90 And We crossed the children of Israel over the sea, and Fir'awn and his hosts followed them in transgression and enmity, until when the drowning overtook him, he said: "I believe that there is no ilah but the One in whom the children of Israel believe, and I am among the submitters."
10:91 "Now — when you had disobeyed before, and were among the corrupters?"
10:92 "But this day We rescue you in your body, that you may be an aya for those after you." And indeed most of mankind are unmindful of Our ayat.
10:93 And We settled the children of Israel in a sadiq settlement, and We provided them from the good things, and they did not differ until the knowledge came to them. Indeed your Rabb will judge between them on the Day of Resurrection over what they used to differ about.
10:94 So if you are in doubt about what We have sent down to you, ask those who read the Kitab before you. The truth has indeed come to you from your Rabb, so do not be among the doubters.
10:95 And do not be among those who denied the ayat of Allah, lest you be among the khasirin.
10:96 Indeed those upon whom the word of your Rabb has come true will not believe.
10:97 Even if every aya comes to them — until they see the painful punishment.
10:98 If only there had been a community that believed, and its faith benefited it — except the people of Yunus: when they believed, We removed from them the punishment of disgrace in the life of this world, and We let them enjoy for a time.
10:99 And had your Rabb willed, all who are in the earth would have believed, all together. Would you then compel mankind until they are muminin?
10:100 And it is not for a nafs to believe except by leave of Allah; and Allah places filth upon those who do not reason.
10:101 Say: "Look at what is in the heavens and the earth." But neither the ayat nor the warnings avail a people who do not believe.
10:102 Do they await anything but the like of the days of those who passed before them? Say: "Then wait — I am with you among those waiting."
10:103 Then We rescue Our rusul and those who believe. Thus it is a right due from Us to rescue the muminin.
10:104 Say: "O mankind, if you are in doubt about my religion, then I do not worship those you worship apart from Allah; rather I worship Allah, who takes you back in death. And I am commanded to be among the muminin."
10:105 And set your face to the religion, upright, and do not be among those who associate.
10:106 And do not call upon besides Allah what can neither benefit you nor harm you; for if you do, then you are among the wrongdoers.
10:107 And if Allah touches you with harm, there is none to remove it but Allah; and if Allah wills you good, none can repel the favor of Allah. It reaches whom Allah wills among the servants. And Allah is the Most-Forgiving, the Most-Raheem.
10:108 Say: "O mankind, the truth has come to you from your Rabb. So whoever is guided — it is only for their own nafs; and whoever strays — it is only against it. And I am not a guardian over you."
10:109 And follow what is revealed to you, and be of the sabirin until Allah judges. And Allah is the best of judges.
11:1 Alif Lam Ra. A writing whose ayat are made firm, then set out in detail, from the presence of the Wise, the Aware
11:2 Serve none but Allah. I am a warner to you from Allah, and a bearer of glad tidings
11:3 And: seek your Rabb's forgiveness, then turn back to Allah; Allah will let you enjoy a good enjoyment until an appointed term, and will give every favored one favor. And if you turn away, then I fear for you the punishment of a great day
11:4 To Allah is your return, and Allah is over everything powerful
11:5 Truly they fold their chests to conceal from Allah; truly, when they cover themselves with their garments, Allah knows what they conceal and what they make open. Truly Allah is knowing of what the chests hold
11:6 There is no creature on the earth but upon Allah is its provision; and Allah knows its dwelling-place and its depositing-place. All is in a clear writing
11:7 And Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth in six days, and the raised-frame was upon the water, that Allah might test you as to which of you is best in deed. And if you say: you will be raised after death, those who disbelieve will surely say: this is nothing but clear sihr
11:8 And if We postpone the punishment from them for a counted term, they will surely say: what holds it back? Truly, on the day it comes to them, there is no averting it from them, and what they used to mock will encircle them
11:9 And if We let man taste a mercy from Us, then pull it out from him, he is despairing, ungrateful
11:10 And if We let him taste blessing after hardship that touched him, he will surely say: the evils have gone from me. Truly he is rejoicing, vaunting
11:11 Except those who sabirly endured and did right deeds; those will have forgiveness and a great reward
11:12 So perhaps you would leave behind some of what is revealed to you, and your chest is straitened by it, because they say: why has no hoard been sent down upon him, or a malakat come with him? You are only a warner, and Allah is over everything a guardian
11:13 Or do they say: he devised it? Say: then bring 10 chapters like it, devised, and call upon whomever you can besides Allah, if you are sadiq
11:14 Then if they do not answer you, know that it was sent down only by Allah's knowledge, and that there is no deity but Allah. Will you then submit?
11:15 Whoever wants the nearer life and its adornment, We will give them their deeds in full in it, and they will not be diminished therein
11:16 Those are the ones who have nothing in the hereafter but the fire; and voided is what they made in it, and null is what they used to do
11:17 Is one who stands upon clear proof from Allah, and a witness from Allah recites it, and before it was the Kitab of Musa as a leader and a mercy, among those who believe in it? And whoever of the parties disbelieves in it, the fire is the promised place. So do not be in doubt about it; it is the truth from your Rabb, but most people do not believe
11:18 And who is more zalimun than one who devises against Allah a lie? Those will be presented to their Rabb, and the witnesses will say: these are the ones who lied against their Rabb. Truly the curse of Allah is upon the zalimun
11:19 Those who turn away from Allah's path and want it crooked, while of the hereafter they are kafirin
11:20 Those could not frustrate on the earth, and they had no protectors besides Allah; the punishment will be doubled for them. They could not hear, and they could not see
11:21 Those are the ones who lost themselves, and what they used to devise strayed away from them
11:22 Doubtless, they in the hereafter are the greatest losers
11:23 Truly those who believed and did right deeds and were humble to their Rabb, those are the companions of the garden; in it they abide forever
11:24 The likeness of the two parties is as the unseeing and the deaf, and the seeing and the hearing. Are the two equal in likeness? Will you not remember?
11:25 And We sent Nuh to his people: I am a clear warner to you
11:26 That you serve none but Allah. I fear for you the punishment of a painful day
11:27 The chiefs who disbelieved from among his people said: we see you as nothing but a human like us, and we see none following you but the lowliest of us, open to view; and we see no superiority for you over us; rather we think you liars
11:28 He said: O my people, have you considered: if I am upon clear proof from my Rabb, and a mercy has been given to me from Allah, but it is hidden from you, shall we bind it fast upon you while you are averse to it?
11:29 And O my people, I ask of you no wealth for it; my reward is only upon Allah. And I am not driving away those who believed; they will meet their Rabb. But I see you as a people who are ignorant
11:30 And O my people, who will help me against Allah if I drive them away? Will you not remember?
11:31 And I do not say to you that I possess the store-houses of Allah, nor do I know the hidden, nor do I say that I am a malakat; nor do I say of those whom your eyes scorn that Allah will never give them good. Allah knows best what is in their nafs; I would then be among the zalimun
11:32 They said: O Nuh, you have disputed with us and multiplied dispute upon us. Bring us what you promise us, if you are of the sadiqin
11:33 He said: only Allah will bring it to you if Allah wills, and you cannot frustrate
11:34 And my sincere counsel will not benefit you if I intend sincerity for you, if Allah wills to mislead you. Allah is your Rabb, and to Allah you return
11:35 Or do they say: he devised it? Say: if I devised it, then upon me is my crime, and I am cleared of what crimes you commit
11:36 And it was revealed to Nuh: none of your people will believe except those who have already believed; so do not grieve at what they do
11:37 And make the ship under Our eyes and Our revelation, and do not plead with Me concerning those who did wrong; they shall be drowned
11:38 And he made the ship, and whenever a band of his chiefs passed by him they mocked him. He said: if you mock us, then we will mock you just as you mock
11:39 Soon you will know to whom comes a punishment that disgraces, and upon whom settles a lasting punishment
11:40 Until when Our command came and the oven boiled over, We said: carry in it two of every pair, and your family, except those against whom the word has preceded, and whoever believes. And none believed with him except a few
11:41 And he said: board it; in the name of Allah be its course and its anchoring. Truly my Rabb is forgiving, merciful
11:42 And it ran with them amid waves like mountains; and Nuh called his son, who was apart: O my son, board with us and do not be with the kafirin
11:43 He said: I will shelter to a mountain that will protect me from the water. He said: there is no protector today from Allah's command except those shown mercy. And the wave came between them, and he was among the drowned
11:44 And it was said: O earth, swallow your water; and O heaven, hold back. And the water subsided, and the command was fulfilled, and it came to rest upon al-Judi. And it was said: away with the zalimun people
11:45 And Nuh called his Rabb and said: Rabb, my son is of my family, and Your promise is the truth, and You are the most just of judges
11:46 Allah said: O Nuh, he is not of your family; it is a deed not right. So do not ask about what you have no knowledge of; I admonish you lest you be among the ignorant
11:47 He said: Rabb, I seek refuge with You from asking You what I have no knowledge of; unless You forgive me and have mercy on me I would be among the khasirin
11:48 It was said: O Nuh, descend with peace from Us, and blessings upon you and upon communities from those with you. And communities We will let enjoy, then a painful punishment from Us will touch them
11:49 That is of the tidings of the hidden that We reveal to you; neither you nor your people knew them before this. So be of the sabirin; the final end is for the ilah-fearing
11:50 And to Ad We sent their brother Hud. He said: O my people, serve Allah; you have no deity other than Allah. You are only devisers
11:51 O my people, I ask you no reward for it; my reward is only upon the one who originated me. Will you not reason?
11:52 And O my people, seek your Rabb's forgiveness, then turn back; Allah will send the heaven over you pouring, and add strength to your strength; and do not turn away as mujrimin
11:53 They said: O Hud, you have brought us no clear proof, and we are not ones to leave our deities on your word, nor are we muminin in you
11:54 We say nothing but that one of our deities has afflicted you with evil. He said: I call Allah to witness, and you bear witness, that I am cleared of what you join
11:55 Other than Allah! So scheme against me all together; then you shall not wait
11:56 I have placed my trust in Allah, my Rabb and your Rabb; there is no creature but Allah holds it by its forelock. Truly my Rabb is on a straight course
11:57 So if you turn away, I have already conveyed to you what I was sent with to you; and my Rabb will appoint a people other than you as successors, and you will not harm Allah in anything. Truly my Rabb is guardian over everything
11:58 And when Our command came, We saved Hud and those who believed with him by a mercy from Us; and We saved them from a hard punishment
11:59 And that was Ad: they denied the ayat of their Rabb, disobeyed their rusul, and followed the command of every obstinate tyrant
11:60 And the curse followed them in this nearer life, and on the day of resurrection. Truly Ad disbelieved their Rabb. Truly away with Ad, the people of Hud
11:61 And to Thamud We sent their brother Salih. He said: O my people, serve Allah; you have no deity other than Allah. Allah raised you up from the earth and settled you in it to build and keep it; so seek forgiveness, then turn back to Allah. Truly my Rabb is near, answering
11:62 They said: O Salih, you had been among us one hoped in before this. Do you now forbid us to serve what our fathers served? We are in doubting doubt about what you call us to
11:63 He said: O my people, have you considered: if I am upon clear proof from my Rabb, and a mercy from Allah has been given to me, who would help me against Allah if I disobeyed? You would add nothing for me but loss
11:64 And O my people, this is the naqah of Allah, an aya for you; leave her to graze in Allah's earth, and do not touch her with evil, or a near punishment will take you
11:65 So they cut her down. He said: enjoy yourselves in your homes 3 days; that is a promise not belied
11:66 So when Our command came, We saved Salih and those who believed with him by a mercy from Us, and from the disgrace of that day. Truly your Rabb is the powerful, the mighty
11:67 And the shout took those who did wrong, and they became sprawled in their homes
11:68 As though they had never flourished in them. Truly Thamud disbelieved their Rabb. Truly away with Thamud
11:69 And Our malakat came to Ibrahim with the glad tidings; they said: peace. He said: peace. And he had hardly tarried when he brought a roasted calf
11:70 And when he saw their hands not reaching toward it, he found them strange and conceived fear of them. They said: fear not; we have been sent to the people of Lut
11:71 And his imra'a was standing; she laughed, and We gave her glad tidings of Ishaq, and behind Ishaq, Ya'qub
11:72 She said: woe to me! Shall I bear a child when I am an jawza, and this ba'l of mine an old man? This is indeed a marvelous thing
11:73 They said: do you marvel at Allah's command? The mercy of Allah and blessing be upon you, people of the house. Truly Allah is praiseworthy, glorious
11:74 And when the terror left Ibrahim, and the glad tidings came to him, he disputed with Us over the people of Lut
11:75 Truly Ibrahim was forbearing, tenderly grieving, ever turning back
11:76 O Ibrahim, turn aside from this; the command of your Rabb has already come, and a punishment not turned back will come to them
11:77 And when Our malakat came to Lut, he was distressed at them and straitened for room by them, and he said: this is a hard day
11:78 And his people came hurrying to him, and before they had been doing evils. He said: O my people, these are my daughters; they are purer for you. So be mindful of Allah and do not disgrace me in my guests. Is there not among you a man of right course?
11:79 They said: you know well we have no right concerning your daughters, and you know well what we want
11:80 He said: if only I had strength against you, or could shelter to a firm support
11:81 They said: O Lut, we are rusul of your Rabb; they shall not reach you. So set out with your family in a piece of the night, and let not any of you look back except your imra'a; what strikes them will strike her. Their appointment is the morning. Is the morning not near?
11:82 So when Our command came, We made its highest part its lowest, and We rained upon it stones of clay, layered
11:83 Marked by your Rabb; and it is not far from the zalimun
11:84 And to Midian We sent their brother Shu'ayb. He said: O my people, serve Allah; you have no deity other than Allah; and do not diminish the measure and the scale. I see you in plenty, and I fear for you the punishment of a muhit day
11:85 And O my people, give full the measure and the scale with equity, and do not diminish people their things, and do not ravage in the earth as corrupters
11:86 What Allah leaves remaining is better for you, if you are muminin; and I am not a guardian over you
11:87 They said: O Shu'ayb, does your prayer command you that we leave what our fathers served, or that we do with our wealth whatever we will? You are indeed the forbearing, the rightly guided
11:88 He said: O my people, have you considered: if I am upon clear proof from my Rabb, and my Rabb has provided me a good provision, and I have no wish to oppose you in what I forbade you; I want only reform, as much as I can, and my success is only through Allah. Upon Allah I trust, and to Allah I turn back
11:89 And O my people, let not your falling out with me move you, that there strike you the like of what struck the people of Nuh, or the people of Hud, or the people of Salih; and the people of Lut are not far from you
11:90 And seek your Rabb's forgiveness, then turn back; truly my Rabb is merciful, most affectionate
11:91 They said: O Shu'ayb, we do not understand much of what you say, and we see you weak among us. Were it not for your clan we would have stoned you, and you are not mighty over us
11:92 He said: O my people, is my clan mightier over you than Allah, and you have cast Allah behind your backs? Truly my Rabb is muhit over what you do
11:93 And O my people, work upon your standing-place; I am working. Soon you will know who will come to the disgracing punishment, and who is the liar. And watch; I am with you, watching
11:94 And when Our command came, We saved Shu'ayb and those who believed with him by a mercy from Us, and the shout took those who did wrong, and they became sprawled in their homes
11:95 As though they had never flourished in them. Away with Midian, just as Thamud went away
11:96 And We sent Musa with Our ayat and a clear warrant
11:97 To Fir'awn and his chiefs, but they followed the command of Fir'awn; and the command of Fir'awn was not rightly guided
11:98 He will go ahead of his people on the day of resurrection, and bring them to the fire; and wretched is the arrival-place arrived at
11:99 And the curse followed them in this life and on the day of resurrection; wretched is the gift bestowed
11:100 That is of the tidings of the towns We narrate to you; of them are standing and mown down
11:101 We did not wrong them, but they wronged themselves; and their deities that they called upon besides Allah availed them nothing when the command of your Rabb came, and they added to them nothing but ruin
11:102 And thus is the seizing of your Rabb when seizing the towns while they are zalimun; truly the seizing is painful, severe
11:103 Truly in that is an aya for those who fear the punishment of the hereafter. That is a day of gathering for humankind, and that is a witnessed day
11:104 And We postpone it only for a counted term
11:105 On the day it comes, no nafs speaks except by permission; and among them are the wretched and the happy
11:106 As for those who were wretched, they are in the fire; for them therein are roaring and panting
11:107 Abiding therein as long as the heavens and the earth endure, except what your Rabb wills. Truly your Rabb is a doer of what Allah wills
11:108 And as for those who were happy, they are in the garden, abiding therein as long as the heavens and the earth endure, except what your Rabb wills: a gift unceasing
11:109 So do not be in doubt about what these ones serve. They serve only as their fathers served before; and We will give them their allotted portion in full, undiminished
11:110 And We gave Musa the Kitab, and discord was made in it; and had not a prior word from your Rabb gone before, judgment would have been passed between them. And they are in doubting doubt about it
11:111 And truly, each and every one, your Rabb will give them in full their deeds; truly Allah is aware of what they do
11:112 So stand firm as you were commanded, and whoever repented along with you; and do not transgress. Truly Allah sees what you do
11:113 And do not incline toward those who did wrong, lest the fire touch you; and you have no protectors besides Allah; then you will not be helped
11:114 And establish the prayer at the two ends of the day and in the early night. Truly the good deeds take away the evils. That is a remembrance for those who remember
11:115 And be of the sabirin; truly Allah does not lose the reward of the muhsinin
11:116 If only there had been, among the generations before you, people of remaining good who forbade corruption in the earth, except a few of those We saved from among them. And those who did wrong pursued the ease in which they were softened, and they were mujrimin
11:117 And it is not for your Rabb to destroy the towns by injustice while their people are reformers
11:118 And had your Rabb willed, Allah would have made humankind one community; and they will not cease to differ
11:119 Except those your Rabb has mercy on; and for that Allah created them. And the word of your Rabb was completed: I will surely fill hell with the jinn and humankind, all together
11:120 And all of it We narrate to you from the tidings of the Rasuls, by which We make your heart firm. And in this, the truth has come to you, and an admonition, and a remembrance for the muminin
11:121 And say to those who do not believe: work upon your standing-place; we are working
11:122 And wait; we are waiting
11:123 And to Allah belongs the hidden of the heavens and the earth, and to Allah returns the whole affair. So serve Allah and trust in Allah; and your Rabb is not unmindful of what you do
12:1 Alif Lam Ra. These are the ayat of the clear Kitab.
12:2 We have sent it down as a reading in clear Arabic, so that you may reason.
12:3 We narrate to you the best of narratives in what We have inspired to you of this reading, though before it you were among the unmindful.
12:4 When Yusuf said to his father: "Father, I saw 11 stars, and the sun and the moon — I saw them bowing down to me."
12:5 He said: "My son, do not narrate your vision to your brothers, lest they scheme against you a scheme. Truly the satan is to the human a clear enemy."
12:6 "Thus your Rabb will choose you and teach you the interpretation of ahadith, and complete favor upon you and upon the family of Ya'qub, as it was completed before upon your two fathers, Ibrahim and Ishaq. Truly your Rabb is knowing, wise."
12:7 Already in Yusuf and his brothers were ayat for those who ask.
12:8 When they said: "Yusuf and his brother are more beloved to our father than we, though we are a band. Truly our father is in clear error."
12:9 "Kill Yusuf, or cast him to some land, so that your father's face may be free for you, and you may be after him a people good."
12:10 A speaker among them said: "Do not kill Yusuf — cast him into the hidden depths of the well, and some of the travelers will pick him up, if you are doers."
12:11 They said: "Father, what is it with you that you do not trust us with Yusuf? We are indeed sincere to him."
12:12 "Send him with us tomorrow, to roam and play; we will keep him safe."
12:13 He said: "It grieves me that you should go off with him, and I fear the wolf will eat him while you are unmindful of him."
12:14 They said: "If the wolf eats him while we are a band, then we are losers indeed."
12:15 So when they went with him and agreed to put him into the hidden depths of the well, We inspired to him: "You shall tell them of this affair of theirs, and they perceive not."
12:16 And they came to their father in the evening, weeping.
12:17 They said: "Father, we went racing and left Yusuf by our goods, and the wolf ate him; and you would not believe us even if we were sadiq."
12:18 And they came with his shirt, false blood upon it. He said: "No — your nafs have allured you into an affair. So, beautiful patience! And Allah is the One whose help is sought against what you describe."
12:19 And a traveling company came and sent their water-drawer, and he let down his bucket. He said: "Good news — here is a boy!" And they concealed him as goods. And Allah knows what they do.
12:20 And they sold him for a diminished price, counted dirhams, and they had little regard for him.
12:21 And the one who bought him, from Riverland, said to his imra'a: "Give him a generous lodging; perhaps he will benefit us, or we may take him as a son." Thus We established Yusuf in the land, that We might teach him the interpretation of ahadith. And Allah overcomes in the affair, but most people do not know.
12:22 And when he reached his full strength, We gave him judgment and knowledge. Thus We reward the muhsinin.
12:23 And she in whose house he was solicited him, and locked the doors, and said: "Here you are!" He said: "I seek refuge in Allah — indeed, my Rabb has given me a good lodging. Truly the wrongdoers do not attain success."
12:24 And she resolved upon him, and he resolved upon her, had he not seen the clear proof of his Rabb. Thus We averted from him the evil and the obscenity. Truly he was among Our abd, the mukhlisin.
12:25 And they raced to the door, and she tore his shirt from behind; and they found her master at the door. She said: "What is the requital of one who intended evil to your family, but that he be imprisoned, or given a painful punishment?"
12:26 He said: "It was she who solicited me." And a witness from her family bore witness: "If his shirt is torn from the front, then she is sadiq and he is among the kazibin."
12:27 "And if his shirt is torn from behind, then she lies, and he is among the sadiqin."
12:28 So when he saw that his shirt was torn from behind, he said: "It is of your scheming; your scheme is great indeed."
12:29 "Yusuf, turn away from this; and ask forgiveness for your offense. Truly you were among the erring."
12:30 And nisa of the town said: "The Aziz's imra'a is soliciting her young servant; love has smitten her through. We see her in clear error."
12:31 So when she heard of their scheme, she sent to them and prepared for them a reclining couch, and gave each one of them a knife, and said: "Come out upon them." So when they saw him they were awed by him and cut their hands, and said: "By Allah, this is no mortal — this is none but a noble malakat."
12:32 She said: "That is the one you blamed me about. And I indeed solicited him, but he protected-held. And if he does not do what I command him, he shall be imprisoned and shall be among the belittled."
12:33 He said: "My Rabb, prison is more beloved to me than what they call me to. And if You do not avert their scheme from me, I will incline to them and be among the ignorant."
12:34 So his Rabb answered him and averted their scheme from him. Truly Allah is the Hearing, the Knowing.
12:35 Then, though they had seen the ayat, it appeared to them that they should imprison him for a time.
12:36 And two youths entered the prison with him. One of them said: "I see myself pressing wine." And the other said: "I see myself carrying bread upon my head, and birds eat of it. Tell us its interpretation — we see you among the muhsinin."
12:37 He said: "No food will come to you both that you are provided with, but I will tell you its interpretation before it comes to you. That is of what my Rabb has taught me. I have left the way of a people who do not believe in Allah, and who of the Hereafter are kafirin."
12:38 "And I have followed the way of my fathers, Ibrahim and Ishaq and Ya'qub. It was not for us to associate anything with Allah. That is of Allah's favor upon us and upon the people — but most people do not give thanks."
12:39 "O my two prison companions — are separate rabbs better, or Allah, the One, the Overpowering?"
12:40 "What you worship apart from Allah is nothing but names you have named, you and your fathers, for which Allah has sent down no warrant. Judgment belongs to Allah alone: the command that you worship none but Allah. That is the upright way — but most people do not know."
12:41 "O my two prison companions: as for one of you, he will serve his master wine; and as for the other, he will be crucified, and birds will eat from his head. Decided is the affair about which you two asked a ruling."
12:42 And he said to the one of the two whom he supposed would be saved: "Remember me with your master." But the satan made him forget to remember his master, so he tarried in the prison several years.
12:43 And the king said: "I see 7 fat cows being eaten by 7 lean ones, and 7 green ears of grain and others dry. O you chiefs, give me a ruling about my vision, if you can interpret visions."
12:44 They said: "A jumble of dreams — and we have no knowledge of dream interpretation."
12:45 And the one saved of the two said, remembering after a time: "I will tell you its interpretation; so send me."
12:46 "Yusuf, O sadiq one, give us a ruling about 7 fat cows being eaten by 7 lean ones, and 7 green ears and others dry — that I may return to the people so that they may know."
12:47 He said: "You will crop 7 years unceasingly; so what you harvest, leave in its ears, except a little that you eat."
12:48 "Then 7 hard years will come after that, eating what you have laid up for them, except a little that you fortify."
12:49 "Then after that a year will come in which the people are given rain, and in which they press."
12:50 And the king said: "Bring him to me." So when the Rasul came to him, he said: "Return to your master and ask him: what of the nisa who cut their hands? Truly my master is knowing of their scheme."
12:51 He said: "What was your affair when you solicited Yusuf?" They said: "By Allah, we know no evil of him." The Aziz's imra'a said: "Now the truth has come clear: it was I who solicited him, and he is among the sadiqin."
12:52 "That is so that he may know that I did not betray him in the ghayb, and that Allah does not guide the kayd of the khā'inīn."
12:53 "And I do not clear myself; truly the self is ever commanding to evil, except what my Rabb has mercy on. Truly my Rabb is Forgiving, Raheem."
12:54 And the king said: "Bring him to me — I will take him exclusively for myself." So when he spoke with him, he said: "You are today with us established and trusted."
12:55 He said: "Set me over the store-houses of the land; I am a keeper, knowing."
12:56 Thus We established Yusuf in the land; he settles in it wherever he wishes. We reach with Our mercy whom We wish, and We do not lose the wage of the muhsinin.
12:57 And the wage of the Hereafter is better for those who believe and were ever guarding.
12:58 And the brothers of Yusuf came and entered upon him, and he recognized them, while they were strangers to him.
12:59 And when he had equipped them with their equipment, he said: "Bring me a brother of yours from your father. Do you not see that I fulfil the measure and am the best of hosts?"
12:60 "But if you do not bring him to me, there will be no measure for you with me, and you will not come near."
12:61 They said: "We will ask his father about him, and we will do it."
12:62 And he said to his youths: "Place their goods in their packs, so that they may recognize it when they turn back to their family — perhaps they will return."
12:63 So when they returned to their father they said: "Father, the measure was withheld from us; so send our brother with us, that we may measure — and we will keep him safe."
12:64 He said: "Should I trust him to you, other than as I trusted you with his brother before? Allah is the best keeper, and the most merciful of the merciful."
12:65 And when they opened their goods they found their merchandise returned to them. They said: "Father, what more could we want? Here is our merchandise returned to us; we will supply our family, keep our brother, and add a camel's measure — that is an easy measure."
12:66 He said: "I will not send him with you until you give me a pledge from Allah that you will bring him to me, unless you are yuhat." So when they gave him their pledge, he said: "Allah is guardian over what we say."
12:67 And he said: "My sons, do not enter by one gate; enter by separate gates. And it avails you nothing against Allah of anything. Judgment belongs to Allah alone; upon Allah I rely, and upon Allah let the reliant rely."
12:68 And when they entered from where their father commanded them, it availed them nothing against Allah of anything, except a need in Ya'qub's nafs that he fulfilled; and he is possessed of knowledge through what We taught him — but most people do not know.
12:69 And when they entered upon Yusuf, he sheltered his brother with him, saying: "I am your brother — so do not grieve at what they used to do."
12:70 So when he had equipped them with their equipment, he placed the drinking-cup in his brother's pack; then a crier cried out: "O caravan — you are thieves!"
12:71 They said, coming at them: "What are you missing?"
12:72 They said: "We miss the king's cup; and whoever brings it shall have a camel's load, and I am its guarantor."
12:73 They said: "By Allah, you know well that we did not come to corrupt the land, and we are not thieves."
12:74 They said: "Then what is its requital, if you are liars?"
12:75 They said: "Its requital is that he in whose pack it is found — he is its requital. Thus we requite the wrongdoers."
12:76 So he began with their packs before his brother's pack; then he drew it out of his brother's pack. Thus We schemed for Yusuf — he could not have taken his brother under the king's law unless Allah willed. We raise in degree whom We wish, and above every knower is a Knower.
12:77 They said: "If he steals — well, a brother of his stole before." So Yusuf kept it hidden in himself and did not disclose it to them. He said: "You are in a worse place. And Allah knows best what you describe."
12:78 They said: "O Aziz, he has a father, an aged sheikh; take one of us in his place — we see you among the muhsinin."
12:79 He said: "I seek refuge in Allah that we should take anyone but him in whom we found our goods — then we would be wrongdoers."
12:80 So when they despaired of him, they withdrew to confer privately. The eldest of them said: "Do you not know that your father has taken from you a pledge from Allah, and how before you fell short in Yusuf? I will not leave the land until my father permits me or Allah decides for me — and Allah is the best of deciders."
12:81 "Return to your father and say: 'Father, your son stole; and we witnessed only what we knew, and we were not keepers of the ghayb.'"
12:82 "'And ask the town in which we were, and the caravan in which we came — and we are sadiq.'"
12:83 He said: "No — your nafs have allured you into an affair. So, beautiful patience! Perhaps Allah will bring them to me all together. Truly Allah is the Knowing, the Wise."
12:84 And he turned away from them and said: "My grief for Yusuf!" And his eyes went white from grief; he held it in.
12:85 They said: "By Allah, you will not cease remembering Yusuf until you are worn to a wreck or among the perishing."
12:86 He said: "I pour out my anguish and my grief only to Allah, and I know from Allah what you do not know."
12:87 "My sons, go and seek news of Yusuf and his brother, and do not despair of the spirit of Allah — none despairs of the spirit of Allah except the committing kufr people."
12:88 So when they entered upon him they said: "O Aziz, hardship has touched us and our family, and we have brought goods of little worth. So fulfil the measure for us and be charitable to us — truly Allah requites the mutasaddiqin."
12:89 He said: "Do you know what you did to Yusuf and his brother, when you were ignorant?"
12:90 They said: "Are you indeed Yusuf?" He said: "I am Yusuf, and this is my brother. Allah has favored us. Whoever guards and is sabir — truly Allah does not lose the wage of the muhsinin."
12:91 They said: "By Allah, Allah has preferred you over us, and we were indeed among the erring."
12:92 He said: "No reproach upon you today. Allah will forgive you, and is the most merciful of the merciful."
12:93 "Go with this shirt of mine and cast it on my father's face; he will come seeing. And bring me your family all together."
12:94 And when the caravan set out, their father said: "I find the scent of Yusuf — were it not that you would deny me."
12:95 They said: "By Allah, you are in your old error."
12:96 So when the bearer of good news came, he cast it upon his face, and he returned seeing. He said: "Did I not tell you that I know from Allah what you do not know?"
12:97 They said: "Father, ask forgiveness for us of our offenses; we were indeed among the erring."
12:98 He said: "I will ask forgiveness for you from my Rabb; truly Allah is the Forgiving, the Raheem."
12:99 So when they entered upon Yusuf, he sheltered his parents with him, and said: "Enter Riverland, if Allah wills, in safety."
12:100 And he raised his parents onto the high-seat, and they fell down before him bowing. He said: "Father, this is the interpretation of my vision of old; my Rabb has made it come true. And Allah has been good to me, bringing me out of the prison and bringing you from the open-land, after the satan had sown discord between me and my brothers. Truly my Rabb is subtle in whatever Allah wills; truly Allah is the Knowing, the Wise."
12:101 "My Rabb, You have given me of the dominion and taught me the interpretation of ahadith. Originator of the heavens and the earth — You are my guardian in the world and the Hereafter. Take me to You in submission and join me with the salihin."
12:102 That is of the tidings of the ghayb that We inspire to you. And you were not with them when they agreed their affair while they were scheming.
12:103 And most people would not believe, though you ardently want it.
12:104 And you ask them no wage for it; it is nothing but a reminder for the worlds.
12:105 And how many an aya in the heavens and the earth they pass by, while they turn away from it.
12:106 And most of them do not believe in Allah except while they are associators.
12:107 Do they feel secure that a covering of Allah's punishment may come upon them, or that the Hour may come to them suddenly while they perceive not?
12:108 Say: "This is my way — I call to Allah upon clear sight, I and whoever follows me. And glory be to Allah; and I am not among the associators."
12:109 And We sent before you none but men, whom We inspired, from the people of the towns. Have they not journeyed in the land to see how was the outcome of those before them? And the abode of the Hereafter is better for those who were ever guarding. Will you not then reason?
12:110 Until, when the Rasuls despaired and supposed they had been denied, Our help came to them, and We saved whom We wished. And Our might is not turned back from the mujrim people.
12:111 Truly in their narratives is a lesson for people of understanding. It is no invented hadith, but a confirmation of what is before it, and a detailing of everything, and guidance and mercy for a people who believe.
13:1 Alif Lam Mim Ra. Those are the ayat of the Kitab, and what has been sent down to you from your Rabb is the truth, but most people do not believe.
13:2 Allah is the one who raised up the heavens without pillars that you can see, then steadied upon the high seat, and subjected the sun and the moon, each running for an appointed term. Allah manages the whole affair and distinguishes the ayat, so that you may be certain of meeting your Rabb.
13:3 And Allah is the one who spread out the earth and placed in it anchors and rivers, and of every fruit made in it two pairs. The night covers the day. Indeed in that are ayat for people who reflect.
13:4 In the earth are adjoining plots, and gardens of grapes and crops, and palms twinned and not twinned, all given to drink with one water, yet some are favored above others in food. Indeed in that are ayat for people who understand.
13:5 And if you marvel, then marvel at their saying: "When we are dust, are we then in a new-made creation?" Those are the ones who disbelieved in their Rabb; and those are the fetters upon their necks; and those are the companions of the fire. They abide in it forever.
13:6 They ask you to hasten the bad before the good, though examples like them have passed before. And indeed your Rabb is forgiving to people despite their wrongdoing, and indeed your Rabb is severe in punishment.
13:7 Those who disbelieve say: "Why has no aya been sent down to him from his Rabb?" You are only a warner, and every people has a guide.
13:8 Allah knows what every female carries, and what the wombs fall short of and what they increase, and everything with Allah is by measure.
13:9 Knower of the hidden and the witnessed, the Great, the Most High.
13:10 Alike among you are one who conceals the word and one who proclaims it openly, and one who is hidden by night and one who moves openly by day.
13:11 There are successive guardians before each person and behind, guarding by Allah's command. Indeed Allah does not change what is with a people until they change what is with themselves. And when Allah wills ill for a people, there is no turning it back, and they have no protector besides Allah.
13:12 It is Allah who shows you the lightning, in fear and in hope, and raises the heavy clouds.
13:13 And the thunder proclaims Allah's praise, and the malakat do so from awe, and Allah sends the thunderbolts, striking with them whom Allah wills — while they dispute about Allah, and Allah is severe in power.
13:14 To Allah belongs the call of truth. Those they call upon besides Allah answer them with nothing, any more than one who stretches out his palms toward the water so that it may reach his mouth, but it never reaches it. And the call of the kafirin is only in straying.
