13:15 And to Allah bows whoever is in the heavens and the earth, willingly and unwillingly, and their shades in the mornings and the late afternoons.
13:16 Say: who is the Rabb of the heavens and the earth? Say: Allah. Say: have you taken protectors besides Allah who own for themselves no benefit and no harm? Say: are the unseeing and the seeing equal, or are the darknesses and the light equal? Or have they made for Allah associates who create as Allah creates, so that creation is alike to them? Say: Allah created everything, and Allah is the One, the Overpowering.
13:17 Allah sent down water from the heaven, and valleys flowed by their measure, and the flood carried swelling foam. And from what they kindle in the fire, seeking adornment or goods, comes foam like it. Thus Allah strikes the truth and the falsehood: as for the foam, it passes away as scum, and as for what benefits people, it stays in the earth. Thus Allah strikes the examples.
13:18 For those who answered their Rabb, the fairest reward; and those who did not answer — if they had all that is in the earth and its like with it, they would ransom themselves with it. Those have the evil reckoning, and their destination is hell, and wretched is the couch.
13:19 Is one who knows that what was sent down to you from your Rabb is the truth like one who is unseeing? Only those of understanding take heed.
13:20 Those who fulfill the pledge of Allah and do not unbind the pact.
13:21 And those who join what Allah commanded to be joined, and hold reverent awe of their Rabb, and fear the evil reckoning.
13:22 And those who were sabir seeking the face of their Rabb, and established the prayer, and spent from what was provided to them secretly and openly, and ward off the bad with the good — those have the best end of the home.
13:23 Gardens of Eden, which they enter, together with those of their fathers, their zawj, and their descendants who were right — and the malakat enter upon them from every door.
13:24 Peace upon you for what you were sabir with; and how good is the end of the home.
13:25 And those who unbind the pledge of Allah after its pact, and sever what Allah commanded to be joined, and do corruption in the earth — those have the curse, and they have the evil of the home.
13:26 Allah spreads out provision for whom Allah wills and restricts it, and they rejoiced in the nearer life — but the nearer life, compared to the final life, is only a passing enjoyment.
13:27 Those who disbelieve say: "Why has no aya been sent down to him from his Rabb?" Say: Allah lets stray whom Allah wills, and guides to Allah whoever turns back.
13:28 Those who believed, and whose hearts are settled by the remembrance of Allah — indeed, by the remembrance of Allah are hearts settled.
13:29 Those who believed and did the salihin deeds — blessedness for them, and a goodly return.
13:30 Thus We sent you to a people before whom other peoples passed, so that you may recite to them what was revealed to you, while they disbelieve in the Rahman. Say: my Rabb is Allah; there is no ilah but Allah; upon Allah I trust, and to Allah is my return.
13:31 And if there were a recitation by which the mountains were set moving, or the earth cleft, or the dead spoken to — rather, to Allah belongs the whole affair. Have those who believed not despaired that if Allah willed, Allah would guide all people? And those who disbelieve will not cease: the striking will befall them for what they made, or alight near their home, until the promise of Allah comes. Indeed Allah does not break the appointment.
13:32 And rusul before you were mocked, and I gave respite to those who disbelieved, then I seized them — and how was the punishment!
13:33 Is the one who stands over every nafs for what it earned...? And they made for Allah associates. Say: name them! Or do you inform Allah of something Allah does not know on the earth, or is it manifest talk? Rather, their plan was made attractive to those who disbelieved, and they barred from the way. And whomever Allah lets stray has no guide.
13:34 They have punishment in the nearer life, and the punishment of the final life is harder, and they have none to shield them from Allah.
13:35 The likeness of the garden promised to the muttaqin: rivers flow beneath it, its food is everlasting, and its shade. That is the end of those who were mindful, and the end of the kafirin is the fire.
13:36 Those whom We gave the Kitab rejoice in what was sent down to you, and among the parties are those who disavow some of it. Say: I was only commanded to serve Allah and not to associate anything with Allah; to Allah I call, and to Allah is the return.
13:37 And thus We sent it down as a clear Arabic ruling. If you were to follow their desires after the knowledge that came to you, you would have from Allah neither protector nor shield.
13:38 And We sent rusul before you, and We made for them zawj and descendants. It is not for a Rasul to bring an aya except by Allah's leave. For every term is a written record.
13:39 Allah effaces what Allah wills and confirms, and with Allah is the mother of the Kitab.
13:40 Whether We show you some of what We promise them, or We take you — upon you is only the delivery, and upon Us is the reckoning.
13:41 Have they not seen that We come to the land, diminishing it from its edges? And Allah judges; none can reverse the judgment, and Allah is swift in the reckoning.
13:42 Those before them planned, but to Allah belongs all the planning. Allah knows what every nafs earns, and the kafirin will know whose is the end of the home.
13:43 Those who disbelieve say: "You are not mursal." Say: "Allah is sufficient as a witness between me and you, and whoever has knowledge of the Kitab."
14:1 Alif Lam Ra. A Kitab We have sent down to you, so that you may bring the people out from darkness into light, by the leave of their Rabb, to the path of the Almighty, the Praised.
14:2 Allah — to whom belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth; and woe to the rejecters from a severe punishment.
14:3 Those who love the nearer life over the later one, and bar from the path of Allah and seek to make it crooked — those are in far error.
14:4 We sent no Rasul except in the tongue of his people, to make things clear to them; then Allah leads astray whomever Allah wills, and guides whomever Allah wills — and Allah is the Almighty, the Wise.
14:5 We sent Musa with Our ayat: "Bring your people out of the darkness into the light, and remind them of the days of Allah." Truly in that are ayat for every steadfast, grateful one.
14:6 And when Musa said to his people: "Remember Allah's favor upon you, when Allah saved you from the house of Fir'awn, who afflicted you with the worst of punishment, slaughtering your sons and sparing your nisa; and in that was a great test from your Rabb.
14:7 And when your Rabb proclaimed: "If you give thanks, I will surely increase you; and if you reject, truly My punishment is severe."
14:8 And Musa said: "If you reject — you and all who are in the earth together — truly Allah is Rich, Praised."
14:9 Has the news not come to you of those before you — the people of Nuh, and Ad, and Thamud, and those after them whom none knows but Allah? Their rusul came to them with clear proofs, but they thrust their hands into their mouths and said: "We reject what you have been sent with, and we are in restless doubt about what you call us to."
14:10 Their rusul said: "Can there be doubt about Allah, the Originator of the heavens and the earth, who calls you to forgive you part of your offenses and to defer you to a named term?" They said: "You are only humans like us; you want to bar us from what our fathers used to serve. So bring us a clear warrant."
14:11 Their rusul said to them: "We are only humans like you, but Allah favors whomever Allah wills among Allah's servants; and it was not for us to bring you a warrant except by Allah's leave. And upon Allah let the muminin rely."
14:12 "And what reason do we have not to rely upon Allah, when Allah has guided us our paths? And we shall surely endure sabirly whatever harm you do us; and upon Allah let the reliant rely."
14:13 And those who rejected said to their rusul: "We will surely drive you out of our land, unless you return to our way." Then their Rabb revealed to them: "We will surely destroy the wrongdoers."
14:14 "And We will surely settle you in the land after them. That is for whoever fears My standing and fears My threat."
14:15 And they sought triumph, and every stubborn tyrant failed.
14:16 Behind them is hell, and they are given drink of foul water.
14:17 He gulps it and can barely swallow it; and death comes to him from every side, yet he cannot die; and behind him is a hard punishment.
14:18 The likeness of those who reject their Rabb: their works are like ashes that a storm-wind drives on a stormy day — they have no power over anything of what they earned. That is the far error.
14:19 Have you not seen that Allah created the heavens and the earth in truth? If Allah wills, Allah can do away with you and bring a new creation.
14:20 And that is not hard for Allah.
14:21 And they come forth to Allah, all of them; then the weak say to those who had grown arrogant: "We were your followers — so can you avail us anything of Allah's punishment?" They say: "If Allah had guided us, we would have guided you. It is all the same to us whether we fret or endure; there is no escape for us."
14:22 And the Shaytan says, when the matter is decided: "Allah promised you a true promise, and I promised you — but I failed you. I had no warrant over you except that I called you and you answered me. So do not blame me, but blame yourselves. I cannot rescue you, nor can you rescue me. I reject what you associated with me before. Truly the wrongdoers have a painful punishment."
14:23 And Allah admits those who believe and do the right works into gardens beneath which rivers flow, to abide therein forever, by the leave of their Rabb. Their greeting there is "Peace."
14:24 Have you not seen how Allah strikes a likeness? A good word is like a good tree: its root is fixed and its branch is in the heaven.
14:25 It gives its fruit every season by the leave of its Rabb; and Allah strikes likenesses for people, so that they may remember.
14:26 And the likeness of a vile word is like a vile tree, torn up from the surface of the earth — it has no firm place.
14:27 Allah makes the muminin stand firm with the firm word, in this life and in the next; and Allah leads the wrongdoers astray; and Allah does whatever Allah wills.
14:28 Have you not seen those who traded Allah's favor for rejection, and settled their people in the house of ruin?
14:29 They enter hell's fire — and what an evil resting-place!
14:30 And they set up equals to Allah, to lead astray from Allah's path. Say: "Enjoy — then your arrival is to the fire."
14:31 Say to My servants who believe: "Establish the prayer, and spend of what We have provided you, secretly and openly, before a day comes in which there is no dealing and no friendship."
14:32 Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth, and sent down from the heaven water, and brought out with it fruits as provision for you; and subjected for you the ships to sail the sea by Allah's command; and subjected for you the rivers.
14:33 And subjected for you the sun and the moon, unceasing; and subjected for you the night and the day.
14:34 And Allah gave you of all that you asked; and if you count Allah's favor, you cannot number it. Truly the human is ever a wrongdoer, a rejecter.
14:35 And when Ibrahim said: "My Rabb, make this town safe, and keep me and my sons from serving the asnam."
14:36 "My Rabb, they have led many people astray; so whoever follows me is of me, and whoever disobeys me — then You are truly Forgiving, Raheem."
14:37 "Our Rabb, I have settled some of my offspring in a valley without crops, near Your sacred house — our Rabb — so that they may establish the prayer. So make hearts of people incline to them, and provide them of the fruits, so that they may give thanks."
14:38 "Our Rabb, You know what we conceal and what we make open; and nothing is hidden from Allah of anything in the earth or in the heaven."
14:39 "Praise be to Allah, who granted me Ismail and Ishaq in old age. Truly my Rabb hears the call."
14:40 "My Rabb, make me an establisher of the prayer, and my offspring too. Our Rabb, accept my call."
14:41 "Our Rabb, forgive me and my parents, and the muminin, on the day the reckoning stands."
14:42 And never think Allah unaware of what the wrongdoers do. Allah only defers them to a day when the eyes stare.
14:43 Hurrying with heads raised, their glance not turning back to them, and their hearts void.
14:44 And warn the people of the day the punishment comes to them; those who did wrong will say: "Our Rabb, defer us to a near term — we will answer Your call and follow the Rasuls." "Did you not swear before that for you there was no passing away?"
14:45 And you dwelt in the dwellings of those who wronged themselves, and it became clear to you how We dealt with them, and We struck likenesses for you.
14:46 And they schemed their scheme, and with Allah is their scheme — even if their scheme were such as to move the mountains.
14:47 So never think that Allah fails the promise to the Rasuls. Truly Allah is the Almighty, the Owner of requital.
14:48 The day the earth is changed for other than the earth, and the heavens; and they come forth to Allah, the One, the Overpowering.
14:49 And you will see the guilty that day, bound together in chains.
14:50 Their garments are of molten seal, and the fire covers their faces.
14:51 So that Allah may repay every nafs what it earned. Truly Allah is swift in reckoning.
14:52 This is a message for the people, so that they may be warned by it, and know that there is only one ilah, and so that those with understanding may remember.
15:1 Alif Lam Ra. These are the ayat of the Kitab and a clear recitation.
15:2 Perhaps those who disbelieve would wish that they had been at peace.
15:3 Leave them to eat and to enjoy, and let hope divert them — soon they will know.
15:4 We never destroyed any town without its having a known decree.
15:5 No community outstrips its term, and they cannot delay it.
15:6 They say: "O you upon whom the Reminder was sent down — you are truly mad."
15:7 "Why do you not bring us the malakat, if you are among the sadiqin?"
15:8 We do not send the malakat down except in truth, and then they are not given respite.
15:9 It is We, even We, who sent down the Reminder, and We are its guardians.
15:10 We sent before you among the parties of the ancients.
15:11 No Rasul came to them but they mocked him.
15:12 Thus We thread it through the hearts of the guilty.
15:13 They do not believe in it, though the way of the ancients has passed before.
15:14 And even if We opened for them a door from the heaven and they kept ascending through it,
15:15 they would say: "Only our eyes have fallen into stupor; no, we are a bewitched people."
15:16 We placed towers in the heaven and adorned it for those who look.
15:17 And We guarded it from every stoned devil,
15:18 except whoever steals a hearing — then a clear fire-missile follows him.
15:19 And the earth — We spread it out, and cast anchors in it, and made grow in it every weighed thing.
15:20 And We made in it livelihoods for you, and for those whom you do not provide.
15:21 There is nothing but its stores are with Us, and We do not send it down except in a known measure.
15:22 And We sent the winds fertilizing, and We sent down from the heaven water, and gave you to drink of it — and you are not its store-keepers.
15:23 It is We who give life and bring death, and We are the final heirs.
15:24 We know those among you who press forward, and We know those who lag behind.
15:25 Your Rabb will gather them — your Rabb is wise and knowing.
15:26 We created the human from clay of molded mud.
15:27 And the jinn — We created them earlier from scorching fire.
15:28 And when your Rabb said to the malakat: "I am creating a human from clay of molded mud,
15:29 and when I have proportioned him and blown into him of My spirit, fall down before him, prostrating."
15:30 So the malakat prostrated, all of them together,
15:31 except Iblis — he refused to be among those prostrating.
15:32 The Rabb said: "Iblis, what is it with you that you are not among those prostrating?"
15:33 He said: "I would not prostrate to a human whom You created from clay of molded mud."
15:34 The Rabb said: "Then get out from it — you are stoned.
15:35 The curse is upon you until the Day of Judgment."
15:36 He said: "Rabb, grant me respite until the day they are raised."
15:37 The Rabb said: "You are among the respited,
15:38 until the day of the appointed time, the known one."
15:39 He said: "Rabb, because You misled me, I will adorn for them on the earth and mislead them all,
15:40 except Your abd, the mukhlisin among them."
15:41 The Rabb said: "This is a path to Me, made straight.
15:42 Over My servants you have no hold, except those of the misled who follow you.
15:43 Hell is the promised meeting for them all.
15:44 It has 7 doors; for each door an allotted portion is assigned.
15:45 the muttaqin are in gardens and springs.
15:46 "Enter them in peace, secure."
15:47 We pull out whatever rancor is in their breasts — as brothers, on couches facing one another.
15:48 No toil touches them in it, and they are never made to leave it.
15:49 Tell My servants that I am the Forgiving, the Raheem,
15:50 and that My punishment is the painful punishment.
15:51 And tell them of the guests of Ibrahim,
15:52 when they entered upon him and said "Peace," and he said: "We are in awe of you."
15:53 They said: "Do not be in awe — we bring you tidings of a knowing boy."
15:54 He said: "You bring me tidings though old age has touched me — what is it you bring?"
15:55 They said: "We bring you tidings in truth — do not be among those who despair."
15:56 He said: "Who despairs of the mercy of the Rabb except those who stray?"
15:57 He said: "What is your affair, O mursalun?"
15:58 They said: "We are sent to a guilty people,
15:59 except the family of Lut — We will surely save them all,
15:60 except his imra'a — We have decreed it: she is surely among the ghābirīn."
15:61 So when Our mursalin came to the family of Lut,
15:62 he said: "You are a disavowed people."
15:63 They said: "No — we bring you that of which they used to doubt,
15:64 and we bring you the truth, and we are sadiq.
15:65 Set out by night with your family in a portion of the night, and go behind them, and let none of you turn around — and press on wherever you are commanded."
15:66 We decreed that matter to him: the end of these will be cut off by morning.
15:67 The people of the town came, rejoicing.
15:68 He said: "These are my guests — do not disgrace me.
15:69 Be mindful of Allah, and do not shame me."
15:70 They said: "Did we not forbid you from all people?
15:71 He said: "These are my daughters, if you must act."
15:72 By your life — they were in their stupor, wandering blind.
15:73 The shout seized them at sunrise.
15:74 We made its heights into its lowest parts, and rained upon them marked stones.
15:75 In that are ayat for those who take heed.
15:76 It lies on an abiding road.
15:77 In that is an aya for the muminin.
15:78 The companions of the Thicket were surely wrongdoers,
15:79 so We requited them — and the two of them lie on a clear waymark.
15:80 The companions of the Stone denied the mursalin,
15:81 and We gave them Our ayat, but they turned away from them,
15:82 for they carved houses from the rocks, feeling secure.
15:83 The shout seized them in the morning.
15:84 What they used to earn availed them nothing.
15:85 We created the heavens and the earth and what is between them only in truth, and the Hour is surely coming — so offer the beautiful pardon.
15:86 Your Rabb is the Creator, the Knowing.
15:87 We gave you 7 of the oft-repeated, and the mighty recitation.
15:88 Do not strain your eyes toward what We let some of them enjoy in pairs, and do not grieve over them — and lower your wing to the muminin.
15:89 And say: "I am the clear warner,
15:90 just as We sent down upon those who divide —
15:91 those who made the recitation into pieces —
15:92 so by your Rabb, We will surely ask them all
15:93 about what they used to do.
15:94 Proclaim openly what you are commanded, and turn away from those who join others with Allah —
15:95 We suffice you against the mockers,
15:96 those who make with Allah another ilah — soon they will know.
15:97 We know that your chest straitens because of what they say.
15:98 So glorify your Rabb with praise, and be among those who prostrate,
15:99 and serve your Rabb until the certainty comes to you.
16:1 The command of Allah has come, so do not hasten it. Glorified is Allah, high above what they associate.
16:2 Allah sends down the malakat with the spirit of the command from Allah upon whom Allah wills of the servants, saying: "Warn that there is no deity but Me, so be mindful of Me."
16:3 Allah created the heavens and the earth in truth. Exalted is Allah above what they associate.
16:4 Allah created the human from a drop, and then that one is an open contender.
16:5 And the cattle — Allah created them for you. In them is warmth and benefit, and from them you eat.
16:6 And in them is beauty for you when you bring them to rest and when you send them out to graze.
16:7 And they carry your loads to a town you could not reach except with hardship to yourselves. Indeed your Rabb is gentle in mercy, merciful.
16:8 And horses, and mules, and donkeys for you to ride and as adornment; and Allah creates what you do not know.
16:9 And upon Allah rests the mid-course of the path, though among them are deviating side-takers; and if Allah willed, Allah would have guided you all together.
16:10 It is Allah who sends down water from the heaven for you; from it is a drink, and from it grow trees in which you pasture your herds.
16:11 Allah grows for you with it the crops, and the olives, and the palms, and the grapes, and every kind of fruit. Indeed in that is an aya for people who reflect.
16:12 And Allah subjected for you the night and the day, and the sun and the moon and the stars, subjected by Allah's command. Indeed in that are ayat for people who understand.
16:13 And what Allah has spread forth on the earth for you, with its colors differing — indeed in that is an aya for people who remember.
16:14 And it is Allah who subjected the sea, so that you may eat from it fresh flesh, and extract from it adornment that you wear, and you see the ships sailing through it — so that you may seek of Allah's bounty, and so that you may give thanks.
16:15 And Allah cast into the earth firm anchors so that it would not sway with you, and rivers and paths, so that you may be guided.
16:16 And landmarks; and by the stars they are guided.
16:17 Is one who creates like one who does not create? Will you not then remember?
16:18 And if you count the blessing of Allah, you cannot count it. Indeed Allah is ever forgiving, merciful.
16:19 And Allah knows what you hide and what you proclaim.
16:20 And those whom they call upon besides Allah create nothing, while they themselves are created.
16:21 They are dead, not alive, and they do not perceive when they will be raised.
16:22 Your deity is one deity. As for those who do not believe in the hereafter, their hearts are in rejection, and they are arrogant.
16:23 Without doubt Allah knows what they hide and what they proclaim. Indeed Allah does not love the mustakbirim.
16:24 And when it is said to them, "What has your Rabb sent down?" they say, "Tales of the ancients."
16:25 So that they may carry their loads complete on the Day of Rising, along with the loads of those whom they mislead without knowledge. Alas, how evil is what they bear!
16:26 Those before them planned, but Allah came at their building from the foundations, and the roof collapsed upon them from above, and the punishment came to them from where they did not perceive.
16:27 Then on the Day of Rising Allah will disgrace them and say, "Where are My associates for whom you used to contend?" Those who were given knowledge will say, "Indeed the disgrace and the evil today are upon the kafirin."
16:28 Those whom the malakat take in death while they wrong themselves; then they cast the submission: "We were not doing any evil." "Yes indeed! Allah knows what you used to do."
16:29 "Enter the gates of hell, to abide therein." How evil is the lodging of the mutakabbirin!
16:30 And it will be said to those who were mindful, "What has your Rabb sent down?" They will say, "Good." For those who did good in this nearer life there is goodness, and the home of the hereafter is better; and excellent is the home of the muttaqin.
16:31 Gardens of Eden they will enter, with rivers flowing beneath them; for them therein is whatever they will. Thus does Allah repay the muttaqin.
16:32 Those whom the malakat take in death in a good state, saying, "Peace be upon you; enter the garden for what you used to do."
16:33 Do they await anything but that the malakat come to them, or the command of your Rabb comes? Thus did those before them act. And Allah did not wrong them, but they used to wrong themselves.
16:34 So the evils of what they did struck them, and there encircled them what they used to mock.
16:35 And those who associated said, "If Allah had willed, we would not have served anything besides Allah — neither we nor our fathers — nor would we have forbidden anything besides Allah." Thus did those before them act. So is there upon the Rasuls anything but the clear delivery?
16:36 And We have surely sent into every community a Rasul: "Serve Allah and avoid the false arbiter." Among them are those whom Allah guided, and among them are those upon whom straying became binding. So journey through the land and look at how was the end of those who denied.
16:37 If you ardently want their guidance, Allah does not guide the one let astray, and they have no helpers.
16:38 And they swore by Allah with the utmost of their oaths: "Allah will not raise the one who dies." Yes — a promise binding upon Allah. But most people do not know.
16:39 So that Allah may make clear to them that about which they differ, and so that those who disbelieved may know that they were liars.
16:40 Our word for a thing when We will it is only that We say to it, "Be," and it is.
16:41 And those who emigrated for Allah after they were wronged — We will surely settle them in this nearer life in a good place, and the reward of the hereafter is greater — if only they knew.
16:42 Those who were sabir and upon their Rabb rely.
16:43 And We sent before you only men to whom We revealed. So ask the people of the reminder, if you do not know.
16:44 With clear proofs and the Kitab. And We sent down to you the reminder, so that you may make clear to people what was sent down to them, and so that they may reflect.
16:45 Do those who planned the evil deeds feel secure that Allah will not sink them into the earth, or that the punishment will not come to them from where they do not perceive?
16:46 Or that Allah will not seize them in their going back and forth? And then they cannot escape.
16:47 Or that Allah will not seize them with a gradual dread? Yet your Rabb is gentle in mercy, merciful.
16:48 Have they not looked at what Allah created — how its shadows turn from the right and from the left, prostrating to Allah in humility?
16:49 And to Allah prostrates whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth of every creature, and the malakat, and they do not act arrogantly.
16:50 They fear their Rabb above them and do what they are commanded.
16:51 And Allah said, "Do not take two deities. Indeed Allah is one deity, so of Me alone be in awe."
16:52 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth, and to Allah is due the constant duty. So is it other than Allah that you fear?
16:53 And whatever blessing is upon you is from Allah; then when harm touches you, to Allah you cry out.
16:54 Then when the harm is uncovered from you, at once a group of you associate others with their Rabb.
16:55 So as to disbelieve what We have given them. Enjoy yourselves; soon you will know.
16:56 And they allot a share of what We provided them to what they do not know. By Allah, you will surely be asked about what you fabricate.
16:57 And they assign daughters to Allah — glorified is Allah — while they have whatever they desire.
16:58 And when one of them is given tidings of a female, his face darkens and he is holding it in.
16:59 He hides from his people because of the evil of what he was given tidings of: shall he keep it in humiliation, or bury it in the dust? Alas, how evil is what they decide!
16:60 For those who do not believe in the hereafter is the likening of evil, and to Allah belongs the highest likening; and Allah is the mighty in power, the wise.
16:61 And if Allah were to take people to task for their wrongdoing, Allah would not leave on it a single moving creature; but Allah defers them to an appointed term. And when their term comes, they cannot delay it by an hour nor advance it.
16:62 And they assign to Allah what they dislike, while their tongues describe the lie that the good is theirs. Without doubt, theirs is the fire, and they are ones who fall short.
16:63 By Allah, We sent to communities before you, but Shaytan adorned their deeds for them; so today he is their protector, and theirs is a painful punishment.
16:64 And We sent down upon you the Kitab only so that you may make clear to them that about which they differ, and as guidance and mercy for people who believe.
16:65 And Allah sends down water from the heaven and revives with it the earth after its death. Indeed in that is an aya for people who hear.
16:66 And indeed in the cattle there is a lesson for you: We give you to drink from what is in their bellies, from between the dregs and the blood — pure milk, palatable to the drinkers.
16:67 And from the fruits of the palms and the grapes you take from it a stupor-drink and a good provision. Indeed in that is an aya for people who understand.
16:68 And your Rabb revealed to the bee: "Take for yourself dwellings in the mountains, and in the trees, and in what they build."
16:69 Then eat of every fruit and thread through the paths of your Rabb, made submissive. From their bellies comes out a drink of varied colors, in which there is healing for people. Indeed in that is an aya for people who reflect.
16:70 And Allah created you, then takes you in death; and among you is one turned back to the most abject of age, so that one knows nothing after having known. Indeed Allah is knowing, powerful.
16:71 And Allah has favored some of you over others in provision; yet those who were favored do not hand back their provision to those whom their right hands possess so that they would be equal in it. So do they deny the blessing of Allah?
16:72 And Allah made for you from yourselves zawj, and made for you from your zawj children and grandchildren, and provided you from the good things. So do they believe in falsehood, and do they disbelieve the blessing of Allah?
16:73 And they serve besides Allah that which has no power over any provision for them from the heavens and the earth, nor are they able.
16:74 So do not strike likenesses for Allah. Indeed Allah knows, and you do not know.
16:75 Allah strikes a parable: a servant owned, with power over nothing, and one whom We provided from Us a good provision — and that one spends from it secretly and openly. Are they equal? Praise be to Allah. Rather, most of them do not know.
16:76 And Allah strikes a parable of two men: one of them is dumb, with power over nothing, and is a burden upon his master — wherever he directs him he brings no good. Is he equal to the one who commands justice and is on a straight path?
16:77 And to Allah belongs the hidden realm of the heavens and the earth, and the command of the hour is but like the blink of an eye, or it is nearer. Indeed Allah has power over everything.
16:78 And Allah brought you out from the bellies of your mothers knowing nothing, and made for you hearing, and sights, and fu'ad, so that you may give thanks.
16:79 Do they not see the birds, subjected in the air of the heaven? Nothing holds them up except Allah. Indeed in that are ayat for people who believe.
16:80 And Allah made for you from your houses a dwelling, and made for you from the hides of the cattle houses that you find light on the day of your moving and the day of your staying; and from their wool, and their hair, and their fur — furnishings and enjoyment for a time.
16:81 And Allah made for you, from what Allah created, shade, and made for you from the mountains shelters, and made for you garments that shield you from the heat, and garments that shield you from your dread. Thus does Allah complete the blessing upon you, so that you may submit.
16:82 So if they turn away, upon you is only the clear delivery.
16:83 They recognize the blessing of Allah, then they disbelieve it; and most of them are the kafirin.
16:84 And on the day We raise from every community a witness, then permission will not be granted to those who disbelieved, nor will they be allowed to make amends.
16:85 And when those who wronged see the punishment, it will not be lightened for them, nor will they be granted respite.
16:86 And when those who associated see their associates, they will say, "Our Rabb, these are our associates whom we used to call upon besides You." But the word will be cast to them: "You are indeed liars."
16:87 And they will cast the submission to Allah that day, and lost from them will be what they used to fabricate.
16:88 Those who disbelieved and turned away from the path of Allah — We will increase them punishment upon punishment for what they used to corrupt.
16:89 And on the day We raise in every community a witness against them from among themselves, and We bring you as a witness against these. And We sent down upon you the Kitab, a clarification of everything, and guidance, and mercy, and glad tidings for those who submit.
16:90 Indeed Allah commands justice, and excellence, and giving to the near of kin, and forbids obscenity, and the rejected, and transgression. Allah admonishes you, so that you may remember.
16:91 And fulfill the pledge of Allah when you pledge, and do not unbind the oaths after confirming them, seeing that you have made Allah your surety. Indeed Allah knows what you do.
16:92 And do not be like the imra'a who unravels her yarn, after it was strong, into loose strands, taking your oaths as deception among you because one community is more numerous than another. Allah only tests you thereby, and Allah will surely make clear to you on the Day of Rising what you used to differ about.
16:93 And if Allah willed, Allah would have made you one community; but Allah lets stray whom Allah wills and guides whom Allah wills. And you will surely be asked about what you used to do.
16:94 And do not take your oaths as deception among you, lest a foot slip after being firm, and you taste the evil for having turned away from the path of Allah; and for you is a tremendous punishment.
16:95 And do not exchange the pledge of Allah for a small price. Indeed what is with Allah is better for you, if you knew.
16:96 What is with you runs out, and what is with Allah remains. And We will surely repay those who were sabir their reward with the best of what they used to do.
16:97 Whoever does a salih deed, whether male or female, while believing — We will surely give that one a goodly life, and We will surely repay them their reward with the best of what they used to do.
16:98 So when you recite the recitation, seek refuge with Allah from Shaytan, the stoned one.
16:99 Indeed Shaytan has no warrant over those who believe and rely upon their Rabb.
16:100 Shaytan's warrant is only over those who take Shaytan as protector and those who associate others with him.
16:101 And when We substitute an aya in place of an aya — and Allah knows best what Allah sends down — they say, "You are only a fabricator." Rather, most of them do not know.
16:102 Say, "The holy spirit sent it down from your Rabb in truth, to steady those who believe, and as guidance and glad tidings for those who submit."
16:103 And We surely know that they say, "It is only a human who teaches him." The tongue of the one to whom they incline is foreign, while this is a clear Arabic tongue.
16:104 Indeed those who do not believe in the ayat of Allah — Allah does not guide them, and theirs is a painful punishment.
16:105 Only those who do not believe in the ayat of Allah fabricate the lie, and those are the kazibin.
16:106 Whoever disbelieves in Allah after their faith — except one who is compelled while their heart is settled in faith — but whoever opens their chest to disbelief, upon those is wrath from Allah, and theirs is a tremendous punishment.
16:107 That is because they preferred the nearer life over the hereafter, and because Allah does not guide the committing kufr people.
16:108 Those are the ones over whose hearts, and hearing, and sight Allah has set a seal; and those are the unmindful.
16:109 Without doubt, in the hereafter they are the khasirin.
16:110 Then indeed your Rabb — for those who emigrated after they were tried, then strove and were sabir — indeed your Rabb after that is forgiving, merciful.
16:111 A day when every nafs comes disputing for itself, and every nafs is paid in full for what it did, and they are not wronged.
16:112 And Allah strikes a parable of a town that was secure and settled, its provision coming to it in plenty from every place; then it was ungrateful for the blessings of Allah, so Allah made it taste the garment of hunger and of fear for what they used to do.
16:113 And a Rasul from among them surely came to them, but they denied him, so the punishment seized them while they were wrongdoers.
16:114 So eat of what Allah has provided you, lawful and good, and give thanks for the blessing of Allah, if it is Allah alone that you serve.
16:115 Forbidden to you are only the carrion, and the blood, and the flesh of the pig, and what has been dedicated to other than Allah. But whoever is compelled, neither desiring nor transgressing — indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
16:116 And do not say, concerning what your tongues describe falsely, "This is lawful and this is forbidden," so as to fabricate a lie against Allah. Indeed those who fabricate a lie against Allah will not attain success.
16:117 A brief enjoyment, and theirs is a painful punishment.
16:118 And as for those who were Yahud — We forbade them what We narrated to you before; and We did not wrong them, but they used to wrong themselves.
16:119 Then indeed your Rabb — for those who did evil in ignorance, then repented after that and made amends — indeed your Rabb after that is forgiving, merciful.
16:120 Indeed Ibrahim was a community in himself, devoted to Allah, upright, and not among those who associate.
16:121 Thankful for the blessings; Allah chose Ibrahim and guided Ibrahim to a straight path.
16:122 And We gave Ibrahim goodness in this nearer life, and indeed in the hereafter Ibrahim is among the salihin.
16:123 Then We revealed to you: "Follow the way of Ibrahim, upright, and not among those who associate."
16:124 The sabbath was imposed only upon those who differed about it; and indeed your Rabb will judge between them on the Day of Rising concerning what they used to differ about.
16:125 Call to the path of your Rabb with wisdom and good admonition, and dispute with them in the best way. Indeed your Rabb knows best who strays from Allah's path, and knows best the guided.
16:126 And if you punish, then punish with the like of what you were punished with. But if you are sabir, it is better for the sabirin.
16:127 And be of the sabirin — and your patience is only by Allah — and do not grieve over them, nor be in straitness over what they plan.
16:128 Indeed Allah is with those who are mindful and those who do good.
17:1 Glory be to the One who journeyed by night with Allah's servant, from the Sacred Mosque to the Farthest Mosque, whose surroundings We blessed, to show him some of Our ayat. Truly, Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Seeing.
17:2 And We gave Musa the Kitab and made it a guidance for the Children of Israel: take no guardian besides Me.
17:3 The offspring of those We carried with Nuh. Truly, that one was a thankful servant.
17:4 And We decreed to the Children of Israel in the Kitab: you will surely spread corruption on the earth twice, and you will surely rise to a great height.
17:5 So when the promise of the first of the two came, We raised against you servants of Ours, possessors of severe might, and they roamed amidst the homes; and it was a promise fulfilled.
17:6 Then We restored the turn to you over them, and We supplied you with wealth and children, and We made you a greater host.
17:7 If you do good, you do good only for your own self; and if you do evil, it is only against it. So when the promise of the last comes, they will smite your faces and enter the Mosque just as they entered it the first time, and they will utterly raze what they overran.
17:8 It may be that your Rabb will have mercy on you; but if you return, We will return. And We made Hell a confinement for the kafirin.
17:9 Truly, this Recitation guides to that which is most upright, and gives good news to the muminin who do the good deeds, that for them is a great reward.
17:10 And those who do not believe in the Last — We have prepared for them a painful punishment.
17:11 And the human calls to evil with the same calling as to good; and the human was ever hasty.
17:12 And We made the night and the day two ayat; then We blotted out the aya of the night and made the aya of the day sight-giving, so that you may seek bounty from your Rabb, and so that you may know the number of years and the reckoning; and everything We have detailed in detail.
17:13 And every human — We bound his omen to his neck; and on the Day of Rising We will bring out for him a Kitab he will find spread open.
17:14 Read your Kitab; your own self suffices you this day as a reckoner against you.
17:15 Whoever is guided is guided only for their own self, and whoever strays strays only against it; and no burden-bearer bears the burden of another; and We were never ones to punish until We raised a Rasul.
17:16 And when We want to destroy a town, We command its ones softened by ease, and they fasuqu in it; so the word comes true upon it, and We raze it utterly.
17:17 And how many generations We destroyed after Nuh! And your Rabb suffices as knower, seer of the offenses of Allah's servants.
17:18 Whoever wants the immediate, We hasten to them in it whatever We wish, for whomever We want; then We made Hell for them, to enter it, outcast and driven away.
17:19 And whoever wants the Last and strives for it with its striving, while believing — those, their striving is thanked.
17:20 To each We extend, these and those, from the gift of your Rabb; and the gift of your Rabb was never withheld.
17:21 See how We favored some of them over others; and the Last has greater degrees and greater favoring.
17:22 Set not up with Allah another ilah, lest you sit outcast and forsaken.
17:23 And your Rabb decreed that you worship none but Allah, and goodness to the two parents: if one or both of them reach old age with you, do not say to them "ugh", nor scold them, but speak to them a generous word.
17:24 And lower for them the wing of humility from mercy, and say: My Rabb, have mercy on them as they raised me when I was little.
17:25 Your Rabb knows best what is in your selves; if you are good, then to the returners Allah is ever forgiving.
17:26 And give the near kin their due, and the needy, and the wayfarer; and do not squander wastefully.
17:27 Truly, the squanderers were brothers of Shaytan; and Shaytan was ever thankless to his Rabb.
17:28 And if you turn away from them, seeking a mercy from your Rabb that you hope for, then speak to them an easy word.
17:29 And do not make your hand bound to your neck, nor stretch it out in all stretching, lest you sit blamed and regretful.
17:30 Truly, your Rabb spreads out provision for whomever Allah wills and measures it; truly, Allah is ever knowing, seeing of Allah's servants.
17:31 And do not kill your children for fear of want; We provide for them and for you. Truly, their killing was a great error.
17:32 And do not come near adultery; it was an obscenity and a worst way.
17:33 And do not kill the nafs that Allah has forbidden except by right; and whoever is killed wronged, We have given their kin a warrant — so let them not exceed in the killing, for they are ever helped.
17:34 And do not come near the wealth of the orphan except with what is better, until they reach their full strength; and fulfill the pledge, for the pledge is ever asked about.
17:35 And fulfill the measure when you measure, and weigh with the upright scale; that is better and best in outcome.
17:36 And do not follow what you have no knowledge of; the hearing, the sight, and the fu'ad — all of those are ever asked about.
17:37 And do not walk on the earth strutting; you will never split the earth, nor ever reach the mountains in height.
17:38 All of that is bad in the sight of your Rabb, detested.
17:39 That is of what your Rabb revealed to you of wisdom; and set not up with Allah another ilah, lest you be cast into Hell, blamed and driven away.
17:40 Has your Rabb then singled you out with sons, and taken females from the malakat? Truly, you say a tremendous thing.
17:41 And We have turned about in this Recitation so that they remember, and it only increases them in aversion.
17:42 Say: if there were alihah with Allah, as they say, they would seek a way to the Owner of the High Seat.
17:43 Glory be to Allah, exalted high above what they say, a great exalting.
17:44 The seven heavens and the earth and whoever is in them glorify Allah, and there is nothing that does not glorify with praise; but you do not understand their glorification. Truly, Allah is ever forbearing, forgiving.
17:45 And when you recite the Recitation, We place between you and those who do not believe in the Last a covering veil.
17:46 And We place covers over their hearts lest they understand it, and in their ears a heaviness; and when you mention your Rabb alone in the Recitation, they turn their backs in aversion.
17:47 We know best what they listen for when they listen to you, and when they whisper, when the wrongdoers say: you follow only a man bewitched.
17:48 See how they strike similitudes for you; so they stray, and cannot find a way.
17:49 And they say: when we are bones and fragments, shall we really be raised a new creation?
17:50 Say: be stones or hadid,
17:51 or a creation from what grows great in your chests. Then they will say: who will bring us back? Say: the One who originated you the first time. Then they will wag their heads at you and say: when is it? Say: perhaps it is near.
17:52 The day Allah calls you and you answer with praise, and you think you tarried only a little.
17:53 And say to My servants that they say what is best; Shaytan indeed sows discord among them. Truly, Shaytan was ever a clear enemy to the human.
17:54 Your Rabb knows you best: if Allah wills, Allah has mercy on you; and if Allah wills, Allah punishes you. And We did not send you over them as a guardian.
17:55 And your Rabb knows best who is in the heavens and the earth; and We have favored some of the nabis over others; and We gave Dawud a the Kitab.
17:56 Say: call on those you claim besides Allah; they possess no power to remove harm from you, nor to turn it away.
17:57 Those whom they call seek a way to their Rabb, whichever is nearest, and they hope for mercy and fear punishment. Truly, the punishment of your Rabb is ever to be feared.
17:58 There is no town but We will destroy it before the Day of Rising, or punish it with a severe punishment; that was ever inscribed in the Kitab.
17:59 Nothing withheld Us from sending the ayat except that the ancients denied them; and We gave Thamud the naqah, plain to see, and they wronged her. And We do not send the ayat except as a warning.
17:60 And when We said to you: your Rabb ahat the people; and We did not make the vision We showed you except a trial for the people, and the cursed tree in the Recitation; and We frighten them, but it only increases them in great transgression.
17:61 And when We said to the malakat: prostrate to Adam, and they prostrated except Iblis; he said: shall I prostrate to one created of clay?
17:62 He said: do You see this one whom You have honored above me? If You postpone me to the Day of Rising, I will gain mastery over their offspring, all but a few.
17:63 Allah said: go; whoever of them follows you — then Hell is your wage, a full wage.
17:64 And provoke with your voice whomever of them you can, and muster against them your horses and your men, and share with them in wealth and children, and promise them; and Shaytan promises them only deception.
17:65 Truly, you have no warrant over My servants; and your Rabb suffices as guardian.
17:66 It is your Rabb who drives the ships for you on the sea, so that you may seek of bounty. Truly, Allah is ever merciful to you.
17:67 And when harm touches you at sea, lost are those you call on except Allah; then when Allah delivers you to dry land, you turn away. And the human is ever thankless.
17:68 Do you feel secure that Allah will not sink you into a side of the dry land, or send upon you a storm of burning cast, and then you find no guardian for yourselves?
17:69 Or do you feel secure that Allah will not return you into it once more, and send upon you a shattering wind, and drown you for what you disbelieved — then you find no pursuer for you against Us?
17:70 And We have honored the children of Adam, and carried them on land and sea, and provided them from the good things, and favored them over many of those We created, with favoring.
17:71 The day We call every people with their leader; whoever is given their Kitab in their right hand — those read their Kutub, and are not wronged a thread.
17:72 Whoever is blind in this one is blind in the Last, and further astray in way.
17:73 And they nearly lured you away from what We revealed to you, so that you might forge against Us another, and then they would have taken you as a bosom friend.
17:74 And had We not made you stand firm, you would nearly have leaned to them a little.
17:75 Then We would have made you taste a double of life and a double of death; then you would have found no helper for you against Us.
17:76 And they nearly provoked you out of the land to drive you from it; and then they would not have tarried behind you except a little.
17:77 A way of those of Our rusul We sent before you; and you will find no change for Our way.
17:78 Keep up the prayer from the sun's decline to the darkness of the night, and the recitation of dawn; the dawn recitation is ever witnessed.
17:79 And from the night keep vigil with it, an extra for you; perhaps your Rabb will raise you to a praised station.
17:80 And say: My Rabb, admit me an entrance of truth, and bring me out an exit of truth, and make for me from Your presence a warranting helper.
17:81 And say: the truth has come, and falsehood has vanished; falsehood is ever vanishing.
17:82 And We send down of the Recitation what is a healing and a mercy for the muminin; and it only increases the wrongdoers in loss.
17:83 And when We bless the human, they turn away and withdraw to their side; and when evil touches them, they are ever despairing.
17:84 Say: each works according to their own manner; and your Rabb knows best who is best guided in way.
17:85 And they ask you about the spirit; say: the spirit is from the command of my Rabb, and you are given of knowledge only a little.
17:86 And if We willed, We would take away what We revealed to you; then you would find no guardian for you against Us over it.
17:87 But for a mercy from your Rabb; truly, the bounty upon you was ever great.
17:88 Say: if humans and jinn assembled to bring the like of this Recitation, they would not bring its like, even if some of them were backers for others.
17:89 And We have turned about for the people in this Recitation every similitude, but most people refused except as kafirin.
17:90 And they say: we will not believe you until you burst forth for us from the earth a spring.
17:91 Or until you have a garden of palms and grapes, and burst rivers through it, bursting.
17:92 Or the heaven falls upon us in fragments, as you claim; or you bring Allah and the malakat before us.
17:93 Or there is for you a house of ornament, or you ascend into the heaven — and we will not believe your ascent until you bring down upon us a Kitab we can read. Say: glory be to my Rabb — am I anything but a human, a Rasul?
17:94 And nothing kept the people from believing when the guidance came to them, except that they said: has Allah sent a human as a Rasul?
17:95 Say: if there were on the earth malakat walking secure, We would have sent down upon them from the heaven a malakat as a Rasul.
17:96 Say: Allah suffices as witness between me and you; Allah is ever knowing, seeing of Allah's servants.
17:97 Whoever Allah guides, that one is guided; and whoever Allah leaves astray, you will find no protectors for them besides Allah; and We will gather them on the Day of Rising on their faces, blind and dumb and deaf; their shelter is Hell; whenever it subsides We increase them a blaze.
17:98 That is their repayment, because they disbelieved Our ayat and said: when we are bones and fragments, shall we really be raised a new creation?
17:99 Do they not see that Allah, who created the heavens and the earth, is able to create the like of them, and made for them a term in which there is no doubt? But the wrongdoers refused except disbelief.
17:100 Say: if you possessed the treasuries of my Rabb's mercy, you would hold back for fear of spending; and the human is ever withholding.
17:101 And We gave Musa 9 clear ayat; ask the Children of Israel when he came to them, and Fir'awn said to him: I think, Musa, that you are bewitched.
17:102 He said: you know that none but the Rabb of the heavens and the earth sent down these as proofs to see; and I think, Fir'awn, that you are doomed.
17:103 So he wanted to provoke them out of the land, and We drowned him and all who were with him.
17:104 And We said after him to the Children of Israel: settle the land; then, when the promise of the Last comes, We will bring you gathered.
17:105 And with truth We sent it down, and with truth it came down; and We did not send you except as a bearer of good news and a warner.
17:106 And a Recitation We apportioned, for you to recite to the people over time; and We sent it down by sending down.
17:107 Say: believe in it or do not believe; those who were given knowledge before it, when it is recited to them, fall down on their chins, bowing.
17:108 And they say: glory be to our Rabb; the promise of our Rabb is ever to be fulfilled.
17:109 And they fall down on their chins, weeping, and it increases them in humility.
17:110 Say: call on Allah or call on the Raheem; whichever you call on, to Allah belong the most beautiful names. And do not be loud in your prayer, nor be hushed in it, but seek between that a way.
17:111 And say: praise belongs to Allah, who took no child, and has no partner in dominion, and has no protector out of abasement; and proclaim Allah's greatness with proclaiming.
18:1 Praise belongs to Allah, who sent down upon the servant of Allah the Kitab, and made no crookedness in it.
18:2 Upright, to warn of a severe might from the presence of Allah, and to bring glad news to the muminin who do the right deeds, that theirs is a goodly reward.
18:3 Abiding in it forever.
18:4 And to warn those who say: Allah has taken a child.
18:5 They have no knowledge of it, nor had their fathers. Enormous is the word that comes from their mouths — they say nothing but a lie.
18:6 Yet perhaps you will destroy yourself with grief over their traces, if they do not believe in this hadith.
18:7 We have made all that is on the earth an adornment for it, so that We may test which of them is best in deed.
18:8 And We shall surely turn all that is on it into a barren dust-plain.
18:9 Or do you think that the Companions of the Cave and of ar-Raqim were a marvel among Our ayat?
18:10 When the fitya sheltered in the Cave and said: Our Rabb, bring us mercy from Your presence, and dispose our affair unto a right course.
18:11 Then We sealed their ears in the Cave for a number of years.
18:12 Then We raised them, so that We might know which of the two parties computed best how long they had tarried.
18:13 We narrate to you their account in truth. They were fitya who believed in their Rabb, and We increased them in guidance.
18:14 And We bound their hearts when they stood and said: Our Rabb is the Rabb of the heavens and the earth; we will never call upon any ilah other than Allah — that would be an utter excess.
18:15 These, our people, have taken alihah other than Allah. Why do they not bring them a clear warrant? Who is more wrong than one who fabricates a lie against Allah?
18:16 So withdraw from them and from all they worship other than Allah, and shelter in the Cave; your Rabb will spread out mercy for you and will dispose for your affair a support.
18:17 And you would see the sun, when it rises, swerving away from their cave toward the right, and when it sets, cutting past them toward the left, while they were in a cleft of it. That is among the ayat of Allah. Whoever Allah guides is the guided one, and whoever Allah leaves astray, you will never find for them a guiding ally.
18:18 You would think them awake, though they were asleep, and We turned them over, to the right and to the left, and their hound stretched its forelegs across the waṣid. Had you looked upon them, you would have turned from them in flight, and would have been filled with terror of them.
18:19 And so We raised them, that they might ask one another. One of them said: How long have you tarried? They said: We tarried a day, or part of a day. They said: Your Rabb knows best how long you tarried. So send one of you with these coins of yours into the town, and let him look for the purest food there, and let him bring you provision from it. Let him be discreet, and let no one perceive you.
18:20 If they emerge upon you, they will stone you or force you back into their way — and then you will never attain success.
18:21 And so We made them known, that they might know that the promise of Allah is true and that the Hour — there is no doubt in it. When they were contending among themselves over their affair, they said: Build over them a building — their Rabb knows them best. Those who prevailed in the affair said: We will surely raise over them a place of prostration.
18:22 They will say: 3, their hound the 4th of them; and they will say: 5, their hound the 6th of them — guessing at the ghayb; and they will say: 7, and their hound the 8th. Say: My Rabb knows best their number; none knows them but a few. So do not dispute about them except an open dispute, and do not ask any of them about them.
18:23 And never say of anything: I will surely do that tomorrow,
18:24 without adding: if Allah wills. And remember your Rabb when you forget, and say: Perhaps my Rabb will guide me nearer than this to a right course.
18:25 And they tarried in their Cave 300 years, and added 9.
18:26 Say: Allah knows best how long they tarried. To Allah belong the hidden things of the heavens and the earth — how well Allah sees and hears! They have no ally besides Allah, and Allah associates no one in judgment.
18:27 And recite what has been revealed to you of the Kitab of your Rabb. None can change the words of Allah, and apart from Allah you will never find a refuge.
18:28 And keep yourself sabir with those who call upon their Rabb morning and evening, seeking the face of Allah; and do not turn your eyes from them, desiring the adornment of the worldly life. And do not obey the one whose heart We have made unmindful of Our remembrance, who follows their desire, and whose affair is mere excess.
18:29 And say: The truth is from your Rabb. So whoever wills, let them believe; and whoever wills, let them disbelieve. We have prepared for the wrongdoers a Fire that ahat them. If they cry for help, they will be given water like molten mire, scalding the faces. Wretched the drink, and evil the resting-place!
18:30 Indeed, those who believe and do the right deeds — We never lose the reward of the one who does the best of deeds.
18:31 Those — theirs are Gardens of Eden, with rivers flowing beneath them; adorned therein with bracelets of gold, clothed in green garments of silk and brocade, reclining upon couches therein. Excellent the reward, and fair the resting-place!
18:32 And strike for them a parable of two men: We made for one of them two gardens of grapes, hedged them with palm-trees, and set a crop between them.
18:33 Each of the two gardens yielded its produce and diminished nothing of it, and We burst forth a river within them.
18:34 And he had fruit, and he said to his companion, conversing with him: I am greater than you in wealth and mightier in following.
18:35 And he entered his garden, wronging himself, and said: I do not think this will ever perish,
18:36 and I do not think the Hour is rising. Even if I am returned to my Rabb, I shall surely find a return better than this.
18:37 His companion said to him, conversing with him: Do you disbelieve in the One who created you from dust, then from a drop, then shaped you into a man?
18:38 As for me, Allah is my Rabb, and I will not associate anyone with my Rabb.
18:39 If only, when you entered your garden, you had said: What Allah wills — there is no strength except with Allah. Though you see me less than you in wealth and children,
18:40 yet perhaps my Rabb will bring me better than your garden, and send upon it a reckoning from the heaven, so that it becomes a smooth, barren tract,
18:41 or its water sinks deep — then you will never be able to seek it out.
18:42 And his produce was uhit, and there he was, turning his hands over all he had spent on it, while it lay collapsed upon its trellises — saying: If only I had not associated anyone with my Rabb!
18:43 He had no band to help him apart from Allah, and he could not be helped.
18:44 There the true Protector is Allah. Allah is better in reward and better in outcome.
18:45 And strike for them the parable of the worldly life: like water that We send down from the heaven, with which the vegetation of the earth mixes — then it becomes dry stubble that the wind scatters. And Allah is powerful over all things.
18:46 Wealth and children are the adornment of the worldly life, but the enduring good deeds are better with your Rabb for reward, and better for hope.
18:47 On the day We set the mountains moving, and you see the earth laid bare, and We gather them — not one of them will We leave behind.
18:48 And they are presented before your Rabb in ranks: You have come to Us just as We created you the first time — yet you claimed We would never make you an appointment.
18:49 And the Kitab is laid down, and you see the guilty dismayed at what is in it, saying: Woe to us! What is this Kitab that leaves nothing, small or great, uncounted? And they find all they did present before them. Your Rabb wrongs no one.
18:50 And when We said to the malakat: Prostrate before Adam — and they prostrated, except Iblis; he was of the jinn and fasaqa from the command of his Rabb. Will you take him and his offspring as allies instead of Me, when they are your enemies? Wretched an exchange for the wrongdoers!
18:51 I did not call them to witness the creation of the heavens and the earth, nor the creation of themselves; nor do I take the misleaders as helpers.
18:52 On the day Allah says: Call those partners of Mine you claimed — they will call them, but they will not answer them; and We shall set between them a gulf.
18:53 And the guilty will see the Fire and think they are about to fall into it, and they will find no way to turn from it.
18:54 We have indeed set forth for the people, in this Recital, every parable — yet humankind is the most disputing of all things.
18:55 Nothing keeps the people from believing when guidance comes to them, and from seeking forgiveness from their Rabb, except that the way of the ancients comes upon them, or the punishment comes to them face to face.
18:56 We send the mursalin only as bearers of glad news and warners — but those who disbelieve dispute with falsehood to strike down the truth with it, and they take My ayat and the warnings given them as mockery.
18:57 Who is more wrong than one who is reminded of the ayat of their Rabb, then turns away from them and forgets what their hands have sent forward? We have set coverings upon their hearts, so that they cannot understand it, and heaviness in their ears. Even if you call them to guidance, they will never be guided — never.
18:58 Your Rabb is the Forgiving, the Possessor of Mercy. Were Allah to seize them for what they have earned, Allah would hasten the punishment for them — but they have an appointment from which they will find no refuge.
18:59 Those towns — We destroyed them when they did wrong, and We set an appointed time for their destruction.
18:60 And when Musa said to his young companion: I will not stop until I reach the meeting of the two seas, even if I journey on for an age.
18:61 Then, when they reached the meeting of the two, they forgot their huut, and it took its course into the sea, slipping away.
18:62 Then, when they had crossed over, he said to his young companion: Bring us our midday meal — we have certainly met toil on this journey of ours.
18:63 He said: Do you remember when we sheltered by the rock? I forgot the huut — and none made me forget it but Shaytan, that I should forget to mention it — and it made its way into the sea in a marvelous manner.
18:64 He said: That is what we were seeking. So the two turned back on their tracks, retracing.
18:65 Then they found one of Our servants, to whom We had given mercy from Our presence, and whom We had taught knowledge from Our presence.
18:66 Musa said to him: May I follow you, so that you teach me of what you have been taught of right course?
18:67 He said: You will never be able to be of the sabirin with me.
18:68 And how could you be of the sabirin over what you have not tuhit in knowledge?
18:69 He said: You will find me sabir, Allah willing, and I will not disobey any command of yours.
18:70 He said: Then if you follow me, do not ask me about anything until I myself speak to you of it.
18:71 So the two set out, until, when they boarded the ship, he breached it. He said: Have you breached it to drown its people? You have certainly done a grievous thing.
18:72 He said: Did I not say that you would never be able to be of the sabirin with me?
18:73 He said: Do not take me to task for my forgetting, and do not overwhelm my affair with hardship.
18:74 So the two set out, until, when they met a boy, he killed him. He said: Have you killed a pure nafs for no nafs? You have certainly done a dreadful thing.
18:75 He said: Did I not tell you that you would never be able to be of the sabirin with me?
18:76 He said: If I ask you about anything after this, then keep my company no longer — from me you have an excuse.
18:77 So the two set out, until, when they came to the people of a town, they asked its people for food, but they refused to host them. And they found in it a wall about to fall down, and he set it up. He said: If you wished, you could have taken a wage for it.
18:78 He said: This is the parting between me and you. I will tell you the interpretation of what you were not able to be of the sabirin over.
18:79 As for the ship, it belonged to poor people working the sea, and I intended to damage it — for behind them was a king who seized every ship by force.
18:80 And as for the boy, his parents were muminin, and we feared he would overwhelm them with transgression and disbelief;
18:81 so we intended that their Rabb should substitute for them one better than him in purity and nearer in mercy.
18:82 And as for the wall, it belonged to two orphan boys in the town, and beneath it was a treasure of theirs; their father had been salih. So your Rabb intended that they should reach their full strength and bring out their treasure — as mercy from your Rabb. I did not do it of my own command. That is the interpretation of what you could not be of the sabirin over.
18:83 And they ask you about Dhul-Qarnayn. Say: I will recite to you an account of him.
18:84 We established him in the land, and gave him the means to all things.
18:85 So he followed a course,
18:86 until, when he reached the setting-place of the sun, he found it setting into a muddy spring, and he found by it a people. We said: O Dhul-Qarnayn, either you punish, or you take among them a good course.
18:87 He said: As for the wrongdoer, we shall punish him; then he will be returned to his Rabb, who will punish him with a dreadful punishment.
18:88 But as for the one who believes and does right — theirs is the fairest reward, and We shall speak to them of Our command with ease.
18:89 Then he followed a course,
18:90 until, when he reached the rising-place of the sun, he found it rising upon a people for whom We had made no shelter from it.
18:91 So it was; and We ahatna in knowledge all that was with him.
18:92 Then he followed a course,
18:93 until, when he reached the stretch between the two barriers, he found beyond them a people who could scarcely understand a word.
18:94 They said: O Dhul-Qarnayn, Ya'juj and Ma'juj are spoilers in the land. Shall we pay you a tribute, on condition that you set a barrier between us and them?
18:95 He said: What my Rabb has established me in is better. So help me with strength, and I will set between you and them a rampart.
18:96 Bring me blocks of hadid. Then, when he had leveled between the two sides, he said: Blow — until, when he had made it a blaze, he said: Bring me molten metal to pour over it.
18:97 So they could neither scale it nor make a breach in it.
18:98 He said: This is a mercy from my Rabb. But when the promise of my Rabb comes, it will be leveled flat — and the promise of my Rabb is ever true.
18:99 And that day We will leave them surging one into another, and the trumpet will be blown, and We will gather them as one gathering.
18:100 And We will present Hell on that day to the kafirin in full view,
18:101 those whose eyes were under a covering from My remembrance, and who could not hear.
18:102 Do the kafirin think that they can take My servants as allies instead of Me? We have prepared Hell for the kafirin as a welcome.
18:103 Say: Shall We tell you of the greatest losers in deeds?
18:104 Those whose striving goes astray in the worldly life, while they think they are doing good work.
18:105 Those are the ones who disbelieve in the ayat of their Rabb and in the meeting with Him — so their deeds come to nothing, and on the Day of Standing We shall set no weight for them.
18:106 That is their recompense — Hell — for that they disbelieved and took My ayat and My rusul as mockery.
18:107 Indeed, those who believe and do the right deeds — theirs are the Gardens of Paradise, as a welcome,
18:108 abiding therein forever, never desiring any change from it.
18:109 Say: If the sea were ink for the words of my Rabb, the sea would run dry before the words of my Rabb ran dry, even if We brought its like as more ink.
18:110 Say: I am only a human like you; it is revealed to me that your ilah is one ilah. So whoever hopes for the meeting of their Rabb, let them do a salih deed, and let them not associate anyone in the worship of their Rabb.
19:1 Kaf Ha Ya 'Ayn Sad
19:2 A mention of the mercy of your Rabb to Allah's servant Zakariya,
19:3 when he secretly called his Rabb,
19:4 saying: My Rabb, my bones have weakened within me and my head has blazed white with age, and never, my Rabb, in calling You have I been wretched.
19:5 And I fear for my kin behind me, and my imra'a is barren; so grant me from Your presence a protector
19:6 who will inherit me and inherit from the family of Yaqub; and make him, my Rabb, a pleasing one.
19:7 It was said: O Zakariya, We give you glad tidings of a boy whose name is Yahya; We have not made for him before a namesake.
19:8 He said: My Rabb, how can there be for me a boy when my imra'a is barren and I have reached extreme old age?
19:9 He said: So it shall be. Your Rabb has said: It is easy for Me, and I created you before when you were nothing.
19:10 He said: My Rabb, make for me an aya. He said: Your aya is that you shall not speak to the people for three nights running.
19:11 Then he came out to his people from the niche and inspired to them that they should glorify morning and evening.
19:12 O Yahya, take the Kitab resolutely; and We gave him judgment while a child,
19:13 and tenderness from Our presence, and purity; and he was of the muttaqin,
19:14 and dutiful to his parents, and not overpowering, disobedient.
19:15 And peace upon him the day he was born, the day he dies, and the day he is raised alive.
19:16 And mention in the Kitab Maryam, when she withdrew from her people to a place to the east.
19:17 And she took a veil, staying apart from them; then We sent Our ruh to her, and it appeared to her as a well-formed human.
19:18 She said: I seek refuge with the Rahman from you, if you are of the muttaqin.
19:19 He said: I am only a Rasul of your Rabb, to grant you a pure boy.
19:20 She said: How can there be for me a boy when no human has touched me and I have not been immoral?
19:21 He said: So it shall be. Your Rabb has said: It is easy for Me, and that We may make him an aya for the people and a mercy from Us; and the matter was decreed.
19:22 So she carried him and withdrew with him to a remote place.
19:23 And the labor pangs drove her to the trunk of the palm. She said: Would that I had died before this and become a forgotten thing, forgotten.
19:24 Then one called her from beneath her: Do not grieve; your Rabb has made beneath you a flowing stream.
19:25 And shake the trunk of the palm toward you; it will drop upon you fresh dates, ripe.
19:26 So eat and drink and comfort your eye; and if you see any human, say: I have vowed to the Raheem an abstention, so I shall not speak today to any human.
19:27 She brought him to her people, carrying him. They said: O Maryam, you have surely brought a shocking thing.
19:28 O sister of Harun, your father was not a man of evil, nor was your mother immoral.
19:29 So she gestured to him. They said: How can we speak to one who is in the cradle, a child?
19:30 He said: I am the servant of Allah; Allah gave me the Kitab and made me a Nabi,
19:31 and made me blessed wherever I may be, and enjoined on me prayer and purity as long as I remain alive,
19:32 and dutiful to my mother, and did not make me a tyrant, wretched.
19:33 And peace upon me the day I was born, the day I die, and the day I am raised alive.
19:34 That is Isa, son of Maryam — a word of truth in which they doubt.
19:35 It is not for Allah to take a child; glory be to Allah. When Allah decrees a matter, Allah only says to it: Be, and it is.
19:36 And indeed Allah is my Rabb and your Rabb, so worship Allah; this is a straight path.
19:37 So the parties differed among themselves. Woe to those who disbelieve from the witnessing of a great day.
19:38 How keenly they will hear and see the day they come to Us; but the wrongdoers are today in clear error.
19:39 And warn them of the day of regret, when the matter is decreed while they are unaware, and they do not believe.
19:40 Indeed We shall inherit the earth and whoever is upon it, and to Us they will be returned.
19:41 And mention in the Kitab Ibrahim; he was a sadiq Nabi,
19:42 when he said to his father: My father, why do you worship what neither hears nor sees nor avails you anything?
19:43 My father, knowledge has come to me that has not come to you; so follow me, I will guide you to a straight path.
19:44 My father, do not worship Shaytan; indeed Shaytan was ever disobedient to the Raheem.
19:45 My father, I fear that a punishment from the Rahman will touch you, so that you become an ally of Shaytan.
19:46 He said: Are you so eager to turn from my alihah, Ibrahim? If you do not cease, I will surely stone you; so leave me for a long while.
19:47 He said: Peace be upon you; I shall ask forgiveness for you from my Rabb; indeed my Rabb has ever been gracious to me.
19:48 And I withdraw from you and from what you call upon besides Allah, and I call upon my Rabb; perhaps in calling my Rabb I shall not be wretched.
19:49 Then when he withdrew from them and from what they worshipped besides Allah, We granted him Ishaq and Yaqub, and each We made a Nabi,
19:50 and We granted them of Our mercy, and We gave them a tongue of truth, raised high.
19:51 And mention in the Kitab Musa; he was devoted, and was a Rasul, a Nabi,
19:52 and We called him from the right side of the Mount and drew him near in private.
19:53 And We granted him of Our mercy his brother Harun, a Nabi.
19:54 And mention in the Kitab Ismail; he was true to the promise, and was a Rasul, a Nabi,
19:55 and he used to command his household with prayer and purity, and he was pleasing near his Rabb.
19:56 And mention in the Kitab Idris; he was a sadiq Nabi,
19:57 and We raised him to a high place.
19:58 Those are the ones upon whom Allah bestowed favor among the nabis: from the offspring of Adam, and from those We carried with Nuh, and from the offspring of Ibrahim and Israel, and from those We guided and chose; when the ayat of the Rahman are recited to them, they fall down prostrating and weeping.
19:59 Then after them came successors who lost the prayer and followed the desires; so they will soon meet error,
19:60 except whoever repents and believes and works salihness; those will enter the Garden and will not be wronged in anything.
19:61 Gardens of Adn that the Rahman promised to Allah's servants in the ghayb; indeed the promise of Allah is sure to come.
19:62 They will not hear idle talk therein, only peace; and their provision is theirs therein morning and evening.
19:63 That is the Garden We make Our servants heirs to — whoever is of the muttaqin.
19:64 And We do not descend except by command of your Rabb. To Allah belongs what is before us and what is behind us and what is between that; and your Rabb is not forgetful.
19:65 Rabb of the heavens and the earth and what is between them, so worship Allah and persevere in Allah's worship. Do you know any namesake of Allah?
19:66 And the human says: When I have died, will I really be brought out alive?
19:67 Does the human not remember that We created him before, when he was nothing?
19:68 So by your Rabb, We will surely gather them and the satans; then We will surely bring them around Jahannam, kneeling.
19:69 Then We will surely draw out from every party which of them was strongest in insolent defiance against the Rahman.
19:70 Then We know best those who are most worthy of it, entering the fire.
19:71 There is not one of you but will arrive at it; upon your Rabb is a decree, decided.
19:72 Then We will save those who were muttaqin and leave the wrongdoers in it, kneeling.
