19:73 And when Our ayat are recited to them clear, those who disbelieve say to those who believe: Which of the two groups is better in station and better in call?
19:74 And how many generations We destroyed before them who were better in goods and appearance.
19:75 Say: Whoever is in error — let the Rahman extend him an extension, until when they see what they are promised: either the punishment or the Hour. Then they will know who is worse in place and weaker in host.
19:76 And Allah increases those who are guided in guidance; and the lasting good deeds are better near your Rabb in reward and better in return.
19:77 Have you seen the one who disbelieved in Our ayat and said: I shall surely be given wealth and children?
19:78 Has he perceived the ghayb, or taken a covenant near the Rahman?
19:79 No indeed; We will write what he says and extend for him from the punishment an extension,
19:80 and We will inherit what he says, and he will come to Us alone.
19:81 And they took alihah besides Allah, that they might be for them a protection.
19:82 No indeed; they will soon disbelieve their worship of them and will be opposed to them.
19:83 Have you not seen that We sent the satans upon the kafirin, inciting them to frenzy?
19:84 So do not hasten over them; We only count for them a counting.
19:85 The day We gather the ilah-fearing to the Rahman as a delegation,
19:86 and We drive the mujrimin to Jahannam, arriving.
19:87 They will not possess intercession, except whoever took a covenant near the Rahman.
19:88 And they said: The Raheem has taken a child.
19:89 You have surely come with a monstrous thing —
19:90 nearly the heavens burst apart from it, and the earth splits open and the mountains fall down in haddan —
19:91 that a child was claimed for the Raheem.
19:92 And it is not befitting for the Raheem to take a child.
19:93 Indeed every one in the heavens and the earth comes to the Rahman only as a servant;
19:94 indeed Allah has counted them all and numbered them one by one,
19:95 and every one of them will come to Allah on the day of Resurrection, alone.
19:96 Indeed those who believe and work salihness — the Rahman will make affection for them.
19:97 So We have made it easy only upon your tongue, that you may give glad tidings with it to the ilah-fearing and warn with it a quarrelsome people.
19:98 And how many generations We destroyed before them — do you perceive anyone of them, or hear from them a sound?
20:1 Ta Ha
20:2 We did not reveal the Recitation upon you to make you wretched,
20:3 but as a reminder for whoever holds reverent awe.
20:4 A revelation from the One who created the earth and the highest heavens —
20:5 the Rahman is established above the high seat.
20:6 To Allah belongs what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and what is between them, and what is beneath the soil.
20:7 And if you speak open and loud, then Allah knows the secret and what is more hidden.
20:8 Allah — there is no ilah except Allah; to Allah belong the finest names.
20:9 Has the hadith of Musa come to you?
20:10 When he saw a fire, and said to his people: "Stay; I have perceived a fire. Perhaps I can bring you from it a flaming brand, or find at the fire some guidance."
20:11 When he came to it, he was called: "O Musa!"
20:12 "Indeed, I am your Rabb. So remove your sandals; you are in the holy valley of Tuwa."
20:13 "And I have chosen you, so listen to what is revealed:"
20:14 "Indeed, I am Allah; there is no ilah except I. So worship Me and establish the prayer for remembrance of Me."
20:15 "Indeed, the Hour is coming — I nearly conceal it — so that every nafs may be recompensed for what it strives."
20:16 So let no one who does not believe in it and has followed his desire turn you away from it, and plunge to ruin.
20:17 "And what is that in your right hand, O Musa?"
20:18 He said: "It is my staff; I lean on it, and I beat down leaves with it for my sheep, and I have other uses for it."
20:19 He said: "Cast it down, O Musa."
20:20 So he cast it down, and behold, it was a living snake, moving swiftly.
20:21 He said: "Take it, and do not fear. We will restore it to its former movement."
20:22 "And clasp your hand to your side; it will come out white, without harm — another aya,"
20:23 that We may show you some of Our greatest ayat.
20:24 Go to Fir'awn; he has transgressed.
20:25 He said: "Rabb, open my chest for me,
20:26 and make my affair easy for me,
20:27 and untie a knot from my tongue,
20:28 that they may understand my speech.
20:29 And appoint for me a vizier from my family,
20:30 Harun, my brother.
20:31 Strengthen my back through him,
20:32 and let him share in my affair,
20:33 that we may glorify You much
20:34 and remember You much.
20:35 Indeed, You have been watchful over us."
20:36 He said: "You have been given your request, O Musa.
20:37 And We favored you another time,
20:38 when We revealed to your mother what was revealed:
20:39 Cast him into the chest, then cast it into the sea, and the sea will throw it onto the shore; an enemy of Mine and an enemy of his will take him. And I cast upon you love from Me, and that you be made under My eye.
20:40 When your sister walked by and said: "Shall I point you to one who will undertake his care?" So We returned you to your mother, that her eye might be comforted and she might not grieve. And you killed a nafs, and We saved you from grief, and We tested you with a test. And you tarried years among the people of Midian; then you came as destined, O Musa.
20:41 And I fashioned you for Myself.
20:42 Go, you and your brother, with My ayat, and do not slacken in remembrance of Me.
20:43 Go, both of you, to Fir'awn; he has transgressed.
20:44 And speak to him a gentle word, so that perhaps he may remember or hold reverent awe.
20:45 They said: "Our Rabb, we fear that he will rush upon us or transgress."
20:46 He said: "Do not fear; indeed, I am with you both — I hear and I see."
20:47 So go, both of you, to him and say: "We are two rusul of your Rabb; so send with us the Children of Israel, and do not punish them. We have come to you with an aya from your Rabb; and peace be upon whoever follows the guidance."
20:48 "It has been revealed to us that the punishment is upon whoever denies and turns away."
20:49 He said: "So who is your Rabb, O Musa?"
20:50 He said: "Our Rabb is the One who gave everything its creation, then guided it."
20:51 He said: "So what of the former generations?"
20:52 He said: "Their knowledge is with my Rabb, in a record; my Rabb does not err and does not forget —
20:53 the One who made the earth a cradle for you, and threaded for you paths in it, and sent down water from the heaven, and with it We brought out pairs of diverse plants.
20:54 Eat and graze your herds. Indeed, in that are ayat for people of understanding.
20:55 From it We created you, and into it We return you, and from it We bring you out another time."
20:56 And We showed him all Our ayat, and he denied and refused.
20:57 He said: "Have you come to us to drive us out of our land with your sihr, O Musa?
20:58 So we will surely bring you sihr like it. Make between us and you an appointment that neither we nor you will break, at a level place."
20:59 He said: "Your appointment is the day of adornment, and that people be gathered in the forenoon."
20:60 So Fir'awn turned away, gathered his scheme, then came.
20:61 Musa said to them: "Woe to you! Do not fabricate a lie against Allah, or Allah will destroy you with a punishment; and whoever fabricates fails."
20:62 So they disputed their affair among themselves, and conferred in secret,
20:63 saying: "These two are surely two sihrians who want to drive you out of your land with their sihr, and to take away your exemplary way.
20:64 So gather your scheme, then come forward in ranks; and success today is for whoever gains the upper hand."
20:65 They said: "O Musa, either you cast, or we will be the ones to cast."
20:66 He said: "No, you cast." And behold, their ropes and staffs were made to appear to him, by their sihr, as if they were moving swiftly,
20:67 and Musa felt a fear within himself.
20:68 We said: "Do not fear; indeed, you are the uppermost.
20:69 Cast what is in your right hand; it will swallow what they have made. They have made only the scheme of a sihrian, and the sihrian does not succeed from wherever he comes."
20:70 So the sihrians were cast down prostrate; they said: "We believe in the Rabb of Harun and Musa."
20:71 He said: "You believed in him before I permitted you? Indeed, he is your chief who taught you sihr. So I will surely cut off your hands and your feet from opposite sides, and crucify you on the trunks of palm trees; and you will surely know which of us is more severe in punishment and more lasting."
20:72 They said: "We will never prefer you over what has come to us of clear proofs, nor over the One who originated us. So decree whatever you will decree; you decree only this worldly life.
20:73 Indeed, we believe in our Rabb, that Allah may forgive us our errors and what you compelled us to of sihr. And Allah is better and more lasting."
20:74 Indeed, whoever comes to his Rabb as a mujrim — for him is Hell: he neither dies in it nor lives.
20:75 And whoever comes to Allah a mumin, having done the salihin deeds — those are the ones for whom are the highest degrees:
20:76 gardens of Eden, beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein. And that is the recompense for whoever purifies himself.
20:77 And We revealed to Musa: "Set out by night with My servants, and strike for them a dry path in the sea; you will not fear being overtaken, nor be in dread."
20:78 So Fir'awn followed them with his hosts, and the sea covered them with what covered them.
20:79 And Fir'awn led his people astray, and did not guide.
20:80 O Children of Israel, We saved you from your enemy, and We promised you the right side of the Mount, and We sent down upon you the manna and the quails:
20:81 eat of the good things We provided you, and do not transgress in it, lest My wrath descend upon you; and upon whoever My wrath descends has indeed plunged."
20:82 And indeed, I am Forgiving for whoever repents and believes and does a salih deed, then is guided.
20:83 "And what made you hurry from your people, O Musa?"
20:84 He said: "They are those on my track, and I hurried to You, Rabb, that You be pleased."
20:85 He said: "We have tested your people after you, and the Samiri has led them astray."
20:86 So Musa returned to his people, angry and grieving. He said: "My people, did your Rabb not promise you a good promise? Did the time seem too long for you, or did you want wrath from your Rabb to descend upon you, so you failed my appointment?"
20:87 They said: "We did not fail your appointment by our own will; but we were made to carry loads of the people's adornment, so we cast them — and so did the Samiri cast."
20:88 And he brought out for them a calf — a body with a lowing sound — and they said: "This is your ilah and the ilah of Musa." And he forgot.
20:89 Do they not see that it does not return to them a word, and possesses for them neither harm nor benefit?
20:90 And Harun had said to them before: "My people, you have only been tested by it; and your Rabb is the Rahman, so follow me and obey my command."
20:91 They said: "We will not leave it, cleaving to it, until Musa returns to us."
20:92 He said: "O Harun, what withheld you, when you saw them going astray,
20:93 that you did not follow me? Did you disobey my command?"
20:94 He said: "O son of my mother, do not take me by my beard or my head. I feared you would say: You divided the Children of Israel and did not wait for my word."
20:95 He said: "So what is your affair, O Samiri?"
20:96 He said: "I saw what they did not see, so I took a handful from the track of the Rasul and threw it, and so my nafs made it seem fair to me."
20:97 He said: "Go! For you in this life is only to say: No touch. And you have an appointment you will not miss. And look at your ilah that you remained cleaving to: we will burn it, then scatter it into the sea."
20:98 Your ilah is only Allah — there is no ilah except Allah — encompassing everything in knowledge.
20:99 Thus We narrate to you accounts of what has passed before; and We have given you from Our presence a remembrance.
20:100 Whoever turns away from it — he will carry, on the Day of Resurrection, a load,
20:101 abiding therein. And evil for them, on the Day of Resurrection, is the load —
20:102 the day the blast is blown, and We gather the mujrimin that day, blinded,
20:103 whispering among themselves: "You tarried only 10."
20:104 We know best what they say, when the most moderate of them in manner says: "You tarried only a day."
20:105 And they ask you about the mountains; so say: "My Rabb will scatter them away
20:106 and leave it a smooth, level plain,
20:107 where you will see neither crookedness nor any rise."
20:108 That day they follow the caller — there is no crookedness in him — and the voices are humbled before the Rahman, and you hear nothing but a whisper.
20:109 That day intercession is of no benefit, except for one the Rahman has permitted and approved a word for.
20:110 Allah knows what is before them and what is behind them, and they do not yuhituna Allah in knowledge.
20:111 And faces are humbled before the Ever-Living, the Self-Sustaining; and whoever carried wrong has failed.
20:112 And whoever does the salihin deeds while a mumin — he will fear neither wrong nor loss.
20:113 And thus We sent it down, a clear Arabic recitation, and varied the warnings in it, so that perhaps they may be wary, or that it may bring them a reminder.
20:114 So exalted is Allah, the True Sovereign. And do not hurry with the recitation before its revelation is completed to you; and say: "Rabb, increase me in knowledge."
20:115 And We pledged to Adam before, and he forgot; and We found no resolve in him.
20:116 And when We said to the malakat: "Prostrate to Adam," and they prostrated — except Iblis; he refused.
20:117 Then We said: "O Adam, this is an enemy to you and to your mate; so let him not drive you both out of the garden, so you become wretched.
20:118 Indeed, for you: you will not go hungry in it, nor be naked,
20:119 and you will not thirst in it, nor be scorched by the forenoon sun."
20:120 Then Shaytan whispered to him; he said: "O Adam, shall I point you to the tree of eternity, and a kingdom that does not wear out?"
20:121 So they both ate of it, and their shame became manifest to them, and they began stitching upon themselves leaves of the garden. And Adam disobeyed his Rabb, and went astray.
20:122 Then his Rabb chose him, turned to him in forgiveness, and guided him.
20:123 He said: "Descend from it, both of you, together. Some of you are an enemy to others. And if guidance comes to you from Me, then whoever follows My guidance will not go astray nor be wretched,
20:124 and whoever turns away from My remembrance — for him is a straitened living, and We will gather him, on the Day of Resurrection, blind."
20:125 He will say: "Rabb, why have You gathered me blind, when I used to see?"
20:126 He will say: "Thus Our ayat came to you, and you forgot them; and thus today you are forgotten."
20:127 And thus We recompense whoever is excessive and does not believe in the ayat of his Rabb; and the punishment of the Hereafter is harder and more lasting.
20:128 Has it not guided them — how many generations We destroyed before them, who walk in their dwellings? Indeed, in that are ayat for people of understanding.
20:129 And had not a word preceded from your Rabb, it would have been binding, and an appointed term.
20:130 So be of the sabirin over what they say, and glorify with the praise of your Rabb before the rising of the sun and before its setting, and in hours of the night — glorify — and at the edges of the day, so that perhaps you may be pleased.
20:131 And do not stretch your eyes toward that with which We have given pairs among them enjoyment — the bloom of worldly life — that We may test them by it. And the provision of your Rabb is better and more lasting.
20:132 And command your family to the prayer and be steadfast upon it. We do not ask you for provision; We provide you. And the outcome is for the wary.
20:133 And they say: "Why does he not bring us an aya from his Rabb?" Has there not come to them the clear proof of what is in the former writings?
20:134 And if We had destroyed them with a punishment before it, they would have said: "Our Rabb, why did You not send us a Rasul, so that we might follow Your ayat before we were abased and disgraced?"
20:135 Say: "Everyone is waiting, so wait; and you will come to know who are the companions of the even path and who is guided."
21:1 The people's reckoning has drawn near, yet they are unaware, turning away.
21:2 Whenever a newly-arriving reminder comes to them from their Rabb, they listen to it while they play.
21:3 Their hearts are in diversion, and those who did wrong keep the secret counsel secret: "Is this anything but a human like you? Do you then come to the sihr while you can see?"
21:4 Allah says: speech in the heaven and the earth is known to Allah; and Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
21:5 No; they say: "A jumble of dreams! No, he fabricated it! No, he is a poet! Let him bring us an aya, as the former ones were sent."
21:6 No town that We destroyed believed before them — will they then believe?
21:7 We sent no one before you but men to whom We gave revelation — so ask the people of the reminder, if you do not know.
21:8 We did not make them bodies that eat no food, nor were they immortal.
21:9 Then We made the promise true for them, and We saved them and whom We willed, and We destroyed the exceeders.
21:10 We have indeed brought down to you a Kitab containing your reminder — will you not reason?
21:11 How many a town that did wrong We have crushed, and We raised up after it other people!
21:12 So when they perceived Our might, there they were, fleeing from it!
21:13 Do not run! Return to the ease-softened life you lived in and to your dwellings — perhaps you will be asked.
21:14 They said: "Woe to us! We were zalimun."
21:15 That cry of theirs did not cease until We made them like a mown field, stilled.
21:16 We did not create the heaven and the earth and what is between them in play.
21:17 Had We willed to take a diversion, We would have taken it from Our presence — if We were to do so.
21:18 Rather, We cast the truth upon the falsehood, and it crushes it — and there it is, vanishing! And woe to you for what you describe!
21:19 Allah has whatever is in the heavens and the earth; and those near Allah are not too proud to worship Allah, nor do they lose heart.
21:20 They glorify night and day, and they never flag.
21:21 Or have they taken alihah from the earth that can raise the dead?
21:22 Had there been in the two alihah other than Allah, both would have corrupted — so glory be to Allah, Rabb of the arsh, above what they describe!
21:23 Allah is not questioned about what Allah does — but they are questioned.
21:24 Or have they taken alihah besides Allah? Say: "Bring your clear proof! This is the reminder of those with me and the reminder of those before me." But most of them do not know the truth, so they are turning away.
21:25 We never sent a Rasul before you without revealing to him: "There is no ilah but Me, so worship Me."
21:26 And they say: "The Rahman has taken a child." Glory be to Allah! Rather, they are honored servants.
21:27 They do not precede Allah in speech, and they act by Allah's command.
21:28 Allah knows what is before them and what is behind them, and they do not intercede except for one Allah approves; and they are ever in awe of Allah.
21:29 And if any of them says: "I am a ilah besides Allah," that one We repay with Hell — thus We repay the wrongdoers.
21:30 Have those who disbelieve not seen that the heavens and the earth were a joined mass, and We split them apart? And We made from water every living thing — will they not then believe?
21:31 And We placed in the earth firm anchors so that it does not sway with them, and We placed in it wide passes as ways, that they may be guided.
21:32 And We made the heaven a protected roof — yet they turn away from its ayat.
21:33 And it is Allah who created the night and the day and the sun and the moon, each in an orbit, swimming on.
21:34 We assigned no human before you immortality — if you die, will they be the immortals?
21:35 Every nafs tastes death, and We test you with evil and with good as a trial — and to Us you return.
21:36 And when those who disbelieve see you, they take you only as a mockery: "Is this the one who mentions your ilahs?" — while it is the mention of the Rahman that they disbelieve in.
21:37 The human was created of haste — I will show you My ayat, so do not press for haste.
21:38 And they say: "When is this promise, if you are sadiq?"
21:39 If only those who disbelieve knew — at the moment when they cannot hold back the fire from their faces nor from their backs, nor will they be helped!
21:40 Rather, it comes to them suddenly and stuns them — they cannot turn it back, nor can they look.
21:41 Rusul before you were indeed mocked, but what they mocked befell those who mocked.
21:42 Say: "Who guards you by night and day against the Rahman?" Rather, they turn away from the reminder of their Rabb.
21:43 Or do they have alihah that withhold them from Us? They cannot help themselves, nor will they be supported by Us.
21:44 Rather, We let these and their fathers enjoy life until their life-term grew long. Do they not see that We come to the land, diminishing it at its edges? Are they the ones who overcome?
21:45 Say: "I only warn you by the revelation." But the deaf do not hear the call when they are warned.
21:46 And if a breath of your Rabb's punishment touches them, they will surely say: "Woe to us! We were wrongdoers."
21:47 We set the fair scales for the Day of Rising — no nafs is wronged in anything; and if it be the weight of a grain of mustard, We bring it. And sufficient is Allah as the One who takes account.
21:48 We gave Musa and Harun the Criterion, and a light and a reminder for those who guard themselves.
21:49 Those who stand in awe of their Rabb in the ghayb, and they are in awe of the Hour.
21:50 And this is a blessed reminder that We brought down — so are you the ones who reject it?
21:51 We gave Ibrahim his right course of old, and We knew him well.
21:52 When he said to his father and his people: "What are these statues that you are so devoted to?"
21:53 They said: "We found our fathers worshipping them."
21:54 He said: "You and your fathers were indeed in clear error."
21:55 They said: "Have you brought us the truth, or are you one of those who play?"
21:56 He said: "Rather, your Rabb is the Rabb of the heavens and the earth, the One who originated them — and I am one of those who bear witness to that."
21:57 And by Allah, I will surely scheme against your asnam after you turn your backs.
21:58 So he reduced them to pieces, except their biggest, so that they would return to it.
21:59 They said: "Who did this to our alihah? He is surely one of the wrongdoers."
21:60 They said: "We heard a youth speaking of them — he is called Ibrahim."
21:61 They said: "Then bring him before the eyes of the people, so that they may bear witness."
21:62 They said: "Was it you who did this to our alihah, Ibrahim?"
21:63 He said: "No — this biggest one of theirs did it. So ask them, if they can speak."
21:64 So they returned to themselves and said: "You yourselves are the wrongdoers."
21:65 Then they hung their heads: "You know well that these do not speak."
21:66 He said: "Do you then worship, besides Allah, what can neither benefit you in anything nor harm you?"
21:67 Shame on you and on what you worship besides Allah! Will you not reason?
21:68 They said: "Burn him and help your ilahs, if you are to do anything!"
21:69 We said: "O fire, be coolness and safety upon Ibrahim."
21:70 They schemed against him, and We made them the greatest losers.
21:71 And We saved him and Lut to the land in which We blessed, for all peoples.
21:72 And We granted him Ishaq and Ya'qub as an additional gift, and each We made salih.
21:73 And We made them leaders guiding by Our command, and We revealed to them the doing of good deeds, the keeping up of prayer, and the giving of alms — and they were worshippers to Us.
21:74 And Lut — We gave him judgment and knowledge, and We saved him from the town that used to do the vile deeds; they were a people of evil, fasiqin.
21:75 And We admitted him into Our mercy — he is indeed among the salihin.
21:76 And Nuh — when he called of old, We answered him, and We saved him and his family from the great distress.
21:77 And We helped him against the people who denied Our ayat; they were a people of evil, so We drowned them all together.
21:78 And Dawud and Sulayman, when they judged concerning the field when the people's flock strayed into it — and We were witness to their judgment.
21:79 So We gave Sulayman understanding of it, and to each We gave judgment and knowledge. And We subjected with Dawud the mountains, glorifying, and the birds — and We were ever doers.
21:80 And We taught him the making of garments for you, to fortify you in your clash of might — so are you grateful?
21:81 And for Sulayman the wind, storming, running at his command to the land in which We blessed — and We knew all things.
21:82 And among the devils were those who dived for him and did work other than that — and We were watching over them.
21:83 And Ayyub, when he called his Rabb: "Harm has touched me, and You are the Raheem of the merciful."
21:84 So We answered him and removed the harm that was on him, and We gave him his family and the like of them with them — a mercy from Us and a reminder for the worshippers.
21:85 And Ismail and Idris and Dhu al-Kifl — each was among the sabirin.
21:86 And We admitted them into Our mercy — they are indeed among the salihin.
21:87 And Dhu al-Nun, when he went off in anger and thought that We would have no power over him; then he called in the darkness: "There is no ilah but You; glory be to You; I was indeed one of the wrongdoers."
21:88 So We answered him and saved him from the gloom — and thus We save the muminin.
21:89 And Zakariya, when he called his Rabb: "Rabb, do not leave me alone, and You are the best of inheritors."
21:90 So We answered him and granted him Yahya, and We made his zawj sound for him. They used to race to the good deeds and call on Us in eagerness and in awe, and they were humble before Us.
21:91 And she who fortified her opening — We blew into her of Our ruh, and We made her and her son an aya for all peoples.
21:92 Indeed, this community of yours is one community, and I am your Rabb — so worship Me.
21:93 Yet they cut their affair into pieces among themselves — but all will return to Us.
21:94 So whoever does salih deeds while a mumin — no rejection of his striving; and We are recording it for him.
21:95 And there is a ban upon any town We destroyed: they will not return.
21:96 Until, when Ya'juj and Ma'juj are let loose and pour forth from every ridge,
21:97 and the true promise draws near — and there, staring, are the eyes of those who disbelieved: "Woe to us! We were indeed unaware of this; rather, we were wrongdoers."
21:98 Indeed, you and what you worship besides Allah are fuel of Hell — you will be arriving at it.
21:99 If these had been alihah, they would not have arrived at it — yet all abide in it forever.
21:100 For them therein is groaning, and therein they hear nothing.
21:101 Indeed, those for whom the good has preceded from Us — those are far removed from it.
21:102 They will not hear its slightest sound, and they abide forever in whatever their nafs desired.
21:103 The greatest dread will not grieve them, and the malakat will receive them: "This is your day that you were promised."
21:104 The day We fold the heaven like the folding of a written scroll — as We began the first creation, We will repeat it. A promise binding on Us — indeed We were ever doers.
21:105 And We wrote in the Kitab after the reminder: "The earth — My salih servants shall inherit it."
21:106 Indeed, in this is a message reaching through, for a people of worshippers.
21:107 And We sent you only as a mercy for all peoples.
21:108 Say: "It is revealed to me only that your ilah is one ilah — so will you be ones who submit?"
21:109 But if they turn away, say: "I have proclaimed to you all alike, and I do not know whether what you are promised is near or far."
21:110 Indeed, Allah knows the open speech and knows what you hide.
21:111 And I do not know — perhaps it is a trial for you, and an enjoyment until a time.
21:112 Say: "Rabb, judge with the truth! Our Rabb is the Rahman, the One whose help is sought against what you describe."
22:1 O people, be ilahfearing toward your Rabb; the quaking of the Hour is a tremendous thing.
22:2 On the day you see it, every nursing mother will be dazed away from what she nursed, and every imra'a bearing a burden will lay down her burden, and you will see people in stupor while they are not drunk — but the punishment of Allah is severe.
22:3 And among people are those who dispute about Allah without knowledge, and follow every defiant devil.
22:4 It is decreed upon him that whoever takes him as an ally, he will lead him astray and guide him to the punishment of the Blaze.
22:5 O people, if you are in doubt about the raising: We created you from dust, then from a drop, then from a clot, then from a lump of tissue, formed and unformed, to make clear to you; and We settle in the wombs what We will until an appointed term; then We bring you out as a child, that you may reach your prime; and some of you die, and some of you are returned to the frailest age, so that they know nothing after once knowing. And you see the earth lifeless; then when We send down water upon it, it stirs and swells and grows from every pair something lovely.
22:6 That is because Allah is the Truth, and gives life to the dead, and has power over everything.
22:7 And that the Hour is coming — no doubt in it — and that Allah will raise those in the graves.
22:8 And among people are those who dispute about Allah without knowledge, without guidance, and without an enlightening Kitab.
22:9 He bends himself aside to lead astray from the way of Allah; for him in this world is disgrace, and on the Day of the Standing We will let him taste the punishment of the burning.
22:10 That is for what your hands sent ahead, and Allah is not zalimun to the servants.
22:11 And among people are those who worship Allah on shifting ground: if good befalls them they settle into it, but if a trial befalls them they flip over on their faces; they lose this world and the next — that is the clear loss.
22:12 He calls upon besides Allah what neither harms him nor benefits him; that is the far error.
22:13 He calls on one whose harm is nearer to him than his benefit — what a wretched patron, and what a wretched companion.
22:14 Allah will surely admit those who believe and do the good deeds into gardens beneath which rivers flow. Allah does what Allah wills.
22:15 Whoever thinks that Allah will not help him in this world and the next — let him stretch a rope toward the heaven, then cut it off, and let him see whether his scheme removes what enrages him.
22:16 And so We sent it down with clear ayat, and Allah guides whom Allah wills.
22:17 Those who believe, and those who joined the rivaling party, and the Sabians, and the Nasara, and the Magians, and those who associate — Allah will surely decide between them on the Day of the Standing; Allah is witness over everything.
22:18 Have you not seen that whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth bows to Allah — the sun, the moon, the stars, the mountains, the trees, the creatures, and many of the people? And many deserve the punishment. And whoever Allah humiliates has no one to honor him. Allah does what Allah wills.
22:19 These are two contending parties who contended about their Rabb. As for those who disbelieved, garments of fire are cut out for them, and scalding water is poured over their heads.
22:20 It melts what is in their bellies, and the skins.
22:21 And they have maces of hadid.
22:22 Whenever they want to leave it because of the gloom, they are put back into it: "Taste the punishment of the burning!"
22:23 Allah will surely admit those who believe and do the good deeds into gardens beneath which rivers flow, adorned in them with bracelets of gold and pearls, and their garment in it is silk.
22:24 And they are guided to the good of speech, and guided to the path of the Praiseworthy.
22:25 Those who disbelieve and bar from the way of Allah and from the Sacred Mosque, which We made for people alike — the one cleaving to it and the desert-dweller — whoever intends in it deviation with injustice, We will make him taste a painful punishment.
22:26 And when We settled Ibrahim at the site of the House: "Associate nothing with Me, and purify My House for those who go around it, for those who stand, and for those who bow and prostrate."
22:27 And proclaim among the people the pilgrimage; they will come to you on foot and on every lean mount, coming from every deep ravine.
22:28 That they may witness benefit for themselves and remember the name of Allah in appointed days over what Allah provided them of the beasts of the herds; so eat of it and feed the wretched and the needy.
22:29 Then let them settle their unkempt rites and fulfill their vows, and go around the Ancient House.
22:30 So it is; and whoever honors the sanctity of Allah — that is better for him with his Rabb. The herds are made lawful for you except what is recited to you; so avoid the filth of idols, and avoid swerving speech.
22:31 Turned upright to Allah, not associating anything with Allah; and whoever associates with Allah, it is as if he fell from the heaven, and a bird snatches him, or the wind sweeps him down into a far-off place.
22:32 So it is; and whoever honors the rites of Allah — that is from the devotion of hearts.
22:33 In them you have benefit until an appointed term; then their destination is the Ancient House.
22:34 For every community We appointed a rite, that they may remember the name of Allah over what Allah provided them of the beasts of the herds. Your ilah is one ilah, so to Allah submit — and give good news to the mukbitin.
22:35 Those who, when Allah is remembered, their hearts tremble with awe, and those sabir over what befalls them, and those who keep up the prayer, and who spend from what We provided them.
22:36 And the sacrificial beasts We made for you among the rites of Allah; in them is good for you. So remember the name of Allah over them, lined in ranks; then when their sides collapse, eat of them and feed the contented and the beggar. Thus We subjected them to you, so that you may give thanks.
22:37 Neither their flesh nor their blood reaches Allah, but the devotion from you reaches Allah. Thus We subjected them to you, that you may magnify Allah for what Allah guided you to. And give good news to the muhsinin.
22:38 Allah pushes harm back from those who believe; Allah does not love any khawwān kafūr.
22:39 Permission is given to those who are fought, because they were wronged; and Allah is powerful to help them.
22:40 Those who were driven from their homes without right, only because they said, "Our Rabb is Allah." Had Allah not pushed back some people by means of others, monasteries, churches, synagogues, and mosques — in which the name of Allah is much remembered — would have been demolished. And Allah will surely help whoever helps Allah. Allah is powerful, mighty.
22:41 Those who, if We establish them in the earth, keep up the prayer, give the purifying share, command what is recognized, and forbid what is rejected; and to Allah belongs the outcome of affairs.
22:42 If they deny you, then before them the people of Nuh denied, and Ad and Thamud,
22:43 and the people of Ibrahim, and the people of Lut,
22:44 and the companions of Midian; and Musa was disbelieved. So I gave respite to the kafirin, then I seized them — and how was My disavowal!
22:45 How many a town We destroyed while it did wrong, and it lies collapsed on its raised frames, with an abandoned well and a towering palace.
22:46 Have they not journeyed through the earth, so as to have hearts they reason with, or ears they hear with? It is not the sights that go blind, but the hearts within the chests that go blind.
22:47 And they urge you to hurry the punishment, but Allah will never fail Allah's promise; and a day with your Rabb is like 1,000 years of what you count.
22:48 And how many a town I gave respite to while it did wrong; then I seized it — and to Me is the arrival.
22:49 Say: O people, I am only a clear warner to you.
22:50 Those who believe and do the good deeds — for them is forgiveness and a generous provision.
22:51 And those who strive against Our ayat, seeking to thwart Us — those are the companions of the Blaze.
22:52 We never sent before you any Rasul or Nabi but that when he wished, the devil cast into his wish; then Allah abolishes what the devil casts, and then Allah confirms the ayat. Allah is knowing, wise.
22:53 That Allah may make what the devil casts a trial for those in whose hearts is sickness and whose hearts are hardened; and the wrongdoers are indeed in far opposition.
22:54 And that those given knowledge may know that it is the truth from your Rabb, and believe in it, and their hearts may grow humble to it; and Allah surely guides those who believe to a straight path.
22:55 And the kafirin will not cease doubting it until the Hour comes upon them suddenly, or there comes to them the punishment of a barren day.
22:56 Sovereignty that day belongs to Allah; Allah judges between them. Those who believe and do the good deeds will be in the Garden of Bliss.
22:57 And those who disbelieve and deny Our ayat — those have a humiliating punishment.
22:58 And those who emigrate in the way of Allah, then are killed or die — Allah will surely provide them a good provision, and Allah is the best of providers.
22:59 Allah will surely admit them to a place that pleases them; and Allah is knowing, forbearing.
22:60 So it is; and whoever requites with the like of what was done to him, and is then transgressed against — Allah will help him. Allah is pardoning, forgiving.
22:61 That is because Allah enters the night into the day and enters the day into the night, and Allah is hearing, seeing.
22:62 That is because Allah is the Truth, and what they call upon besides Allah is falsehood, and Allah is the High, the Great.
22:63 Have you not seen that Allah sends down water from the heaven, and the earth turns green? Allah is subtle, aware.
22:64 To Allah belongs what is in the heavens and what is on the earth; and Allah is the Free-of-Need, the Praiseworthy.
22:65 Have you not seen that Allah subjected to you what is on the earth, and the ship runs on the sea by Allah's command, and Allah holds the heaven so it will not fall on the earth except by Allah's leave? Allah is gentle, compassionate to people.
22:66 And it is Allah who gave you life, then makes you die, then gives you life again; the human being is truly ungrateful.
22:67 For every community We appointed a rite that they perform; so let them not pull you away in the matter. Call to your Rabb; you are indeed upon straight guidance.
22:68 And if they dispute with you, say: Allah knows best what you do.
22:69 Allah will judge between you on the Day of the Standing concerning what you used to differ about.
22:70 Do you not know that Allah knows what is in the heaven and the earth? That is indeed in a record; that is easy for Allah.
22:71 And they worship besides Allah that for which Allah sent down no warrant, and of which they have no knowledge; and the wrongdoers have no helper.
22:72 And when Our clear ayat are recited to them, you recognize disbelief on the faces of those who disbelieve; they nearly lunge at those who recite Our ayat to them. Say: Shall I tell you of worse than that? The Fire — Allah has promised it to those who disbelieve, and wretched is the arrival.
22:73 O people, a parable is struck, so listen to it: those you call upon besides Allah will never create a fly, even if they gathered together for it; and if a fly snatches something from them, they cannot rescue it from it. Weak are the seeker and the sought.
22:74 They did not measure Allah with the measure due to Allah; Allah is powerful, mighty.
22:75 Allah chooses rusul from the malakat and from people; Allah is hearing, seeing.
22:76 Allah knows what is before them and what is behind them, and to Allah all affairs return.
22:77 O you who believe, bow and prostrate, and worship your Rabb, and do good — that you may attain success.
22:78 And strive in Allah with the striving due to Allah. Allah has chosen you, and has laid on you in the religion no constriction — the way of your father Ibrahim. Allah named you submitters before and in this, so that the Rasul may be a witness over you, and you be witnesses over people. So keep up the prayer, give the purifying share, and hold fast to Allah; Allah is your patron — so what an excellent patron, and what an excellent helper.
23:1 Successful indeed are the muminin,
23:2 those who are humbled in their prayers,
23:3 and who turn away from idle talk,
23:4 and who are doers of the alms-due,
23:5 and who guard their private parts,
23:6 except with their mates or what their right hands possess — for they are not to be blamed;
23:7 but whoever seeks beyond that — those are the transgressors.
23:8 And those who keep their amanats and their pledges,
23:9 and who guard their prayers —
23:10 those are the inheritors,
23:11 who inherit the Garden; they abide in it forever.
23:12 We created the human from an extract of clay.
23:13 Then We made it a drop in a firm resting-place.
23:14 Then We created the drop into a clinging clot, then We created the clot into a lump, then We created the lump into bones, then We clothed the bones with flesh, then We raised it up as another creation. So blessed be Allah, the best of creators.
23:15 Then after that you will surely die.
23:16 Then on the Day of the Rising you will be raised.
23:17 We created above you 7 paths, and We were never unmindful of the creation.
23:18 And We sent down water from the heaven in due measure, and We settled it in the earth; and We are able to take it away.
23:19 Then We raised up for you, by it, gardens of palms and grapes; in them are many fruits for you, and from them you eat.
23:20 And a tree that comes out from Mount Sinai, growing with oil and a dye for those who eat.
23:21 And in the livestock there is a lesson for you: We give you drink from what is in their bellies, and in them are many benefits for you, and from them you eat.
23:22 And on them and on ships you are carried.
23:23 We sent Nuh to his people, and he said: "O my people, worship Allah — you have no ilah but Allah. Will you not be Allah-fearing?"
23:24 The chiefs of his people who disbelieved said: "This is nothing but a human like you, who wants to gain favor over you. Had Allah willed, Allah would have sent down malakat. We never heard of this among our forefathers of old."
23:25 "He is only a man with madness upon him — so wait with him for a time."
23:26 He said: "My Rabb, help me against their denial."
23:27 So We inspired him: "Make the ship under Our eyes and Our inspiration. Then, when Our command comes and the oven boils over, take into it two of every kind, and your household — except those of them against whom the word has already preceded — and do not plead with Me for those who did wrong; they will be drowned."
23:28 Then, when you and those with you are settled on the ship, say: "Praise belongs to Allah, who rescued us from the wrongdoing people."
23:29 And say: "My Rabb, bring me down in a blessed resting-place — You are the best of settlers."
23:30 In that are ayat, and We were surely testing.
23:31 Then We raised up after them another generation,
23:32 and We sent among them a Rasul from themselves: "Worship Allah — you have no ilah but Allah. Will you not be Allah-fearing?"
23:33 The chiefs of his people who disbelieved and denied the meeting of the Hereafter — and We had eased them in the lower life — said: "This is nothing but a human like you; he eats of what you eat and drinks of what you drink.
23:34 If you obey a human like yourselves, you will surely be the khasirin.
23:35 Does he promise you that when you die and become dust and bones, you will be brought out?
23:36 Far, far away is what you are promised!
23:37 There is nothing but our nearer life: we die and we live, and we will not be raised.
23:38 He is only a man who has fabricated a lie against Allah — and we will not believe him."
23:39 He said: "My Rabb, help me against their denial."
23:40 "In a little while they shall surely be regretful."
23:41 So the shout seized them in truth, and We made them like worthless chaff. Away with the wrongdoing people!
23:42 Then We raised up after them other generations.
23:43 No nation can outstrip its term, and they do not delay.
23:44 Then We sent Our rusul in succession. Whenever a Rasul came to a nation, they denied him, so We made some of them follow others and turned them into bygone ahadith. Away with people who do not believe!
23:45 Then We sent Musa and his brother Harun with Our ayat and a clear warrant
23:46 to Fir'awn and his chiefs, but they grew arrogant and were a people grown high,
23:47 and they said: "Shall we believe two humans like ourselves, when their people serve us?"
23:48 So they denied them both, and they were among the destroyed.
23:49 We gave Musa the Kitab, so that they might be guided.
23:50 And We made the son of Maryam and his mother an aya, and sheltered them both on a highland with a settled resting-place and a spring.
23:51 O rusul, eat of the good things and do what is right — I know well what you do.
23:52 Truly this community of yours is one community, and I am your Rabb — so be Allah-fearing.
23:53 But they split their affair into portions — every party rejoicing in what it has.
23:54 Leave them in their flood until a time.
23:55 Do they reckon that the wealth and children We supply them with
23:56 are Us hastening them the good things? No — they do not perceive.
23:57 Those who are awe-struck with fear of their Rabb,
23:58 and who believe in the ayat of their Rabb,
23:59 and who do not associate others with their Rabb,
23:60 and who give what they give with hearts trembling in awe, knowing that to their Rabb they will return —
23:61 those race toward the good things, and they are the forerunners.
23:62 We burden no nafs beyond its capacity; and with Us is a Kitab that speaks the truth, and they will not be wronged.
23:63 But their hearts are drowned in this, and they have works besides that which they do.
23:64 Until, when We seize their softened ones with punishment, at once they cry out.
23:65 "Do not cry out today — no help from Us will reach you."
23:66 My ayat were recited to you, but you used to turn back on your heels,
23:67 arrogant toward it, talking by night, turning away.
23:68 Have they not pondered the word? Or has there come to them what did not come to their forefathers of old?
23:69 Or do they not recognize their Rasul, and so they disavow him?
23:70 Or do they say, "He is mad"? No — he brought them the truth, and most of them are averse to the truth.
23:71 If the truth followed their desires, the heavens and the earth and whoever is in them would be corrupted. No — We brought them their reminder, but they turn away from their reminder.
23:72 Or do you ask them for a tribute? The tribute of your Rabb is better — and Allah is the best of providers.
23:73 You call them to a straight path.
23:74 Those who do not believe in the Hereafter — from the path they surely swerve.
23:75 If We had mercy on them and uncovered the harm they suffer, they would plunge deeper into their transgression, wandering blind.
23:76 We seized them with punishment, but they did not humble themselves before their Rabb, nor do they plead humbly.
23:77 Until, when We open upon them a door of severe punishment, at once they are struck with despair.
23:78 It is Allah who raised up for you hearing, sights, and fu'ad — little do you give thanks.
23:79 It is Allah who spread you forth over the earth, and to Allah you will be gathered.
23:80 It is Allah who gives life and death, and to Allah belongs the alternation of night and day — will you not reason?
23:81 No — they say the like of what the ancients said.
23:82 They say: "When we die and become dust and bones, will we really be raised?
23:83 We and our fathers were promised this before. This is nothing but the tales of the ancients."
23:84 Say: "Whose is the earth and whoever is in it, if you know?"
23:85 They will say: "To Allah." Say: "Will you not remember?"
23:86 Say: "Who is the Rabb of the 7 heavens, and the Rabb of the mighty arsh?"
23:87 They will say: "To Allah." Say: "Will you not be Allah-fearing?"
23:88 Say: "In whose hand is the dominion of all things, who grants refuge and from whom none is granted refuge — if you know?"
23:89 They will say: "To Allah." Say: "Then how are you bewitched?"
23:90 No — We brought them the truth, and they are the kazibin.
23:91 Allah has taken no child, and there is no ilah beside Allah — for then each ilah would carry off what it created, and some would rise high over others. Exalted be Allah above what they describe!
23:92 Knower of the ghayb and the witnessed — exalted is Allah above what they associate.
23:93 Say: "My Rabb, if You show me what they are promised,
23:94 my Rabb, do not place me among the wrongdoing people."
23:95 We are well able to show you what We promise them.
23:96 Push back evil with what is best — We know best what they describe.
23:97 And say: "My Rabb, I seek refuge in You from the promptings of the devils,
23:98 and I seek refuge in You, my Rabb, that they should be present with me."
23:99 Until, when death comes to one of them, he says: "My Rabb, send me back,
23:100 that I may do right in what I left behind." Never — it is only a word he speaks; and behind them is a barrier until the day they are raised.
23:101 When the blast is blown, on that day there will be no kinship between them, and they will not ask one another.
23:102 Those whose scales weigh heavy — those are the muflihin.
23:103 And those whose scales weigh light — those are the ones who lost themselves; in Jahannam they abide forever.
23:104 The fire scorches their faces, and in it they are scowling.
23:105 "Were My ayat not recited to you, yet you denied them?"
23:106 They say: "Our Rabb, our wretchedness overcame us, and we were a people astray.
23:107 Our Rabb, bring us out of it — and if we return, then we are wrongdoers."
23:108 It is said: "Be driven away into it, and do not speak."
23:109 "There was a group of My servants who said: 'Our Rabb, we believe — so forgive us and have mercy on us; You are the best of the merciful.'"
23:110 You took them in mockery until they made you forget My reminder, and you used to laugh at them.
23:111 I have rewarded them this day for their steadfastness — they are the winners.
23:112 Say: "How long did you tarry in the earth, in counted years?"
23:113 They say: "We tarried a day or part of a day — ask the counters."
23:114 Say: "You tarried only a little — if only you knew."
23:115 Did you reckon We created you in vain, and that to Us you will not return?
23:116 Exalted be Allah, the true King — there is no ilah but Allah, Rabb of the noble arsh.
23:117 Whoever calls upon another ilah beside Allah, with no proof for it — his reckoning is only with his Rabb. The kafirin will not succeed.
23:118 And say: "My Rabb, forgive and have mercy — You are the best of the merciful."
24:1 A chapter We sent down and ordained, and We sent down in it clear ayat, so that you may remember.
24:2 The adulteress and the adulterer: flog each one of them with 100 lashes, and let no pity for the two of them take hold of you in the law of Allah, if you believe in Allah and the last day; and let a party of the muminin witness their punishment.
24:3 The adulterer shall marry none but an adulteress or an idolatress, and the adulteress shall be married by none but an adulterer or an idolater; and that is forbidden to the muminin.
24:4 And those who accuse the fortified nisa, then do not bring 4 witnesses: flog them with 80 lashes, and never accept their testimony again; and those are the fasiqun.
24:5 Except those who turned back after that and reformed — then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
24:6 And those who accuse their zawj and have no witnesses but themselves: the testimony of one of them is 4 testimonies by Allah that he is truly among the sadiqin.
24:7 And the 5th, that the curse of Allah be upon him if he is among the kazibin.
24:8 And the punishment shall be warded off from her by her testifying with 4 testimonies by Allah that he is among the kazibin.
24:9 And the 5th, that the wrath of Allah be upon her if he is among the sadiqin.
24:10 Were it not for Allah's favor upon you and Allah's mercy — and Allah is relenting, wise.
24:11 Truly those who brought the fabrication are a band of you; think it not an evil for you — rather it is good for you; for every person of them is what he earned of the sin, and those who took charge of the greatest part of it, his is a punishment most great.
24:12 Why did not the believing men and the believing nisa, when you heard it, assume good of themselves and say: this is a clear fabrication?
24:13 Why did they not bring against it 4 witnesses? So when they did not bring the witnesses, those are, in the sight of Allah, the kazibin.
24:14 Were it not for Allah's favor upon you and Allah's mercy, in the present world and the world to come, a most great punishment would have touched you over what you poured forth into.
24:15 When you received it with your tongues and said with your mouths what you had no knowledge of, and reckoned it slight, while in Allah's sight it was great.
24:16 Why did you not, when you heard it, say: it is not for us to speak of this; glory be to You — this is a stunning slander, great.
24:17 Allah admonishes you never to return to the like of it, if you are muminin.
24:18 And Allah makes clear for you the ayat; and Allah is knowing, wise.
24:19 Truly those who love that obscenity be spread among those who believe: for them is a painful punishment in the present world and the world to come; and Allah knows, and you know not.
24:20 Were it not for Allah's favor upon you and Allah's mercy — and Allah is compassionate, merciful.
24:21 O you who believe, do not follow the footsteps of Shaytan; and whoever follows the footsteps of Shaytan — he commands obscenity and the rejected; and were it not for Allah's favor upon you and Allah's mercy, not one of you would ever be purified, but Allah purifies whomever Allah wills; and Allah is all-hearing, knowing.
24:22 And let not those of bounty among you and of abundance withhold from giving to their relatives, and the needy, and those who emigrated in the way of Allah; and let them pardon and overlook — do you not love that Allah forgive you? And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
24:23 Truly those who accuse the fortified, unmindful, believing nisa: cursed are they in the present world and the world to come, and for them is a punishment most great.
24:24 On a day when their tongues and their hands and their feet will testify against them for what they used to do.
24:25 On that day Allah will pay them their due in full, and they will know that Allah is the clear truth.
24:26 The vile nisa belong to the vile men, and the vile men belong to the vile nisa; and the good nisa belong to the good men, and the good men belong to the good nisa; those are cleared of what they say; for them is forgiveness and a generous provision.
24:27 O you who believe, do not enter any house other than your own until you have asked leave and greeted the people of it; that is better for you, so that you may remember.
24:28 So if you find no one in it, do not enter it until leave is given to you; and if you are told to turn back, then turn back — that is purer for you; and Allah knows what you do.
24:29 There is no blame on you that you enter houses not lived in, in which there is provision for you; and Allah knows what you make manifest and what you hide.
24:30 Tell the believing men that they lower some of their sights and guard their openings; that is purer for them; truly Allah is all-aware of what they do.
24:31 And tell the believing nisa that they lower some of their sights and guard their openings, and that they not display their adornment except what appears of it, and that they draw their coverings over their chests, and that they not display their adornment except to their husbands, or their fathers, or the fathers of their husbands, or their sons, or the sons of their husbands, or their brothers, or the sons of their brothers, or the sons of their sisters, or their nisa, or those whom their right hands possess, or the attendants following them without desire among men, or the young children who have not yet become aware of nisa's nakedness; and let them not strike with their feet so that what they hide of their adornment may be known; and turn to Allah all together, O muminin, so that you may attain success.
24:32 And arrange the marriage of the single ones among you and the salihin among your servants male and maids; if they are poor, Allah will enrich them of Allah's bounty; and Allah is all-ample, knowing.
24:33 And let those who find no means of marriage keep chaste until Allah enriches them of Allah's bounty; and those who seek the contract from among those whom your right hands possess — write the contract with them if you know good in them, and give them from the wealth of Allah that was given to you; and do not compel your young nisa into prostitution if they desire fortification, seeking the goods of the worldly life; and whoever compels them — then Allah, after their being compelled, is forgiving, merciful.
24:34 And We have sent down to you clear ayat, and an example from those who passed on before you, and an admonition for the muttaqin.
24:35 Allah is the light of the heavens and the earth; the likeness of Allah's light is as a niche wherein is a lamp, the lamp in a glass, the glass as if a brilliant star — a lamp kindled from a blessed tree, an olive neither of the east nor of the west, whose oil nearly shines though no fire has touched it: light upon light; Allah guides whomever Allah wills to Allah's light; and Allah strikes the examples for humankind; and Allah has knowledge of all things.
24:36 In houses Allah permitted to be raised, and that Allah's name be remembered in them; glory be to Allah is proclaimed in them by morning and by evening.
24:37 Men whom neither commerce nor trade turns from the remembrance of Allah, and from establishing the prayer, and from giving the alms; they hold reverent awe of a day in which hearts and sights turn over.
24:38 So that Allah may requite them the best of what they did, and increase them from Allah's bounty; and Allah provides for whomever Allah wills without measure.
24:39 And those who disbelieve — their deeds are as a mirage in a lowland, which the thirsty one thinks water, until, when one comes to it, one finds nothing, and finds Allah there; so Allah pays the account in full; and Allah is swift in reckoning.
24:40 Or as darknesses in a deep sea, covered by a wave above which is a wave, above which is a cloud — darknesses, some over others; when one puts out one's hand, one nearly does not see it; and whoever Allah has not made a light for has no light.
24:41 Have you not seen that Allah is glorified by all who are in the heavens and the earth, and the birds ranked in rows? Each has known its prayer and its glorification; and Allah knows what they do.
24:42 And to Allah belongs the dominion of the heavens and the earth; and to Allah is the arrival.
24:43 Have you not seen that Allah drives clouds along, then joins them together, then makes them a mass, and you see the rain coming forth from within it? And Allah sends down from the heaven, from mountains within it, hail, and strikes with it whomever Allah wills, and turns it away from whomever Allah wills; the flash of its lightning nearly takes away the sights.
24:44 Allah turns the night and the day about; truly in that is a lesson for those endowed with sights.
24:45 And Allah created every creature from water; so among them are those who walk on their bellies, and among them are those who walk on 2 legs, and among them are those who walk on 4; Allah creates what Allah wills; truly Allah is powerful over all things.
24:46 We have sent down clear ayat; and Allah guides whomever Allah wills to a straight path.
24:47 And they say: we believe in Allah and the Rasul and we obey; then a party of them turns away after that; and those are not muminin.
24:48 And when they are called to Allah and the Rasul so that judgment be made between them, behold a party of them turns away.
24:49 And if the truth be on their side, they come to it willingly, submissive.
24:50 Is there a sickness in their hearts, or did they doubt, or do they fear that Allah and the Rasul should be zalimun to them? No, those are the zalimun.
24:51 The word of the muminin, when they are called to Allah and the Rasul so that judgment be made between them, is only that they say: we hear and we obey; and those are the ones who attain success.
24:52 And whoever obeys Allah and the Rasul, and holds reverent awe of Allah, and is mindful of Allah: those are the winners.
24:53 And they swore by Allah with the utmost of their oaths: if you order them they will surely go out; say: do not swear; known obedience; truly Allah is aware of what you do.
24:54 Say: obey Allah and obey the Rasul; so if they turn away, upon the Rasul is only the burden carried, and upon you is only the burden you carried; if you obey the Rasul you are guided; and upon the Rasul is only the clear delivery.
24:55 Allah has promised those among you who believe and do the salihin deeds: Allah will surely make them successors in the land as Allah made successors those before them, and will surely establish for them their religion that was approved for them, and will surely substitute for them, after their fear, security — they shall worship Me, not joining anything with Me; and whoever disbelieves after that, those are the fasiqun.
24:56 And establish the prayer and give the alms and obey the Rasul, so that you may be shown mercy.
24:57 Do not reckon those who disbelieve able to frustrate Allah in the land; and their refuge is the fire — and wretched is the arrival.
24:58 O you who believe, let those whom your right hands possess ask your leave, and those among you who have not reached maturity, 3 times: before the dawn prayer, and when you lay aside your garments at midday, and after the evening prayer; 3 times of nakedness for you; there is no blame on you and no blame on them after these; go about among one another as you please; thus Allah makes clear for you the ayat; and Allah is knowing, wise.
24:59 And when the children among you reach maturity, let them ask leave as leave was asked by those before them; thus Allah makes clear for you Allah's ayat; and Allah is knowing, wise.
24:60 And the nisa who stay seated, who do not hope for marriage: there is no blame on them that they lay aside their outer garments without displaying the adornment; and that they refrain is better for them; and Allah is all-hearing, knowing.
24:61 There is no restriction upon the blind, nor upon the lame, nor upon the sick, nor upon yourselves, that you eat from your houses, or the houses of your fathers, or the houses of your mothers, or the houses of your brothers, or the houses of your sisters, or the houses of your paternal uncles, or the houses of your paternal aunts, or the houses of your maternal uncles, or the houses of your maternal aunts, or what you hold the keys of, or your trusted friends; there is no blame on you that you eat together or separately; so when you enter houses, greet one another with a salutation from the presence of Allah, blessed and good; thus Allah makes clear for you the ayat, so that you may reason.
24:62 Truly the muminin are those who believe in Allah and the Rasul, and who, when they are with the Rasul upon a matter that gathers them, do not go away until they ask the Rasul's leave; truly those who ask your leave are those who believe in Allah and the Rasul; so when they ask your leave for some affair of theirs, give leave to whichever of them you will, and ask Allah's forgiveness for them; truly Allah is forgiving, merciful.
24:63 Do not make the calling of the Rasul among you like the calling of some of you to others; truly Allah knows those among you who slip away stealthily; so let those who differ from the Rasul's command beware, lest a trial befall them or a painful punishment strike them.
24:64 Unquestionably to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth; Allah has full knowledge of what you are upon; and on the day they are returned to Allah, Allah will inform them of all that they did; and Allah has knowledge of all things.
25:1 Blessed be the One who sent down the Criterion upon Allah's servant, that it may be a warner to the worlds
25:2 The One to whom belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth, who took no child and has no partner in the kingdom, and created everything, then measured it with a measure
25:3 Yet they have taken, besides Allah, alihah that create nothing while they themselves are created, that possess for themselves neither harm nor benefit, and possess neither death nor life nor any rising
25:4 The kafirin say: This is nothing but a fabrication he fabricated, and other people helped him with it. So they have come with wrong and swerving falsehood
25:5 And they say: Tales of the ancients — he had them written down, and they are dictated to him morning and evening
25:6 Say: Allah sent it down — the One who knows the secret in the heavens and the earth. Allah is ever Forgiving, Raheem
25:7 And they say: What is with this Rasul — he eats food and walks in the markets? Why has no malakat been sent down to him, to be a warner beside him?
25:8 Or that a hoard be cast to him, or that there be a garden for him to eat from. And the wrongdoers say: You follow only a man under a spell
25:9 See how they strike examples for you — they have gone astray and can find no way
25:10 Blessed be the One who, if Allah willed, would assign to you better than that: gardens beneath which rivers flow, and would assign you palaces
25:11 No — they denied the Hour, and We readied a blaze for whoever denies the Hour
25:12 When they see it from a distant place, they hear its raging and its roaring
25:13 And when they are cast, bound together, into a straitened place, they call there for destruction
25:14 Call not today for one destruction — call for many destructions
25:15 Say: Is that better, or the Garden of Eternity that the Raheem promised the muttaqin? It shall be for them a recompense and a final arrival
25:16 They shall have in it whatever they will, abiding forever. That was upon your Rabb a promise to be asked for
25:17 And the day We gather them and what they worship besides Allah, Allah will say: Did you lead these servants of Mine astray, or did they stray from the way?
25:18 They will say: Glory be to You! It was not fitting for us to take protectors besides You; but You let them and their fathers enjoy themselves until they forgot the Remembrance and became a ruined people
25:19 So they have already denied you in what you say, and you can neither avert anything nor find help. And whoever of you does wrong, We make that one taste a great punishment
25:20 We never sent before you any of the mursalin but that they ate food and walked in the markets, and We made some of you a trial for others. Will you be of the sabirin? Your Rabb is All-Seeing
25:21 Those who have no hope of meeting Us say: If only malakat were sent down to us, or we could see our Rabb! They have grown arrogant within themselves and waxed greatly insolent
25:22 The day they see the malakat — no glad tidings that day for the mujrimin, and they will say: A barrier, barred
25:23 We will proceed to what they did of deeds and make it scattered dust
25:24 The companions of the Garden that day — a better settling place and a finer resting place
25:25 The day the heaven splits open with the gloom-clouds and the malakat are sent down in a sending-down
25:26 True rule that day belongs to the Raheem, and it shall be a hard day for the kafirin
25:27 The day the wrongdoer bites his hands and says: If only I had taken a way with the Rasul
25:28 Woe is me! If only I had not taken so-and-so for a bosom friend
25:29 That one led me astray from the Reminder after it came to me, and Shaytan is ever a forsaker of humankind
25:30 The Rasul will say: O my Rabb, my people took this Recitation as a forsaken thing
25:31 Thus We made for every Nabi an enemy from among the mujrimin, and your Rabb suffices as Guide and Helper
25:32 The kafirin say: Why was the Recitation not sent down to him all at once, as a single whole? Thus — so that We may firm your fu'ad with it — and We arranged it in sequence
25:33 They never bring you an example but We bring you the truth and the finest interpretation
25:34 Those who are gathered on their faces toward Jahannam — those are the worst in place and the most astray in way
25:35 We gave Musa the Kitab and made his brother Harun a vizier beside him
25:36 We said: Go, both of you, to the people who denied Our ayat — then We razed them, a razing
25:37 And the people of Nuh, when they denied the Rasuls, We drowned them and made them an aya for humankind, and We readied a painful punishment for the wrongdoers
25:38 And Aad and Thamud, and the people of al-Rass, and many generations between those
25:39 Each of them We struck examples for, and each We utterly destroyed
25:40 They passed by the town that was rained the rain of evil — were they not seeing it? No, they had no hope of any rising
25:41 And when they see you they take you only in mockery: Is this the one Allah sent as a Rasul?
25:42 He nearly led us astray from our alihah, had we not been sabir toward them. And they will know when they see the punishment who was most astray in way
25:43 Have you seen the one who takes desire for a ilah? Will you be a guardian over that one?
25:44 Or do you reckon most of them hear or understand? They are only like cattle — rather, further astray in way
25:45 Have you not turned to your Rabb? See how Allah spread the shadow. Had Allah willed, it would be still; then We made the sun its pointer
25:46 Then We draw it in toward Us, an easy drawing-in
25:47 Allah is the One who made the night a garment for you, and sleep a rest, and made the day a rising
25:48 Allah is the One who sent the wind as glad tidings before Allah's mercy, and We sent down from the heaven purifying water
25:49 To give life by it to a dead town, and to give drink with it to much of what We created of livestock and people
25:50 We have turned it about among them so they might remember, yet most people refused, being nothing but kafirin
25:51 Had We willed, We would have raised a warner in every town
25:52 So do not obey the kafirin, and strive against them with it in a great striving
25:53 Allah is the One who let the two seas flow free — this one sweet and fresh, this one salty and bitter — and set between them a barrier and a barred partition
25:54 Allah is the One who created from water a human being, and made that one kin by lineage and kin by marriage, and your Rabb is fully capable
25:55 Yet they worship besides Allah what neither benefits nor harms them, and the kafir is a backer against their Rabb
25:56 We sent you only as a bearer of glad tidings and a warner
25:57 Say: I ask of you no wage for it, except that whoever wills may take a way to their Rabb
25:58 And trust in the Living One who does not die, and glorify with praise of Allah, and Allah suffices as All-Aware of the faults of the servants
25:59 The One who created the heavens and the earth and what is between them in six days, then settled upon the high seat — the Raheem — so ask concerning that one who is informed
25:60 And when it is said to them: Bow down to the Raheem, they say: And what is the Raheem? Shall we bow to whatever you command us? And it increased them in aversion
25:61 Blessed be the One who set in the heaven great towers, and set in it a lamp and a luminous moon
25:62 Allah is the One who made night and day follow in succession for whoever wills to remember or wills to be grateful
25:63 The servants of the Raheem are those who walk upon the earth lightly, and when the ignorant address them, they say: Peace
25:64 And those who pass the night before their Rabb, bowing and standing
25:65 Those who say: Our Rabb, avert from us the punishment of Jahannam — its punishment is a binding liability
25:66 Evil it is as a settling place and a dwelling
25:67 Those who, when they spend, neither exceed nor hold back, but hold to the just stand between the two
25:68 Those who call upon no other ilah with Allah, and kill not the nafs Allah has forbidden except by right, and commit not adultery. Whoever does that will meet a load of sin
25:69 The punishment is doubled for that one on the Day of Standing, and that one abides in it, humiliated
25:70 Except whoever turns back and believes and does a salih deed — those, Allah will change their evils into good deeds, and Allah is Forgiving, Raheem
25:71 And whoever turns back and does salihness — that one turns to Allah in a true turning
25:72 Those who do not witness falsehood, and when they pass by idle talk, they pass on with dignity
25:73 Those who, when reminded of their Rabb's ayat, do not fall upon them deaf and unseeing
25:74 Those who say: Our Rabb, grant us from our zawj and our offspring a comfort of the eyes, and make us leaders of the muttaqin
25:75 Those are recompensed the chambers above for their patience, and meet therein greeting and peace
25:76 Abiding therein — fair the settling place and good the dwelling
25:77 Say: My Rabb would have no care for you were it not for your calling. You have denied — so soon it will cling fast to you
26:1 Ta Sin Mim
26:2 These are the ayat of the clear Kitab
26:3 Perhaps you would kill yourself with grief that they will not be muminin
26:4 If We will, We shall send down upon them from the heaven an aya, and their necks will become humbled to it
26:5 And there comes to them no newly-arriving reminder from the Rahman but they turn away from it
26:6 So they have denied; and there will come to them tidings of what they used to mock
26:7 Have they not seen the earth, how much We have grown in it of every honored kind
26:8 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:9 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:10 And when your Rabb called Musa: Go to the wrongdoing people
26:11 The people of Fir'awn — will they not fear
26:12 He said: My Rabb, I fear that they will deny me
26:13 And my chest is straitened and my tongue will not set out, so send to Harun
26:14 And they hold a charge against me, so I fear they will kill me
26:15 Allah said: Nay — so go, both of you, with Our ayat; We are with you, hearing
26:16 So go, both of you, to Fir'awn and say: We are rusul of the Rabb of the worlds
26:17 That you send with us the children of Israel
26:18 Fir'awn said: Did we not raise you among us as a child, and you tarried among us years of your life-term
26:19 And you did your deed that you did, and you are of the unmuminin
26:20 Musa said: I did it when I was of the dallin
26:21 So I fled from you when I feared you; then my Rabb granted me judgment and made me one of the mursalin
26:22 And is that the favor you hold over me — that you enslaved the children of Israel
26:23 Fir'awn said: And what is the Rabb of the worlds
26:24 Musa said: The Rabb of the heavens and the earth and what is between them, if you are certain
26:25 Fir'awn said to those around him: Do you not hear
26:26 Musa said: Your Rabb and the Rabb of your ancient fathers
26:27 Fir'awn said: Your Rasul who was sent to you is surely a madman
26:28 Musa said: The Rabb of the east and the west and what is between them, if you reason
26:29 Fir'awn said: If you take a ilah other than me, I shall surely make you one of the imprisoned
26:30 Musa said: Even if I bring you something clear
26:31 Fir'awn said: Then bring it, if you are of the sadiqin
26:32 So he cast his staff, and there it was, a clear serpent
26:33 And he pulled out his hand, and there it was, white for the onlookers
26:34 Fir'awn said to the chiefs around him: This is surely a knowing sorcerer
26:35 He intends to drive you out of your land by his sorcery — so what do you order
26:36 They said: Postpone him and his brother, and send into the towns gatherers
26:37 Who will bring you every knowing sorcerer
26:38 So the sorcerers were gathered for the appointed time of a known day
26:39 And it was said to the people: Will you gather
26:40 So that we may follow the sorcerers, if they are the overcomers
26:41 So when the sorcerers came, they said to Fir'awn: Is there for us a reward if we are the overcomers
26:42 Fir'awn said: Yes — and you shall then be among the brought-near
26:43 Musa said to them: Cast whatever you are casting
26:44 So they cast their ropes and their staffs, saying: By the might of Fir'awn, we shall surely be the overcomers
26:45 So Musa cast his staff, and there it was, swallowing what they were fabricating
26:46 Then the sorcerers were thrown down, prostrating
26:47 They said: We believe in the Rabb of the worlds
26:48 The Rabb of Musa and Harun
26:49 Fir'awn said: You believed him before I gave you leave? He is your chief, the one who taught you sorcery! Soon you will know: I shall cut off your hands and your feet from behind, and I shall crucify you all together
26:50 They said: No harm — to our Rabb we are turning
26:51 We hope that our Rabb will forgive us our errors, that we were the first of the muminin
26:52 And We revealed to Musa: Set out by night with My servants — you will indeed be followed
26:53 So Fir'awn sent into the towns gatherers
26:54 These are a small band, few indeed
26:55 And they are surely a rage to us
26:56 And we are surely all on guard
26:57 So We brought them out of gardens and springs
26:58 And hoards, and a noble station
26:59 Thus — and We made the children of Israel inherit them
26:60 So they pursued them at sunrise
26:61 So when the two hosts came in sight of each other, the companions of Musa said: We are surely overtaken
26:62 Musa said: Nay! My Rabb is with me — Allah will guide me
26:63 So We revealed to Musa: Strike the sea with your staff. And it split open, and each part became like the great towering rock
26:64 And We brought the others near
26:65 And We delivered Musa and all who were with him
26:66 Then We drowned the others
26:67 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:68 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:69 And relate to them the account of Ibrahim
26:70 When he said to his father and his people: What do you serve
26:71 They said: We serve asnam, and we stay cleaving to them
26:72 He said: Do they hear you when you call
26:73 Or do they benefit you, or harm you
26:74 They said: Rather, we found our fathers doing likewise
