26:75 He said: Have you considered what you have been serving
26:76 You and your ancient fathers
26:77 Then they are an enemy to me, except the Rabb of the worlds
26:78 The One who created me, so Allah will guide me
26:79 And the One who feeds me and gives me drink
26:80 And when I am sick, Allah heals me
26:81 And the One who puts me to death, then brings me to life
26:82 And the One in whom I hope will forgive me my fault on the day of judgment
26:83 My Rabb, grant me judgment and join me with the salihin
26:84 And make for me a tongue of truth among the later ones
26:85 And make me one of the inheritors of the garden of bliss
26:86 And forgive my father — he was of the dallin
26:87 And do not disgrace me on the day they are raised
26:88 The day when neither wealth nor children avail
26:89 Except one who comes to Allah with a sound heart
26:90 And the garden is brought near to the ones who fear
26:91 And the blaze comes forth for the misled
26:92 And it is said to them: Where is what you used to serve
26:93 Apart from Allah? Will they help you, or help themselves
26:94 So they are hurled into it — they and the misled ones
26:95 And all the hosts of Iblis
26:96 Saying, while in it they contend
26:97 By Allah, we were surely in clear astray
26:98 When we made you equal with the Rabb of the worlds
26:99 And nothing misled us but the mujrimin
26:100 So we have no intercessors
26:101 And no devoted bosom-friend
26:102 If only we had a turn back, that we might be among the muminin
26:103 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:104 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:105 The people of Nuh denied the mursalin
26:106 When their brother Nuh said to them: Will you not fear
26:107 I am to you a Rasul, trustworthy
26:108 So fear Allah and obey me
26:109 And I ask you no reward for it — my reward is only upon the Rabb of the worlds
26:110 So fear Allah and obey me
26:111 They said: Shall we believe you, when the vilest ones follow you
26:112 He said: And what knowledge have I of what they used to do
26:113 Their reckoning is only upon my Rabb — if only you perceived
26:114 And I am not one to drive away the muminin
26:115 I am only a clear warner
26:116 They said: If you do not desist, Nuh, you shall be among the stoned ones
26:117 He said: My Rabb, my people have denied me
26:118 So decide between me and them with a decisive decision, and deliver me and those with me of the muminin
26:119 So We delivered him and those with him in the laden ship
26:120 Then We drowned the rest
26:121 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:122 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:123 Ad denied the mursalin
26:124 When their brother Hud said to them: Will you not fear
26:125 I am to you a Rasul, trustworthy
26:126 So fear Allah and obey me
26:127 And I ask you no reward for it — my reward is only upon the Rabb of the worlds
26:128 Do you build on every height an aya, amusing yourselves
26:129 And you take grand works, that perhaps you may live forever
26:130 And when you seize, you seize as overpowering ones
26:131 So fear Allah and obey me
26:132 And fear the One who supplied you with what you know
26:133 Supplied you with cattle and sons
26:134 And gardens and springs
26:135 I fear for you the punishment of a mighty day
26:136 They said: It is all one to us whether you admonish or are not among the admonishers
26:137 This is nothing but the creation of the ancients
26:138 And we shall not be punished
26:139 So they denied him, and We destroyed them. Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:140 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:141 Thamud denied the mursalin
26:142 When their brother Salih said to them: Will you not fear
26:143 I am to you a Rasul, trustworthy
26:144 So fear Allah and obey me
26:145 And I ask you no reward for it — my reward is only upon the Rabb of the worlds
26:146 Will you be left secure in what is here
26:147 In gardens and springs
26:148 And crops and palms whose spathes are tender
26:149 And you hew houses from the rocky landforms, skillfully
26:150 So fear Allah and obey me
26:151 And do not obey the command of the exceeders
26:152 Who corrupt in the earth and do not set right
26:153 They said: You are only one of the bewitched ones
26:154 You are only a human like us — so bring an aya, if you are of the sadiqin
26:155 He said: This is a naqah — she has her drinking-place and you have your drinking-place on a known day
26:156 And do not touch her with harm, lest the punishment of a mighty day seize you
26:157 So they cut her down, and in the morning they were regretters
26:158 So the punishment seized them. Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:159 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:160 The people of Lut denied the mursalin
26:161 When their brother Lut said to them: Will you not fear
26:162 I am to you a Rasul, trustworthy
26:163 So fear Allah and obey me
26:164 And I ask you no reward for it — my reward is only upon the Rabb of the worlds
26:165 Do you come to the males of the worlds
26:166 And leave what your Rabb created for you of your zawj? Nay, you are a transgressing people
26:167 They said: If you do not desist, Lut, you shall be among the driven-out ones
26:168 He said: I am indeed among those who detest your deed
26:169 My Rabb, deliver me and my family from what they do
26:170 So We delivered him and his family, all together
26:171 Except an jawza among the left-behind
26:172 Then We razed the others
26:173 And We rained upon them a rain — and evil was the rain of the warned ones
26:174 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:175 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:176 The people of al-Aykah denied the mursalin
26:177 When Shu'ayb said to them: Will you not fear
26:178 I am to you a Rasul, trustworthy
26:179 So fear Allah and obey me
26:180 And I ask you no reward for it — my reward is only upon the Rabb of the worlds
26:181 Give full measure and do not be among the khasirin
26:182 And weigh with the upright scale
26:183 And do not diminish the people their things, and do not ravage the earth, corrupting
26:184 And fear the One who created you and the ancient generations
26:185 They said: You are only one of the bewitched ones
26:186 And you are only a human like us — and we think you are among the kazibin
26:187 So make fragments of the heaven fall upon us, if you are of the sadiqin
26:188 He said: My Rabb knows best what you do
26:189 So they denied him, and the punishment of the day of the canopy seized them — it was the punishment of a mighty day
26:190 Indeed in that is an aya, and most of them were not muminin
26:191 And your Rabb is the Mighty, the Raheem
26:192 And it is surely a sending-down from the Rabb of the worlds
26:193 The trustworthy spirit brought it down
26:194 Upon your heart, that you may be among the warners
26:195 In a clear Arabic tongue
26:196 And it is surely in the Kitab of the ancients
26:197 Was it not an aya for them that the learned ones of the children of Israel knew it
26:198 And had We sent it down upon some of the non-Arabs
26:199 And he had recited it to them, they would not have believed in it
26:200 Thus We threaded it into the hearts of the mujrimin
26:201 They will not believe in it until they see the painful punishment
26:202 It will come to them suddenly, while they do not perceive
26:203 Then they say: Are we to be reprieved
26:204 Do they ask Our punishment to hasten
26:205 Have you seen: if We let them enjoy for years
26:206 Then there comes to them what they were promised
26:207 Of no avail to them is what they were given to enjoy
26:208 And We destroyed no town but that it had warners
26:209 A reminder — and We were not zalimun
26:210 And the devils did not bring it down
26:211 And it does not befit them, nor are they able
26:212 They are indeed cut off from the hearing
26:213 So do not call upon any other ilah with Allah, lest you be among the punished
26:214 And warn your kin, the nearest ones
26:215 And lower your wing to those who follow you of the muminin
26:216 So if they disobey you, say: I am clear of what you do
26:217 And rely upon the Mighty, the Raheem
26:218 The One who sees you when you stand
26:219 And your turning among those who prostrate
26:220 Allah is the Hearing, the Knowing
26:221 Shall I inform you of those upon whom the devils descend
26:222 They descend upon every fabricating sinner
26:223 They cast the hearing, and most of them are liars
26:224 And the poets — the misled ones follow them
26:225 Do you not see that they wander in every valley
26:226 And that they say what they do not do
26:227 Except those who believe and do salih deeds and remember Allah much, and vindicate themselves after they were wronged — and the wrongdoers will soon know to what turning-place they turn
27:1 Ta Sin. Those are the ayat of the Recitation and a clear Kitab.
27:2 Guidance and good news for the muminin.
27:3 Those who keep up the prayer and give the alms, and they of the hereafter are certain.
27:4 Those who do not believe in the hereafter — their deeds are adorned to them, so they wander blind.
27:5 Those are the ones for whom is the evil punishment, and they in the hereafter are the greatest losers.
27:6 You are indeed receiving the Recitation from the presence of a Wise, Knowing one.
27:7 When Musa said to his household: I have perceived a fire; I will bring you news from it, or bring you a flaming brand, so that you may warm yourselves.
27:8 When he came to it, a call came: Blessed is whoever is in the fire and whoever is around it; exalted is Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
27:9 O Musa, indeed it is I, Allah, the Mighty, the Wise.
27:10 Cast your staff. When he saw it quivering as if it were a serpent, he turned and fled and did not look back. O Musa, do not fear; with Me the mursalin do not fear.
27:11 Except one who has done wrong, then substituted good after evil — then I am All-Forgiving, Raheem.
27:12 Put your hand into your pocket; it will come out white without any evil — among 9 ayat to Fir'awn and his people. They were fasiqin.
27:13 When Our ayat came to them, clear to see, they said: This is clear sihr.
27:14 They denied them while their own selves were certain of them, out of wrong and arrogance. So see how was the end of the corrupters.
27:15 We gave Dawud and Sulayman knowledge, and they said: Praise belongs to Allah who favored us over many of Allah's believing servants.
27:16 Sulayman succeeded Dawud, and said: O people, we have been taught the speech of birds, and we have been given of everything. This is indeed the clear bounty.
27:17 Gathered for Sulayman were his hosts of jinn and humans and birds, and they were marshaled.
27:18 Until when they came upon the Valley of the Ants, an ant said: O ants, enter your dwellings, lest Sulayman and his hosts crush you without perceiving.
27:19 He smiled, laughing at her words, and said: My Rabb, inspire me to thank You for Your blessing which You blessed me and my parents with, and to do good that pleases You, and admit me by Your mercy among Your salih servants.
27:20 He reviewed the birds and said: Why do I not see the hoopoe? Is it among the absent?
27:21 I will surely punish it with a severe punishment, or slaughter it, or it must bring me a clear warrant.
27:22 It stayed not long, then said: I ahat what you did not tuhit, and I bring you from Saba certain news.
27:23 I found an imra'a ruling them, and she has been given of everything, and she has a mighty high-seat.
27:24 I found her and her people prostrating to the sun instead of Allah, and the devil has adorned their deeds to them, so he barred them from the way, and they are not guided.
27:25 Will they not prostrate to Allah, who brings out the hidden in the heavens and the earth, and knows what you conceal and what you declare?
27:26 Allah — there is no ilah but Allah — Rabb of the mighty high-seat.
27:27 He said: We shall see whether you have spoken the truth or are one of the kazibin.
27:28 Take this letter of mine, cast it to them, then turn away from them, and see what they return.
27:29 She said: O chiefs, a noble letter has been cast to me.
27:30 It is from Sulayman, and it reads: In the name of Allah, the Compassionate, the Raheem.
27:31 Do not rise against me, but come to me submitting.
27:32 She said: O chiefs, advise me in my affair; I was not one to decide an affair until you bear witness.
27:33 They said: We have strength and severe might, and the affair is yours; so see what you command.
27:34 She said: When kings enter a town they corrupt it and abase its honored people, and so they do.
27:35 I will send them a gift, and I will see what the mursalin return with.
27:36 When it came to Sulayman he said: Are you supplying me with wealth? What Allah has given me is better than what you gave. Rather, you rejoice in your gift.
27:37 Go back to them; We will come to them with hosts they cannot withstand, and We will bring them out of it, abased and belittled.
27:38 He said: O chiefs, which of you will bring me her high-seat before they come to me submitting?
27:39 A mighty one of the jinn said: I will bring it to you before you rise from your place; I am strong and trustworthy for it.
27:40 The one who had knowledge of the Kitabings said: I will bring it to you before your glance returns to you. And when he saw it settled before him, he said: This is from the bounty of my Rabb, to test me whether I am grateful or ungrateful. Whoever gives thanks does so only for his own self; and whoever is ungrateful — my Rabb is free of need and noble.
27:41 He said: Alter the look of her high-seat for her; we shall see whether she is guided or is one of those who are not guided.
27:42 When she came, it was said: Is this your high-seat? She said: It is as if it is it. We had been given knowledge before this, and we were submitters.
27:43 What she used to worship instead of Allah barred her; she was from a committing kufr people.
27:44 It was said to her: Enter the palace. When she saw it she thought it was a deep pool, and she uncovered her shins. He said: It is a palace paved with crystal-glass. She said: My Rabb, I have wronged myself, and I submit with Sulayman to Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
27:45 We sent to Thamud their brother Salih: Worship Allah. But they were two parties contending.
27:46 He said: My people, why do you hasten the evil before the good? Why do you not seek forgiveness of Allah, that you may be shown mercy?
27:47 They said: We augur ill of you and of those with you. He said: Your omen rests with Allah; rather, you are a people being tested.
27:48 There were in the town 9 men who corrupted in the land and did not set right.
27:49 They swore to one another by Allah: We will attack him and his household by night, then we will say to his next of kin: We did not witness the destruction of his household, and we are sadiq.
27:50 They planned a plan, and We planned a plan, while they did not perceive.
27:51 So see how was the end of their plan: We razed them and their people all together.
27:52 Those are their houses, collapsed, because of the wrong they did. In that is an aya for people who know.
27:53 We saved those who believed and were mindful.
27:54 And Lut, when he said to his people: Do you commit the obscenity while you see?
27:55 Do you indeed approach the males of the worlds and abandon the mates your Rabb created for you? Rather, you are an ignorant people.
27:56 The answer of his people was only to say: Drive out the family of Lut from your town; they are people who purify themselves.
27:57 We saved him and his family except his imra'a — We decreed her among those left behind.
27:58 We rained upon them a rain; evil was the rain of those who were warned.
27:59 Say: Praise belongs to Allah, and peace upon the servants whom Allah has chosen. Is Allah better, or those they associate?
27:60 Who created the heavens and the earth, and sent down for you water from the heaven, and with it We grew gardens of splendor whose trees you could not grow — is there a ilah with Allah? Rather, they are a people who set up equals.
27:61 Who made the earth a settled abode, and made rivers amidst it, and made anchors for it, and made between the two seas a barrier — is there a ilah with Allah? Rather, most of them do not know.
27:62 Who answers the one in distress when he calls, and uncovers the evil, and makes you successors on the earth — is there a ilah with Allah? Little do you remember.
27:63 Who guides you in the darknesses of the dry land and the sea, and who sends the wind as good news ahead of Allah's mercy — is there a ilah with Allah? Exalted is Allah above what they associate.
27:64 Who begins the creation then repeats it, and who provides you from the heaven and the earth — is there a ilah with Allah? Say: Bring your clear proof, if you are sadiq.
27:65 Say: None in the heavens and the earth knows the ghayb except Allah. They do not perceive when they will be raised.
27:66 Rather, their knowledge overtakes them in the hereafter; rather, they are in doubt about it; rather, they are unperceiving of it.
27:67 Those who disbelieve say: When we have become dust, and our fathers, shall we indeed be brought out?
27:68 We and our fathers were promised this before. This is nothing but tales of the ancients.
27:69 Say: Journey through the earth, and see how was the end of the mujrimin.
27:70 Do not grieve over them, and do not be in straitness over what they plan.
27:71 They say: When is this promise, if you are sadiq?
27:72 Say: Perhaps some of what you hasten is close behind you.
27:73 Your Rabb is full of bounty upon people, but most of them are not grateful.
27:74 Your Rabb knows what their chests conceal and what they declare.
27:75 Nothing absent is in the heaven and the earth but it is in a clear record.
27:76 This Recitation narrates to the children of Israel most of what they differ about.
27:77 It is guidance and mercy for the muminin.
27:78 Your Rabb will judge between them by Allah's judgment; Allah is the Mighty, the Knowing.
27:79 So trust in Allah; you are upon the clear truth.
27:80 You do not make the dead hear, nor do you make the deaf hear the call when they turn their backs.
27:81 You cannot guide the blind out of their straying; you make none hear except whoever believes in Our ayat, and they are submitters.
27:82 When the word befalls them, We will bring out for them a creature from the earth speaking to them that the people had no certainty in Our ayat.
27:83 The day We gather from every people a company of those who denied Our ayat, and they are marshaled.
27:84 Until when they come, Allah will say: Did you deny My ayat without having tuhitu them in knowledge? What was it that you were doing?
27:85 The word befalls them for the wrong they did, and they do not speak.
27:86 Do they not see that We made the night for them to rest in, and the day for seeing? In that are ayat for people who believe.
27:87 The day the trumpet is blown, and all who are in the heavens and the earth are seized with dread except whom Allah wills; and all come to Allah humbled.
27:88 You see the mountains, thinking them motionless, while they pass with the passing of the clouds — the doing of Allah, who perfected everything. Allah is fully aware of what you do.
27:89 Whoever comes with the good will have better than it, and they that day are secure from dread.
27:90 Whoever comes with the evil, their faces are thrust into the fire. Are you recompensed except for what you used to do?
27:91 I was commanded only to serve the Rabb of this town, who made it sacred; to Allah belongs everything. And I was commanded to be one of the submitters.
27:92 And to recite the Recitation. Whoever is guided is guided only for himself; and whoever goes astray — say: I am only one of the warners.
27:93 And say: Praise belongs to Allah. Allah will show you Allah's ayat, and you will recognize them. Your Rabb is not unmindful of what you do.
28:1 Ta Sin Mim
28:2 Those are the ayat of the clear Kitab.
28:3 We recite to you from the account of Musa and Fir'awn in truth, for a people who believe.
28:4 Fir'awn exalted himself in the land and made its people parties, subjecting a party of them, slaughtering their sons and letting their nisa live; he was indeed among the corrupters.
28:5 And We wanted to favor those who were subjected in the land, and to make them leaders, and make them the heirs,
28:6 and to establish them in the land, and to show Fir'awn and Haman and their hosts, from them, that which they used to beware.
28:7 And We inspired Musa's mother: nurse him; then, when you fear for him, cast him into the sea, and do not fear and do not grieve; We will return him to you and make him among the mursalin.
28:8 So the house of Fir'awn picked him up, to be for them an enemy and a grief; Fir'awn and Haman and their hosts were indeed erring.
28:9 And Fir'awn's imra'a said: a delight to my eye and yours; do not kill him; perhaps he may benefit us, or we may take him as a son; and they did not perceive.
28:10 And the heart of Musa's mother became empty; she had nearly disclosed him, had We not bound her heart, so that she would be among the muminin.
28:11 She said to his sister: track him; so she watched him from the side, while they did not perceive.
28:12 And We barred him from the wet-nurses before; so she said: shall I point you to a household that will undertake him for you, and they are sincere to him?
28:13 So We returned him to his mother, that her eye might be comforted and she might not grieve, and that she might know Allah's promise is true; but most of them do not know.
28:14 And when he reached his full strength and was settled, We gave him judgment and knowledge; and thus We repay the muhsinin.
28:15 And he entered the town at a time of unmindfulness of its people, and found in it two men fighting — one of his party and one of his enemy; so the one of his party called him for help against the one of his enemy, and Musa struck him and finished him. He said: this is of the work of Shaytan; he is an enemy, clearly misguiding.
28:16 He said: my Rabb, I have wronged myself, so forgive me. So Allah forgave him; indeed, Allah alone is the Forgiving, the Raheem.
28:17 He said: my Rabb, for what You have bestowed on me, I will never be a backer of the guilty.
28:18 So he was in the town come morning, fearful, watching; and the one who had sought his help yesterday was crying out to him for help. Musa said to him: you are clearly misled.
28:19 So when he wanted to seize with might the one who was an enemy to both of them, he said: Musa, do you want to kill me as you killed a nafs yesterday? You only want to be an overpowering one in the land, and you do not want to be among those who set things right.
28:20 And a man came from the far end of the town, hastening; he said: Musa, the chiefs are conferring about you to kill you, so leave; I am among the nasihin to you.
28:21 So he left it, fearful, watching; he said: my Rabb, deliver me from the wrongdoing people.
28:22 And when he set out toward Madyan, he said: perhaps my Rabb will guide me to the straight way.
28:23 And when he arrived at the water of Madyan, he found at it a group of people giving drink, and found apart from them two nisa holding back. He said: what is your affair? They said: we do not give drink until the keepers depart, and our father is an old man.
28:24 So he gave drink to the two, then turned aside to the shade and said: my Rabb, whatever good You send down to me, I am in need.
28:25 Then one of the two came to him, walking shyly; she said: my father invites you, to repay you the reward for what you gave drink to us. So when he came to him and narrated to him the account, he said: do not fear; you have been delivered from the wrongdoing people.
28:26 One of the two said: father, hire him; the best you can hire is the strong, the trustworthy.
28:27 He said: I want to marry you to one of these two daughters of mine, on condition that you hire me for 8 pilgrimages; and if you complete 10, it is from your own favor. And I do not want to be hard on you; you will find me, Allah willing, among the salihin.
28:28 He said: that is between me and you; whichever of the two terms I complete, there is no wrong against me, and Allah is trustee over what we say.
28:29 And when Musa completed the term and journeyed on with his family, he saw from the side of the Mount a fire; he said to his family: stay, I have seen a fire; perhaps I will bring you news from it, or a brand from the fire, so that you may warm yourselves.
28:30 And when he came to it, he was called from the bank of the valley, on the right, in the blessed spot, from the tree: O Musa, I am Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
28:31 And: cast your staff. So when he saw it shaking as if it were a serpent, he turned away fleeing and did not turn back. O Musa, come forward and do not fear; you are among the secure.
28:32 Thread your hand into your pocket: it will come out white, without any blemish; and press your wing to yourself, in awe. Those are two clear proofs from your Rabb to Fir'awn and his assembly; they were fasiqin.
28:33 He said: my Rabb, I killed a nafs among them, so I fear that they will kill me.
28:34 And my brother Harun is more eloquent of tongue than I; so send him with me as a backer, to confirm me; I fear that they will give me the lie.
28:35 Allah said: We will strengthen your arm with your brother, and give you two a warrant, so they will not reach you two. With our ayat, you two and whoever follows you are the overcomers.
28:36 And when Musa came to them with our clear ayat, they said: this is nothing but fabricated sihr, and we have not heard of this among our fathers of old.
28:37 And Musa said: my Rabb knows best who came with the guidance from Allah, and whose will be the final end of the abode; the wrongdoers do not attain success.
28:38 And Fir'awn said: O chiefs, I know of no ilah for you other than me; so kindle for me, O Haman, over the clay, and make for me a tower, so that I may climb up to the ilah of Musa — I think he is among the kazibin.
28:39 And he and his hosts grew arrogant in the land without right, and they supposed that they would not be returned to us.
28:40 So We seized him and his hosts, and cast them into the sea; so look how was the final fate of the wrongdoers.
28:41 And We made them leaders calling to the fire; and on the Day of Standing they will not be helped.
28:42 And We made a curse follow them in this nearer life; and on the Day of Standing they are among the despised.
28:43 And We surely gave Musa the Kitab, after We destroyed the former generations — insights for the people, and guidance and a mercy, so that they might remember.
28:44 And you were not by the side of the western slope when We decreed to Musa the affair, and you were not among the witnesses.
28:45 But We raised up generations, and the life-term was prolonged over them; and you were not lodging among the people of Madyan, reciting to them our ayat, but We were the senders.
28:46 And you were not by the side of the Mount when We called; but it is a mercy from your Rabb, that you may warn a people to whom no warner came before you, so that they might remember.
28:47 And lest an affliction strike them for what their hands have sent forward, and they say: our Rabb, why did You not send us a Rasul, so that we would follow your ayat and be among the muminin?
28:48 So when the truth came to them from us, they said: why was he not given the like of what Musa was given? Did they not disbelieve in what was given to Musa before? They said: two sihrs backing each other; and they said: we are committing kufr in everything.
28:49 Say: then bring a Kitab from Allah, more guiding than these two, and I will follow it, if you are sadiq.
28:50 So if they do not answer you, then know that they only follow their desires; and who is more misled than the one who follows their desire without guidance from Allah? Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.
28:51 And We surely joined the word for them, so that they might remember.
28:52 Those to whom We gave the Kitab before it — they believe in it.
28:53 And when it is recited to them, they say: we believe in it; it is the truth from our Rabb; we were, before it, submitted.
28:54 Those will be given their reward twice, for what they sabirly bore; and they ward off the bad with the good, and from what We provided them they spend.
28:55 And when they hear idle talk, they turn aside from it and say: our deeds are for us and your deeds are for you; peace be upon you — we do not seek the ignorant.
28:56 You do not guide whom you love; but Allah guides whom Allah wills, and Allah knows best the guided ones.
28:57 And they say: if we follow the guidance with you, we will be snatched from our land. Did We not establish for them a secure sanctuary, to which are gathered the fruits of everything, as provision from our presence? But most of them do not know.
28:58 And how many a town We destroyed that exulted in its living; so those are their dwellings — hardly dwelt in after them — and We were the inheritors.
28:59 And your Rabb would never destroy the towns until raising in their mother-town a Rasul, reciting to them our ayat; and We were never destroyers of the towns unless their people were wrongdoers.
28:60 And whatever you are given of anything is the enjoyment of the nearer life and its adornment; and what is with Allah is better and more lasting; will you not reason?
28:61 So is the one We promised a good promise — and they will meet it — like the one We let enjoy the enjoyment of the nearer life, and then they, on the Day of Standing, are among the brought-before?
28:62 And on the day they are called — where are the associates of Allah that you used to claim?
28:63 Those on whom the word holds true said: our Rabb, these are the ones we misled; we misled them as we were misled. We disavow them before you; they were not worshipping us.
28:64 And it was said: call your associates; so they called them, but they did not answer them, and they saw the chastisement — if only they had been guided.
28:65 And on the day they are called — what did you answer the mursalin?
28:66 So the tidings were unperceived to them on that day, and they will not ask one another.
28:67 As for the one who repented and believed and did salih work — then perhaps they will be among the muflihin.
28:68 And your Rabb creates what Allah wills and chooses; the choice was not theirs. Glory be to Allah, exalted above what they associate.
28:69 And your Rabb knows what their chests conceal and what they declare openly.
28:70 And Allah — there is no ilah but Allah; to Allah belongs the praise in the first and the last, and to Allah belongs the judgment, and to Allah you are returned.
28:71 Say: have you considered — if Allah made the night unceasing over you until the Day of Standing, what ilah other than Allah would bring you light? Will you not hear?
28:72 Say: have you considered — if Allah made the day unceasing over you until the Day of Standing, what ilah other than Allah would bring you night in which you rest? Will you not see?
28:73 And of Allah's mercy, Allah made for you the night and the day, that you may rest in it and seek of Allah's bounty, and so that you may give thanks.
28:74 And on the day they are called — where are the associates of Allah that you used to claim?
28:75 And We will pull out from every community a witness, and We will say: produce your clear proof; then they will know that the truth belongs to Allah, and lost from them will be what they used to fabricate.
28:76 Qarun was of the people of Musa, and he wronged them; and We gave him from the hoards such that their keys would strain a band of the mighty. When his people said to him: do not rejoice; Allah does not love the rejoicing ones.
28:77 And seek, in what Allah has given you, the last abode; and do not forget your allotted portion of the nearer life; and do good as Allah did good to you; and do not seek corruption in the land — Allah does not love the corrupters.
28:78 He said: I was only given it on account of knowledge I have. Did he not know that Allah destroyed before him, among the generations, ones stronger than him in strength and greater in gathering? And the guilty are not asked about their offenses.
28:79 So he went out to his people in his adornment; those who desire the nearer life said: would that we had the like of what Qarun was given — he is indeed one of mighty portion.
28:80 And those given the knowledge said: woe to you; Allah's recompense is better for the one who believes and does salih work — and none meets it but the sabirin ones.
28:81 So We sank the earth with him and with his dwelling; and he had no band to help him apart from Allah, and he was not among the helped ones.
28:82 And come morning, those who had wished for his place yesterday were saying: it is as if Allah spreads the provision for whom Allah wills among the servants, and straitens; if Allah had not shown us favor, Allah would have sunk us too; it is as if the kafirin do not attain success.
28:83 That is the last abode; We make it for those who do not desire exaltation in the land nor corruption; and the final end is for the muttaqin ones.
28:84 Whoever comes with the good, theirs is better than it; and whoever comes with the bad — the doers of the bad deeds are only repaid what they used to do.
28:85 Indeed, the one who ordained the Recitation upon you will surely return you to the place of return. Say: my Rabb knows best who came with the guidance, and who is in clear error.
28:86 And you were not hoping that the Kitab would be cast to you, except as a mercy from your Rabb; so never be a backer of the kafirin.
28:87 And let them not bar you from the ayat of Allah after they were sent down to you; and call to your Rabb, and never be among the associaters.
28:88 And do not call, along with Allah, any other ilah; there is no ilah but Allah; everything perishes except the face of Allah; to Allah belongs the judgment, and to Allah you are returned.
29:1 Alif Lam Mim
29:2 Do people think that they will be left alone just by saying "We believe," while they will not be tried?
29:3 We have surely tried those before them, so Allah will surely know those who were sadiq and will surely know the kazibin.
29:4 Or do those who do evil deeds think they will outstrip Us? Evil is what they decree.
29:5 Whoever hopes to meet Allah — the term of Allah is surely coming, and Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
29:6 Whoever strives, strives only for his own nafs; Allah is free of need of the worlds.
29:7 Those who believe and do salih deeds — We will surely absolve them of their evil deeds, and We will surely reward them with the best of what they used to do.
29:8 We charged the human with good toward the two parents; but if they both strive against you to make you associate with Allah that of which you have no knowledge, do not obey them. To Allah is your return, and Allah will tell you what you used to do.
29:9 Those who believe and do salih deeds — We will surely enter them among the salihin.
29:10 Among the people is one who says "We believe in Allah," but when harmed in Allah's cause, he regards the trial of people as the punishment of Allah. Yet if help comes from your Rabb, he says: "We were with you." Is not Allah best aware of what is in the breasts of the worlds?
29:11 Allah will surely know those who believe, and Allah will surely know the munafiqin.
29:12 Those who disbelieve say to those who believe: "Follow our way, and we will bear your errors." Yet they bear nothing of their errors at all — truly they are liars.
29:13 They will surely bear their own weights, and weights along with their weights; and they will be asked on the Day of Resurrection about what they used to fabricate.
29:14 We sent Nuh to his people, and he tarried among them 1,000 years less 50 years; then the deluge seized them while they were wrongdoers.
29:15 We saved him and the companions of the ship, and We made it an aya for the worlds.
29:16 And Ibrahim, when he said to his people: "Worship Allah and be mindful of Allah; that is best for you, if you know."
29:17 You worship only idols besides Allah, and you invent a fabrication. Those you worship besides Allah possess no provision for you — so seek provision from Allah, worship Allah, and be grateful to Allah; to Allah you return.
29:18 If you deny, then nations before you denied; and the Rasul's duty is only the clear delivery.
29:19 Have they not seen how Allah begins the creation, then repeats it? That is easy for Allah.
29:20 Say: "Journey through the earth and look at how the creation began; then Allah will raise up the final raising. Allah has power over everything."
29:21 Allah punishes whom Allah wills and shows mercy to whom Allah wills, and to Allah you are turned.
29:22 There is no escaping for you on earth or in heaven, and you have, besides Allah, no protector and no helper.
29:23 Those who disbelieve in the ayat of Allah and in meeting with Allah — those have despaired of Allah's mercy, and for them is a painful punishment.
29:24 The answer of his people was only that they said: "Kill him or burn him." So Allah saved him from the fire. In that are ayat for people who believe.
29:25 He said: "You have taken idols besides Allah for affection among you in the life of this world. Then on the Day of Resurrection you will disown one another, and you will curse one another. Your destination is the Fire, and you have no helpers."
29:26 And Lut believed in him and said: "I am emigrating to my Rabb; indeed Allah is the Mighty, the Wise."
29:27 We granted him Ishaq and Yaqub, and placed among his descendants Nabihood and the Kitab, and gave him his reward in this world; and in the later world he is surely among the salihin.
29:28 And Lut, when he said to his people: "You commit the obscenity that no one in the worlds has done before you."
29:29 Do you come to men, cut off the way, and commit the dreadful in your gatherings? The answer of his people was only that they said: "Bring us the punishment of Allah, if you are among the sadiqin."
29:30 He said: "My Rabb, help me against the people who do corruption."
29:31 And when Our malakat came to Ibrahim with the good news, they said: "We are to destroy the people of this town, for its people were wrongdoers."
29:32 He said: "Yet Lut is in it." They said: "We know best who is in it. We will surely save him and his people, except his imra'a — she was among those left behind."
29:33 And when Our malakat came to Lut, he was distressed on their account and felt straitened for room. They said: "Do not fear and do not grieve; We will save you and your people, except your imra'a — she was among the ghābirīn."
29:34 We are sending down upon the people of this town an affliction from the heaven, for what they used to yafsuquna.
29:35 We have surely left from it a clear aya for people who reason.
29:36 And to Madyan, their brother Shu'ayb; he said: "O my people, worship Allah, hope for the Last Day, and do not ravage the earth as corrupters."
29:37 They denied him, so the quake seized them, and they became sprawled in their home.
29:38 And Aad and Thamud — and it has been made clear to you from their dwellings. The devil adorned their deeds for them and barred them from the way, though they were perceptive.
29:39 And Qarun and Fir'awn and Haman — Musa came to them with the clear proofs, but they grew arrogant in the earth, and they were not ahead.
29:40 Each We seized for its offense: among them are those upon whom We sent a burning storm, and among them are those whom the shout seized, and among them are those whom We caused the earth to swallow, and among them are those whom We drowned. Allah would not wrong them, but they were wronging themselves.
29:41 The likeness of those who took protectors besides Allah is the likeness of the spider that took a house. The weakest of houses is the spider's house — if they knew.
29:42 Allah knows whatever they call upon besides Allah — anything at all — and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
29:43 These are the likenesses We strike for people, and none reason them out except those who know.
29:44 Allah created the heavens and the earth in truth. In that is an aya for the muminin.
29:45 Recite what was revealed to you of the Kitab, and establish the prayer. The prayer restrains from obscenity and the dreadful, and the remembrance of Allah is greatest. Allah knows what you do.
29:46 Do not dispute with the people of the Kitab except in the best way, except for those of them who wronged. And say: "We believe in what was revealed to us and what was revealed to you. Our ilah and your ilah is One, and to Allah we submit."
29:47 And thus We revealed the Kitab to you. Those to whom We gave the Kitab believe in it, and among these are some who believe in it; and none disbelieve Our ayat except the kafirin.
29:48 You did not recite any Kitab before it, nor did you trace it with your right hand — otherwise the voiders would have doubted.
29:49 Rather, it is clear ayat in the breasts of those who were given knowledge, and none deny Our ayat except the wrongdoers.
29:50 They say: "Why are no ayat sent down to him from his Rabb?" Say: "The ayat are only with Allah, and I am only a clear warner."
29:51 Is it not sufficient for them that We revealed to you the Kitab, recited to them? In that is a mercy and a reminder for people who believe.
29:52 Say: "Allah is sufficient as a witness between me and you. Allah knows what is in the heavens and the earth. Those who believe in falsehood and disbelieve in Allah — those are the khasirin."
29:53 They ask you to hasten the punishment; were it not for an appointed term, the punishment would have come to them — and it will surely come to them suddenly while they do not perceive.
29:54 They hasten you for the punishment, and Jahannam indeed is muhit over the kafirin.
29:55 On the day the punishment covers them from above them and from beneath their feet, and it says: "Taste what you used to do."
29:56 O servants of Allah who believe, the earth of Allah is spacious — so worship Allah alone.
29:57 Every nafs tastes death; then to Us you return.
29:58 Those who believe and do salih deeds — We will surely settle them in the Garden in upper chambers, with rivers flowing beneath them, abiding therein forever. An excellent reward for the doers.
29:59 Those who are sabir and rely upon their Rabb.
29:60 How many a creature there is that does not carry its provision — Allah provides for it and for you; Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing.
29:61 If you ask them who created the heavens and the earth and subjected the sun and the moon, they will surely say: "Allah." So how are they turned away?
29:62 Allah spreads out provision for whom Allah wills among the servants, and restricts it; indeed Allah knows everything.
29:63 If you ask them who sends down water from the heaven and revives the earth with it after its death, they will surely say: "Allah." Say: "Praise belongs to Allah." Yet most of them do not reason.
29:64 This worldly life is nothing but diversion and play, and the later dwelling is the true life — if only they knew.
29:65 When they ride in the ship, they call upon Allah, devoting religion to Allah alone; but when Allah saves them to dry land, they associate partners.
29:66 To disbelieve in what We gave them and to enjoy themselves — soon they will know.
29:67 Have they not seen that We have made a sanctuary secure, while people are snatched away all around them? Do they believe in falsehood and disbelieve in the blessing of Allah?
29:68 Who is more wrong than one who fabricates a lie against Allah, or disbelieves the truth when it comes to him? Is Jahannam not a lodging for the kafirin?
29:69 Those who strive for Us — We will surely guide them on Our ways, and Allah is surely with the muhsinin.
30:1 Alif Lam Mim
30:2 The Romans have been overcome
30:3 In the nearest land, and they, after their overcoming, will overcome
30:4 Within a few years; to Allah belongs the command, before and after; and that day the muminin will rejoice
30:5 In the help of Allah; Allah helps whom Allah wills, and Allah is the Mighty in Power, the Raheem
30:6 Allah's promise; Allah does not fail the promise, but most people do not know
30:7 They know the surface of the lower life, and of the last they are unmindful
30:8 Have they not reflected within themselves? Allah did not create the heavens and the earth and what is between them except in truth and for an appointed term; yet many of the people are kafirin in the meeting of their Rabb
30:9 Have they not journeyed in the land and seen how was the end of those before them? They were greater in strength than them, and they stirred up the land and built it up more than these built it, and their envoys came to them with clear proofs; Allah was not to wrong them, but they wronged themselves
30:10 Then the end of those who did the worst was that they denied the ayat of Allah and mocked them
30:11 Allah begins the creation, then repeats it; then to Allah you return
30:12 And the day the Hour arises, the mujrimin will be despair-struck
30:13 And there will be none from their associates to intercede for them, and they had been kafirin in their associates
30:14 And the day the Hour arises, that day they part
30:15 Then as for those who believed and did good deeds, they are in a garden, delighting
30:16 And as for those who disbelieved and denied our ayat and the meeting of the last, those are arraigned in the punishment
30:17 So glory be to Allah when you enter evening and when you enter morning
30:18 And to Allah belongs praise in the heavens and the earth, and at evening, and when you come forth at midday
30:19 Allah brings out the living from the dead and brings out the dead from the living, and revives the earth after its death; and thus you are brought out
30:20 And among the ayat: that Allah created you from dust, then suddenly you are humans spreading abroad
30:21 And among the ayat: that Allah made for you mates from yourselves that you may find rest in them, and placed between you affection and mercy; in that are ayat for a people who reflect
30:22 And among the ayat: the creation of the heavens and the earth, and the difference of your tongues and your colors; in that is an aya for those who know
30:23 And among the ayat: your sleep by night and by day, and your seeking of the bounty; in that are ayat for a people who hear
30:24 And among the ayat: Allah shows you lightning, for fear and for hope, and sends down from the heaven water, and revives the earth after its death; in that are ayat for a people who understand
30:25 And among the ayat: that the heaven and the earth stand by Allah's command; then when Allah calls you with a call from the earth, suddenly you come out
30:26 And to Allah belongs whoever is in the heavens and the earth; all are devout to Allah
30:27 And Allah is the one who begins the creation, then repeats it, and that is lighter upon Allah; and the highest likeness in the heavens and the earth belongs to Allah, and Allah is the Mighty in Power, the Wise
30:28 Allah strikes for you a parable from yourselves: do you have, from what your right hands possess, any partners in what we provided you, so that you are equal in it, fearing them as you fear your own selves? Thus we spell out the ayat for a people who understand
30:29 Rather, those who wronged followed their desires without knowledge; who guides one whom Allah has led astray? And there are no helpers for them
30:30 So set your face to the religion, upright; it is the origination of Allah upon which Allah originated humankind; there is no change in the creation of Allah; that is the upright religion, but most people do not know
30:31 Turning back to Allah; be mindful of Allah, establish the prayer, and do not be among those who associate partners
30:32 Those who divided their religion and became parties, each party rejoicing in what they have
30:33 And when harm touches people they call their Rabb, turning back to Allah; then when Allah lets them taste from it mercy, suddenly a group of them associate partners with their Rabb
30:34 To disbelieve in what we gave them; so enjoy, and soon you will know
30:35 Or did we send down to them a warrant that speaks of what they used to associate?
30:36 And when we let people taste mercy they rejoice in it; and if an evil strikes them for what their hands sent ahead, suddenly they despair
30:37 Do they not see that Allah spreads provision for whom Allah wills and measures it? In that are ayat for a people who believe
30:38 So give the near-relative the due, and the needy, and the wayfarer; that is better for those who want the face of Allah, and those are the ones who attain success
30:39 And what you give of usury to grow in people's wealth grows not with Allah; and what you give of growth-dues wanting the face of Allah, those are the ones for whom it is multiplied
30:40 Allah is the one who created you, then provided you, then gives you death, then gives you life; is there among your associates any who does anything of that? Glory be to Allah, exalted above what they associate
30:41 Corruption has appeared on the land and the sea for what people's hands have earned, to let them taste some of what they did, so that they may return
30:42 Say: journey in the land and see how was the end of those before; most of them were those who associate partners
30:43 So set your face to the upright religion before a day comes from Allah for which there is no warding off; that day they are split apart
30:44 Whoever disbelieves, the disbelief is upon them; and whoever does good, they prepare smooth ground for themselves
30:45 Allah rewards those who believed and did good deeds from the bounty; Allah does not love the kafirin
30:46 And among the ayat: that Allah sends the winds bearing glad tidings, to let you taste of the mercy, and so ships sail by Allah's command, and so you seek of the bounty, so that you give thanks
30:47 And we sent before you envoys to their people, and they came to them with clear proofs; then we requited those who committed crimes, and helping the muminin was a right due upon us
30:48 Allah is the one who sends the wind, and it stirs up clouds, and Allah spreads them in the heaven as Allah wills, and makes them fragments, and you see the rain coming out from within them; then when with it Allah strikes whom Allah wills of the servants, suddenly they rejoice
30:49 Though before it was sent down upon them they had been despair-struck
30:50 So look to the vestiges of Allah's mercy, how Allah revives the earth after its death; that is a reviver of the dead, and Allah is powerful over everything
30:51 And if we send a wind and they see it yellowed, they persist after it in disbelief
30:52 You cannot make the dead hear, nor can you make the deaf hear the call when they turn their backs
30:53 You do not guide the unseeing out of their straying; you make none hear except those who believe in our ayat, and those are the ones who submit
30:54 Allah is the one who created you from weakness, then made, after weakness, strength, then made, after strength, weakness and grayness; Allah creates what Allah wills, and Allah is the Knowing, the Powerful
30:55 And the day the Hour arises the mujrimin swear they tarried only an hour; thus they were turned away
30:56 And those given knowledge and faith say: you tarried in the decree of Allah until the day of raising; this is the day of raising, but you did not know
30:57 That day the excuses of those who wronged benefit them not, nor are they allowed to make amends
30:58 We have struck for people in this recitation every parable; and if an aya comes to them, those who disbelieve will surely say: you are nothing but voiders
30:59 Thus Allah sets a seal on the hearts of those who do not know
30:60 So be steadfast; the promise of Allah is true, and let not those who have no certainty take you lightly
31:1 Alif Lam Mim.
31:2 These are the ayat of the wise Kitab.
31:3 A guidance and a mercy for the muhsinin.
31:4 Those who keep up the prayer and give the alms, and it is the Hereafter that they are certain of.
31:5 Those are upon guidance from their Rabb, and those are the ones who attain success.
31:6 And among people are those who buy diversionary hadith to lead astray from the path of Allah without knowledge, and take it in mockery; for those there is a humiliating punishment.
31:7 And when Our ayat are recited to him, he turns away disdainfully, as though he had not heard them, as though there were a heaviness in his ears; so give him tidings of a painful punishment.
31:8 Indeed, those who believe and do good works — for them are gardens of bliss.
31:9 Abiding therein forever; that is Allah's promise in truth, and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
31:10 Allah created the heavens without any pillars that you see, and cast into the earth firm anchors so it would not sway with you, and scattered in it every kind of creature; and We sent down water from the heaven and grew in it every noble kind.
31:11 This is Allah's creation; so show Me what those other than Allah have created. Rather, the wrongdoers are in clear error.
31:12 And We gave Luqman wisdom: "Be grateful to Allah." Whoever is grateful is grateful only for the good of his own nafs, and whoever is ungrateful — indeed, Allah is Self-Sufficient, Praised.
31:13 And when Luqman said to his son, admonishing him: "My son, do not associate others with Allah; indeed, to associate others with Allah is a tremendous wrong."
31:14 And We charged the human being concerning his parents: his mother carried him in weakness upon weakness, and his weaning came at two years: "Be grateful to Me and to your parents. To Me is the final arrival."
31:15 But if the two of them strive against you to make you associate with Me that of which you have no knowledge, then do not obey them; and accompany them in this present world with kindness, and follow the path of whoever turns back to Me. Then to Me is your return, and I will tell you what you used to do.
31:16 "My son, if it be but the weight of a mustard-grain, and it lie within a rock, or in the heavens, or in the earth, Allah will bring it forth. Indeed, Allah is Subtle, All-Aware."
31:17 "My son, keep up the prayer, enjoin the good, forbid the rejected, and be of the sabirin over whatever befalls you. Indeed, that is among the matters of firm resolve."
31:18 "And do not turn your cheek away from people, and do not walk upon the earth exulting; indeed, Allah does not love any conceited vaunter."
31:19 "And walk a middle course in your stride, and lower your voice; indeed, the most dreadful of voices is the voice of the donkey."
31:20 Have you not seen that Allah has subjected to you whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth, and has lavished upon you blessings outward and inward? Yet among people are those who dispute about Allah without knowledge, without guidance, and without an illuminating Kitab.
31:21 And when it is said to them, "Follow what Allah has sent down," they say, "Rather, we follow what we found our fathers upon." Even if Shaytan were calling them toward the punishment of the blaze?
31:22 Whoever surrenders his whole being to Allah while doing good has held fast to the firmest handhold; and to Allah is the final outcome of all affairs.
31:23 Whoever disbelieves, let not his disbelief grieve you; to Us is their return, and We will tell them what they did. Indeed, Allah is All-Knowing of the innermost hearts.
31:24 We let them enjoy a little, then We drive them toward a harsh punishment.
31:25 If you ask them who created the heavens and the earth, they will surely say, "Allah." Say: "Praise be to Allah." Yet most of them do not know.
31:26 To Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and the earth; indeed, Allah is the Self-Sufficient, the Praised.
31:27 If all the trees on earth were pens, and the sea were supplied after it by 7 further seas, the words of Allah would never run out; indeed, Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
31:28 Your creation and your raising are but as a single nafs; indeed, Allah is All-Hearing, All-Seeing.
31:29 Do you not see that Allah merges the night into the day and merges the day into the night, and has subjected the sun and the moon, each running to an appointed term? And Allah is All-Aware of what you do.
31:30 That is because Allah is the Truth, and that what they call upon other than Allah is falsehood, and because Allah is the Most High, the Great.
31:31 Do you not see that the ships sail through the sea by Allah's blessing, so that Allah may show you something of Allah's ayat? Indeed in that are ayat for every sabir and grateful one.
31:32 And when waves cover them like a canopy, they call upon Allah, devoting all devotion to Allah alone; then, when Allah delivers them to dry land, some of them are moderate. And none disbelieves Our ayat except every treacherous kafir.
31:33 O people, be mindful of your Rabb and dread a day when no parent will answer for a child and no born one will answer for a parent in anything. Indeed, Allah's promise is true; so let not the life of this present world deceive you, and let not the Deceiver deceive you concerning Allah.
31:34 Indeed, with Allah is knowledge of the Hour; Allah sends down the rain and knows what is in the wombs. No nafs knows what it will earn tomorrow, and no nafs knows in which land it will die. Indeed, Allah is All-Knowing, All-Aware.
32:1 Alif Lam Mim.
32:2 The sending down of the Kitab, in which there is no doubt, from the Rabb of all knowledge.
32:3 Do they say, "He invented it"? No — it is the truth from your Rabb, to warn a people to whom no warner came before you, so that perhaps they may be guided.
32:4 Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth and what is between them in six days, then settled upon the high seat. You have no guardian other than Allah and no intercessor. Will you not remember?
32:5 Allah directs the affair from the heaven to the earth, then it ascends to Allah in a day whose measure is 1,000 years of what you count.
32:6 Such is Allah's knowledge of the hidden and the witnessed. Allah is the Mighty, the Raheem.
32:7 It is Allah who gave everything the excellence of its making; Allah began the creation of the human from clay.
32:8 Then Allah made its offspring from an extract of a lowly water.
32:9 Then Allah shaped it and blew into it of Allah's spirit, and gave you hearing and sights and fu'ad. Little do you give thanks!
32:10 And they say: "When we are lost in the earth, shall we really be in a new creation?" No — they disbelieve in the meeting with their Rabb.
32:11 Say: "The malakat of death, who is charged with you, will take you; then to your Rabb you will be returned."
32:12 If only you could see when the guilty are bowing their heads before their Rabb: "Our Rabb, we have seen and we have heard; send us back, we will do right — we are certain now."
32:13 If Allah had willed, Allah would have given every nafs its guidance; but the word from Allah stands true: "I will surely fill hell with jinn and people all together."
32:14 So taste, because you forgot the meeting of this day of yours — Allah has made you forgotten — and taste the everlasting punishment for what you used to do.
32:15 Only those believe in Allah's ayat who, when they are reminded of them, fall down prostrate and glorify with the praise of their Rabb, and they are not proud.
32:16 Who turn their sides from the beds, calling their Rabb in fear and in hope, and spend from what Allah has provided them.
32:17 No nafs knows what comfort for the eyes is hidden away for them, as a reward for what they used to do.
32:18 Is one who was a mumin like one who was fasiq? They are not equal.
32:19 As for those who believed and did salih deeds, theirs are the gardens of shelter, as a welcome gift for what they used to do.
32:20 As for those who fasuqu, their shelter is the fire. Every time they want to get out of it, they are put back into it, and it is said to them: "Taste the punishment of the fire that you used to call a lie."
32:21 And Allah will surely make them taste the nearer punishment before the greater punishment, so that they may return.
32:22 And who is more zalimun than one who is reminded of the ayat of their Rabb and then turns away from them? Allah will surely requite the guilty.
32:23 And Allah gave Musa the Kitab — so do not be in doubt about the meeting with Allah — and Allah made it a guide for the children of Israel.
32:24 And Allah made among them leaders who guide by Allah's command, because they were sabir and had certainty in Allah's ayat.
32:25 Your Rabb will decide between them on the day of rising concerning that in which they used to differ.
32:26 Has Allah not shown them how many generations before them Allah destroyed, while they walk in their dwellings? Truly in that are ayat — will they not hear?
32:27 Do they not see that Allah drives the water to the barren land and brings forth crops by it, from which their cattle and they themselves eat? Will they not see?
32:28 And they say: "When is this triumph, if you are sadiq?"
32:29 Say: "On the day of triumph, the belief of those who disbelieved will not benefit them, nor will they be given respite."
32:30 So turn away from them and wait; they too are waiting.
33:1 O Nabi, be mindful of Allah and do not obey the kafirin and the munafiqin; Allah is knowing, wise.
33:2 And follow what is inspired to you from your Rabb; Allah is aware of all you do.
33:3 And trust in Allah; sufficient is Allah as trustee.
33:4 Allah has not made for any man two hearts inside his body, and Allah has not made your nisa, whom you declare against as their mothers, actually your mothers, nor has Allah made your adopted sons your true sons. That is only your saying with your mouths, while Allah speaks the truth and guides the way.
33:5 Call them after their own fathers; that is more equitable with Allah. If you do not know their fathers, then they are your brothers in religion and your allies. There is no blame on you for what you err in, but only for what your hearts do deliberately. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
33:6 The Nabi is closer to the muminin than their own selves, and the Nabi's nisa are their mothers. And those joined by blood ties are closer to one another in Allah's decree than the muminin and the muhajirin, unless you do kindness to your allies. That has been written down in the decrees.
33:7 And when We took from the nabis their pact, and from you, and from Nuh, and Ibrahim, and Musa, and Isa son of Maryam, We took from them a hard pledge.
33:8 So that Allah may ask the sadiqin about their sadiqness, and has prepared for the kafirin a painful punishment.
33:9 O you who believe, remember Allah's favor upon you when hosts came at you and We sent against them a wind and hosts you never saw; and Allah sees all you do.
33:10 When they came at you from above you and from below you, and when the sights swerved and the hearts reached the throats, and you thought conjecture about Allah.
33:11 There the muminin were tested and shaken with a severe shaking.
33:12 And when the munafiqin and those in whose hearts is sickness said, "What Allah and the Rasul promised us is nothing but deception."
33:13 And when a party of them said, "O people of Yathrib, there is no standing place for you here, so turn back." And a group of them asked leave of the Nabi, saying, "Our houses are exposed," though they were not exposed; they wanted only to flee.
33:14 If the enemy had entered on them from all sides and they had been asked to betray, they would have done it, and would not have tarried over it but briefly.
33:15 They had already pledged to Allah that they would not turn their backs; and a pledge to Allah will be asked about.
33:16 Say: "Flight will not benefit you if you flee from death or from killing, and even then you will enjoy only a little."
33:17 Say: "Who is it that can shield you from Allah if Allah intends harm for you, or intends mercy for you?" And they will find for themselves, besides Allah, no protector and no helper.
33:18 Allah knows those among you who hold back, and those who say to their brothers, "Come over to us," yet they hardly come to the fight at all.
33:19 They are stingy toward you; and when fear comes you see them looking at you, their eyes rolling like one overcome by death; and when the fear passes they lash at you with hadad tongues, stingy toward any good. Those are the ones who have not believed, so Allah has voided their deeds; and that is easy for Allah.
33:20 They think the parties have not gone. If the parties came, they would wish they were out in the open land among the desert Arabs, asking after news of you. And if they were among you, they would fight only a little.
33:21 Indeed, in the Rasul of Allah you have a good pattern for whoever hopes in Allah and the Last Day and remembers Allah much.
33:22 And when the muminin saw the parties, they said, "This is what Allah and the Rasul promised us, and Allah and the Rasul were true." And it increased them only in faith and submission.
33:23 Among the muminin are men who were true to the pledge they owed Allah; some of them fulfilled their vow, and some are still waiting, and they have not changed in the least.
33:24 So that Allah may repay the sadiqin for their sadiqness and punish the munafiqin if Allah wills, or turn to them in forgiveness; Allah is forgiving, merciful.
33:25 Allah drove back the kafirin in their rage; they gained no good. And Allah spared the muminin from the fighting; Allah is strong, mighty.
33:26 And Allah brought down those of the People of the Kitab who had backed them from their strongholds, and cast terror into their hearts: a party you slew and a party you took captive.
33:27 And Allah made you inherit their land, their homes, their wealth, and a land you had never trodden; and Allah is powerful over all things.
33:28 O Nabi, say to your nisa: "If you desire the life of this world and its adornment, then come, I will provide for you and release you with a beautiful release."
33:29 But if you desire Allah and the Rasul and the final home, then Allah has prepared for those of you who do good a great reward.
33:30 O nisa of the Nabi, whoever of you commits a clear obscenity, the punishment will be doubled for her; and that is easy for Allah.
33:31 And whoever of you is devout to Allah and the Rasul and does right, We will give her reward twice over, and We have prepared for her a generous provision.
33:32 O nisa of the Nabi, you are not like any other nisa. If you are mindful, then do not soften your speech, so that one in whose heart is sickness may hope; and speak a recognized word.
33:33 Settle in your homes and do not display yourselves with the display of the former pagan ignorance; establish the prayer, give the purifying dues, and obey Allah and the Rasul. Allah only wills to remove from you all filth, people of the household, and to purify you thoroughly.
33:34 And remember what is recited in your homes of Allah's ayat and the wisdom; Allah is subtle-kind, aware.
33:35 Indeed, the muslimin men and muslimat nisa, the muminin men and muminat nisa, the qanitin men and qanitat nisa, the sadiqin men and sadiqat nisa, the sabirin men and sabirat nisa, the khashi'in men and khashi'at nisa, the mutasaddiqin men and mutasaddiqat nisa, the sa'imin men and sa'imat nisa, the hafizin men who guard their openings and the hafizat nisa who guard, and the dhakirin Allah much, men and the dhakirat nisa — Allah has prepared for them forgiveness and a great reward.
33:36 It is not for a believing man or a believing imra'a, when Allah and the Rasul have decided a matter, to have any choice in their own affair; whoever disobeys Allah and the Rasul has strayed into clear error.
33:37 And when you said to the one whom Allah had favored and whom you had favored, "Hold fast to your zawj and be mindful of Allah," while you concealed within yourself what Allah was to make manifest, fearing people, though Allah is more worthy to be feared. So when Zaid had done with her, We married her to you, so that there would be no constriction on the muminin concerning the nisa of their adopted sons once they have done with them; and Allah's command is always carried out.
33:38 No constriction lies on the Nabi in what Allah has ordained for him; that is the way of Allah with those who passed before; and Allah's command is a decreed decree.
33:39 Those are the ones who deliver the messages of Allah and hold reverent awe of Allah, and fear no one except Allah; and sufficient is Allah as reckoner.
33:40 Muhammad is not the father of any of your men, but the Rasul of Allah and the seal of the nabis; and Allah knows all things.
33:41 O you who believe, remember Allah with much remembrance.
33:42 And glorify Allah morning and evening.
33:43 It is Allah who sends blessings upon you, and the malakat, to bring you out from darkness into light; and Allah is ever merciful to the muminin.
33:44 Their greeting on the day they meet Allah will be "Peace"; and Allah has prepared for them a generous reward.
33:45 O Nabi, We have sent you as a witness, a bearer of glad tidings, and a warner.
33:46 And a caller to Allah by Allah's permission, and a shining lamp.
33:47 And give the muminin glad tidings that they will have from Allah a great favor.
33:48 And do not obey the kafirin and the munafiqin; disregard their harm, and trust in Allah; sufficient is Allah as trustee.
33:49 O you who believe, when you marry believing nisa and then divorce them before you have touched them, you have no waiting period to count against them; so provide for them and release them with a beautiful release.
33:50 O Nabi, We have made lawful for you your zawj to whom you have given their dowries, and those whom your right hand possesses from what Allah granted you as spoils, and the daughters of your paternal uncles, the daughters of your paternal aunts, the daughters of your maternal uncles, and the daughters of your maternal aunts who emigrated with you; and a believing imra'a if she gives herself to the Nabi, should the Nabi wish to marry her — exclusively for you, apart from the muminin. We know what We have ordained for them concerning their nisa and those their right hands possess, so that no constriction falls on you; and Allah is forgiving, merciful.
33:51 You may put off whom you wish of them and take to yourself whom you wish; and if you seek again one you had set aside, there is no blame on you. That is nearer to comforting their eyes, so that they do not grieve and are content with what you have given them all. Allah knows what is in your hearts; and Allah is knowing, forbearing.
33:52 No (other) nisa are lawful for you after this, nor may you exchange them for other nisa, even if their beauty pleases you — except those whom your right hand possesses; and Allah watches all things.
33:53 O you who believe, do not enter the houses of the Nabi unless permission is given to you for a meal, without waiting for it to be ready; but when you are invited, enter, and when you have eaten, disperse, without lingering for hadith. That used to harm the Nabi, and the Nabi was too shy to ask you to leave; but Allah is not shy of the truth. And when you ask the Nabi's nisa for some goods, ask them from behind a veil; that is purer for your hearts and for their hearts. It is not for you to harm the Rasul of Allah, nor ever to marry the Nabi's nisa after the Nabi; that, in Allah's sight, is immense.
33:54 If you make a thing manifest or conceal it, Allah knows all things.
33:55 No blame rests on the Nabi's nisa in appearing before their fathers, their sons, their brothers, their brothers' sons, their sisters' sons, their nisa, and those whom their right hands possess. Be mindful of Allah; Allah witnesses all things.
33:56 Allah and the malakat send blessings upon the Nabi. O you who believe, send blessings upon the Nabi and greet with a greeting of submission.
33:57 Those who harm Allah and the Rasul — Allah curses them in this world and the next, and has prepared for them a humiliating punishment.
33:58 And those who harm believing men and nisa for what they have not earned — they have taken up stunning-slander and clear sin.
33:59 O Nabi, tell your nisa and your daughters and the nisa of the muminin to draw over themselves some of their outer cloaks; that is nearer to their being recognized and not harmed; and Allah is forgiving, merciful.
33:60 If the munafiqin, and those in whose hearts is sickness, and the alarm-mongers in the town do not cease, We will surely stir you against them, and then they will not dwell beside you in it except a little while.
33:61 Cursed; wherever they are overtaken, they will be seized and slain utterly.
33:62 That is the way of Allah with those who passed before, and you will never find any change in the way of Allah.
33:63 People ask you about the Hour. Say: "Its knowledge is with Allah alone. What can tell you — perhaps the Hour is near."
33:64 Allah has cursed the kafirin and prepared for them a blaze.
33:65 Abiding in it forever, finding no protector and no helper.
33:66 The day their faces are turned over in the Fire, they will say, "If only we had obeyed Allah and obeyed the Rasul."
33:67 And they will say, "Our Rabb, we obeyed our masters and our great ones, and they led us astray from the way."
33:68 "Our Rabb, give them double the punishment, and curse them with a great curse."
33:69 O you who believe, do not be like those who harmed Musa; Allah cleared Musa of what they said, and Musa was ever honored with Allah.
33:70 O you who believe, be mindful of Allah and speak a straight word.
33:71 Allah will set your deeds right for you and forgive you your offenses; whoever obeys Allah and the Rasul has won a great win.
33:72 We offered the amanat to the heavens and the earth and the mountains, but they refused to bear it and shrank in awe from it, and man bore it; truly, man is ever wrongful, ignorant.
33:73 So that Allah may punish the hypocritical men and nisa and the associating men and nisa, and turn in forgiveness to the believing men and nisa; and Allah is forgiving, merciful.
34:1 Praise be to Allah, to whom belongs what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and to whom belongs the praise in the Hereafter; and Allah is the Wise, the Aware.
34:2 Allah knows what enters the earth and what comes out of it, and what comes down from the heaven and what ascends into it; and Allah is the Raheem, the Forgiving.
34:3 Those who disbelieve say: "The Hour will not come to us." Say: "Yes, by my Rabb, it shall surely come to you — knower of the ghayb; not the weight of a mote in the heavens or in the earth escapes Allah, nor anything smaller than that or greater, but it is in a clear record."
34:4 So that Allah may repay those who believe and do the good works — for them is forgiveness and a generous provision.
34:5 And those who strive against Our ayat, seeking to overpower — for them is a punishment of a painful affliction.
34:6 And those given knowledge see that what has been sent down to you from your Rabb is the truth, and it guides to the path of the Mighty, the Praised.
34:7 And those who disbelieve say: "Shall we point you to a man who informs you that when you are torn apart, utterly torn, you will be in a new creation?"
34:8 Has he fabricated a lie against Allah, or is there madness in him? No — rather, those who do not believe in the Hereafter are in the punishment and the far error.
34:9 Have they not looked at what is before them and behind them of the heaven and the earth? If We will, We sink the earth with them, or We drop upon them fragments from the heaven. In that is an aya for every servant who turns back.
34:10 And We gave Dawud a bounty from Us: "O mountains, return his praise with him, and the birds," and We softened the hadid for him.
34:11 "Work ample coats of mail, and measure well in the links; and all of you do good — truly I see what you do."
34:12 And to Sulayman the wind: its morning a month and its evening a month; and We caused a spring of molten metal to flow for him. And of the jinn, some worked before him by the leave of his Rabb; and whichever of them swerved from Our command, We made him taste the punishment of the Blaze.
34:13 They made for him what he wished: niches, and images, and basins like great cisterns, and firmly anchored cauldrons. "Work, family of Dawud, in thanks." And few of My servants are thankful.
34:14 Then when We decreed death upon him, nothing pointed them to his death but a creature of the earth eating his staff; and when it fell down, the jinn realized that if they had known the ghayb they would not have tarried in the humiliating punishment.
34:15 For Saba there was an aya in their dwelling: two gardens, on the right and on the left. "Eat of your Rabb's provision and give thanks for it — a good town and a forgiving Rabb."
34:16 But they turned away, so We sent upon them the flood of the dam, and We substituted for their two gardens two gardens bearing bitter food, and tamarisk, and a few lote-trees.
34:17 That is how We repaid them for their disbelief — and do We repay anyone but the kafirin?
34:18 And We placed between them and the towns We had blessed towns in plain sight, and We measured the journey through them: "Journey through them nights and days, secure."
34:19 But they said: "Our Rabb, stretch out our journeys," and they wronged themselves, so We made them into ahadith and tore them to pieces, utterly torn. In that are ayat for every sabir, thankful one.
34:20 And Iblis indeed proved true in his thinking about them, and they followed him — except for a party of the muminin.
34:21 He had no warrant over them except that We might know who believes in the Hereaway from the one who stands in doubt of it; and your Rabb is guardian over everything.
34:22 Say: "Call those you claim besides Allah; they own not the weight of a mote in the heavens or in the earth, and they have no partnership in the two, and no support comes to Allah from them."
34:23 And intercession avails nothing with Allah except for the one given leave; until, when the dread is lifted from their hearts, they say: "What did your Rabb say?" They say: "The truth." And Allah is the High, the Great.
34:24 Say: "Who provides for you from the heavens and the earth?" Say: "Allah." And either we or you are upon guidance or in clear error.
34:25 Say: "You will not be questioned about what we committed, nor will we be questioned about what you do."
34:26 Say: "Our Rabb will gather us together, then decide between us in truth; and Allah is the Deciding, the Knowing."
34:27 Say: "Show me those you have joined to Allah as partners." No indeed! Rather, Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
34:28 And We sent you only to all people as a bringer of glad news and a warner — but most people do not know.
34:29 And they say: "When is this promise, if you are sadiq?"
34:30 Say: "You have an appointed day which you will not delay by an hour, nor will you come early for it."
34:31 And those who disbelieve say: "We will not believe in this Recitation, nor in what came before it." If you could only see when the wrongdoers stand before their Rabb, turning to one another in speech: those who were weakened say to those who grew proud, "Had it not been for you, we would have been muminin."
34:32 Those who grew proud say to those who were weakened: "Did we turn you away from the guidance after it came to you? No — you were committers of crime."
