34:33 And those who were weakened say to those who grew proud: "No — it was your planning of night and day, when you commanded us to disbelieve in Allah and to make equals for Allah." And they hide their regret when they see the punishment; and We put fetters on the necks of those who disbelieved. Are they repaid except for what they did?
34:34 And We never sent a warner into a town without its ease-softened ones saying: "We disbelieve in what you have been sent with."
34:35 And they say: "We have more wealth and children, and we will not be punished."
34:36 Say: "My Rabb spreads the provision for whom Allah wills and measures it — but most people do not know."
34:37 Neither your wealth nor your children are what bring you near to Us in nearness, except for the one who believes and does good work; for those is a double repayment for what they did, and they are secure in the upper chambers.
34:38 And those who strive against Our ayat, seeking to overpower — those are present in the punishment.
34:39 Say: "My Rabb spreads the provision for whichever of Allah's servants Allah wills, and measures it; and whatever you spend of anything, Allah replaces it — and Allah is the best of providers."
34:40 And on the day Allah gathers them all, then says to the malakat: "Was it these who were worshipping you?"
34:41 They say: "Glory be to You! You are our protector, not them." No — they worshipped the jinn; most of them believed in them.
34:42 So today none of you has power over another for benefit or harm; and We say to those who wronged: "Taste the punishment of the Fire that you used to deny."
34:43 And when Our clear ayat are recited to them, they say: "This is nothing but a man who wants to turn you away from what your fathers used to worship." And they say: "This is nothing but a fabricated lie." And those who disbelieved say of the truth when it reached them: "This is nothing but clear sihr."
34:44 We did not give them Kutub to study, nor did We send to them before you any warner.
34:45 And those before them denied — though they did not reach a tenth of what We gave them — and they denied My rusul; so how dreadful was the rejection!
34:46 Say: "I admonish you with one thing: that you rise before Allah in twos and singly, and then reflect — there is no madness in your companion; he is only a warner to you, before a severe punishment."
34:47 Say: "Whatever wage I asked of you, it is yours; my wage is only upon Allah — and Allah is witness over everything."
34:48 Say: "My Rabb casts the truth — knower of the unseen."
34:49 Say: "The truth has come, and falsehood neither begins nor returns."
34:50 Say: "If I err, I err only against myself; and if I am guided, it is by what my Rabb reveals. Truly Allah is Hearing, Near."
34:51 If you could see when they are seized with dread, and there is no escape, and they are taken from a near place.
34:52 And they say: "We believe in it." But how can they reach it from a far place?
34:53 They disbelieved in it before, and they cast their guesses at the ghayb from a far place.
34:54 And a barrier is set between them and what they desire, as was done with their parties before them; they were in disquieting doubt.
35:1 Praise belongs to Allah, originator of the heavens and the earth, who makes the malakat rusul with wings of 2, and of 3, and of 4; Allah adds to the creation whatever Allah wills. Indeed Allah is over every thing powerful.
35:2 Whatever Allah opens for people of mercy, none can hold it back; and whatever Allah holds back, none can send it forth after that. And Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
35:3 O people, remember the blessing of Allah upon you. Is there any creator besides Allah providing you from the heaven and the earth? There is no ilah but Allah. So how are you turned away?
35:4 And if they deny you, rusul were denied before you; and to Allah the affairs return.
35:5 O people, the promise of Allah is true, so let not the nearer life deceive you, and let not the deceiver deceive you about Allah.
35:6 Indeed Shaytan is an enemy to you, so take him for an enemy; he only calls his party to be among the companions of the blaze.
35:7 Those who disbelieve, for them is severe punishment; and those who believe and do salih deeds, for them is forgiveness and a great reward.
35:8 So is one whose evil deed is adorned for him so that he sees it as good? Indeed Allah leads astray whomever Allah wills and guides whomever Allah wills, so do not let your nafs perish over them in regret; indeed Allah is knowing of what they do.
35:9 And Allah is the one who sends the winds, and they stir up clouds, and We drive them to a dead town, and We revive the earth with it after its death. Likewise is the rising.
35:10 Whoever wants might, then to Allah belongs the might entirely. To Allah ascend the good word, and the salihin deed raises it. And those who plan evil deeds, for them is severe punishment; and the plan of those is ever ruined.
35:11 And Allah created you from dust, then from a drop, then made you pairs; and no female bears or gives birth except by Allah's knowledge. And no life-term is prolonged for one of long life, nor diminished from its term, except in a record. Indeed that is easy for Allah.
35:12 Not equal are the two seas: this one fresh, sweet, pleasant to drink, and this one salty, bitter; and from both you eat fresh flesh, and you extract adornment which you wear, and you see the ships plowing through it, that you may seek of Allah's bounty and that you may give thanks.
35:13 Allah makes the night enter into the day and makes the day enter into the night, and subjected the sun and the moon, each running for an appointed term. That is Allah, your Rabb; to Allah belongs the kingdom. And those whom you call upon besides Allah possess not even a speck.
35:14 If you call them, they do not hear your call; and if they heard, they would not answer you. And on the Day of Resurrection they will disown your partnership, and none informs you like the All-Aware.
35:15 O people, you are the needy toward Allah, and Allah is the Self-Sufficient, the Praiseworthy.
35:16 If Allah wills, Allah does away with you and brings a new creation.
35:17 And that is not hard upon Allah.
35:18 And no laden nafs bears the load of another; and if one burdened with weight calls another to carry it, nothing of it is carried, even if he is a close relative. You only warn those who revere their Rabb unseen and establish the prayer; and whoever purifies only purifies for his own nafs; and to Allah is the arrival.
35:19 And the blind and the seeing are not equal,
35:20 nor are darkness and light,
35:21 nor shade and scorching heat.
35:22 And not equal are the living and the dead. Indeed Allah hears whomever Allah wills, and you cannot make hear those in the graves.
35:23 You are only a warner.
35:24 We sent you with the truth, as a bearer of glad tidings and a warner; and there is no nation but a warner has passed on among them.
35:25 And if they deny you, those before them denied; their rusul came to them with clear proofs, and the Kitab, and the enlightening Kitab.
35:26 Then those who disbelieved were seized — and how was the rejection!
35:27 Have you not seen that Allah sends down water from the heaven, and We bring forth with it fruits of differing colors? And from the mountains, fresh tracts white and red, of differing colors, and dusky black.
35:28 And among people, and creatures, and livestock, likewise of differing colors. Only those among Allah's servants who have knowledge revere Allah; indeed Allah is mighty, forgiving.
35:29 Indeed those who recite the Kitab of Allah, establish the prayer, and spend of what We provided them, secretly and openly, hope for a trade that will never perish.
35:30 To pay them their rewards in full and increase them from Allah's bounty; indeed Allah is forgiving, thankful.
35:31 And what We revealed to you of the Kitab is the truth, confirming what is before it; indeed Allah is ever well-aware, all-seeing of Allah's servants.
35:32 Then We bequeathed the Kitab to those We chose from Our servants; among them is one who wrongs himself, and among them one who takes the middle course, and among them a forerunner in good deeds by Allah's leave. That is the great bounty.
35:33 Gardens of Eden they enter, adorned in them with bracelets of gold and pearls; and their garments therein are silk.
35:34 They say: praise belongs to Allah, who removed from us sorrow; indeed our Rabb is forgiving, thankful.
35:35 Who settled us, by Allah's bounty, in the abode of residence; no toil touches us therein, and no weariness touches us therein.
35:36 And those who disbelieve, for them is the fire of Hell: it is not decreed for them that they die, nor is anything of its punishment lightened for them. Thus We repay every kafir.
35:37 They cry out in it: our Rabb, take us out; we will do salihness, other than what we used to do. Did We not grant you a life long enough for whoever would remember to remember? And the warner came to you. So taste — and the wrongdoers have no helper.
35:38 Indeed Allah knows the ghayb of the heavens and the earth; indeed Allah is knowing of what the breasts hide.
35:39 It is Allah who made you successors upon the earth; whoever disbelieves, his disbelief is upon him, and their disbelief increases the kafirin nothing with their Rabb but loathing, and their disbelief increases them nothing but loss.
35:40 Say: have you seen your associates whom you call upon besides Allah? Show me what they created of the earth, or do they have a share in the heavens? Or did We give them a the Kitab, so that they stand upon clear proof from it? No — the wrongdoers promise one another nothing but deception.
35:41 Indeed Allah holds the heavens and the earth so they do not vanish; and if they were to vanish, none could hold them after Allah. Indeed Allah is ever forbearing, forgiving.
35:42 They swore by Allah their utmost oaths: if a warner comes to them, they will surely be more rightly guided than any of the nations. But when the warner came to them, he increased them only in aversion.
35:43 Out of arrogance in the land and evil scheming; and evil scheming encircles none but its own people. So do they look for anything but the way of the former ones? You will find no change in Allah's way, and no turning of Allah's way.
35:44 Have they not journeyed in the land to see how the end was of those before them, though they were stronger than them in strength? And nothing in the heavens or the earth can render Allah powerless; indeed Allah is ever knowing, powerful.
35:45 If Allah were to seize people for what they earn, Allah would not leave upon its surface any creature; but Allah postpones them to an appointed term. And when their term comes, indeed Allah is ever seeing of Allah's servants.
36:1 Ya Sin
36:2 By the Wise Quran
36:3 You are indeed one of the mursalin
36:4 Upon a straight path
36:5 It is sent down by the Almighty, the Raheem
36:6 To warn a people whose forefathers were never warned, and so they are unmindful
36:7 The word has already confirms true against most of them, yet they do not believe
36:8 We have placed fetters around their necks, and they reach up to their chins, so they cannot raise their heads
36:9 And We placed a barrier in front of them and a barrier behind them, and We covered them over, so they cannot see
36:10 And it is all the same to them whether you warn them or do not warn them — they will not believe
36:11 You can only warn the one who follows the reminder and stands in awe of the Rahman in the ghayb; so give him good news of forgiveness and a noble reward
36:12 It is We who bring the dead to life and record what they send ahead and their tracks, and everything We have counted is in a clear record
36:13 And set forth for them a parable: the people of the town, when the mursalin came to them
36:14 When We sent two to them and they denied them both, so We strengthened them with a third, and they said, "We are MuRasul to you"
36:15 They said, "You are nothing but humans like us, and the Rahman has sent down nothing; you are only lying"
36:16 They said, "Our Rabb knows that we are truly mursalin to you"
36:17 And nothing is upon us but the clear delivery
36:18 They said, "We take you as a bad omen; if you do not stop, we will stone you, and a painful punishment from us will touch you"
36:19 They said, "Your bad omen rests with you — is it because you are reminded? No, you are a people who exceed all bounds"
36:20 And from the far end of the town came a man, hastening; he said, "O my people, follow the mursalin"
36:21 Follow those who ask you for no reward, and who are rightly guided
36:22 And why should I not worship the One who originated me, when to Allah you will all be returned
36:23 Shall I take other alihah besides Allah? If the Rahman wills harm for me, their intercession cannot avail me at all, nor can they rescue me
36:24 Then I would indeed be in clear error
36:25 I have believed in your Rabb — so listen
36:26 It was said, "Enter the Garden." He said, "If only my people knew
36:27 how my Rabb forgave me and made me one of the honored ones"
36:28 And We sent down upon his people after him no host from the heaven, nor had We any need to send one down
36:29 It was but a single shout, and at once they were stilled
36:30 Oh, the regret for the servants! No Rasul comes to them without their mocking him
36:31 Do they not see how many generations We destroyed before them? They do not return to them
36:32 And surely all of them, all together, will be brought before Us, present
36:33 And an aya for them is the dead earth: We gave it life and brought grain out of it, and from it they eat
36:34 And We placed in it gardens of palms and grapes, and We made springs burst forth in it
36:35 So that they may eat of its fruit, which their hands did not make — will they not then give thanks
36:36 Glory be to the One who created all the pairs — from what the earth grows, and from themselves, and from what they do not know
36:37 And an aya for them is the night: We strip the day from it, and at once they are in darkness
36:38 And the sun runs to its settling place; that is the decree of the Almighty, the Knowing
36:39 And the moon — We decreed for it stations, until it returns like an old, withered stalk
36:40 It is not for the sun to overtake the moon, nor does the night race ahead of the day; and each swims in an orbit
36:41 And an aya for them is that We carried their offspring in the laden ship
36:42 And We created for them the like of it, which they ride
36:43 And if We will, We drown them, and there is none to answer their cry, nor are they rescued
36:44 Except as a mercy from Us, and enjoyment for a while
36:45 And when it is said to them, "Guard against what lies before you and behind you, so that you may be shown mercy"
36:46 not an aya of their Rabb's ayat comes to them without their turning away from it
36:47 and when it is said to them, "Spend of what Allah has provided you," those who disbelieve say to those who believe, "Are we to feed those whom Allah, if Allah willed, would feed? You are only in clear error"
36:48 And they say, "When is this promise, if you are sadiq"
36:49 They await nothing but a single blast that seizes them while they are contending
36:50 Then they cannot make a bequest, nor do they return to their families
36:51 And the blast is blown, and at once from the graves to their Rabb they come pouring forth
36:52 They say, "Woe to us! Who has raised us from our resting place? This is what the Rahman promised, and the mursalin spoke the truth"
36:53 It was but a single blast, and at once they are all brought before Us, present
36:54 So today no nafs is wronged in anything, and you are requited only for what you used to do
36:55 Indeed, the companions of the Garden are this day busy with delight
36:56 They and their zawj, in shade upon the couches, reclining
36:57 They have therein fruits, and whatever they call for is theirs
36:58 "Peace" — a word from a Raheem Rabb
36:59 "And stand apart today, O mujrimin"
36:60 "Did I not charge you, O children of Adam, not to worship Shaytan? Truly, Shaytan is a clear enemy to you"
36:61 "And that you should serve Me — this is the straight path"
36:62 "Yet Shaytan has surely led a great multitude of you astray. Did you not understand"
36:63 "This is the Hell you were promised"
36:64 "Enter it today, because of what you used to disbelieve"
36:65 Today We seal their mouths, and their hands speak to Us, and their feet testify to what they used to earn
36:66 And if We willed, We would blot out their eyes; then they would race along the path — but how could they see
36:67 And if We willed, We would transform them where they are, so they could not move on nor return
36:68 And whomever We grant a long life, We turn back in creation. Will they not understand
36:69 We did not teach him poetry, nor does it befit him; it is only a reminder and a clear Quran
36:70 To warn whoever is alive, and so that the word may confirms true against the kafirin
36:71 Have they not seen that We created for them, by the work of Our hands, cattle, and they are their masters
36:72 And We subdued them for them; from them come their mounts, and from them they eat
36:73 And they have in them benefits and drink. Will they not give thanks
36:74 And they have taken alihah besides Allah, so that they might help them
36:75 They cannot help them, and they themselves are a host brought before them
36:76 So let not their words grieve you; We know what they hide and what they make open
36:77 Has man not seen that We created him from a drop — and then he is an open contender
36:78 And he sets forth a parable for Us, having forgotten his own creation; he says, "Who will give life to the bones when they are decayed"
36:79 Say: "The One who originated them the first time will give them life, and Allah is knowing of every creation"
36:80 the One who made for you from the green tree a fire, and at once you kindle from it
36:81 Is the One who created the heavens and the earth not able to create the like of them? Yes — and Allah is the Creator, the Knowing
36:82 Allah's command, when Allah wills a thing, is only to say to it, "Be," and it is
36:83 So glory be to the One in whose hand is the dominion of all things, and to whom you will be returned
37:1 By those drawn up in ranks,
37:2 and the drive-back driving back,
37:3 and those who follow with a reminder,
37:4 your deity is surely One,
37:5 Rabb of the heavens and the earth and what is between them, and Rabb of the east.
37:6 We adorned the lowest heaven with the adornment of stars,
37:7 a guard against every defiant devil,
37:8 so that they cannot listen to the high assembly, and they are cast at from every side,
37:9 driven away, and for them is a lasting punishment.
37:10 Except one who snatches a snatch; then a piercing fire-missile follows him.
37:11 So ask them: are they stronger in creation, or those whom We created? We created them from clinging clay.
37:12 Nay, they marvel, and they mock.
37:13 And when they are reminded, they do not remember.
37:14 And when they see an aya, they mock.
37:15 And they say: this is nothing but clear sihr.
37:16 When we die and become dust and bones, shall we be raised?
37:17 Or our former fathers?
37:18 Say: yes, and you will be subdued.
37:19 It is but a single drive, and at once they are looking.
37:20 And they say: woe to us, this is the day of requital.
37:21 This is the day of decision which you used to deny.
37:22 Gather those who wronged, and their mates, and what they used to worship
37:23 besides Allah, then guide them to the path of the blaze.
37:24 Stop them; they will be questioned.
37:25 Why do you not help one another?
37:26 Nay, today they have surrendered.
37:27 And some of them turn to one another, asking.
37:28 They say: you used to come to us from the right.
37:29 They say: nay, you were not muminin.
37:30 We had no warrant over you; rather, you were a transgressing people.
37:31 The word of our Rabb has befallen us; we will surely taste.
37:32 We misled you; we were misleaders.
37:33 So that day they share in the punishment.
37:34 Thus We do with the mujrimin.
37:35 When it was said to them: there is no deity but Allah, they were arrogant,
37:36 and they say: shall we abandon our alihah for a mad poet?
37:37 Nay, he came with the truth and confirmed the mursalin.
37:38 You will surely taste the painful punishment,
37:39 and you are repaid only for what you used to do.
37:40 Except the devoted servants of Allah:
37:41 those have a known provision,
37:42 fruits, and they are honored,
37:43 in gardens of bliss,
37:44 on couches, facing one another.
37:45 A cup from a flowing spring is carried around among them,
37:46 white, a delight for the drinkers,
37:47 with no headiness in it, and they are not cut off from it.
37:48 And with them are companions whose glances are restrained, wide of eye,
37:49 as if they were concealed eggs.
37:50 And some of them turn to one another, asking.
37:51 One of them, a speaker, says: I had a companion
37:52 who used to say: are you really one of those who affirm?
37:53 When we die and become dust and bones, shall we be requited?
37:54 He says: will you take a look?
37:55 He looked and saw him in the midst of the blaze.
37:56 He says: by Allah, you nearly plunged me into ruin.
37:57 Were it not for the grace of my Rabb, I would have been among those brought before.
37:58 So are we not dead,
37:59 except for our first death, and we will not be punished?
37:60 This is truly the great win.
37:61 For the like of this let the workers work.
37:62 Is that a better lodging, or the Zaqqum tree?
37:63 We made it a trial for the wrongdoers.
37:64 It is a tree that comes out in the root of the blaze.
37:65 Its spathes are like the heads of devils.
37:66 They will eat from it and fill their bellies with it.
37:67 Then they have upon it a drink of scalding water,
37:68 then their return is to the blaze.
37:69 They found their fathers astray,
37:70 and they rush on their tracks.
37:71 Most of the ancients went astray before them,
37:72 and We sent warners among them.
37:73 See how the end of the warned ones was.
37:74 Except the devoted servants of Allah.
37:75 Nuh called Us, and excellent are We as answerers.
37:76 We saved him and his family from the great distress,
37:77 and We made his offspring the ones who remained,
37:78 and We left a mention of him among the later ones.
37:79 Peace on Nuh among the worlds.
37:80 Thus We repay the muhsinin.
37:81 He is one of Our believing servants.
37:82 Then We drowned the others.
37:83 Among his party was Ibrahim,
37:84 when he came to his Rabb with a sound heart.
37:85 When he said to his father and his people: what do you worship?
37:86 Do you want false alihah besides Allah?
37:87 What then do you suppose of the Rabb of the worlds?
37:88 He looked with a look at the stars,
37:89 and said: I am sick.
37:90 So they turned away from him and left.
37:91 He turned to their alihah and said: do you not eat?
37:92 What is wrong with you that you do not speak?
37:93 He turned on them, striking with the right hand.
37:94 They came to him hurrying.
37:95 He said: do you worship what you carve,
37:96 when Allah created you and what you make?
37:97 They said: build him a structure and cast him into the blaze.
37:98 They intended a scheme against him, so We made them the lowest.
37:99 And he said: I am going to my Rabb, who will guide me.
37:100 My Rabb, grant me one of the salihin.
37:101 So We gave him good news of a forbearing boy.
37:102 And when he reached with him the age of striving, he said: my son, I see in a dream that I am to slaughter you, so see what you think. He said: my father, do what you are commanded; you will find me, if Allah wills, of the sabirin.
37:103 When both had submitted and he laid him down upon the forehead,
37:104 We called him: O Ibrahim,
37:105 you have confirmed the vision; thus We repay the muhsinin.
37:106 This is the clear test.
37:107 We ransomed him with a great slaughter,
37:108 and We left a mention of him among the later ones.
37:109 Peace on Ibrahim.
37:110 Thus We repay the muhsinin.
37:111 He is one of Our believing servants.
37:112 And We gave him good news of Isaac, a Nabi of the salihin.
37:113 We blessed him and Isaac; and among their offspring are one who does good and one who clearly wrongs himself.
37:114 We surely favored Musa and Harun.
37:115 We saved them and their people from the great distress,
37:116 and We helped them, and they were the overcomers.
37:117 We gave them the clear the Kitab,
37:118 and We guided them on the straight path.
37:119 We left a mention of them among the later ones.
37:120 Peace on Musa and Harun.
37:121 Thus We repay the muhsinin.
37:122 They are two of Our believing servants.
37:123 Ilyas was one of the mursalin,
37:124 when he said to his people: will you not be mindful?
37:125 Do you call on Ba'l and abandon the best of creators?
37:126 Allah is your Rabb and the Rabb of your former fathers.
37:127 They denied him, and they will be brought before,
37:128 except the devoted servants of Allah.
37:129 We left a mention of him among the later ones.
37:130 Peace on Ilyasin.
37:131 Thus We repay the muhsinin.
37:132 He is one of Our believing servants.
37:133 Lut was one of the mursalin,
37:134 when We saved him and all his family,
37:135 except an jawza among those left behind.
37:136 Then We razed the others,
37:137 and you pass by them in the morning,
37:138 and by night; will you not reason?
37:139 Yunus was one of the mursalin,
37:140 when he fled to the laden ship,
37:141 cast lots, and was among the mudhadin.
37:142 The huut swallowed him, and he was blameworthy.
37:143 Had he not been one of those who glorify,
37:144 he would have tarried in its belly until the day they are raised.
37:145 So We cast him on the open ground, while he was sick,
37:146 and We grew over him a gourd tree,
37:147 and We sent him to 100,000, or more.
37:148 They believed, so We gave them enjoyment for a time.
37:149 So ask them: does your Rabb have daughters while they have sons?
37:150 Or did We create the malakat female, while they are witnesses?
37:151 They surely say from their fabrication:
37:152 Allah has begotten, and they are liars.
37:153 Did Allah choose daughters over sons?
37:154 What is wrong with you, how do you judge?
37:155 Will you not remember?
37:156 Or do you have a clear warrant?
37:157 Then bring your the Kitab, if you are sadiq.
37:158 They made kinship between Allah and the jinn, and the jinn knew that they will be brought before.
37:159 Glory be to Allah above what they describe,
37:160 except the devoted servants of Allah.
37:161 But you and what you worship,
37:162 you cannot mislead anyone with it,
37:163 except whoever enters the blaze.
37:164 There is none of us but has a known station,
37:165 and we are the ones in ranks,
37:166 and we are the ones who glorify.
37:167 They used to say:
37:168 if only we had a reminder from the ancients,
37:169 we would be the devoted servants of Allah.
37:170 They disbelieved in it, so they will know.
37:171 Our word has gone ahead for Our servants, the mursalin:
37:172 they are the helped ones,
37:173 and Our hosts are the overcomers.
37:174 So turn away from them for a time,
37:175 and see them; they will soon see.
37:176 Do they seek to hasten Our punishment?
37:177 When it descends on their courtyard, evil will be the morning of the warned ones.
37:178 And turn away from them for a time,
37:179 and see; they will soon see.
37:180 Glory be to your Rabb, the Rabb of might, above what they describe,
37:181 and peace be on the mursalin,
37:182 and praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
38:1 Sad. By the Qur'an, possessor of the reminder.
38:2 Nay, those who disbelieve are in might and opposition.
38:3 How many a generation We destroyed before them, and they cried out — but there was no time of escape.
38:4 And they marveled that a warner had come to them from among themselves. And the kafirin said: "This is a lying sorcery."
38:5 "Has he made the alihah into one ilah? Truly this is an astounding thing!"
38:6 And their chiefs set out, saying: "Go on, and be of the sabirin over your ilahs — this is surely something intended."
38:7 "We have not heard of this in the later way; this is nothing but a fabrication."
38:8 "Has the reminder been sent down to him from among us?" Nay, they are in doubt about My reminder — nay, they have not yet tasted the punishment.
38:9 Or do they have the treasures of the mercy of your Rabb, the Mighty, the Grantor?
38:10 Or is the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is between them theirs? Then let them ascend by means.
38:11 There, every host is routed from among the confederates.
38:12 The people of Nuh, Aad, and Fir'awn of the Stakes denied before them.
38:13 And Thamud, and the people of Lut, and the companions of the Thicket — those were the confederates.
38:14 Every one of them denied the Rasuls, and so the punishment came true.
38:15 These people await nothing but a single shout, with no interval in it.
38:16 And they say: "Our Rabb, hasten our portion to us before the Day of Reckoning."
38:17 Be sabir over what they say, and remember Our servant Dawud, the possessor of strength; truly he was ever-turning back.
38:18 We subjected the mountains with him, glorifying at evening and at sunrise.
38:19 And the birds gathered, each of them ever-returning.
38:20 And We strengthened his kingdom, and gave him wisdom and decisive speech.
38:21 Has the account of the contenders come to you — when they climbed into the chamber?
38:22 When they entered upon Dawud, and he dreaded them, they said: "Do not fear — two contenders, one of whom has wronged the other; so judge between us with the truth, and do not transgress, and guide us to the even path."
38:23 "This brother of mine has 99 ewes, and I have 1 ewe; and he said: 'Entrust her to me,' and he overpowered me in the affair."
38:24 He said: "He has wronged you by asking for your ewe to add to his ewes; and indeed many partners wrong one another, except those who believe and do salih deeds — and few they are." And Dawud thought that We had only tested him, so he sought forgiveness of his Rabb, and fell down bowing, and turned back.
38:25 So We forgave him that; and truly with Us he has near access and a good return.
38:26 O Dawud, We have made you a successor upon the earth, so judge between the people with the truth, and do not follow desire, lest it lead you astray from the way of Allah. Those who go astray from the way of Allah — theirs is a severe punishment for what they forgot of the Day of Reckoning.
38:27 We did not create the heaven and the earth and what is between them in vain; that is the thought of those who disbelieve. So woe to those who disbelieve from the Fire.
38:28 Shall We make those who believe and do salih deeds like the corrupters on the earth? Shall We make the muttaqin like the immoral?
38:29 A blessed Kitab that We have sent down to you, so that they may reflect on its ayat, and that people of understanding may remember.
38:30 And We granted Sulayman to Dawud — an excellent servant; truly he was ever-turning back.
38:31 When there were presented to him at evening al-Ṣāfināt, al-Jiyād.
38:32 He said: "I have loved the love of good over the remembrance of my Rabb, until it vanished behind the veil."
38:33 "Bring them back to me" — and he set about stroking the shins and the necks.
38:34 And We tested Sulayman, and cast upon his kursi a body; then he turned back.
38:35 He said: "My Rabb, forgive me, and grant me a kingdom not fitting for anyone after me; truly You are the Grantor."
38:36 So We subjected to him the wind, running at his command, gently, wherever it turned.
38:37 And the devils — every builder and diver.
38:38 And others, bound together in fetters.
38:39 This is Our gift; so give unstintingly, or withhold, without reckoning.
38:40 And truly with Us he has near access and a good return.
38:41 And remember Our servant Ayyub, when he called to his Rabb: "The devil has touched me with toil and torment."
38:42 Strike with your foot: here is a cool bathing-place and a drink.
38:43 And We granted him his family, and the like of them with them, as a mercy from Us, and a reminder for people of understanding.
38:44 And take a bundle in your hand, and strike with it, and do not swear falsely. We found him sabir — an excellent servant; truly he was ever-turning back.
38:45 And remember Our servants Ibrahim and Ishaq and Yaqub, possessors of strength and vision.
38:46 We made them devoted, with an exclusive remembrance of the Home.
38:47 And truly with Us they are among the chosen, the best.
38:48 And remember Ismail and Al-Yasa and Dhu'l-Kifl — each among the best.
38:49 This is a reminder; and truly the muttaqin have a good return.
38:50 Gardens of Eden, opened wide to them are the doors.
38:51 Reclining therein, they are called therein to abundant fruit and drink.
38:52 And with them are those who restrain their glances, age-mates alike.
38:53 This is what you were promised for the Day of Reckoning.
38:54 Truly this is Our provision; it has no end.
38:55 This — and truly the transgressors have an evil return.
38:56 Hell, which they enter — how evil a resting-place!
38:57 This, then let them taste it: scalding water and ice.
38:58 And others of its kind, in pairs.
38:59 Here is a host rushing in with you. No welcome to them; they enter the Fire.
38:60 They say: "No — you: no welcome to you! You brought it upon us. How evil a settled place!"
38:61 They say: "Our Rabb, whoever brought this upon us, increase for them a doubled punishment in the Fire."
38:62 And they say: "Why do we not see men whom we used to count among the evil ones?"
38:63 Did we take them as a laughing-stock, or have their eyes swerved from them?
38:64 Truly that is the truth: the people of the Fire disputing with one another.
38:65 Say: "I am only a warner, and there is no ilah but Allah, the One, the Overpowering."
38:66 Rabb of the heavens and the earth and what is between them, the Mighty, the Forgiving.
38:67 Say: "It is a mighty tiding."
38:68 You are turning away from it.
38:69 I had no knowledge of the Highest Assembly when they disputed.
38:70 Nothing is revealed to me except that I am a clear warner.
38:71 When your Rabb said to the malakat: "I am creating a human from clay."
38:72 And when I have proportioned him and breathed into him of My spirit, then fall down to him prostrating.
38:73 And the malakat prostrated, all of them together.
38:74 Except Iblis; he grew proud, and was among the kafirin.
38:75 Allah said: "O Iblis, what kept you from prostrating to what I created with My hands? Were you proud, or were you among the exalted?"
38:76 He said: "I am better than him: You created me from fire, and created him from clay."
38:77 Allah said: "Then get out of it — you are cast out."
38:78 And My curse is upon you until the Day of Judgment.
38:79 He said: "My Rabb, grant me respite until the day they are raised."
38:80 Allah said: "Then you are among the respited."
38:81 Until the known appointed time.
38:82 He said: "Then by Your might, I will surely mislead them all."
38:83 Except Your abd, the mukhlisin among them.
38:84 Allah said: "Then the truth — and the truth I speak:"
38:85 I will surely fill Hell with you, and with those of them who follow you, all together.
38:86 Say: "I ask you no reward for it, and I am not one of those who take on burdens."
38:87 It is nothing but a reminder for the worlds.
38:88 And you will surely know its account after a while.
39:1 The sending down of the Kitab from Allah, the Almighty, the Wise.
39:2 Indeed, We have sent down to you the Kitab with truth; so worship Allah, devoting the religion exclusively to Allah.
39:3 Indeed, devoted religion is for Allah. And those who have taken protectors besides Allah say: "We worship them only so that they may bring us near to Allah by nearness." Indeed, Allah judges between them in that over which they differ. Indeed, Allah does not guide one who is a lying kafir.
39:4 If Allah willed to take a son, Allah would have chosen, from what Allah creates, whatever Allah wills. Glory be to Allah! Allah is the One, the Overpowering.
39:5 Allah created the heavens and the earth with truth; Allah wraps the night over the day and wraps the day over the night, and subjected the sun and the moon, each running for an appointed term. Indeed, Allah is the Almighty, the Forgiving.
39:6 Allah created you from a single nafs, then made from it its mate, and sent down for you from the livestock 8 pairs. Allah creates you in the bellies of your mothers, creation after creation, in three darknesses. That is Allah, your Rabb; to Allah belongs dominion. There is no ilah but Allah. So where do you turn away?
39:7 If you disbelieve, indeed Allah has no need of you, and Allah is not pleased with disbelief for the servants; but if you are grateful, Allah is pleased with it for you. No burden-bearer bears the burden of another. Then to your Rabb is your return, and Allah will inform you of what you used to do. Indeed, Allah is knowing of what is in the hearts.
39:8 And when harm touches a human, he calls his Rabb, turning back to Allah; then when Allah bestows on him a blessing from Allah, he forgets what he used to call upon before, and sets up equals to Allah, to lead astray from the way of Allah. Say: "Enjoy your disbelief a little; indeed you are among the companions of the Fire."
39:9 Is one who is devout during the hours of the night, prostrating and standing, bewareful of the Hereafter and hoping for the mercy of his Rabb? Say: "Are those who know equal to those who do not know?" Only the people of understanding remember.
39:10 Say: "O My servants who believe, be mindful of your Rabb. For those who do good in this world is good, and the earth of Allah is spacious. Indeed, the sabirin are paid their reward in full without reckoning."
39:11 Say: "Indeed, I am commanded to worship Allah, devoting the religion exclusively to Allah."
39:12 And I am commanded to be the first of those who submit.
39:13 Say: "Indeed, I fear, if I disobey my Rabb, the punishment of a great day."
39:14 Say: "Allah I worship, devoting my religion exclusively to Allah."
39:15 So worship what you wish besides Allah. Say: "Indeed, the khasirin are those who lose themselves and their families on the Day of Resurrection. Truly, that is the clear loss."
39:16 For them are, from above them, canopies of fire and from beneath them canopies. With this Allah warns the servants of Allah. O My servants, be mindful of Me.
39:17 And those who avoid the false arbiter so as not to worship it, and turn back to Allah — for them is the glad news. So give glad news to My servants.
39:18 Those who listen to the word and follow the best of it — those are the ones Allah has guided, and those are the people of understanding.
39:19 So is one upon whom the word of punishment has become due — can you rescue one who is in the Fire?
39:20 But those who were mindful of their Rabb — for them are chambers, and above them chambers built, with rivers flowing beneath them. A promise of Allah; Allah does not fail the promise.
39:21 Do you not see that Allah sends down from the heaven water, and threads it through as springs in the earth, then brings out with it crops of differing colors; then it withers and you see it yellowed; then Allah makes it crumbled chaff. Indeed, in that is a reminder for people of understanding.
39:22 So is one whose chest Allah has opened to submission, so that he is upon a light from his Rabb? Woe to those whose hearts are hardened against the remembrance of Allah. Those are in clear error.
39:23 Allah has sent down the best hadith as a Kitab, consistent and oft-repeated; the skins of those who stand in awe of their Rabb shiver from it, then their skins and their hearts soften toward the remembrance of Allah. That is the guidance of Allah; with it Allah guides whom Allah wills. And whomever Allah leaves astray, for them there is no guide.
39:24 So can one shield, with their face, from the evil of punishment on the Day of Resurrection? And it will be said to the wrongdoers: "Taste what you used to earn."
39:25 Those before them denied, so the punishment came to them from where they did not perceive.
39:26 So Allah made them taste disgrace in the life of this world; and the punishment of the Hereafter is greater, if only they knew.
39:27 And We have set forth for people in this Quran every example, so that they may remember.
39:28 An Arabic Quran, without crookedness, so that they may be mindful.
39:29 Allah sets forth an example: a man with quarreling partners, and a man wholly devoted to a single man — are the two equal as an example? Praise belongs to Allah. Rather, most of them do not know.
39:30 Indeed, you will die, and indeed, they will die.
39:31 Then indeed, on the Day of Resurrection, before your Rabb, you will dispute.
39:32 So who is more wrong than one who lies about Allah and disbelieves the truth when it comes to him? Is there not in Hell a lodging for the kafirin?
39:33 And the one who brought the truth and confirmed it — those are the muttaqin.
39:34 For them is whatever they wish with their Rabb. That is the recompense of the muhsinin.
39:35 So that Allah may wipe away from them the worst of what they did, and recompense them their reward with the best of what they used to do.
39:36 Is not Allah sufficient for the servant of Allah? Yet they frighten you with those besides Allah. And whomever Allah leaves astray, for them there is no guide.
39:37 And whomever Allah guides — for them there is no misleader. Is not Allah the Almighty, the Rabb of requital?
39:38 And if you ask them who created the heavens and the earth, they will surely say: "Allah." Say: "Have you considered what you call upon besides Allah? If Allah should intend harm for me, can they remove the harm from me? Or if Allah should intend mercy for me, can they hold back from me the mercy of Allah?" Say: "Allah is sufficient for me. Upon Allah rely those who rely."
39:39 Say: "O my people, act according to your station; indeed, I am acting. Soon you will know."
39:40 Who is it that punishment will come to, disgracing them, and a lasting punishment will descend upon them?
39:41 Indeed, We have sent down upon you the Kitab for people, with truth. So whoever is guided — it is for their own nafs; and whoever goes astray — they go astray only against their own nafs. And you are not over them a guardian.
39:42 Allah takes the nafs at the time of their death, and those that have not died, in their sleep. Then Allah holds the nafs on which death has been decreed, and sends the others back until an appointed term. Indeed, in that are ayat for people who reflect.
39:43 Or have they taken intercessors besides Allah? Say: "Even though they possess nothing and do not reason?"
39:44 Say: "All intercession belongs to Allah. To Allah belongs the dominion of the heavens and the earth; then to Allah you will be returned."
39:45 And when Allah alone is mentioned, the hearts of those who do not believe in the Hereafter shrink with aversion; and when those besides Allah are mentioned, at once they rejoice.
39:46 Say: "O Allah, Originator of the heavens and the earth, Knower of the ghayb and the seen, You judge between Your servants in that over which they differ."
39:47 And if those who did wrong had all that is in the earth and the like of it besides, they would offer it as ransom from the evil of punishment on the Day of Resurrection. And there appeared to them from Allah what they did not reckon.
39:48 And the evil of what they earned appeared to them, and there befell them that which they used to mock.
39:49 So when harm touches a human, he calls Us; then when We bestow on him a blessing from Us, he says: "I was given it only because of knowledge." Rather, it is a trial, but most of them do not know.
39:50 Those before them said the same, but what they used to earn availed them nothing.
39:51 So the evils of what they earned struck them. And those of these who did wrong — the evils of what they earned will strike them, and they cannot escape.
39:52 Do they not know that Allah spreads out provision for whom Allah wills and measures it? Indeed, in that are ayat for people who believe.
39:53 Say: "O My servants who have exceeded against themselves, do not despair of the mercy of Allah. Indeed, Allah forgives all sins. Indeed, Allah is the Forgiving, the Raheem."
39:54 And turn back to your Rabb and submit to Allah before the punishment comes to you; then you will not be helped.
39:55 And follow the best of what has been sent down to you from your Rabb, before the punishment comes to you suddenly while you do not perceive.
39:56 Lest a nafs say: "O my regret, for what I fell short in on the side of Allah, and I was indeed among the mockers."
39:57 Or lest it say: "If only Allah had guided me, I would have been among the muttaqin."
39:58 Or lest it say, when it sees the punishment: "If only I had a return, then I would be among the muhsinin."
39:59 Yes indeed, My ayat came to you, but you disbelieved them and were arrogant, and you were among the kafirin.
39:60 And on the Day of Resurrection you will see those who lied about Allah, their faces blackened. Is there not in Hell a lodging for the mutakabbirin?
39:61 And Allah will save those who were mindful, with their triumph. Evil will not touch them, nor will they grieve.
39:62 Allah created everything, and Allah is guardian over everything.
39:63 To Allah belong the keys of the heavens and the earth. And those who disbelieve in the ayat of Allah — those are the khasirin.
39:64 Say: "Is it other than Allah that you command me to worship, O ignorant ones?"
39:65 And it has been revealed to you and to those before you: "If you associate partners, your work will surely be voided, and you will surely be among the khasirin."
39:66 Rather, worship Allah and be among the shakirin.
39:67 And they did not value Allah at the true worth; the whole earth will be in the grasp of Allah on the Day of Resurrection, and the heavens folded in the right hand of Allah. Glory be to Allah, exalted above what they associate.
39:68 And the Trumpet is blown, and whoever is in the heavens and whoever is in the earth is thunderstruck, except whom Allah wills. Then it is blown a second time, and at once they are standing, watching.
39:69 And the earth shines with the light of its Rabb, and the records are laid down, and the nabis and the witnesses are brought, and judgment is made between them with justice, and they are not wronged.
39:70 And every nafs is paid in full for what it did, and Allah knows best what they do.
39:71 And those who disbelieved are driven to Hell in groups, until when they come to it, its gates are opened and its keepers say to them: "Did rusul not come to you from among yourselves, reciting to you the ayat of your Rabb and warning you of the meeting of this day of yours?" They say: "Yes indeed, but the word of punishment has proved true against the kafirin."
39:72 It is said: "Enter the gates of Hell, to abide therein forever." How wretched is the lodging of the mutakabbirin.
39:73 And those who were mindful of their Rabb are driven to the Garden in groups, until when they come to it and its gates are opened and its keepers say to them: "Peace be upon you; you have done well, so enter it, abiding forever."
39:74 And they say: "Praise belongs to Allah, who was true to us in the promise and made us inherit the earth; we settle in the Garden wherever we wish." So how good is the reward of the workers.
39:75 And you see the malakat circling around the arsh, glorifying their Rabb with praise, and judgment is made between them with justice. And it is said: "Praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of the worlds."
40:1 Ha Mim
40:2 The sending down of the Kitab from Allah, the Mighty, the Knowing
40:3 Forgiver of offense, Accepter of repentance, Severe in punishment, of long reach — there is no deity but Allah — to Allah is the arrival
40:4 None disputes concerning the ayat of Allah except those who disbelieved, so let not their moving about in the towns deceive you
40:5 Before them the people of Nuh cried lies, and the parties after them; and every community resolved against their Rasul to seize him, and they disputed with falsehood to refute the truth with it, so I seized them — and how was the punishment
40:6 And thus the word of your Rabb proved true against those who disbelieved: that they are companions of the Fire
40:7 Those who bear the raised-frame and those around it glorify with the praise of their Rabb, and believe in Allah, and seek forgiveness for those who believed: Our Rabb, Your mercy and Your knowledge encompass everything, so forgive those who turned and followed Your way, and shield them from the punishment of the blaze
40:8 Our Rabb, and admit them into the gardens of Eden that You promised them, and those of their fathers and their zawj and their offspring who are salih — truly You are the Mighty, the Wise
40:9 And shield them from the evils; and whomever You shield from the evils that day, You have had mercy on them — and that is the immense win
40:10 Those who disbelieve will be called out: Allah's loathing is greater than your loathing of yourselves, when you were called to belief but you disbelieved
40:11 They will say: Our Rabb, You caused us to die twice and You gave us life twice, so we acknowledge our faults — is there then any way of exit
40:12 That is because, when Allah alone was called upon, you disbelieved; but if partners were associated with Allah, you believed — so the judgment belongs to Allah, the High, the Great
40:13 It is Allah who shows you Allah's ayat and sends down for you provision from the heaven — and none takes heed except whoever turns back
40:14 So call upon Allah, devoting the religion exclusively to Allah, even if the kafirin are averse
40:15 Exalted in degrees, Possessor of the raised-frame — casts the spirit of Allah's command upon whom Allah wills of Allah's servants, to warn of the Day of Meeting
40:16 The day they come forth, nothing of them is concealed from Allah: Whose is the sovereignty this day? Allah's — the One, the Overpowering
40:17 This day every nafs is requited for what it earned; no wrong this day — truly Allah is swift in reckoning
40:18 And warn them of the approaching day, when the hearts are at the throats, held in; the wrongdoers will have no bosom-friend and no intercessor who is obeyed
40:19 Allah knows the khā'ina of the eyes and what the chests conceal
40:20 And Allah judges with the truth; and those whom they call upon besides Allah judge nothing at all — truly Allah is the Hearing, the Seeing
40:21 Have they not journeyed through the land to look how the end was of those before them? They were stronger than them in strength and in tracks through the land, but Allah seized them for their offenses, and they had no shielder from Allah
40:22 That is because their rusul used to come to them with the clear proofs, but they disbelieved, so Allah seized them — truly Allah is powerful, severe in punishment
40:23 And We sent Musa with Our ayat and a clear warrant
40:24 To Fir'awn and Haman and Qarun, but they said: a sorcerer, a liar
40:25 But when he brought them the truth from Us, they said: kill the sons of those who believed with him, and let their nisa live — yet the scheme of the kafirin is nothing but error
40:26 And Fir'awn said: leave me to kill Musa, and let him call upon his Rabb — I fear he may change your religion, or bring corruption to appear in the land
40:27 And Musa said: I seek refuge in my Rabb and your Rabb from every proud one who does not believe in the Day of Reckoning
40:28 And a believing man from Fir'awn's family, who hid his belief, said: will you kill a man because he says my Rabb is Allah, when he has brought you the clear proofs from your Rabb? And if he is a liar, upon him is his lie; and if he is sadiq, some of what he promises you will befall you — truly Allah does not guide whoever is an extravagant liar
40:29 O my people, the kingdom is yours today, manifest in the land — but who will help us against Allah's might if it comes to us? Fir'awn said: I show you nothing but what I see, and I guide you to nothing but the way of right course
40:30 And the mumin said: O my people, I fear for you a day like the day of the parties
40:31 Like the way of the people of Nuh, and Aad, and Thamud, and those after them — and Allah wills no wrong for the servants
40:32 And, O my people, I fear for you the Day of Calling
40:33 A day when you turn back, fleeing — you will have no protector from Allah; and whomever Allah leads astray has no guide
40:34 And Yusuf came to you before with the clear proofs, but you never ceased to doubt what he brought you, until when he perished you said: Allah will never send a Rasul after him — thus does Allah lead astray whoever is extravagant and doubting
40:35 Those who dispute the ayat of Allah without any warrant having come to them — how great is the loathing in the sight of Allah and in the sight of those who believe — thus does Allah set a seal upon every heart of a proud, overpowering one
40:36 And Fir'awn said: Haman, build for me a tower, that I may reach the means
40:37 The means of the heavens, to look over to the deity of Musa — and I think he is a liar — thus was made attractive to Fir'awn the evil of his deed, and he was turned away from the way; and the scheme of Fir'awn was in nothing but ruin
40:38 And the mumin said: O my people, follow me — I will guide you to the way of right course
40:39 O my people, this worldly life is only an enjoyment, and the Last is the abode of settlement
40:40 Whoever does an evil is requited nothing but its like; and whoever does good, male or female, and is a mumin — those will enter the Garden, provided for in it without reckoning
40:41 And, O my people, what is it with me that I call you to deliverance while you call me to the Fire
40:42 You call me to disbelieve in Allah and to associate with Allah that of which I have no knowledge, while I call you to the Mighty, the Forgiving
40:43 No doubt: that to which you call me has no claim in this world or in the Last — and that our return is to Allah, and that the musrifin ones are the companions of the Fire
40:44 Soon you will remember what I say to you; and I entrust my affair to Allah — truly Allah sees the servants
40:45 So Allah shielded him from the evils of what they planned, and the evil of punishment befell Fir'awn's family
40:46 The Fire — they are presented to it morning and evening; and the day the Hour rises: admit Fir'awn's family into the most severe punishment
40:47 And when they argue in the Fire, the weak will say to those who were proud: we were followers for you — will you then avail us against a share of the Fire
40:48 Those who were proud will say: we are all in it — Allah has indeed judged between the servants
40:49 And those in the Fire will say to the keepers of Jahannam: call upon your Rabb to lighten for us a day of the punishment
40:50 They will say: did your rusul not come to you with the clear proofs? They will say: yes indeed. They will say: then call — but the call of the kafirin is in nothing but error
40:51 We will surely help Our rusul and those who believed in this worldly life, and on the day the witnesses rise
40:52 A day when the excuse of the wrongdoers will not benefit them — theirs is the curse, and theirs is the evil abode
40:53 And We gave Musa the guidance, and We made the children of Israel inherit the Kitab
40:54 A guidance and a reminder for people of understanding
40:55 So be of the sabirin — the promise of Allah is true — and seek forgiveness for your offense, and glorify with the praise of your Rabb in the evening and the early mornings
40:56 Those who dispute the ayat of Allah without any warrant having come to them — in their chests is nothing but pride, and they will never reach it — so seek refuge in Allah; truly Allah is the Hearing, the Seeing
40:57 The creation of the heavens and the earth is greater than the creation of people, but most people do not know
40:58 And the unseeing and the seeing are not equal, nor are those who believed and did the good deeds and the evil-doer — little do you remember
40:59 The Hour is coming — no doubt in it — but most people do not believe
40:60 And your Rabb has said: call upon Me — I will answer you; those who scorn My worship will enter Jahannam, entering abject
40:61 It is Allah who made for you the night to rest in, and the day for sight — truly Allah is Possessor of bounty over people, but most people do not give thanks
40:62 That is Allah, your Rabb, Creator of everything — there is no deity but Allah — so how are you turned away
40:63 Thus are turned away those who used to deny the ayat of Allah
40:64 It is Allah who made the earth a settlement for you and the heaven a structure, and formed you and formed you excellently, and provided you from the good things — that is Allah, your Rabb; so blessed be Allah, Rabb of the worlds
40:65 Allah is the Living — there is no deity but Allah — so call upon Allah, devoting the religion exclusively to Allah; praise belongs to Allah, Rabb of the worlds
40:66 Say: I have been forbidden to worship those you call upon besides Allah, since the clear proofs came to me from my Rabb; and I have been commanded to submit to the Rabb of the worlds
40:67 It is Allah who created you from dust, then from a drop, then from a clot; then brings you out a child; then that you reach your strength; then that you become old — and among you is one who dies before — and that you reach an appointed term, that perhaps you may reason
40:68 It is Allah who gives life and causes death; so when Allah decrees an affair, Allah only says to it be, and it is
40:69 Have you not seen those who dispute the ayat of Allah — how they are turned away
40:70 Those who cried lies to the Kutub and to what Our rusul brought — they will soon know
40:71 When the fetters are on their necks, and the chains, they are dragged
40:72 Into the scalding water, then into the Fire they are burnt
40:73 Then it will be said to them: where is that which you used to associate besides Allah
40:74 They will say: they are lost to us — no, we never used to call upon anything before — thus does Allah lead the kafirin astray
40:75 That is because you used to rejoice in the land without truth, and because you used to exult
40:76 Enter the doors of Jahannam, abiding in it — what an evil lodging for the proud
40:77 So be of the sabirin — the promise of Allah is true; so either We show you some of what We promise them, or We take you — to Us they return
40:78 And We sent rusul before you — among them are those whose story We narrated to you, and among them are those whose story We did not narrate to you; and it was not for a Rasul to bring an aya except by Allah's permission — so when Allah's command came, it was judged with the truth, and there the falsifiers lost
40:79 It is Allah who made the cattle for you, that you ride on them and eat from them
40:80 And in them you have benefits, and that you may reach thereby a need in your chests, and upon them and upon the ships you are carried
40:81 And Allah shows you Allah's ayat — so which of Allah's ayat do you disavow
40:82 Have they not journeyed through the land to look how the end was of those before them? They were more numerous than them and stronger in strength and in tracks through the land — but what they earned did not avail them
40:83 But when their rusul came to them with the clear proofs, they rejoiced in the knowledge they had, and there befell them what they used to mock
40:84 But when they saw Our might, they said: we believe in Allah alone, and we disbelieve in what we used to associate
40:85 But their faith did not benefit them when they saw Our might — the way of Allah which has passed before among Allah's servants — and there the kafirin lost
41:1 Ha Mim
41:2 A sending-down from the Rahman, the Rahim
41:3 A Kitab whose ayat are made clear, an Arabic recital for a people who know
41:4 A herald of glad news and a warner; yet most of them turn away, so they do not hear
41:5 They say: our hearts are in covers against what you call us to, and in our ears is a heaviness, and between us and you is a veil; so act — we are acting
41:6 Say: I am only a human like you; it is revealed to me that your ilah is one ilah; so stand upright toward Allah and seek forgiveness from Allah; and woe to the associators
41:7 Those who do not give the alms, and of the Hereafter they are rejectors
41:8 Truly those who believe and do good deeds — for them is a reward unstinted
41:9 Say: do you indeed reject the One who created the earth in 2 days, and set up equals for Allah? That is the Rabb of the worlds
41:10 And Allah set in it anchors from above it, and blessed abundance in it, and measured in it its provision in 4 days, alike for all who ask
41:11 Then Allah turned to the heaven while it was smoke, and said to it and to the earth: come willingly or unwillingly; they said: we come willingly
41:12 So Allah completed them into 7 heavens in 2 days, and revealed in each heaven its command; and We adorned the nearest heaven with lamps as a guard — that is the decree of the Mighty, the Knowing
41:13 So if they turn away, say: I have warned you of a thunderstrike like the thunderstrike of Ad and Thamud
41:14 When the Rasuls came to them from before them and from behind them — worship none but Allah — they said: if our Rabb willed, malakat would be sent down; so we reject what you were sent with
41:15 As for Ad, they grew arrogant in the land without right and said: who is stronger than us in strength? Have they not seen that Allah, who created them, is stronger than them in strength? And they used to deny Our ayat
41:16 So We sent upon them a raging wind on days of misfortune, to let them taste the punishment of disgrace in the life of this world; and the punishment of the Hereafter is more disgraceful — and they will not be helped
41:17 And as for Thamud, We guided them, but they preferred blindness to the guidance, so the thunderstrike of the humiliating punishment seized them for what they used to earn
41:18 And We saved those who believed and were ever mindful
41:19 And on the day Allah's enemies are gathered to the Fire, they are marshaled into it
41:20 Until, when they come to it, their hearing and their sight and their skins testify against them for what they used to do
41:21 And they will say to their skins: why did you testify against us? They will say: Allah gave us speech — Allah who gives speech to all things — who created you the first time; and to Allah you return
41:22 And you were not hiding from your hearing, your sight, and your skins testifying against you; but you thought that Allah does not know much of what you do
41:23 And that is the supposition you supposed about your Rabb; it has ruined you, so you became losers
41:24 So if they endure, the Fire is their lodging; and if they plead for pardon, they are not among the pardoned
41:25 And We assigned to them companions who made attractive to them what was before them and what was behind them; and the word came true against them among nations that passed away before them, of jinn and humans; truly they were losers
41:26 And those who reject say: do not listen to this recital, and drown it in idle talk, so that you may prevail
41:27 So We will surely let those who reject taste a severe punishment, and We will recompense them the worst of what they used to do
41:28 That is the recompense of Allah's enemies: the Fire; for them in it is the abode of eternity, as recompense for their denying Our ayat
41:29 And those who reject will say: our Rabb, show us those who led us astray from among the jinn and humans; we will set them beneath our feet, so that they become among the lowest
41:30 Truly those who said our Rabb is Allah, and then stood firm — the malakat are sent down to them: do not fear and do not grieve, and be glad with the Garden you were promised
41:31 We are your protectors in the life of this world and in the Hereafter; and for you in it is what your nafs desire, and for you in it is what you call for
41:32 A gift sent down from the Forgiving, the Rahim
41:33 And who speaks better than one who calls to Allah, does good, and says: I am among the submitters
41:34 And the good deed and the bad deed are not equal; repel with that which is better, and the one between whom and you there is enmity becomes as though a close friend
41:35 And none is granted it but those who endure, and none is granted it but one of a great portion
41:36 And if a sting from Shaytan stings you, seek refuge with Allah; truly Allah is the Hearing, the Knowing
41:37 And of Allah's ayat are the night and the day and the sun and the moon; do not bow to the sun or to the moon, but bow to Allah who created them, if it is Allah you worship
41:38 And if they grow arrogant, those near your Rabb glorify Allah by night and by day, and they never weary
41:39 And of Allah's ayat is that you see the earth humbled; and when We send down water upon it, it stirs and rises; truly the One who revived it will surely revive the dead; truly Allah is over all things powerful
41:40 Truly those who deviate about Our ayat are not hidden from Us; is one cast into the Fire better, or one who comes secure on the Day of Rising? Do what you will — truly Allah sees what you do
41:41 Truly those who reject the Reminder when it comes to them — and truly it is a mighty Kitab
41:42 Falsehood does not come to it from before it or from behind it; a sending-down from the Wise, the Praiseworthy
41:43 Nothing is said to you but what was said to the Rasuls before you; truly your Rabb is forgiving and possessor of a painful penalty
41:44 Had We made it a recital in a foreign tongue, they would have said: why are its ayat not made clear — is it foreign and clear-Arabic? Say: it is, for those who believe, guidance and healing; and those who do not believe — in their ears is a heaviness, and it is blindness over them; those are the ones called from a far-off place
41:45 And We gave Musa the Kitab, and they differed over it; and had not a word preceded from your Rabb, judgment would have been made between them; and truly they are in a doubting doubt about it
41:46 Whoever does good, it is for their own nafs, and whoever does ill, it is against it; and your Rabb is not zalimun to the servants
41:47 To Allah returns the knowledge of the Hour; no fruit comes out of its husk, and no female bears or gives birth except with Allah's knowledge; and on the day Allah calls them — where are My partners? — they will say: we declare to You that none of us is a witness
41:48 And lost to them is what they used to call upon before, and they will think there is no escape for them
41:49 Man does not weary of calling for good; but if evil touches him, he is despairing, hopeless
41:50 And if We let him taste a mercy from Us after distress that touched him, he will surely say: this is mine; and I do not think the Hour is coming; and if I return to my Rabb, the best is surely mine with Allah; so We will surely tell those who reject what they did, and We will let them taste a hard punishment
41:51 And when We bless man, he turns aside and keeps to his side; and when evil touches him, his calling is wide
41:52 Say: tell me — if it is from Allah and then you reject it, who is more astray than one in far opposition?
41:53 We will show them Our ayat on the horizons and in themselves, until it becomes clear to them that it is the truth; is it not sufficient concerning your Rabb that Allah is witness over all things?
41:54 Truly they are in doubt about meeting their Rabb; truly Allah is muhit over all things
42:1 Ha Mim
42:2 Ayn Sin Qaf
42:3 Thus Allah, the Mighty, the Wise, reveals to you and to those who were before you.
42:4 To Allah belongs what is in the heavens and what is in the earth, and Allah is the Exalted, the Supreme.
42:5 The heavens nearly cleave asunder from above them, and the malakat declare the glory of their Rabb in praise and ask forgiveness for those on earth. Indeed, Allah is the Forgiving, the Raheem.
42:6 And those who take protectors besides Allah — Allah keeps watch over them, and you are not set over them as a disposer.
42:7 And thus We revealed to you an Arabic recitation, that you may warn the mother of the towns and those around it, and warn of the Day of Gathering in which there is no doubt: a party in the Garden and a party in the Blaze.
42:8 And if Allah had willed, Allah would have made them one community; but Allah admits whom Allah wills into mercy, and the wrongdoers have no protector and no helper.
42:9 Or have they taken protectors besides Allah? Yet Allah is the Protector, and Allah gives life to the dead, and Allah is over all things powerful.
42:10 And whatever you differ about in anything, its decision rests with Allah. That is Allah, my Rabb; upon Allah I trust, and to Allah I turn back.
42:11 Originator of the heavens and the earth, Allah made for you zawj from yourselves and pairs from the cattle, spreading you forth thereby. There is nothing like a likeness of Allah, and Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Seeing.
42:12 To Allah belong the keys of the heavens and the earth; Allah spreads provision for whom Allah wills and measures it. Indeed, Allah is knowing of all things.
42:13 Allah laid down for you of the Way what was charged to Nuh, and what We revealed to you, and what We charged to Ibrahim and Musa and Isa: establish the Way and do not divide therein. Grievous to the associators is what you call them to. Allah chooses for it whom Allah wills and guides to it whoever turns back.
42:14 They did not divide except after knowledge came to them, out of envy among themselves. And had not a decree from your Rabb preceded to an appointed term, judgement would have been passed between them. And indeed those who received the Kitab after them are in disquieting doubt about it.
42:15 So to that call, and stand firm as you were commanded, and do not follow their desires, and say: I believe in what Allah has sent down of Kutub, and I was commanded to do justice among you. Allah is our Rabb and your Rabb; for us our deeds and for you your deeds; no proof is there between us and you. Allah will gather us together, and to Allah is the arrival.
42:16 And those who argue about Allah after the answer has been given to it — their argument is void before their Rabb, and upon them is wrath, and for them is a severe punishment.
42:17 Allah is the one who sent down the Kitab with the truth and the Balance. And what tells you — perhaps the Hour is near.
42:18 Those who do not believe in it hasten it on, while those who believe are in awe of it and know that it is the truth. Indeed, those who doubt the Hour are in error far away.
42:19 Allah is subtle-kind to the servants, providing for whom Allah wills, and Allah is the Powerful, the Mighty.
42:20 Whoever wills the tillage of the Hereafter, We increase for that one in their tillage; and whoever wills the tillage of this near world, We give them thereof, but they have in the Hereafter no allotted portion.
42:21 Or have they partners who laid out for them of the Way what Allah did not permit? And were it not for the decisive word, judgement would have been passed between them. And indeed the wrongdoers have a painful punishment.
42:22 You will see the wrongdoers in awe of what they earned, and it will befall them; and those who believed and did the good deeds are in the meadows of the Gardens, having what they wish with their Rabb. That is the great favor.
42:23 That is the good news Allah gives to the servants who believe and do good deeds. Say: I do not ask of you a reward for it except affection among kin; and whoever commits a good deed, We increase for that one good therein. Indeed, Allah is Forgiving, Thankful.
42:24 Or do they say: he fabricated a lie against Allah? But if Allah willed, Allah would seal up your heart; and Allah blots out the falsehood and establishes the truth by Allah's words. Indeed, Allah is knowing of what the chests hold.
42:25 And Allah is the one who accepts repentance from the servants and passes over the bad deeds, and knows what you do.
42:26 And Allah answers those who believe and do good deeds, and increases them of favor; and for the kafirin is a severe punishment.
42:27 And if Allah spread out provision for the servants, they would transgress in the earth; but Allah sends down in measure what Allah wills. Indeed, Allah is aware of, seeing, the servants.
42:28 And Allah is the one who sends down the rain after they despaired, and unfolds Allah's mercy, and Allah is the Protector, the Praised.
42:29 And among the ayat of Allah are the creation of the heavens and the earth, and the creatures scattered through them, and Allah is powerful over gathering them whenever Allah wills.
42:30 And whatever affliction befalls you is for what your hands earned, and Allah passes over much.
42:31 And you cannot go beyond power in the earth, and you have besides Allah no protector and no helper.
42:32 And among the ayat of Allah are the ships that run in the sea, like towering landmarks.
42:33 If Allah wills, Allah stills the wind, and they become lying motionless upon its surface. Indeed in that are ayat for everyone sabir, grateful.
42:34 Or Allah destroys them for what they earned, and passes over much.
42:35 And Allah lets those who dispute about Our ayat know that they have no escape-way.
42:36 So whatever you are given of anything, enjoy the life of this near world; but what is with Allah is better and more lasting, for those who believe and trust in their Rabb.
42:37 And those who avoid the great sins and the obscenitys, and when they are angry, they forgive.
42:38 And those who answer their Rabb, establish the prayer, whose affair is consultation among them, and spend of what We provided them.
42:39 And those who, when wrong afflicts them, stand up for themselves.
42:40 And the recompense of a bad deed is a bad deed like it; but whoever passes over and sets things right, their reward is upon Allah. Indeed, Allah does not love the wrongdoers.
42:41 And whoever stands up for themselves after being wronged — those have no way of blame against them.
42:42 Blame is only upon those who wrong the people and transgress in the earth without right. Those have a painful punishment.
42:43 And whoever is sabir and forgives — indeed that is of the resolve of great affairs.
42:44 And whomever Allah leads astray has no protector after that. And you will see the wrongdoers, when they see the punishment, saying: is there any way of return?
42:45 And you will see them presented before it, humbled by abasement, looking with a hidden glance. And those who believed will say: indeed the khasirin are those who lose themselves and their families on the Day of Rising. Indeed the wrongdoers are in a lasting punishment.
42:46 And they have no protectors to help them besides Allah, and whomever Allah leads astray has no way.
42:47 Answer your Rabb before a day comes for which there is no turning back from Allah. You have no refuge that day, and no one to disavow for you.
42:48 So if they turn away, We did not send you as a keeper over them; upon you is only the conveyance. And when We let a human taste mercy from Us, that one rejoices in it; and if a bad deed afflicts them for what their hands sent forth, then indeed the human is ungrateful.
42:49 To Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth; Allah creates what Allah wills; Allah grants to whom Allah wills females, and grants to whom Allah wills males.
42:50 Or Allah pairs them, males and females, and makes whomever Allah wills barren. Indeed, Allah is knowing, powerful.
42:51 It is not for a human that Allah should speak to them except by revelation, or from behind a veil, or by sending a Rasul who reveals by Allah's permission what Allah wills. Indeed, Allah is the Exalted, the Wise.
42:52 And thus We revealed to you a spirit of Our command. You did not know what the Kitab was, nor faith, but We made it a light by which We guide whom We will of Our servants. And indeed you guide to a straight path.
42:53 The path of Allah, to whom belongs what is in the heavens and what is in the earth. Indeed, to Allah all affairs arrive.
43:1 Ha Mim
43:2 By the clear Kitab
43:3 We have made it an Arabic recitation, so that you may reason
43:4 And it is in the mother of the Kitab with Us, exalted and wise
43:5 Shall We then turn the remembrance away from you in pardon, since you are a people exceeding?
43:6 How many a Nabi We sent among the former ones
43:7 No Nabi came to them, but they used to mock him
43:8 So We destroyed those stronger than them in might, and the example of the former ones passed on
43:9 If you ask them who created the heavens and the earth, they will surely say: "The Mighty, the Knowing created them"
43:10 The one who made the earth a bed for you and made for you ways therein, so that you may be guided
43:11 And the one who sent down from the heaven water in measure, and We raised with it a dead town — thus you are brought out
43:12 And the one who created all the pairs, and made for you from the ships and the cattle what you ride
43:13 So that you sit firm upon their backs, then remember the favor of your Rabb when you are settled upon it, and say: "Glory to the one who made this subject for us, and we were not bound with it"
43:14 And to our Rabb we are indeed returning
43:15 They made for Allah a share of Allah's servants — indeed the human is clearly committing kufr
43:16 Has Allah taken, from what Allah creates, daughters, and chosen you with sons?
43:17 And when one of them is given glad tidings of what is set forth as a likeness for the Raheem, his face becomes black while he holds it in
43:18 Or one raised amid adornment, who in a dispute cannot make things plain?
43:19 They made the malakat, who are servants of the Rahman, females. Did they witness their creation? Their witness will be written, and they will be asked
43:20 They said: "If the Rahman had willed, we would not have worshipped them." They have no knowledge of that — they only conjecture
43:21 Or did We bring them a Kitab before it, to which they hold fast?
43:22 No, but they said: "We found our fathers upon a way, and we are guided upon their tracks"
43:23 Likewise, We never sent a warner into a town before you but its softened ones said: "We found our fathers upon a way, and we are following their tracks"
43:24 Say: "What if I bring you better guidance than that upon which you found your fathers?" They said: "We disbelieve in that with which you were sent"
43:25 So We requited them. See how was the outcome of the mukazzibin
43:26 And when Ibrahim said to his father and his people: "I am clear of what you worship"
43:27 Except the One who originated me — that One will guide me
43:28 And Allah made it a lasting word among his posterity, so that they may return
43:29 But We gave enjoyment to these and their fathers, until the truth came to them, and a Rasul making clear
43:30 And when the truth came to them, they said: "This is sihr, and we disbelieve in it"
43:31 They said: "Why was this recitation not sent down upon a great man from one of the two towns?"
43:32 Do they divide the mercy of your Rabb? We divided among them their livings in the worldly life, and We raised some of them above others by degrees, so that some of them take others in subjection. And the mercy of your Rabb is better than what they gather
