43:33 Were it not that people would become one community, We would have made for whoever disbelieves in the Rahman roofs of silver for their houses, and stairways upon which they mount
43:34 And for their houses doors, and couches upon which they recline
43:35 And adornment — yet all of that is but the enjoyment of the worldly life, and the hereafter with your Rabb is for those who guard
43:36 And whoever turns away from the remembrance of the Rahman, We assign to that one a devil, and it becomes a companion to that one
43:37 They bar them from the way, while they think they are guided
43:38 Until, when they come to Us, one says: "If only between me and you there were the distance of the two easts." What an evil companion!
43:39 It will not benefit you today, since you wronged, that you share in the punishment
43:40 Can you then make the deaf hear, or guide the unseeing, and whoever is in clear straying?
43:41 So if We take you away, We shall requite them
43:42 Or We show you that which We promised them — We are powerful over them
43:43 So hold fast to what was revealed to you — you are on a straight path
43:44 It is a reminder for you and for your people, and soon you will be asked
43:45 Ask those of Our rusul We sent before you: did We ever make deities besides the Rahman to be worshipped?
43:46 Indeed We sent Musa with Our ayat to Fir'awn and his chiefs, and he said: "I am the Rasul of the Rabb of the worlds"
43:47 And when he brought them Our ayat, they laughed at them
43:48 We showed them no aya but that it was greater than its sister, and We seized them with the punishment, so that they might return
43:49 They said: "O sorcerer, call on your Rabb for us according to the pledge with you — we shall be guided"
43:50 And when We uncovered the punishment from them, they broke the pledge
43:51 Fir'awn called out among his people, saying: "My people, is not the kingdom of the Riverland mine, and these rivers flowing beneath me? Do you not see?"
43:52 Or am I not better than this one, who is despised and can hardly make plain?
43:53 Why were not bracelets of gold cast upon him, or the malakat come with him joined?
43:54 So he made his people take it lightly, and they obeyed him — they were fasiqin
43:55 And when they grieved Us, We requited them and drowned them all
43:56 And We made them a precedent and an example for the later ones
43:57 And when the son of Mary was set forth as an example, your people turned away from it
43:58 They said: "Are our deities better, or is he?" They only set it forth to you for dispute — they are a contentious people
43:59 He is only a servant whom We favored, and We made him an example for the children of Israel
43:60 If We willed, We could make malakat among you, succeeding one another in the earth
43:61 He is knowledge for the Hour — so do not doubt it — and follow Me. This is a straight path
43:62 Let not the devil turn you aside — the devil is to you a clear enemy
43:63 And when Isa came with the clarity, he said: "I have brought you wisdom, and I will make plain to you some of what you differ about. So guard Allah and obey me"
43:64 Indeed Allah is my Rabb and your Rabb, so worship Allah. This is a straight path
43:65 The parties differed among themselves. Woe to those who wronged from the punishment of a painful day
43:66 Do they wait for anything but the Hour, that it comes to them suddenly while they do not perceive?
43:67 Friends on that day are enemies to one another, except the guarded ones
43:68 Servants, no fear upon you today, nor will you grieve
43:69 Those who believed in Our ayat and were submitting
43:70 Enter the garden, you and your zawj, in delight
43:71 They are moved around among with sheets of gold and cups, and therein is what nafs desire and eyes delight in, and therein you abide forever
43:72 That is the garden you have inherited for what you used to do
43:73 In it you have abundant fruit, from which you eat
43:74 Indeed the guilty are in the punishment of hell, abiding forever
43:75 It is never lightened for them, and they are despair-struck in it
43:76 We did not wrong them, but they were the ones who wronged
43:77 They will call out: "O warden, let your Rabb decree an end for us." It will be said: "You are staying"
43:78 We brought you the truth, but most of you are averse to the truth
43:79 Or have they settled a matter? Then We are settling
43:80 Or do they think We do not hear their secret and their private talk? Yes indeed, and Our rusul are with them recording
43:81 Say: "If the Raheem had a child, I would be the first of the servants"
43:82 Glory to the Rabb of the heavens and the earth, the Rabb of the high seat, above what they describe
43:83 So leave them to wade idle and play, until they meet the day they are promised
43:84 And Allah is the one who is a deity in the heaven and a deity in the earth — and Allah is the Wise, the Knowing
43:85 Blessed is the one to whom belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth and what is between them; with Allah is the knowledge of the Hour, and to Allah you return
43:86 Those they call upon besides Allah possess no intercession — except one who bears witness to the truth while they know
43:87 And if you ask them who created them, they will surely say: "Allah." So how are they turned away?
43:88 And the word of Allah: "My Rabb, these are a people who do not believe"
43:89 So pardon them and say: "Peace." Soon they will know
44:1 Ha Mim
44:2 By the clear Kitab
44:3 We sent it down on a blessed night — We had been warners
44:4 In it is made distinct every wise decree
44:5 A command from Us — We were the senders
44:6 A mercy from your Rabb — Allah is the All-Hearing, the All-Knowing
44:7 Rabb of the heavens and the earth and all that lies between them, if you are certain
44:8 There is no ilah but Allah: Allah gives life and gives death — Rabb of you and Rabb of your forefathers of old
44:9 But they are in doubt, playing around
44:10 So watch for the day when the heaven brings clear smoke
44:11 It will cover the people: "This is a painful punishment."
44:12 Our Rabb, lift this punishment from us — we are muminin
44:13 How can they take the reminder, when a clear Rasul has come to them?
44:14 Then they turned away from him and said: "A tutored madman!"
44:15 We shall lift the punishment a little — you are sure to fall back
44:16 On the day We seize with the greatest seizing, We shall be requiting
44:17 And indeed We tested Fir'awn's people before them, and a noble Rasul came to them,
44:18 Saying: "Give up the servants of Allah — I am a trustworthy Rasul to you,
44:19 And do not rise up over Allah — I bring you a clear warrant,
44:20 And I seek refuge with my Rabb and your Rabb from your stoning me.
44:21 And if you do not believe me, then keep apart from me."
44:22 So he called his Rabb: "These are a guilty people."
44:23 So travel by night with My servants — you will surely be followed
44:24 And leave the sea behind, becalmed — they are a drowned host
44:25 How many gardens and springs they left behind
44:26 And crops, and a noble dwelling
44:27 And blessings in which they took delight
44:28 So it was; and We had another people inherit it all
44:29 Nor did the heaven or the earth weep for them, nor were they given respite
44:30 And We did rescue the Children of Israel from the humiliating punishment,
44:31 From Fir'awn — he was arrogant, among the transgressors
44:32 And We chose them with knowledge, above all peoples
44:33 And We gave them ayat in which was a clear test
44:34 Indeed, these people say:
44:35 "There is nothing but our first death, and we shall not be raised."
44:36 So bring back our fathers, if you are sadiq
44:37 Are they better, or the people of Tubba and those before them? We destroyed them — they were a guilty people
44:38 We did not create the heavens and the earth and what lies between them in play
44:39 We created them only in truth, yet most of them do not know
44:40 The Day of Decision is the appointed time for them all
44:41 The day when a protector shall not avail a protector anything, and they will not be helped
44:42 Except one upon whom Allah has mercy — Allah is the Mighty, the Raheem
44:43 The tree of Zaqqum
44:44 Is the food of the guilty,
44:45 Like scalding mire, boiling in the bellies,
44:46 Like the seething of scalding water
44:47 Seize him and drag him to the heart of the blaze
44:48 Then pour over his head the punishment of scalding water
44:49 Taste — you it is who were the mighty, the noble!
44:50 This is what you used to doubt
44:51 The guarded ones are in a secure abode,
44:52 In gardens and springs,
44:53 Clothed in fine silk and rich brocade, facing one another,
44:54 So it is; and We shall wed them to fair-eyed companions,
44:55 There they call for every fruit, in safety
44:56 They taste no death there except the first death, and the punishment of the blaze is warded from them,
44:57 As a bounty from your Rabb — that is the great triumph
44:58 We have made it easy upon your tongue, so that they may remember
44:59 So keep watch — they are watching
45:1 Ha Mim
45:2 The sending-down of the Kitab is from Allah, the Almighty, the Wise.
45:3 Indeed, in the heavens and the earth are ayat for the muminin.
45:4 And in your creation, and in what is scattered of creatures, are ayat for people who are certain.
45:5 And in the alternation of the night and the day, and in what Allah sends down from the heaven of provision and brings the earth to life with it after its death, and in the directing of the winds — are ayat for people who reason.
45:6 These are the ayat of Allah, which We recite to you in truth. So in what hadith after Allah and the ayat of Allah will they believe?
45:7 Woe to every fabricating sinner.
45:8 Who hears the ayat of Allah recited to him, then persists, obstinate, as if he had not heard them — so give him tidings of a painful punishment.
45:9 And when he knows anything of Our ayat, he takes them in mockery. Those — for them is a humiliating punishment.
45:10 Behind them is Hell, and nothing of what they earned avails them, nor what they took besides Allah as protectors; and for them is a great punishment.
45:11 This is guidance. And those who disbelieve in the ayat of their Rabb — for them is a punishment of painful affliction.
45:12 It is Allah who subjected the sea for you, so that the ships sail in it by the command of Allah, and so that you may seek of the bounty of Allah, and so that you may be grateful.
45:13 And Allah has subjected to you whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth, all from Allah. Indeed, in that are ayat for people who reflect.
45:14 Say to those who believe to forgive those who do not hope for the days of Allah, that Allah may repay a people for what they used to earn.
45:15 Whoever does salihness, it is for their own nafs; and whoever does evil, it is against it. Then to your Rabb you will be returned.
45:16 And We gave the Children of Israel the Kitab, and judgment, and Nabihood, and We provided them from the good things, and favored them above the worlds.
45:17 And We gave them clear proofs of the matter, and they did not differ except after the knowledge came to them, out of envy among themselves. Indeed, your Rabb will judge between them on the Day of Resurrection concerning what they used to differ about.
45:18 Then We placed you upon a laid-course of the matter; so follow it, and do not follow the desires of those who do not know.
45:19 Indeed, they will never avail you anything against Allah. And the wrongdoers — some of them are protectors of others; and Allah is the protector of the salihin.
45:20 These are insights for people, and guidance and mercy for people who are certain.
45:21 Or do those who commit the evil deeds think that We will make them like those who believe and do the salihin deeds — equal in their living and their dying? Evil is what they judge.
45:22 And Allah created the heavens and the earth in truth, so that every nafs may be repaid for what it earned; and they will not be wronged.
45:23 Have you seen the one who has taken desire as a ilah, and Allah led that one astray upon knowledge, and sealed over the hearing and the heart, and placed a covering over the sight? So who can guide that one after Allah? Then will you not remember?
45:24 And they say: "There is nothing but our worldly life; we die and we live, and nothing destroys us but time." And they have no knowledge of that; they only assume.
45:25 And when Our ayat are recited to them as clear proofs, their only argument is that they say: "Bring our forefathers, if you are sadiq."
45:26 Say: "Allah gives you life, then brings you to death, then gathers you to the Day of Resurrection, in which there is no doubt — but most people do not know."
45:27 And to Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth; and on the day the Hour rises, that day the falsifiers will lose.
45:28 And you will see every nation kneeling; every nation will be called to its record: "Today you are repaid for what you used to do."
45:29 "This is Our record, speaking to you in truth. We were recording what you used to do."
45:30 So as for those who believed and did the salihin deeds, their Rabb will admit them into mercy. That is the clear winning.
45:31 But as for those who disbelieved: "Were My ayat not recited to you, yet you were arrogant and were a mujrim people?"
45:32 And when it is said: "The promise of Allah is true, and the Hour — there is no doubt in it," you say: "We do not know what the Hour is; we only assume an assumption, and we are not certain."
45:33 And the evils of what they did become manifest to them, and what they used to mock encircles them.
45:34 And it will be said: "Today We forget you, as you forgot the meeting of this day of yours; and your destination is the Fire, and you have no helpers."
45:35 That is because you took the ayat of Allah in mockery, and worldly life deceived you. So today they will not come out of it, nor will they be allowed to seek appeasement.
45:36 So to Allah belongs all praise — Rabb of the heavens, and Rabb of the earth, Rabb of the worlds.
45:37 And grandeur belongs to Allah in the heavens and the earth; Allah is the Almighty, the Wise.
46:1 Ha Mim
46:2 The sending down of the Kitab, from Allah, the Mighty, the Wise.
46:3 We did not create the heavens and the earth and what is between them except in truth and for an appointed term, and those who disbelieve turn away from that of which they are warned.
46:4 Say: Tell me about those you call upon besides Allah — show me what they created of the earth, or do they have a share in the heavens? Bring me a the Kitab from before this, or a trace of knowledge, if you are sadiq.
46:5 And who is further astray than one who calls upon, besides Allah, those who do not answer him until the Day of Rising? And they are unmindful of their calling.
46:6 And when people are gathered, those called upon will be enemies to them, and will disbelieve that they were ever worshipped.
46:7 And when our clear ayat are recited to them, those who disbelieve say, when the truth comes to them: This is clear sihr.
46:8 Or do they say: He fabricated it? Say: If I fabricated it, you possess for me nothing against Allah. Allah knows best what you pour forth into it. Sufficient is Allah as a witness between me and you, and Allah is the Forgiving, the Raheem.
46:9 Say: I am not an innovation among the Rasuls, and I do not know what will be done with me or with you. I follow only what is revealed to me, and I am nothing but a clear warner.
46:10 Say: Have you considered — if it is from Allah and you disbelieve it, and a witness from the Children of Israel bears witness to the like of it and believes, while you grow arrogant? Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.
46:11 And those who disbelieve say to those who believe: If it were any good, they would not have preceded us to it. And since they were not guided by it, they will say: This is an old fabrication.
46:12 And before it was the Kitab of Musa, a guide and a mercy, and this is a confirming the Kitab in a clear Arabic tongue, to warn those who wronged and as glad tidings for the muhsinin.
46:13 Those who say: Our Rabb is Allah, and then stand firm — no fear is upon them, nor will they grieve.
46:14 Those are the companions of the garden, abiding in it forever, as repayment for what they used to do.
46:15 We enjoined the human to kindness toward his parents: his mother carried him unwillingly and bore him unwillingly, and his carrying and his weaning are 30 months, until, when he reaches his full strength and reaches 40 years, he says: My Rabb, inspire me to thank for Your blessing which You bestowed upon me and upon my parents, and to do salih work that You approve, and make my descendants salih. I have repented to You, and I am one of those who submit.
46:16 Those are the ones from whom we accept the best of what they did and pass over their misdeeds, among the companions of the garden — the true promise they were promised.
46:17 And the one who says to his parents: Fie upon you both! Do you promise me that I will be brought forth, when generations have passed away before me? And they both cry to Allah for help: Woe to you! Believe — the promise of Allah is true. But he says: This is nothing but tales of the ancients.
46:18 Those are the ones upon whom the word proved true, among the nations that passed away before them, of jinn and humankind. They were the khasirin.
46:19 And for each are degrees according to what they did, and their deeds will be paid to them in full, and they will not be wronged.
46:20 And the day the kafirin are presented to the fire: You exhausted your good things in the life of this world and enjoyed them. So today you are repaid the punishment of humiliation for what you used to do arrogantly on earth without truth, and for what you used to tafsuquna.
46:21 And remember the brother of Aad, when he warned his people in Al-Ahqaf — though warners had passed before him and after him — that you worship none but Allah; I fear for you the punishment of a tremendous day.
46:22 They said: Have you come to us to turn us away from our alihah? Then bring us what you promise us, if you are of the sadiqin.
46:23 He said: Knowledge rests only with Allah, and I convey to you what I was sent with; but I see that you are a people who are ignorant.
46:24 Then, when they saw it as a cloud advancing toward their valleys, they said: This is a cloud that will bring us rain. Rather, it is what you hastened — a wind in which is painful punishment.
46:25 It razes everything by the command of its Rabb, and by morning nothing was to be seen but their dwellings. Thus we repay the mujrim people.
46:26 We had established them in what we have not established you, and gave them hearing and sights and fu'ad; yet their hearing, their sights, and their fu'ad availed them nothing when they denied the ayat of Allah, and what they used to mock befell them.
46:27 We destroyed the settlements around you and turned the ayat toward them, that they might turn back.
46:28 So why did those not help them — the ones they took as alihah besides Allah, seeking nearness through them? Rather, they were lost to them, and that was their fabrication and what they used to invent.
46:29 And when we turned to you a band of the jinn listening to the recitation: when they were present at it, they said: Listen quietly. And when it was finished, they returned to their people as warners.
46:30 They said: O our people, we have heard a the Kitab sent down after Musa, confirming what is before it, guiding to the truth and to a straight path.
46:31 O our people, answer the caller of Allah and believe in Allah; Allah will forgive you your offenses and shield you from a painful punishment.
46:32 Whoever does not answer the caller of Allah can never escape on earth, and will have no protectors besides Allah. Those are in clear error.
46:33 Do they not see that Allah, who created the heavens and the earth and was not wearied by creating them, is able to give life to the dead? Yes, Allah is over everything able.
46:34 And the day the kafirin are presented to the fire: Is this not the truth? They will say: Yes, by our Rabb. It will be said: Then taste the punishment for what you used to disbelieve.
46:35 So be of the sabirin, as those of resolve among the Rasuls were sabir, and do not hasten for them. On the day they see what they are promised, it will be as though they tarried but an hour of a day. The message has been conveyed. Are any destroyed but the fasiqin?
47:1 Those who disbelieve and bar the path of Allah — their works are lost.
47:2 And those who believe and do the right deeds, and believe in what was sent down upon Muhammad — and it is the truth from their Rabb — Allah wipes away their bad deeds and sets their state right.
47:3 That is because those who disbelieve followed falsehood, and those who believe followed the truth from their Rabb. Thus does Allah set forth for the people their parables.
47:4 So when you meet those who disbelieve, strike the necks, until when you have struck them down heavily, bind fast the bonds. Then, afterward, either an act of favor or ransom, until the war lays down its burdens. That is so; and if Allah willed, Allah would take victory over them, but rather so that Allah may test some of you by means of others. And those who are slain in the path of Allah — their works will never be lost.
47:5 Allah will guide them and set their state right.
47:6 And admit them into the garden, whose ways Allah has made known to them.
47:7 O you who believe: if you help Allah, Allah will help you and make your feet firm.
47:8 And those who disbelieve — wretchedness is theirs, and their works are lost.
47:9 That is because they loathed what Allah sent down, so their works are void.
47:10 Have they not journeyed through the land to see how was the end of those before them? Allah razed them, and for the kafirin there is the like of it.
47:11 That is because Allah is the protector of those who believe, and the kafirin have no protector.
47:12 Allah will admit those who believe and do the right deeds into gardens beneath which rivers flow. And those who disbelieve take their enjoyment and eat as the cattle eat — and the fire is their lodging.
47:13 How many a town, stronger in might than your town which expelled you, have We destroyed — and no helper was there for them.
47:14 Is one who stands upon clear proof from their Rabb like those whose evil deeds are made to look attractive to them, and who follow their desires?
47:15 The likeness of the garden promised to the muttaqin: in it rivers of water, never stale; and rivers of milk whose taste never changes; and rivers of wine, a delight for the drinkers; and rivers of strained honey. And theirs therein is every fruit, and forgiveness from their Rabb. Is that like one who abides forever in the fire, given scalding water to drink, that cuts through their bowels?
47:16 And among them are those who listen to you, until when they leave your presence they say to those given knowledge: "What did he say just now?" Those are the ones upon whose hearts Allah has set a seal, and they follow their desires.
47:17 And those who are guided — Allah increases them in guidance and gives them their mindfulness.
47:18 So do they wait for anything but the Hour, that it come upon them suddenly? Its signs have already come. How, then, will it be for them when their reminder comes to them?
47:19 So know that there is no ilah but Allah, and seek forgiveness for your offense, and for the believing men and the believing nisa. And Allah knows your comings and goings and your lodgings.
47:20 And those who believe say: "Why has no chapter been sent down?" Then, when a firm chapter is sent down and fighting is mentioned in it, you see those in whose hearts is sickness looking at you with the look of one covered over by death. They would be better off.
47:21 Obedience and a kind word. Then, when the matter is resolved, if only they had been true to Allah, it would have been better for them.
47:22 So might it be that, if you turn away, you would spread corruption in the land and sever your kin-ties?
47:23 Those are the ones Allah has cursed — made them deaf and blinded their sight.
47:24 Do they not reflect upon the Recitation, or are there locks upon their hearts?
47:25 Those who turned back on their heels after the guidance had become clear to them — Shaytan beguiled them and gave them respite.
47:26 That is because they said to those who loathed what Allah sent down: "We will obey you in part of the matter." And Allah knows their secrets.
47:27 So how will it be when the malakat take them, striking their faces and their backs?
47:28 That is because they followed what angered Allah and loathed Allah's approval — so their works are void.
47:29 Or do those in whose hearts is sickness suppose that Allah will never bring out their rancor?
47:30 And if We willed, We would show them to you, and you would know them by their marks. And you will surely know them by the slant of their speech. And Allah knows your works.
47:31 And We will surely test you, until We know the strivers among you and the sabirin, and We test your report.
47:32 Those who disbelieve and bar the path of Allah and oppose the Rasul after the guidance has become clear to them will never harm Allah at all, and their works will be void.
47:33 O you who believe: obey Allah and obey the Rasul, and do not void your works.
47:34 Those who disbelieve and bar the path of Allah, then die while they are kafirin — Allah will never forgive them.
47:35 So do not falter and call for peace while you have the upper hand. Allah is with you, and Allah will never let your works go to waste.
47:36 The life of this world is nothing but play and diversion. And if you believe and are mindful, your rewards will be given to you, and Allah will not ask you for your wealth.
47:37 If Allah were to ask you for it and press you hard, you would stint, and your rancor would be brought out.
47:38 Here you are, called to spend in the path of Allah, yet among you are those who stint. Whoever stints only stints against himself — Allah is the Self-Sufficient, and you are the needy. And if you turn away, Allah will substitute another people in your place, and they will not be the likes of you.
48:1 Indeed We have bestowed on you a clear triumph
48:2 So that Allah may forgive you the offense that came before and the offense that comes after, and complete the blessing upon you, and guide you on a straight path
48:3 And Allah will help you with a mighty help
48:4 Allah is the One who sent down sakina into the hearts of the muminin, so that they increase in faith along with their faith; to Allah belong the hosts of the heavens and the earth, and Allah has always been knowing, wise
48:5 So that Allah may admit the believing men and the believing nisa into gardens beneath which rivers flow, to stay forever in them, and wipe their evil deeds away from them — and that is, with Allah, a tremendous win
48:6 And Allah will punish the munafiq men and the munafiq nisa, and the associating men and the associating nisa who think of Allah the thought of evil; the turn of evil circles upon them, and the wrath of Allah is upon them, and Allah has cursed them, and prepared hell for them — and evil is their arrival
48:7 And to Allah belong the hosts of the heavens and the earth, and Allah has always been mighty, wise
48:8 We have sent you as a witness, a bearer of good news, and a warner
48:9 So that you believe in Allah and in Allah's Rasul, and give Allah support, and honor Allah, and glorify Allah morning and evening
48:10 Those who make a deal with you are in fact making a deal with Allah; the hand of Allah is above their hands; so whoever breaks the pledge, breaks it only against themselves, and whoever fulfills what was pledged to Allah, Allah will give them a tremendous reward
48:11 Those who stayed behind of the desert Arabs will say to you: our wealth and our families kept us busy, so ask forgiveness for us. They say with their tongues what is not in their hearts. Say: who can own anything for you against Allah, if Allah intends harm for you or intends benefit for you? No — Allah has always been aware of what you do
48:12 You thought that the Rasul and the muminin would never return to their families, and that was adorned in your hearts, and you thought the thought of evil, and you were a people ruined
48:13 And whoever does not believe in Allah and in Allah's Rasul — We have prepared a blaze for the unmuminin
48:14 And to Allah belongs the dominion of the heavens and the earth; Allah forgives whomever Allah wills and punishes whomever Allah wills, and Allah has always been forgiving, merciful
48:15 The ones who stayed behind will say, when you set out to take the gains: let us follow you. They want to substitute the word of Allah. Say: you will never follow us. This is what Allah declared to you before. Then they will say: no, you envy us. No — they understood only a little
48:16 Say to the desert Arabs who stayed behind: you will be called against a people of severe fighting — you will kill them or they will submit. If you obey, Allah will give you a good reward; but if you turn away as you turned away before, Allah will punish you with a painful punishment
48:17 There is no blame on the blind, no blame on the lame, and no blame on the sick; whoever obeys Allah and Allah's Rasul, Allah will admit them into gardens beneath which rivers flow, and whoever turns away, Allah will punish them with a painful punishment
48:18 Allah was pleased with the muminin when they made a deal with you under the tree; Allah knew what was in their hearts, so Allah sent down sakina upon them and rewarded them with a near triumph
48:19 And many gains they will take — and Allah has always been mighty, wise
48:20 Allah promised you many gains that you will take, so Allah hastened this one for you and held back the hands of people from you, so that it may be an aya for the muminin and so that Allah may guide you on a straight path
48:21 And others that you had no power over — Allah has ahat them, and Allah has always been able over everything
48:22 If those who disbelieved had fought you, they would have turned their backs, and then found no protector and no helper
48:23 Such is the way of Allah that has passed before, and you will never find any change in the way of Allah
48:24 And Allah is the One who held back their hands from you and your hands from them, in the inner part of Makkah, after Allah had given you victory over them; and Allah sees all that you do
48:25 They are the ones who disbelieved and barred you from the sacred mosque, and the offering held back from reaching its place. Were it not for believing men and believing nisa you did not know — that you might trample them and disgrace strike you from them without knowledge — so that Allah may admit whomever Allah wills into Allah's mercy. Had they stood apart, We would have punished those of them who disbelieved with a painful punishment
48:26 When those who disbelieved put the blaze in their hearts — the blaze of the pagan way — then Allah sent down Allah's sakina upon Allah's Rasul and upon the muminin, and bound them fast to the word of ilahfearing, and they were most deserving of it and most worthy of it; and Allah has always been knowing of everything
48:27 Allah has made the Rasul's vision come true in truth: you will surely enter the sacred mosque, if Allah wills, secure, shaving your heads and cutting your hair short, without fear. Allah knew what you did not know, and appointed, besides that, a near triumph
48:28 Allah is the One who sent Allah's Rasul with the guidance and the religion of truth, to make it appear over all religion — and Allah is sufficient as a witness
48:29 Muhammad is the Rasul of Allah, and those with him are hard against the unmuminin, merciful among themselves. You see them bowing, prostrating, seeking bounty from Allah and Allah's pleasure. Their mark is on their faces, from the trace of prostration. That is their likeness in the Tawrah, and their likeness in the Injil: like a crop that sends out its shoot, then strengthens it, then grows hard, then stands straight on its stalk, so that the growers marvel — so that Allah may enrage the unmuminin with them. Allah has promised those of them who believe and do salih deeds forgiveness and a tremendous reward
49:1 O you who believe, do not go ahead of Allah and Allah's Rasul, and be mindful of Allah. Allah hears and knows.
49:2 O you who believe, do not raise your voices above the voice of the Nabi, and do not speak loudly to him as some of you speak loudly to one another, lest your deeds be voided while you perceive nothing.
49:3 Those who lower their voices in the presence of Allah's Rasul are the ones whose hearts Allah has tested for mindfulness. Theirs is forgiveness and a great reward.
49:4 Those who call out to you from behind the chambers — most of them do not reason.
49:5 If only they had waited sabirly until you came out to them, it would have been better for them. Allah is forgiving, merciful.
49:6 O you who believe, if a fasiq comes to you with a report, verify it, so that you do not strike a people out of ignorance and end up regretting what you did.
49:7 Know that among you is Allah's Rasul. If he obeyed you in much of the matter, you would fall into hardship. But Allah has made faith dear to you and adorned it in your hearts, and made unbelief, fusuq, and disobedience hateful to you. Those are the ones on the right course.
49:8 A bounty from Allah and a blessing; Allah is knowing, wise.
49:9 If two parties of muminin fight each other, make peace between them. If one of them aggresses against the other, then fight the one that aggresses until it turns back to the command of Allah. And if it turns back, make peace between them with justice and act equitably. Allah loves the equitable.
49:10 The muminin are but brothers, so make peace between your two brothers, and be mindful of Allah, so that you may be shown mercy.
49:11 O you who believe, let no people mock a people — they may be better than them — and let no nisa mock nisa — they may be better than them. Do not belittle one another, and do not call one another by nicknames. Wretched is the name of fusuq after faith. Whoever does not repent — those are the wrongdoers.
49:12 O you who believe, avoid much suspicion, for some suspicion is sin. Do not spy, and let none of you backbite another. Would any of you like to eat the flesh of his dead brother? You would loathe it. Be mindful of Allah. Allah is ever-accepting of repentance, merciful.
49:13 O people, We created you from a male and a female, and made you into peoples and tribes so that you may know one another. The most honored of you with Allah is the most mindful of Allah among you. Allah is knowing, aware.
49:14 The desert Arabs said: "We believe." Say: "You do not believe; rather say: we have submitted." For faith has not yet entered your hearts. But if you obey Allah and Allah's Rasul, nothing will be detracted from your deeds. Allah is forgiving, merciful.
49:15 The muminin are only those who believe in Allah and Allah's Rasul, then do not doubt, and strive with their wealth and their lives in the way of Allah. Those are the sadiqin.
49:16 Say: "Do you make Allah know your religion, when Allah knows what is in the heavens and what is in the earth? Allah knows everything."
49:17 They hold it as a favor over you that they submitted. Say: "Do not hold your submission up as a favor. Rather, Allah holds it as a favor over you that Allah guided you to faith, if you are sadiq."
49:18 Allah knows the ghayb of the heavens and the earth, and Allah sees all that you do.
50:1 Qaf. By the Qur'an, the glorious.
50:2 No, they marvel that a warner from among them has come to them; the kafirin said, "This is a marvellous thing."
50:3 "When we have died and become dust? That is a far return."
50:4 We know what the earth diminishes of them, and with Us is a guarded record.
50:5 No, they denied the truth when it came to them, so they are in a mixed-up affair.
50:6 Have they not looked at the heaven above them — how We built it and adorned it, and it has no openings?
50:7 And the earth, We spread it out and cast into it anchors, and We grew in it of every kind, splendid.
50:8 An insight and a reminder for every returning servant.
50:9 And We sent down from the heaven water blessed, and We grew with it gardens and grain of the harvest.
50:10 And palms towering, with spathes in clusters.
50:11 A provision for the servants; and We revived with it a dead town. Likewise is the coming-forth.
50:12 Before them the people of Nuh denied, and the companions of ar-Rass, and Thamud,
50:13 and Aad, and Fir'awn, and the brethren of Lut,
50:14 and the companions of the Thicket, and the people of Tubba'. Each denied the Rasuls, so the warning came true.
50:15 Were We worn out by the first creation? No, they are in doubt about a new creation.
50:16 And We created the human, and We know what his nafs whispers to him, and We are nearer to him than the rope of the vein.
50:17 When the two receivers receive, on the right and on the left, seated.
50:18 Not a word is uttered but with him is a watcher, ready.
50:19 And sakrat al-mawt comes with the truth. That is what you used to turn away from.
50:20 And the trumpet is blown. That is the Day of the Warning.
50:21 And every nafs comes, with it a driver and a witness.
50:22 You were indeed unmindful of this, so We uncovered your covering from you, and your sight today is hadid.
50:23 And its companion says, "This is what is ready with me."
50:24 Cast into Jahannam every stubborn kafir,
50:25 withholder of good, transgressor, doubter,
50:26 who set up with Allah another ilah — so cast him into the severe punishment."
50:27 His companion says, "Our Rabb, I did not make him transgress, but he was in far error."
50:28 It is said, "Do not contend in My presence, for I already brought you the warning.
50:29 The word is not changed with Me, and I am not zalimun to the servants."
50:30 The Day We say to Jahannam, "Are you filled?" and it says, "Is there more?"
50:31 And the Garden is brought near to the muttaqin, not far.
50:32 "This is what you were promised, for every returning watcher,
50:33 who revered the Rahman in the ghayb and came with a returning heart.
50:34 Enter it in peace. That is the Day of Eternity.
50:35 They have therein what they wish, and with Us is more."
50:36 And how many generations We destroyed before them who were harder than them in might; they tunnelled through the land — is there any escape?
50:37 Indeed in that is a reminder for one who has a heart, or who casts the hearing while witnessing.
50:38 And We created the heavens and the earth and what is between them in six days, and no weariness touched Us.
50:39 So be of the sabirin over what they say, and glorify with the praise of your Rabb before the rising of the sun and before the setting,
50:40 and in the night glorify Allah, and after the prostration.
50:41 And listen for the Day the Caller calls from a near place.
50:42 The Day they hear the shout in truth — that is the Day of the Coming-Out.
50:43 Indeed it is We who give life and cause death, and to Us is the arrival.
50:44 The Day the earth splits from them swiftly — that is a gathering easy for Us.
50:45 We know best what they say, and you are not an overpowerer over them. So remind, by the Qur'an, whoever fears the warning.
51:1 By the scatterers scattering,
51:2 and the bearers of a heavy load,
51:3 and the runners running with ease,
51:4 and the apportioners of the affair —
51:5 that which you are promised is true,
51:6 and the Reckoning will surely befall.
51:7 By the heaven with its woven courses,
51:8 you are in discordant speech,
51:9 turned away from it, whoever turns away.
51:10 Doom to the conjecturers,
51:11 those who are in a fog, unheeding.
51:12 They ask: "When is the Day of Reckoning?"
51:13 The Day they are burned upon the Fire:
51:14 "Taste your trial — this is what you used to hasten."
51:15 Indeed, the vigilant are in gardens and springs,
51:16 taking what their Rabb gave them; they were before that muhsinin.
51:17 Little of the night would they sleep,
51:18 and in the small hours they would seek forgiveness.
51:19 And in their wealth is a due for the asker and the deprived.
51:20 And in the earth are ayat for those of certainty —
51:21 and in yourselves; will you not see?
51:22 And in the heaven is your provision and what you are promised.
51:23 So by the Rabb of the heaven and the earth, it is surely true, just as you speak.
51:24 Has the hadith of the honored guests of Ibrahim reached you?
51:25 When they entered upon him and said, "Peace," he said: "Peace — a people unknown to us."
51:26 He turned aside to his household and came with a fat calf,
51:27 brought it near to them, and said: "Will you not eat?"
51:28 Then he sensed fear from them; they said: "Do not fear," and gave him glad tidings of a knowing boy.
51:29 Then his imra'a came forward in obstinacy, struck her face, and said: "An jawza, barren!"
51:30 They said: "Thus has your Rabb said — Allah is the Wise, the Knowing."
51:31 He said: "So what is your affair, O mursalun?"
51:32 They said: "We have been sent to a guilty people,
51:33 to send upon them stones of clay,
51:34 marked by your Rabb for the exceeders."
51:35 Then We brought out whoever of the muminin was in it;
51:36 We found none in it but one house of the submitted.
51:37 And We left in it an aya for those who fear the painful punishment.
51:38 And in Musa — when We sent him to Fir'awn with a clear warrant;
51:39 then he turned away with his backing and said: "A sorcerer or a madman."
51:40 So We seized him and his hosts and cast them into the sea, and he was blameworthy.
51:41 And in Ad — when We sent upon them the barren wind;
51:42 it left nothing it came upon without making it like decayed rubble.
51:43 And in Thamud — when it was said to them: "Take enjoyment until a time."
51:44 Then they defied the command of their Rabb, so the thunderstrike seized them as they looked on.
51:45 They could not stand up at all, nor were they the ones given victory.
51:46 And the people of Nuh before — they were fasiqin.
51:47 And the heaven — We built it with strength, and We are the Vast.
51:48 And the earth — We spread it; how excellent are the bed-makers.
51:49 And of everything We created two, that you may remember.
51:50 So flee to Allah — I am a clear warner to you from Allah.
51:51 And do not set up with Allah another ilah — I am a clear warner to you from Allah.
51:52 Likewise, no Rasul came to those before them but they said: "A sorcerer or a madman."
51:53 Did they exhort one another with it? No — they were a transgressing people.
51:54 So turn away from them; you are not to be blamed.
51:55 And remind — indeed, the reminder benefits the muminin.
51:56 I created jinn and humankind only to serve Allah;
51:57 I want no provision from them, nor do I want them to feed Me.
51:58 Allah is the Provider, the One Firm in strength.
51:59 So those who wronged will have a guilt like the guilt of their companions — let them not hasten.
51:60 So woe to those who disbelieve, from their Day which they are promised.

52:2 and by written lines
52:3 on a sheet spread open
52:4 and by the House built and kept
52:5 and by the roof raised up
52:6 and by the sea set ablaze
52:7 truly the punishment of Allah is befalling
52:8 nothing can push it back
52:9 the day the heaven churns in turmoil
52:10 and the mountains are set moving
52:11 woe that day to the mukazzibin
52:12 who wade in idle play
52:13 the day they are called to the fire of Hell with a call
52:14 "This is the fire you used to deny
52:15 is this sihr, or do you not see
52:16 enter it and endure, or do not endure — it is all the same to you; you are only repaid what you used to do
52:17 truly the ilah-fearing are in gardens and bliss
52:18 delighting in what Allah gave them, and Allah protected them from the punishment of the blaze
52:19 "Eat and drink in delight for what you used to do
52:20 reclining on couches ranged in rows, and We pair them with fair-eyed companions
52:21 and those who believed, whose offspring followed them in belief — We join their offspring with them, and We will not cheat them of anything of their deeds. Every person is held as a pledge for what he earned
52:22 and We supply them with fruit and flesh of whatever they desire
52:23 they pass a cup around among themselves — no idle talk is in it, and no sinning
52:24 and their boys move among them as though they were pearls kept hidden
52:25 and they turn to one another, asking
52:26 They say: "Once, among our people, we lived in awe
52:27 so Allah favored us and protected us from the punishment of the scorching blast
52:28 we used to call upon Allah before. Truly Allah is the Good, the Raheem
52:29 So remind — for by the favor of Allah you are no soothsayer, nor mad
52:30 or do they say: a poet — one for whom we await the turn of death
52:31 Say: wait — I am waiting with you
52:32 is it their minds that command them this, or are they a transgressing people
52:33 or do they say: you have simply said it? No, they do not believe
52:34 so let them bring a hadith like it, if they are sadiq
52:35 were they created without a thing, or are they the creators
52:36 or did they create the heavens and the earth? No, they have no certainty
52:37 or do they have the storehouses of Allah, or are they the ones in control
52:38 or do they have a ladder by which they listen? Then let their listener bring a clear warrant
52:39 or does Allah have daughters while you have sons
52:40 or do you ask them a reward, so that they are weighed down with liability
52:41 or do they have the ghayb, so they are writing it down
52:42 or do they intend a kayd? Those who kafara — they are the makīdūn
52:43 or do they have a ilah other than Allah? Glorified is Allah above all they associate
52:44 and if they see a fragment of the heaven falling, they say: piled-up clouds
52:45 so leave them until they meet their day in which they are struck down
52:46 a day when their scheming will avail them nothing, nor will they be helped
52:47 and there is a punishment besides that for those who did wrong, but most of them do not know
52:48 so be of the sabirin for the judgment of Allah, for you are in Our sight, and glorify with the praise of Allah when you rise

53:1 By the star when it plunges
53:2 Your companion has not strayed, nor been misled
53:3 Nor does he speak from desire
53:4 It is nothing but revelation revealed
53:5 Taught by one mighty in power
53:6 One of enduring strength, then he poised himself evenly
53:7 While at the highest horizon
53:8 Then he drew near and hung low
53:9 And was two bow-lengths away, or nearer
53:10 Then Allah revealed to Allah's servant what was revealed
53:11 The heart did not lie about what it saw
53:12 Will you then doubt what he sees
53:13 And certainly he saw it in another descent
53:14 At the Lote Tree of the utmost limit
53:15 Near it is the Garden of Shelter
53:16 When that which covers covered the Lote Tree
53:17 The eye neither swerved nor transgressed
53:18 He certainly saw some of the greatest ayat of his Rabb
53:19 Have you seen al-Lat and al-Uzza
53:20 And Manat, the third, the other
53:21 Is yours the male and Allah the female
53:22 That indeed would be an unfair division
53:23 They are nothing but names which you and your fathers named; Allah has sent down no warrant for them; they follow only conjecture and what the selves desire, yet guidance has certainly come to them from their Rabb
53:24 Or shall the human have whatever the self wishes
53:25 To Allah belong the Last and the First
53:26 And how many malakat are in the heavens whose intercession avails nothing, except after Allah gives permission for whomever Allah wills and is pleased with
53:27 Indeed those who do not believe in the Last name the malakat with the naming of the female
53:28 And they have no knowledge of it; they follow only conjecture, and conjecture avails nothing against the truth
53:29 So turn away from whoever turns away from Allah's reminder and wants only the nearer life
53:30 That is the utmost of their knowledge; indeed your Rabb knows best who strays from Allah's way, and your Rabb knows best who is guided
53:31 And to Allah belongs whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth, so that Allah may recompense those who do evil for what they did, and recompense those who do good with the best
53:32 Those who avoid the great sins and the obscenitys, except for small faults; indeed your Rabb is abundant in forgiveness, knowing you best when you were raised up from the earth and when you were embryos in the bellies of your mothers; so do not claim purity for yourselves; your Rabb knows best who guards
53:33 Have you seen the one who turned away
53:34 And gave a little, then held back
53:35 Does he have knowledge of the ghayb, so he sees
53:36 Or was he not told of what is in the Kitabten sheets of Musa
53:37 And Ibrahim who fulfilled
53:38 That no bearer of a load bears the load of another
53:39 And that the human shall have nothing but what the self strives for
53:40 And that the striving shall soon be seen
53:41 Then the fullest recompense is given
53:42 And that to your Rabb is the ultimate end
53:43 And that Allah is the one who makes laugh and makes weep
53:44 And that Allah is the one who gives death and gives life
53:45 And that Allah created the two kinds, the male and the female
53:46 From a drop when it is emitted
53:47 And that upon Allah is the second raising
53:48 And that Allah is the one who enriches and grants sufficiency
53:49 And that Allah is the Rabb of Sirius
53:50 And that Allah destroyed the first Ad
53:51 And Thamud, so nothing remained
53:52 And the people of Nuh before; indeed they were more zalimun and more transgressing
53:53 And the overturned cities, Allah plunged them
53:54 So that which covers covered them
53:55 So which of your Rabb's bounties do you doubt
53:56 This is a warner from among the former warners
53:57 The imminent has drawn near
53:58 There is none besides Allah to uncover it
53:59 Do you then marvel at this hadith
53:60 And you laugh and do not weep
53:61 And you are heedless
53:62 So prostrate yourselves to Allah and worship
54:1 The Hour has drawn near and the moon has split.
54:2 But if they see an aya they turn away and say: "A persistent sihr."
54:3 And they denied and followed their desires; and every matter is settled.
54:4 And news has come to them containing a deterrent.
54:5 Far-reaching wisdom, yet the warnings do not avail.
54:6 So turn away from them. On the day the caller calls to something dreadful,
54:7 their eyes humbled, they come out of the graves as if they were locusts spread abroad,
54:8 hastening to the caller. The rejectors say: "This is a day of hardship."
54:9 Before them the people of Nuh denied; they denied Our servant, saying: "A madman," and drove him away.
54:10 So he called to his Rabb: "I am overcome," and he was granted victory.
54:11 So We opened the gates of the heaven with water poured down,
54:12 and We burst the earth with springs, and the waters met upon a decree decreed.
54:13 And We carried him upon a vessel of planks and nails,
54:14 running under Our watch — a recompense for one who had rejected.
54:15 And We left it as an aya; so is there any who will remember?
54:16 So how was My punishment and My warnings?
54:17 And We have made the Recitation easy for remembrance; so is there any who will remember?
54:18 Ad denied; so how was My punishment and My warnings?
54:19 We sent upon them a screaming wind on a day of unrelenting ill-omen,
54:20 plucking up people as if they were trunks of uprooted palms.
54:21 So how was My punishment and My warnings?
54:22 And We have made the Recitation easy for remembrance; so is there any who will remember?
54:23 Thamud denied the warnings.
54:24 And they said: "Are we to follow a mortal, one from among us? Then we would be in error and in a blaze."
54:25 Shall the Reminder be cast upon him from among us? Rather, he is an insolent liar.
54:26 They will know by morning who the insolent liar is.
54:27 We are sending the naqah as a trial for them; so watch them and be of the sabirin.
54:28 And tell them that the water is to be divided among them: every draught is brought in turn.
54:29 So they called their companion, and he took in hand and hamstrung her.
54:30 So how was My punishment and My warnings?
54:31 We sent upon them a single shout, and they became like trampled chaff.
54:32 And We have made the Recitation easy for remembrance; so is there any who will remember?
54:33 The people of Lut denied the warnings.
54:34 We sent upon them a burning blast — except the family of Lut; We rescued them at dawn,
54:35 a favor from Us; thus We repay the shakir.
54:36 And We warned them of Our seizing, but they cast doubt on the warnings.
54:37 And they demanded his guests of him, so We blotted out their eyes: "Taste My punishment and My warnings."
54:38 And there came upon them, in the early morning, an abiding punishment.
54:39 "Taste My punishment and My warnings."
54:40 And We have made the Recitation easy for remembrance; so is there any who will remember?
54:41 And the warnings came to the family of Fir'awn.
54:42 They denied all Our ayat, so We seized them with a seizure mighty and overwhelming.
54:43 Are your rejectors better than those of old, or do you have immunity in the Kitab?
54:44 Or do they say: "We are a host, triumphant"?
54:45 The host shall be routed and they will turn their backs.
54:46 Nay, the Hour is their tryst, and the Hour is more calamitous and more bitter.
54:47 Indeed the mujrimin are in error and in a blaze.
54:48 On the day they are dragged in the fire upon their faces: "Taste the touch of Saqr."
54:49 Indeed We created everything by measure,
54:50 and Our command is but one, like the blink of an eye.
54:51 And We destroyed your own kind; so is there any who will remember?
54:52 And everything they did is in the Kitab,
54:53 and every small and great thing is written down.
54:54 Indeed the ilah-fearing are in a garden and a river,
54:55 in a seat of truth, in the presence of an all-powerful Sovereign.
