56:1 When the Befalling befalls.
56:2 There is no giving the lie to its occurrence.
56:3 It brings some low and raises others high.
56:4 When the earth is shaken.
56:5 And the rocky landforms are leveled and scattered.
56:6 And they become scattered dust.
56:7 And you become 3 companies.
56:8 And the companions of the right — what of the companions of the right!
56:9 And the companions of the left — what of the companions of the left!
56:10 And the sabiqun, the sabiqun!
56:11 Those are the muqarrabun.
56:12 In the garden of delight.
56:13 A company of the ancients.
56:14 And a few of the later ones.
56:15 Upon inlaid couches.
56:16 Reclining on them, facing one another.
56:17 Eternal youths move about among them.
56:18 With cups and ewers and a cup from a running spring.
56:19 They suffer no splitting head from it and no draining.
56:20 And fruit of whatever they choose.
56:21 And flesh of birds from whatever they desire.
56:22 And fair-eyed ones.
56:23 Like pearls concealed.
56:24 A reward for what they used to do.
56:25 They hear in it no idle talk and no word of sin.
56:26 Only a saying: "Peace, peace."
56:27 And the companions of the right — what of the companions of the right!
56:28 In pruned lote-trees.
56:29 And banana-trees ranged in rows.
56:30 And shade spread out.
56:31 And water poured forth.
56:32 And fruit in plenty.
56:33 Never cut off and never withheld.
56:34 And couches raised high.
56:35 We raised them up with a raising.
56:36 And made them maidens.
56:37 Fair and equal in age.
56:38 For the companions of the right.
56:39 A company of the ancients.
56:40 And a company of the later ones.
56:41 And the companions of the left — what of the companions of the left!
56:42 In scorching wind and scalding water.
56:43 And shade of scalding smoke.
56:44 Neither cool nor generous.
56:45 They were before that softened by ease.
56:46 And they persisted obstinately in the great sin.
56:47 And they used to say: "What! When we die and become dust and bones, are we indeed to be raised?
56:48 And our earliest fathers?"
56:49 Say: the ancients and the later ones —
56:50 will be gathered to the appointed time of a known day.
56:51 Then you, the straying ones, the mukazzibin!
56:52 You will eat from the zaqqum-tree.
56:53 Filling your bellies from it.
56:54 And drinking over it scalding water.
56:55 Drinking the drink of the thirst-maddened.
56:56 This is their lodging on the day of reckoning.
56:57 We created you — so why do you not believe?
56:58 Have you considered what you emit?
56:59 Is it you who create it, or are We the Creators?
56:60 We decreed death among you, and We cannot be outstripped.
56:61 So that We may substitute the likes of you and raise you up in what you do not know.
56:62 You have surely known the first raising — so why do you not remember?
56:63 Have you considered what you till?
56:64 Is it you who sow it, or are We the sowers?
56:65 If We willed, We would make it crumbled, and you would remain laughing.
56:66 We are in liability indeed!
56:67 Nay, we are deprived!
56:68 Have you considered the water you drink?
56:69 Is it you who sent it down from the clouds, or are We the senders-down?
56:70 If We willed, We would make it bitter — so why do you not give thanks?
56:71 Have you considered the fire you kindle?
56:72 Is it you who raised up its tree, or are We the raisers-up?
56:73 We made it a reminder and a provision for those who draw strength.
56:74 So glorify the name of your Rabb the Great.
56:75 I swear by the falling-place of the stars.
56:76 And it is indeed a great oath, if only you knew.
56:77 It is a noble recitation.
56:78 In concealed scrolls.
56:79 None touch it but the purified.
56:80 A sending-down from the Rabb of all worlds.
56:81 Do you take this hadith so lightly?
56:82 And you make your living out of denying it.
56:83 Why then, when it reaches the throat,
56:84 and you, at that moment, are looking on,
56:85 and We are nearer to it than you, but you do not see —
56:86 so if you are not to be called to account,
56:87 send it back, if you are sadiq.
56:88 But if such a one is among the brought-near,
56:89 then rawh and rayhan, and a garden of delight.
56:90 And if such a one is among the companions of the right,
56:91 then: "Peace to you," from the companions of the right.
56:92 And if such a one is among the mukazzibin, the strayers,
56:93 then a lodging of scalding water,
56:94 and entering into the blaze.
56:95 Indeed this is the truth of certainty.
56:96 So glorify the name of your Rabb the Great.
57:1 All that is in the heavens and the earth glorifies Allah, and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
57:2 For Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth; Allah gives life and brings death, and Allah is over all things powerful.
57:3 Allah is the First and the Last, the Outward and the Inward, and Allah is knowing of all things.
57:4 Allah is the one who created the heavens and the earth in six days, then settled upon the high-seat; Allah knows what enters into the earth and what comes out of it, and what descends from the heaven and what ascends into it, and Allah is with you wherever you are, and Allah sees all that you do.
57:5 For Allah belongs the kingdom of the heavens and the earth, and to Allah the affairs return.
57:6 Allah enters the night into the day and enters the day into the night, and Allah is knowing of what is within the chests.
57:7 Believe in Allah and in Allah's Rasul, and spend of what Allah has made you successors to; those of you who believe and spend — for them is a great reward.
57:8 What is the matter with you that you do not believe in Allah, when the Rasul calls you to believe in your Rabb? And Allah has already taken your pledge, if you are muminin.
57:9 Allah is the one who sends down upon Allah's servant clear ayat, to bring you out from darknesses into the light; and indeed Allah is toward you gentle in mercy, merciful.
57:10 What is the matter with you that you do not spend in the way of Allah, when to Allah belongs the inheritance of the heavens and the earth? Not equal among you are those who spent before the triumph and fought; those are greater in degree than those who spent after and fought. Yet to each Allah has promised the most beautiful, and Allah is aware of all that you do.
57:11 Who is it that will loan Allah a good loan, so that Allah may multiply it for them, and for them is a generous reward?
57:12 On the day you see the believing men and believing nisa, their light hastening before them and on their right hands: glad tidings to you this day of gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever. That is the great win.
57:13 On the day the munafiqin, men and nisa, say to those who believed: "Wait for us, let us touch some of your light," it will be said: "Go back behind yourselves and seek a light." Then a wall is struck between them, having a door: its inner side holds mercy, and its outer side, facing them, holds the punishment.
57:14 They will call to them: "Were we not with you?" They will say: "Yes, but you put yourselves to trial and you waited and doubted, and idle wishes deceived you until the command of Allah came, and the deceiver deceived you about Allah."
57:15 So today no ransom is taken from you, nor from those who disbelieved. Your shelter is the fire — it is your master — and what an evil arrival.
57:16 Has the time not come for those who believed for their hearts to be humbled to the remembrance of Allah and to what has descended of the truth, and not to be like those who were given the Kutub before? The length grew long upon them and their hearts hardened, and many of them are fasiqun.
57:17 Know that Allah gives life to the earth after its death; We have made the ayat clear to you, so that you may reason.
57:18 Indeed the musaddiqin men and musaddiqat nisa who loan Allah a good loan — it will be multiplied for them, and for them is a generous reward.
57:19 Those who believe in Allah and in Allah's rusul — those are the sadiqin and the witnesses with their Rabb; theirs is their reward and their light. And those who disbelieve and deny our ayat — those are the companions of the blaze.
57:20 Know that the life of this world is only play and diversion and adornment, and vaunting among yourselves, and vying in increase in wealth and in children: like a rain-shower whose growth marvels the kafirin, then it withers and you see it yellow, then it becomes crumbled. And in the final world is severe punishment and forgiveness from Allah and pleasure. And the life of this world is nothing but the provision of deception.
57:21 Race ahead toward forgiveness from your Rabb and toward a garden whose width is as the width of the heaven and the earth, prepared for those who believe in Allah and in Allah's rusul. That is the bounty of Allah, which Allah gives to whom Allah wills, and Allah is the possessor of the great bounty.
57:22 No calamity strikes in the earth or in yourselves except it is in a Kitab before We bring it forth; indeed that is easy for Allah.
57:23 So that you may not grieve over what has escaped you, nor rejoice over what has come to you; and Allah does not love every conceited vaunter.
57:24 Those who stint and order people to stint; and whoever turns away — then indeed Allah is the rich, the praised.
57:25 We have surely sent our rusul with clear proofs, and sent down with them the Kitab and the scale, so that people may stand in equity; and We sent down the hadid, in which is severe strength and benefit for people, and so that Allah may know who helps Allah and Allah's rusul in the ghayb. Indeed Allah is powerful, mighty.
57:26 We have surely sent Nuh and Ibrahim, and placed Nabihood and the Kutub among their descendants; some of them are guided, and many of them are fasiqun.
57:27 Then We followed in their tracks with our rusul, and We followed with Isa son of Maryam, and gave him the gospel, and placed in the hearts of those who followed him gentle mercy and mercy. And a monasticism they invented — We did not write it upon them — except as seeking the pleasure of Allah, yet they did not keep it with its due keeping. So We gave those of them who believed their reward, and many of them are fasiqun.
57:28 O you who believe, be mindful of Allah and believe in Allah's Rasul; Allah will give you a double portion of Allah's mercy, and make for you a light by which you walk, and forgive you. And Allah is forgiving, merciful.
57:29 So that the people of the Kitab may know that they have power over nothing of the bounty of Allah, and that the bounty is in Allah's hand; Allah gives it to whom Allah wills, and Allah is the possessor of the great bounty.
58:1 Allah has heard the words of the imra'a who disputes with you about her zawj and complains to Allah. Allah hears your exchange with each other; truly Allah hears and sees.
58:2 Those of you who declare of their nisa that they are not their mothers — their mothers are none but those who gave birth to them. Yet they say a disavowed word and a swerving one; yet Allah passes over and forgives.
58:3 And those who declare of their nisa and then go back on what they said — then the freeing of a neck before the two touch one another. That is what you are admonished by, and Allah is aware of what you do.
58:4 So whoever does not find the means, then a fast of two consecutive months before the two touch one another; and whoever is unable, then feeding sixty of the poor. That is so that you may trust in Allah and the Rasul. And those are the hadud of Allah, and for the kafirin there is a painful punishment.
58:5 Those who yuhadduna Allah and the Rasul shall be brought low, just as those before them were brought low. We have already sent down clear ayat, and for the kafirin there is a humiliating punishment.
58:6 On the day Allah raises them all together, then tells them what they did. Allah counted it while they forgot it, and Allah is witness over all things.
58:7 Do you not see that Allah knows what is in the heavens and what is in the earth? No private talk of three takes place but Allah is the fourth of them, and no private talk of five but Allah is the sixth of them, and nothing less than that or more but Allah is with them wherever they are. Then Allah will tell them what they did on the day of standing. Truly Allah knows all things.
58:8 Do you not see those who were forbidden from private talk, yet they go back to what they were forbidden from, and whisper together in sin and hostility and disobedience to the Rasul? And when they come to you, they greet you with what Allah never greeted you with, and they say to themselves: why does Allah not punish us for what we say? Sufficient for them is Jahannam — they enter it. How evil an arrival.
58:9 O you who trust, when you whisper together, do not whisper in sin and hostility and disobedience to the Rasul, but whisper in goodness and mindfulness. And be mindful of Allah, to whom you shall be gathered.
58:10 Private talk is only from Shaytan, to grieve those who trust; yet it harms them nothing except by Allah's leave. And upon Allah let the trusting place their trust.
58:11 O you who trust, when you are told to make room in the gatherings, then make room; Allah will make room for you. And when it is said rise up, then rise up. Allah raises up those among you who trust, and those given knowledge, by degrees; and Allah is aware of what you do.
58:12 O you who trust, when you whisper with the Rasul, offer ahead of your whisper a donation. That is better for you and purer. And if you do not find the means, then Allah is forgiving, merciful.
58:13 Were you in awe of offering ahead of your whispers donations? So, since you did not do it and Allah relented toward you, then establish the prayer, and give the alms, and obey Allah and the Rasul; and Allah is aware of what you do.
58:14 Do you not see those who turn toward a people upon whom Allah's wrath rests? They are neither of you nor of them, and they swear to the falsehood while they know.
58:15 Allah has prepared for them a severe punishment; evil indeed is what they used to do.
58:16 They took their oaths as a shield, and turned others away from the path of Allah; so for them there is a humiliating punishment.
58:17 Nor will their wealth or their children avail them anything against Allah. Those are the companions of the fire; they abide in it forever.
58:18 On the day Allah raises them all together, they will swear to Allah as they swear to you, and they think they stand upon something. Truly they — they are the kazibin.
58:19 Shaytan gained mastery over them and made them forget the remembrance of Allah. Those are the party of Shaytan. Truly the party of Shaytan — they are the khasirin.
58:20 Those who yuhadduna Allah and the Rasul — those are among the abased.
58:21 Allah has written: I and the rusul of Allah shall surely overcome. Truly Allah is strong, mighty.
58:22 You will not find a people who trust in Allah and the last day showing affection to those who hadda Allah and the Rasul, even if they are their fathers or their sons or their brothers or their kin. Those are the ones in whose hearts trust is written, and whom Allah strengthened with a spirit from Allah. And Allah will enter them into gardens beneath which rivers flow, to abide therein forever. Allah is pleased with them and they are pleased with Allah. Those are the party of Allah. Truly the party of Allah — they are the ones who attain success.
59:1 Whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth glorifies Allah, and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
59:2 It is Allah who drove out those who disbelieved among the people of the Kitab from their dwellings at the first gathering. You did not think they would leave, and they thought their fortresses would hold them back from Allah; but Allah came upon them from where they did not reckon, and cast terror into their hearts. They tore down their houses with their own hands and with the hands of the muminin; so take lesson, O people of vision.
59:3 Had Allah not decreed exile upon them, Allah would have punished them in this world; and they have in the hereafter the punishment of the Fire.
59:4 That is because they opposed Allah and Allah's Rasul; and whoever opposes Allah should know that Allah is severe in punishment.
59:5 Whatever palm-trees you cut down or left standing on their roots, it was by Allah's leave, so that Allah might disgrace the fasiqin.
59:6 And whatever Allah turned back to Allah's Rasul from them, you charged against it neither horses nor riding mounts; rather, Allah sets Allah's rusul over whom Allah wills, and Allah has power over all things.
59:7 And whatever Allah turns back to the Rasul from the people of the towns belongs to Allah and to the Rasul, and to near kin, to orphans, to the needy, and to the wayfarer, so that it may not keep circulating among the rich among you. And whatever the Rasul gives you, take it; and whatever the Rasul forbids you, refrain from it. And be mindful of Allah; truly Allah is severe in punishment.
59:8 It is for the needy muhajirin who were driven out of their dwellings and their possessions, seeking bounty from Allah and approval, and helping Allah and Allah's Rasul. Those are the true ones.
59:9 And those who settled in the dwelling and in faith before them love whoever emigrates to them, and they find no want in their hearts for what they were given, and they prefer others above themselves, even though need be upon them. And whoever is preserved from the stinginess of their own nafs — those are the ones who attain success.
59:10 And those who came after them say: Our Rabb, forgive us and our brothers who preceded us in faith, and place no rancor in our hearts toward those who believe. Our Rabb, truly Allah is gentle in mercy and mercy-giving.
59:11 Have you not regarded the munafiqin saying to their brothers among the people of the Kitab who disbelieved: If you are driven out, we will surely go out with you, and we will never obey anyone against you; and if you are fought against, we will surely help you. And Allah bears witness that they are truly liars.
59:12 If they are driven out, they will not go out with them; and if they are fought against, they will not help them; and even if they help them, they will surely turn their backs, and then they will find no help.
59:13 You are indeed a greater dread in their hearts than Allah. That is because they are a people who do not understand.
59:14 They will not fight you all together except in fortified towns or from behind walls. Their might among themselves is severe; you reckon them united, yet their hearts are diverse. That is because they are a people who do not reason.
59:15 They are like those who came shortly before them: they tasted the heavy cost of their course, and they have a painful punishment.
59:16 Like the satan when it said to the human: Disbelieve. And when the human disbelieved, it said: I disown you; I fear Allah, Rabb of the worlds.
59:17 So the end of them both is that they are in the Fire, abiding in it forever; and that is the recompense of the wrongdoers.
59:18 O you who believe, be mindful of Allah, and let every nafs look to what it has sent ahead for the morrow. And be mindful of Allah; truly Allah is fully aware of what you do.
59:19 And do not be like those who forgot Allah, so Allah made them forget themselves. Those are the fasiqun.
59:20 Not equal are the companions of the Fire and the companions of the Garden. The companions of the Garden are the winners.
59:21 If We had sent down this recitation upon a mountain, you would have seen it humbled, splitting apart from awe of Allah. And these are the likenesses We strike for the people, so that they may reflect.
59:22 That is Allah: there is no ilah but Allah, the Knower of the hidden and the witnessed. Allah is the Compassionate, the Raheem.
59:23 That is Allah: there is no ilah but Allah — the Sovereign, the Holy, the Peace, the Giver of faith, the Watcher, the Mighty, the Overpowering, the Most High. Allah is exalted far above what they associate.
59:24 That is Allah, the Creator, the Bringer-forth, the Former. To Allah belong the most beautiful names. Whatever is in the heavens and the earth glorifies Allah, and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
60:1 O you who believe, do not take My enemy and your enemy as allies, offering them affection, when they have disbelieved in the truth that came to you and drove out the Rasul and you for believing in Allah your Rabb. If you went out striving your utmost in My way and seeking My pleasure, do you then show them affection in secret, when I know what you conceal and what you make open? Whoever of you does that has strayed from the right way.
60:2 If they overtake you they will be enemies to you, and they will stretch out toward you their hands and their tongues with evil, and they wish that you would disbelieve.
60:3 Neither your kin nor your children will ever benefit you on the Day of Standing; Allah will decide between you, and Allah sees what you do.
60:4 There has been a good pattern for you in Ibrahim and those with him, when they said to their people: We are clear of you and of what you worship besides Allah; we reject you, and enmity and hatred have shown openly between us and you forever, until you believe in Allah alone. Except for the word of Ibrahim to his father: I will surely ask forgiveness for you, though I possess nothing from Allah for you. Our Rabb, upon You we rely, and to You we turn back, and to You is the arrival.
60:5 Our Rabb, do not make us a trial for those who disbelieve, and forgive us, our Rabb; You are the Mighty, the Wise.
60:6 There was a good pattern in them for you, for whoever hopes for Allah and the Last Day; and whoever turns away, Allah is the Self-Sufficient, the Praised.
60:7 Perhaps Allah will place affection between you and those among them whom you counted as enemies, and Allah is Able, and Allah is Forgiving, Raheem.
60:8 Allah does not forbid you, regarding those who have not killed you in religion nor driven you from your homes, that you be good to them and act with equity toward them; Allah loves the equitable.
60:9 Allah only forbids you from taking as allies those who killed you in religion, drove you from your homes, and backed your expulsion; and whoever takes them as allies, those are the wrongdoers.
60:10 O you who believe, when believing nisa come to you as muhajirin, test them; Allah knows best their faith. If you know them to be believing, do not send them back to the kafirin; they are not lawful for them, nor are they lawful for them. And give them what they spent. There is no blame on you if you marry them, once you have given them their rewards, and do not hold fast to the marriage bonds of the kafirin. Ask for what you spent, and let them ask for what they spent; that is the decree of Allah — Allah judges between you, and Allah is Knowing, Wise.
60:11 And if anything of your nisa slips away to the kafirin, and you then settle the score, give those whose nisa have gone the like of what they spent. And be mindful of Allah, the One you believe in.
60:12 O Nabi, when believing nisa come to you pledging that they will associate nothing with Allah, will not steal, will not commit adultery, will not kill their children, will not bring a slander they fabricate between their hands and their legs, and will not disobey you in what is recognized as good — then accept their pledge and seek forgiveness for them from Allah. Allah is Forgiving, Raheem.
60:13 O you who believe, do not take as allies a people upon whom the wrath of Allah has fallen, who have despaired of the Hereafter just as the kafirin have despaired of the companions of the graves.
61:1 All that is in the heavens and all that is in the earth glorifies Allah, and Allah is the Almighty, the Wise.
61:2 O you who believe, why do you say what you do not do?
61:3 It is a matter of great loathing to Allah that you say what you do not do.
61:4 Indeed, Allah loves those who fight in the way of Allah in ranks, as if they were a solid, well-compacted structure.
61:5 And when Musa said to his people: O my people, why do you harm me when you know that I am the Rasul of Allah to you? So when they swerved, Allah swerved their hearts, and Allah does not guide the fasiqin.
61:6 And when Isa son of Maryam said: O children of Israel, I am the Rasul of Allah to you, confirming what lies before me of the Tawrah and bringing good news of a Rasul who comes after me, whose name is Ahmad. But when he came to them with the clear proofs, they said: this is clear sorcery.
61:7 And who is more wrong than one who fabricates against Allah a lie while he is called to Islam? And Allah does not guide the people who do wrong.
61:8 They want to extinguish the light of Allah with their mouths, but Allah completes the light, even if the kafirin are averse.
61:9 It is Allah who sent the Rasul with the guidance and the religion of truth, to raise it above all religion, even if the associators are averse.
61:10 O you who believe, shall I point you toward a trade that will save you from a painful punishment?
61:11 You believe in Allah and the Rasul, and you strive in the way of Allah with your wealth and your lives. That is better for you, if you know.
61:12 Allah forgives you your offenses and admits you into gardens beneath which rivers flow, and pleasant dwellings in gardens of Eden. That is the great win.
61:13 And another you love: help from Allah and a triumph near at hand. And give good news to the muminin.
61:14 O you who believe, be helpers of Allah, as Isa son of Maryam said to the answerers: who are my helpers unto Allah? The answerers said: we are Allah's helpers. So a party of the children of Israel believed and a party disbelieved, and We strengthened those who believed against their enemy, and they gained the upper hand.
62:1 All that is in the heavens and all that is in the earth glorifies Allah, the Sovereign, the Holy, the Mighty, the Wise.
62:2 Allah is the one who raised up among the unlettered a Rasul from among themselves, to recite to them the ayat of Allah and to purify them and to teach them the Kitab and the wisdom — though before that, they were surely in clear error.
62:3 and others among them who have not yet joined them — and Allah is the Mighty, the Wise.
62:4 That is the bounty of Allah, which Allah bestows on whomever Allah wills; and Allah is the possessor of the great bounty.
62:5 The likeness of those who were made to carry the Tawrah, then did not carry it, is like the donkey carrying written leaves. Wretched is the likeness of the people who rejected the ayat of Allah — and Allah does not guide the wrongdoing people.
62:6 Say: O you of the rivaling party, if you warrant that you are allies of Allah apart from other people, then wish for death — if you are sadiq.
62:7 Yet they will never wish it, for what their hands have sent ahead — and Allah is knowing of the wrongdoers.
62:8 Say: The death you flee from will surely meet you; then you will be returned to the knower of the ghayb and the witnessed, and Allah will tell you what you used to do.
62:9 O you who believe, when the call is made for the prayer on the day of gathering, hasten to the remembrance of Allah and leave your dealing — that is better for you, if you know.
62:10 Then when the prayer is completed, spread out in the land and seek from the bounty of Allah, and remember Allah much, so that you may attain success.
62:11 And when they saw a trade or a diversion, they rushed to it and left you standing. Say: What is with Allah is better than diversion and better than trade — and Allah is the best of providers.
63:1 When the munafiqin come to you, they say: We testify that you are the Rasul of Allah. And Allah knows that you are the Rasul of Allah, and Allah testifies that the munafiqin are liars.
63:2 They have taken their oaths as a shield, and they turned away from the path of Allah. Evil is what they used to do.
63:3 That is because they believed, then disbelieved, so a seal was stamped over their hearts, and they do not understand.
63:4 And when you see them, their bodies impress you; and when they speak, you listen to their speech. They are like propped-up wood. They think every shout is against them. They are the enemy, so beware of them. May Allah kill them! How are they turned away?
63:5 And when it is said to them: Come, so that the Rasul of Allah may ask forgiveness for you, they twist aside their heads, and you see them turning away while they are arrogant.
63:6 It is all the same to them whether you ask forgiveness for them or do not ask forgiveness for them: Allah will never forgive them. Indeed, Allah does not guide the fasiqin.
63:7 They are the ones who say: Do not spend on those who are with the Rasul of Allah until they disperse. Yet to Allah belong the storehouses of the heavens and the earth. But the munafiqin do not understand.
63:8 They say: If we return to the city, the mightiest among us will surely drive out from it the most humbled. But might belongs to Allah, and to the Rasul, and to the muminin. Yet the munafiqin do not know.
63:9 O you who believe, let not your wealth nor your children divert you from the remembrance of Allah. And whoever does that, those are the khasirin.
63:10 And spend from what We have provided you, before death comes to one of you and one says: My Rabb, if only You had delayed me to a near term, so that I could give charity and be among the salihin.
63:11 And Allah will never delay a nafs when its term comes. And Allah is fully aware of what you do.
64:1 Whatever is in the heavens and whatever is in the earth glorifies Allah. To Allah belongs the kingdom, and to Allah belongs the praise, and Allah is over everything able.
64:2 It is the One who created you, so among you is one who disbelieves and among you is one who believes. And Allah sees what you do.
64:3 Created the heavens and the earth in truth, and formed you, and made your forms good. And to Allah is the arrival.
64:4 Knows what is in the heavens and the earth, and knows what you conceal and what you make open. And Allah is knowing of what lies in the chests.
64:5 Has there not come to you the news of those who disbelieved before? They tasted the heavy cost of their affair, and for them is a painful punishment.
64:6 That is because their envoys kept coming to them with the clear proofs, and they said: "Are mere humans to guide us?" So they disbelieved and turned away, and Allah was free of need. And Allah is self-sufficient, praiseworthy.
64:7 Those who disbelieved claim that they will never be raised. Say: Yes indeed, and by my Rabb, you will surely be raised, then you will surely be told of what you did. And that is easy for Allah.
64:8 So believe in Allah and the Rasul and the light that We sent down. And Allah is fully aware of what you do.
64:9 The day gathering you for the day of gathering — that is the day of loss. And whoever believes in Allah and does what is salih, Allah will remit the evil deeds from them and will enter them into a garden beneath which rivers flow, remaining therein forever. That is the great win.
64:10 And those who disbelieved and denied Our ayat — those are companions of the fire, remaining therein forever. And evil is the arrival.
64:11 No calamity strikes except by Allah's permission. And whoever believes in Allah, Allah guides their heart. And Allah is knowing of everything.
64:12 And obey Allah and obey the Rasul. But if you turn away, then upon Our Rasul is only the clear conveying.
64:13 Allah — there is no ilah but Allah. And upon Allah let the muminin rely.
64:14 O you who believe, indeed among your zawj and your children are enemies to you, so beware of them. And if you pass over and pardon and forgive, then indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
64:15 Only your wealth and your children are a trial, and with Allah is a great reward.
64:16 So be mindful of Allah as much as you are able, and listen and obey, and spend good for yourselves. And whoever is guarded from the stinginess of their own self — those are the ones who attain success.
64:17 If you lend Allah a good loan, Allah will multiply it for you and forgive you. And Allah is grateful, forbearing.
64:18 Knower of the ghayb and the witnessed, the mighty, the wise.
65:1 O Nabi, when you divorce nisa, then divorce them for their count and count the count; and be mindful of Allah your Rabb. Do not turn them out of their houses, and let them not go out, unless they come with a clear obscenity. And those are the hadud of Allah; and whoever transgresses the hadud of Allah has wronged themselves. You know not — Allah may bring about a matter after that.
65:2 So when they reach their term, hold them fast with fairness, or part with them with fairness; and call as witnesses men of justice from among you, and establish the witnessing for Allah. That is what one who believes in Allah and the Last Day is admonished with. And whoever is mindful of Allah, Allah will make a way out for them.
65:3 And Allah will provide them from whence they reckon not; and whoever relies upon Allah, Allah is sufficient for them. Indeed, Allah reaches Allah's command; Allah has set a measure for every thing.
65:4 And those of your nisa who have despaired of the monthly course — if you are in doubt — their count is 3 months, and those who have not yet had the course; and those with burdens, their term is that they lay down their burdens. And whoever is mindful of Allah, Allah will make ease for them in their affair.
65:5 That is the command of Allah, which Allah has sent down to you; and whoever is mindful of Allah, Allah will erase their misdeeds for them and make great for them a reward.
65:6 Lodge them where you lodge according to your means, and do not harm them so as to straiten them. And if they are with burden, spend upon them until they lay down their burdens. Then if they nurse for you, give them their wages; and settle the matter between yourselves with fairness. And if you suffer hardship, another shall nurse for them.
65:7 Let the one of abundance spend from their abundance; and the one whose provision is measured, let them spend from what Allah has given them. Allah charges no nafs except with what Allah has given it; Allah will make ease after hardship.
65:8 And how many a township was insolent toward the command of its Rabb and Allah's rusul; so Allah reckoned it a severe reckoning and punished it a dreadful punishment.
65:9 So it tasted the heavy cost of its affair, and the outcome of its affair was loss.
65:10 Allah has prepared for them a severe punishment. So be mindful of Allah, O people of understanding who believe. Allah has sent down to you a reminder.
65:11 A Rasul reciting to you the ayat of Allah, clear, to bring those who believe and do salih deeds out of darkness into light. And whoever believes in Allah and does salihness, Allah will make them enter gardens beneath which rivers flow, abiding therein forever; Allah has made good provision for them.
65:12 Allah is the one who created 7 heavens, and of the earth the like of them; the command descends between them, so that you may know that Allah is powerful over every thing, and that Allah ahat every thing in knowledge.
66:1 O Nabi, why do you forbid what Allah has permitted for you, seeking the approval of your nisa? And Allah is Forgiving, Raheem.
66:2 Allah has ordained for you a dissolution of your oaths, and Allah is your Protector; and Allah is the Knowing, the Wise.
66:3 And when the Nabi confided a hadith to one of his nisa, and she reported it, and Allah disclosed it to him, he knew part of it and turned away from part. And when he told her of it, she said, "Who told you this?" He said, "The Knowing, the Well-Aware told me."
66:4 If you two repent to Allah — your hearts have inclined — and if you support each other against him, then Allah is his Protector, and Jibril, and the salihin among the muminin, and the malakat after that are backers.
66:5 Perhaps, if he divorces you, his Rabb will substitute for him nisa better than you: submitting, believing, devout, repentant, worshipping, wandering, previously married and virgins.
66:6 O you who believe, guard yourselves and your families from a fire whose fuel is people and stones, over which are hard, severe malakat who do not disobey Allah in whatever Allah has commanded them, and they do what they are commanded.
66:7 O you who disbelieve, do not offer excuses today; you are only recompensed for what you used to do.
66:8 O you who believe, turn to Allah in sincere repentance; perhaps your Rabb will remove from you your evil deeds and admit you into gardens beneath which rivers flow — a day when Allah will not disgrace the Nabi and those who believed with him. Their light hastens before them and at their right hands, as they say, "Our Rabb, complete our light for us and forgive us; You are over everything able."
66:9 O Nabi, strive against the kafirin and the munafiqin, and be hard on them; their shelter is Jahannam, and evil is the arrival.
66:10 Allah struck an example for those who disbelieved: the imra'a of Nuh and the imra'a of Lut. They were under two of Our salih servants, and they betrayed them, yet the two availed them nothing against Allah at all. And it was said, "Enter the fire with those entering."
66:11 And Allah struck an example for those who believed: the imra'a of Fir'awn, when she said, "Rabb, build for me a house with You in the garden, and shelter me from Fir'awn and his work, and shelter me from the wrongdoing people."
66:12 And Maryam, daughter of Imran, who fortified her opening, and We blew into it of Our ruh, and she affirmed the words of her Rabb and His Kutub, and she was among the qanitin.
67:1 Blessed be the One in whose hand is all rule, and who is able over everything.
67:2 The One who created death and life to test which of you is best in deed; and Allah is the mighty, the forgiving.
67:3 The One who created 7 heavens in layers — you see no gap in the creation of the Rahman. Turn the gaze back: do you see any crack?
67:4 Then turn the gaze back twice over; it returns to you defeated and regretful.
67:5 We adorned the nearest heaven with lamps and made it a stoning for the satans; and We prepared for them the punishment of the blaze.
67:6 For those who disbelieve in their Rabb there is the punishment of Jahannam — and evil is the arrival.
67:7 When they are cast into it they hear it roaring while it seethes.
67:8 It nearly bursts apart with rage. Every time a host is cast into it, its keepers ask them: "Did no warner come to you?"
67:9 They say: "Yes — a warner came to us, but we rejected him and said: Allah has sent down nothing; you are in nothing but great error."
67:10 And they say: "If only we had listened or understood, we would not be among the companions of the blaze."
67:11 So they confess their guilt. Away with the companions of the blaze!
67:12 Those who hold reverent awe toward their Rabb in the ghayb — for them is forgiveness and a great reward.
67:13 Whether you hide your speech or speak it aloud, Allah knows what the breasts hide.
67:14 Would not the One who created know, when Allah is the subtle, the aware?
67:15 It is Allah who subdued the earth for you: walk upon its surface and eat of Allah's provision; to Allah is the rising.
67:16 Do you feel safe from the One in the heaven, that Allah will make the earth sink with you — and then it sways?
67:17 Or do you feel safe from the One in the heaven, that Allah will send a burning blast down upon you? Then you will know what My warning is like.
67:18 Those before them rejected too — so how was the rejection?
67:19 Have they not seen the birds above them, ranged in ranks and drawing in? Nothing holds them fast but the Rahman; Allah sees all things.
67:20 What host is this that is yours, to help you other than the Rahman? The kafirin are in nothing but deception.
67:21 What one is this who will provide for you if Allah held back the provision? No — they persist in insolent defiance and aversion.
67:22 Who is better guided: one who walks fallen on his face, or one who walks upright on a straight path?
67:23 Say: it is Allah who raised you up and gave you hearing, sights, and fu'ad — little do you give thanks.
67:24 Say: it is Allah who spread you out over the earth, and to Allah you will be gathered.
67:25 And they say: "When is this promise, if you are sadiq?"
67:26 Say: the knowledge is only with Allah; I am only a clear warner.
67:27 So when they see it brought near, the faces of those who disbelieved turn wretched; and it will be said: "This is the very thing you used to call for."
67:28 Say: tell me — if Allah destroys me and those with me, or shows mercy to us, who will shield the kafirin from a painful punishment?
67:29 Say: Allah is the Rahman; in Allah we believe, and on Allah we rely. You will soon know who is in clear error.
67:30 Say: tell me — if your water sinks away, who will bring you flowing water?
68:1 N, by the pen and what they write.
68:2 You are not, by the favor of your Rabb, mad.
68:3 And indeed for you is a reward without stint.
68:4 And indeed you are of a mighty character.
68:5 So you will see, and they will see.
68:6 Which of you is the one afflicted.
68:7 Indeed, your Rabb knows best who strays from the way, and knows best the rightly guided.
68:8 So do not obey the mukazzibin.
68:9 They wish that you would soften, so that they would soften.
68:10 And do not obey every vile swearer.
68:11 A backbiter, going about with slander.
68:12 A withholder of good, a transgressor, a sinner.
68:13 Harsh, and after that, base-born.
68:14 Just because he has wealth and sons.
68:15 When Our ayat are recited to him, he says: 'Tales of the ancients!'
68:16 We shall brand him on the snout.
68:17 We tested them as We tested the companions of the garden, when they swore to cut it by morning.
68:18 And they made no exception.
68:19 Then a deluge from your Rabb swept over it while they slept.
68:20 And by morning it was like the cut-down one.
68:21 So they called out to one another in the morning.
68:22 Set out early to your tillage, if you are going to cut.
68:23 So they set out, whispering under their breath.
68:24 Let no poor person enter it upon you today.
68:25 And they went early, hard in purpose, fully able.
68:26 But when they saw it, they said, 'We have gone astray.'
68:27 'No, we are the ones deprived.'
68:28 The most moderate of them said, 'Did I not tell you? Why did you not glorify?'
68:29 They said, 'Glory to our Rabb — we were wrongdoers.'
68:30 Then they turned on one another, blaming.
68:31 They said, 'Woe to us — we were transgressors.'
68:32 'Perhaps our Rabb will substitute for us something better than it; indeed, to our Rabb we turn eagerly.'
68:33 Such is the punishment; and the punishment of the hereafter is greater, if only they knew.
68:34 Indeed, for the muttaqin are gardens of bliss with their Rabb.
68:35 Shall We make the submitters like the guilty?
68:36 What is the matter with you? How do you judge?
68:37 Or do you have a Kitab in which you study?
68:38 That you have in it whatever you choose?
68:39 Or do you have oaths binding upon Us, reaching to the Day of Rising, that you shall have whatever you decree?
68:40 Ask them which of them is guarantor of that.
68:41 Or do they have partners? Then let them bring their partners, if they are sadiq.
68:42 The Day the shin is bared, and they are called to prostrate, but they cannot.
68:43 Their eyes humbled, abasement overwhelming them — and they had been called to prostrate while they were whole.
68:44 So leave Me with the one who denies this hadith; We shall draw them on gradually from where they know not.
68:45 And I give them respite — My scheme is firm.
68:46 Or do you ask them a wage, so that they are weighed down with liability?
68:47 Or do they have the ghayb, that they write it down?
68:48 So be of the sabirin for the judgment of your Rabb, and do not be like the companion of the huut, when he cried out, choked with grief.
68:49 Had not a favor from his Rabb overtaken him, he would have been cast out onto the open ground, disgraced.
68:50 So his Rabb chose him and made him among the salihin.
68:51 And indeed those who disbelieve almost make you slip with their eyes when they hear the reminder, and they say, 'He is surely mad.'
68:52 And it is nothing but a reminder for all the world.
69:1 The Reality
69:2 What is the Reality?
69:3 What could tell you what the Reality is?
69:4 Thamud and Aad denied the Striking.
69:5 As for Thamud, they were destroyed by the Transgression.
69:6 And as for Aad, they were destroyed by a roaring, violent wind.
69:7 It was set loose on them for 7 nights and 8 days without letup, so that you would see the people lying dead in it as if they were trunks of collapsed palm-trees.
69:8 So do you see any remnant of them?
69:9 And Fir'awn came, and those before him, and the Overturned Cities, in their sin.
69:10 They disobeyed the Rasul of their Rabb, so a towering seizure seized them.
69:11 When the water transgressed, We carried you in the running vessel.
69:12 So that We might make it a reminder for you, and a heeding ear might take it to heart.
69:13 Then, when the trumpet is blown with a single blast,
69:14 and the earth and the mountains are lifted up and pounded with a single pounding,
69:15 then on that day the Event comes to pass.
69:16 And the heaven splits open, and on that day it is frail.
69:17 And the malakat will be ranged at its edges, and the arsh of your Rabb will be carried above them that day by 8 bearers.
69:18 On that day you will be brought forth, and nothing of you will remain hidden.
69:19 As for the one who is given his Kitab in his right hand, he will say: "Here, take and read my Kitab.
69:20 I was sure I would meet my reckoning.
69:21 So he lives in contentment,
69:22 in a lofty garden,
69:23 with fruit-clusters hanging within reach.
69:24 Eat and drink with pleasure for what you sent ahead in the days now past."
69:25 But as for the one given his Kitab in his left hand, he will say: "If only I had not been given my Kitab,
69:26 and had never known what my reckoning was!
69:27 If only it had been the decisive end!
69:28 My wealth was no use to me;
69:29 my power has perished away from me."
69:30 Seize him and shackle him,
69:31 then into the Blazing Fire with him,
69:32 then bind him in a chain 70 cubits long — thread him through.
69:33 He did not believe in Allah the Great,
69:34 and never urged the feeding of the needy.
69:35 So today he has no devoted friend here,
69:36 and no food except foul, festering filth
69:37 that none but the erring eat.
69:38 Nay, I swear by all that you see
69:39 and by all that you do not see:
69:40 it is indeed the word of a noble Rasul,
69:41 not the word of a poet — little do you believe —
69:42 nor the word of a soothsayer — little do you remember.
69:43 It is a sending-down from the Rabb of the worlds.
69:44 If he had invented some sayings in Our name,
69:45 We would have seized him by the right hand,
69:46 then We would have cut off his lifeline,
69:47 and none of you could have held Us back.
69:48 Yet it is a reminder for the muttaqin,
69:49 and We know that some of you deny it.
69:50 And it is surely bitter regret for the kafirin.
69:51 And it is the certain truth.
69:52 So glorify the name of your Rabb, the Most Great.
70:1 A questioner asked about the punishment that will befall.
70:2 For the kafirin there is none to push it back.
70:3 It is from Allah, possessor of the ascents.
70:4 The malakat and the spirit ascend to Allah on a day whose measure is 50,000 years.
70:5 So be of the sabirin with beautiful patience.
70:6 They see it as far away,
70:7 and We see it as near.
70:8 On the day the heaven becomes like boiling mire,
70:9 and the mountains become like drifting fluff,
70:10 no bosom friend will ask after a bosom friend,
70:11 for they are made to see one another. The guilty one would wish to ransom himself from the punishment of that day with his children,
70:12 with his spouse and his brother,
70:13 with his kin who sheltered him,
70:14 and with all who are on earth, every one of them, if only that could save him.
70:15 No indeed — it is a blaze,
70:16 one that keeps tearing at the flesh,
70:17 calling whoever turned their back and walked away,
70:18 who gathered and hoarded.
70:19 Indeed the human being was created fretful and grasping:
70:20 when evil touches him, he is full of distress,
70:21 and when good touches him, he is tight-fisted.
70:22 Except those who pray,
70:23 who are constant in their prayer,
70:24 and in whose wealth there is a known right
70:25 for the beggar and the deprived,
70:26 and who affirm the Day of Recompense,
70:27 and who stand in awe of the punishment of their Rabb —
70:28 for no one is safe from the punishment of their Rabb —
70:29 and who guard their private parts,
70:30 except with their zawj or those their right hands possess; for those are beyond blame,
70:31 but whoever seeks beyond that — those are the ones who transgress.
70:32 And those who keep their amanats and their pledges,
70:33 who stand firm in their testimony,
70:34 and who carefully guard their prayer.
70:35 Those shall be in gardens, honored.
70:36 So what is with those who disbelieve, rushing ahead of you,
70:37 in bands to the right and to the left?
70:38 Does every one of them hope to be admitted into a garden of bliss?
70:39 No indeed! We created them out of what they know.
70:40 So I swear by the Rabb of the east and the west — We are truly able
70:41 to replace them with people better than them, and none can outstrip Us.
70:42 So leave them to wade in idle talk and to play, until they meet the day they are promised,
70:43 the day they come out of the graves in haste, as if rushing toward an erected post,
70:44 their eyes humbled, abasement overwhelming them. That is the day they were promised.
71:1 We sent Nuh to his people to warn his people before a painful punishment comes to them.
71:2 He said: O my people, I am a clear warner to you.
71:3 Worship Allah, and be mindful of Allah, and obey me.
71:4 Allah will forgive you of your offenses and grant you extension to a named term; the term of Allah, when it comes, cannot be delayed — if only you knew.
71:5 He said: My Rabb, I called my people night and day.
71:6 But my call has only increased them in fleeing.
71:7 And whenever I called them, that You might forgive them, they put their fingers in their ears, and covered themselves with their garments, and persisted, and grew ever more arrogant.
71:8 Then I called them out loud.
71:9 Then I declared it to them openly, and confided it to them in secret.
71:10 And I said: Seek forgiveness of your Rabb; your Rabb has always been forgiving.
71:11 Allah will send the heaven upon you pouring.
71:12 And supply you with wealth and sons, and make for you gardens, and make for you rivers.
71:13 What is the matter with you, that you do not hope for gravity for Allah?
71:14 And Allah created you in stages.
71:15 Do you not see how Allah created 7 heavens in layers?
71:16 And made the moon a light in them, and made the sun a blazing lamp.
71:17 And Allah grew you from the earth as a growth.
71:18 Then Allah returns you into it, and brings you out.
71:19 And Allah made the earth for you a spread-out carpet.
71:20 So that you may thread through it wide ways.
71:21 Nuh said: My Rabb, they have disobeyed me, and followed those whose wealth and children have only increased them in loss.
71:22 And they planned a great plan.
71:23 And they said: Do not leave your ilahs, and do not leave Wadd, nor Suwa, nor Yaghuth, and Ya'uq, and Nasr.
71:24 And they have led many astray; so increase the wrongdoers only in straying.
71:25 For their errs they were drowned and made to enter a fire, and they found for themselves, besides Allah, no helpers.
71:26 And Nuh said: My Rabb, do not leave upon the earth a single dwelling of the kafirin.
71:27 If You leave them, they will lead Your servants astray, and they will beget none but an immoral kafir.
71:28 My Rabb, forgive me and my children, and whoever enters my house as a mumin, and the believing men and the believing nisa; and do not increase the wrongdoers except in ruin.
72:1 Say: it was revealed to me that a band of the jinn listened and said: We heard a marvellous recitation.
72:2 It guides to the course, so we believed in it, and we will never associate with our Rabb anyone.
72:3 And that exalted is the new-made of our Rabb, that has taken no companion and no child.
72:4 And that the foolish one among us used to say of Allah a transgression.
72:5 And we thought that humankind and the jinn would never say of Allah a lie.
72:6 And that men of humankind used to seek refuge with men of the jinn, and they increased them in overwhelming.
72:7 And they thought, as you thought, that Allah would never raise anyone.
72:8 And we touched the heaven and found it filled with severe guards and fire-missiles.
72:9 And we used to sit in it, seats for listening; but whoever listens now finds a fire-missile keeping watch over him.
72:10 And we do not know whether evil is intended for those on earth, or whether their Rabb intends for them a course.
72:11 And among us are the sound ones, and among us are below that: we were paths torn apart.
72:12 And we thought that we could never be beyond Allah's power on earth, nor could we be beyond the power in fleeing.
72:13 And when we heard the guidance we believed in it, so whoever believes in the Rabb fears neither diminution nor overwhelming.
72:14 And among us are the submitters, and among us are the deviators; so whoever submits — those have sought a course.
72:15 And as for the deviators, they were firewood for Hell.
72:16 And if they had kept straight upon the path, We would have given them water to drink, abundant.
72:17 To try them thereby; and whoever turns away from the remembrance of the Rabb, a mounting punishment is threaded through them.
72:18 And that the place of prostration belongs to Allah, so invoke no one alongside Allah.
72:19 And that when the servant of Allah stood up calling upon Allah, they nearly became a thick mass upon him.
72:20 Say: I only call upon my Rabb, and I associate no one with Allah.
72:21 Say: I have no power over you, neither for harm nor for a course.
72:22 Say: no one can shelter me from Allah, and I will not find, apart from Allah, any refuge sought.
72:23 Except for a reaching from Allah and the Rasuls; and whoever disobeys Allah and the Rasul, there awaits the fire of Hell, remaining in it forever.
72:24 Until, when they see what they are promised, they will know who is weaker as a helper and fewer in number.
72:25 Say: I do not know whether what you are promised is near, or whether my Rabb appoints for it a term.
72:26 Knower of the ghayb, and the ghayb is disclosed to no one.
72:27 Except one approved as a Rasul; then threads through, before and behind, watch-posts.
72:28 So that Allah may know that they have conveyed the messages of their Rabb, and Allah ahat all that is with them, and has counted everything in number.
73:1 O you who are wrapped up
73:2 Stand through the night, except a little of it
73:3 its half, or diminish from it a little
73:4 or add to it, and recite the Qur'an with a measured recitation
73:5 Indeed We shall bring down upon you a weighty word
73:6 Indeed the rising of the night presses harder and is more upright in word
73:7 Indeed you have in the day a long occupation
73:8 And remember the name of your Rabb, and devote yourself to your Rabb with a full devotion
73:9 Rabb of the east and of the west; there is no ilah but Allah; so take Allah as a trustee
73:10 And be of the sabirin over what they say, and forsake them with a beautiful forsaking
73:11 Leave Me with the mukazzibin, the possessors of blessing, and give them a little respite
73:12 Indeed with Us are warning-seizures and a blaze
73:13 and a food that chokes, and a painful punishment
73:14 the day the earth and the rocky landforms quake, and the rocky landforms become a dune poured loose
73:15 Indeed We sent to you a Rasul as a witness over you, just as We sent to Fir'awn a Rasul
73:16 Fir'awn disobeyed the Rasul, so We seized him with a seizure heavy in cost
73:17 So how will you guard yourselves, if you disbelieve a day that turns children white-haired
73:18 the heaven is split open by it; that was ever a promise to be done
73:19 Indeed this is a reminder; so whoever wills, let them take a way to their Rabb
73:20 Indeed your Rabb knows that you stand less than two-thirds of the night, and its half, and its third, and a party of those with you; and Allah measures the night and the day. Knowing that you will not count it, Allah has relented toward you, so recite what is made easy of the Qur'an. Allah knows that among you will be sick ones, and others striking out across the land seeking from the bounty of Allah, and others killed in the way of Allah. So recite what is easy of it, establish the prayer, give the alms, and lend Allah a goodly loan. Whatever good you send ahead for yourselves, you will find it with Allah; it is better and greater in reward. And seek forgiveness of Allah; indeed Allah is forgiving, merciful.
74:1 O you who are enwrapped,
74:2 Rise and warn.
74:3 And your Rabb, magnify.
74:4 And your garments, purify.
74:5 And the affliction, forsake.
74:6 And do not bestow a favor to gain more.
74:7 And for your Rabb, be of the sabirin.
74:8 Then when the blast is sounded in the Trumpet,
74:9 That day is a day of hardship,
74:10 Not at all easy upon the kafirin.
74:11 Leave Me with the one whom I created alone,
74:12 And I made for him wealth spread wide,
74:13 And sons ever-present,
74:14 And I smoothed for him a smooth path.
74:15 Yet he hopes that I should give yet more.
74:16 No — he was set against Our ayat.
74:17 I will overwhelm him with a hard ascent.
74:18 He reflected and calculated.
74:19 Perdition — how he calculated!
74:20 Then again, perdition — how he calculated!
74:21 Then he looked,
74:22 Then he frowned and scowled,
74:23 Then he turned away and grew arrogant,
74:24 And said: "This is nothing but sihr handed down —
74:25 Nothing but the speech of a human."
74:26 I will thrust him into the Fire of Saqar.
74:27 And what tells you what Saqar is?
74:28 It leaves nothing behind and spares nothing,
74:29 A scorching against humankind.
74:30 Over it are 19.
74:31 We made the keepers of the Fire nothing but malakat, and We made their number nothing but a trial for those who disbelieve — so that those given the Kitab may be certain, and those who believe may increase in faith, and so that those given the Kitab and the muminin may not doubt, and so that those in whose hearts is sickness and the kafirin may say: "What did Allah intend by this as an example?" Thus Allah leads astray whom Allah wills and guides whom Allah wills. None knows the hosts of your Rabb but Allah, and it is nothing but a reminder for humankind.
74:32 No — by the moon,
74:33 And by the night when it retreats,
74:34 And by the morning when it shines,
74:35 Surely it is one of the greatest.
74:36 A warner to humankind,
74:37 For whoever of you wills to advance or to stay behind.
74:38 Every nafs is a pledge for what it earned,
74:39 Except the companions of the Right.
74:40 In gardens they ask one another
74:41 About the mujrimin:
74:42 "What drove you down into Saqar?"
74:43 They say: "We were not of those who pray,
74:44 And we did not feed the needy,
74:45 And we used to wade along with the waders,
74:46 And we used to deny the Day of Judgment,
74:47 Until certainty came to us."
74:48 So no intercession of intercessors avails them.
74:49 So what is with them that they turn away from the reminder,
74:50 As if they were startled donkeys
74:51 Fleeing from a hunting lion?
74:52 No — every one of them wants to be given sheets spread out.
74:53 No — they do not fear the Hereafter.
74:54 No — it is a reminder,
74:55 And whoever wills remembers it.
74:56 And none remember unless Allah wills. Allah alone is worthy of awe and worthy of forgiveness.
75:1 I swear by the Day of Resurrection.
75:2 And I swear by the blaming self.
75:3 Does man think We shall not gather his bones?
75:4 Indeed, We are able to shape his fingertips.
75:5 Rather, man wants to carry on in immorality ahead of him.
75:6 He asks, "When is the Day of Resurrection?"
75:7 So when the sight is dazzled with lightning,
75:8 and the moon sinks away,
75:9 and the sun and the moon are gathered together,
75:10 man says that day, "Where is the escape?"
75:11 No! There is no helper.
75:12 To your Rabb that day is the settling.
75:13 Man is told that day what he sent ahead and left behind.
75:14 Rather, man has clear insight into himself,
75:15 even if he casts his excuses.
75:16 Do not move your tongue with it to hurry it.
75:17 Indeed, upon Us is its gathering and its recitation.
75:18 So when We recite it, follow its recitation.
75:19 Then upon Us is its clarification.
75:20 No! Rather, you love the immediate
75:21 and you leave behind the Hereafter.
75:22 Faces that day are radiant,
75:23 gazing toward their Rabb.
75:24 And faces that day are gloomy,
75:25 thinking a crushing blow will be dealt them.
75:26 No! When it reaches the throat,
75:27 and it is said, "Who can cure?"
75:28 and he knows that it is the parting,
75:29 and leg is wrapped around leg,
75:30 to your Rabb that day is the driving.
75:31 He neither believed nor prayed,
75:32 but denied and turned away,
75:33 then went off to his family, strutting.
75:34 Woe to you — woe to you!
75:35 Then woe to you — woe to you!
75:36 Does man think he will be left aimless?
75:37 Was he not a drop of emitted fluid?
75:38 Then he was a clot, created and proportioned,
75:39 and made from him the two mates, the male and the female.
75:40 Is not that One able to give life to the dead?
76:1 Has there come upon the human a span of time when he was nothing mentioned?
76:2 We created the human from a mingled drop, to test him, and made him hearing and seeing.
76:3 We guided him the way, either thankful or thankless.
76:4 We have prepared for the thankless chains and fetters and a blaze.
76:5 The good ones drink from a cup whose mixture is Kafoor,
76:6 a spring Allah's servants drink from, bursting it forth in full flow.
76:7 They fulfill the vows and fear a day whose evil was spreading far and wide.
76:8 And they feed the food, though they love it, to a needy one, an orphan, and a captive,
76:9 saying: We feed you only for the face of Allah; we want from you no recompense and no thanks.
76:10 Indeed, we fear from our Rabb a day frowning and pitch dark.
76:11 So Allah shielded them from the evil of that day, and gave them radiance and joy,
76:12 and recompensed them for what they endured: a garden and silk.
76:13 Reclining in it upon couches, they see in it neither sun nor bitter cold;
76:14 close over them its shade, and its fruit clusters are subdued, easy to reach.
76:15 And they are moved among with vessels of silver and cups of crystal,
76:16 crystal of silver, measured to the full measure.
76:17 and they are given to drink in it a cup whose mixture is Zanjabeel,
76:18 a spring in it called Salsabeel.
76:19 And youths, everlasting, move among them; when you see them you would count them scattered pearls,
76:20 and when you look, then you look upon bliss and a great dominion.
76:21 Upon them garments of green Sundus and Istabraq, adorned with bracelets of silver; and their Rabb gave them to drink a beverage that purifies.
76:22 This was for you a recompense, and your striving was thanked.
76:23 We, We brought down upon you the Quran, a bringing down.
76:24 So endure for the command of your Rabb, and obey not among them a sinner or a thankless one,
76:25 and remember the name of your Rabb morning and evening,
76:26 and in the night bow down to Allah and glorify Allah the night long.
76:27 These love the swift and leave behind them a heavy day.
76:28 We created them and strengthened their sinews; and when We will, We substitute their likes, a substitution.
76:29 Indeed, this is a reminder; so whoever wills, let him take a way to his Rabb.
76:30 You will not except that Allah wills; Allah is ever knowing, wise.
76:31 Allah admits whom Allah wills into Allah's mercy; and for the wrongdoers is prepared a painful punishment.
77:1 By those sent forth one after another,

77:35 This is the day they shall not speak,
77:36 nor be permitted to offer excuses.
77:37 Woe, that day, to the mukazzibin!

77:39 So if you have a scheme, then scheme against us!
77:40 Woe, that day, to the mukazzibin!

77:42 and fruits of what they desire.

77:45 Woe, that day, to the mukazzibin!
77:46 Eat and enjoy a little — you are mujrimin.
77:47 Woe, that day, to the mukazzibin!

77:49 Woe, that day, to the mukazzibin!

79:2 and those who rush, rushing,

79:14 then suddenly they are upon the surface.

96:9 Have you seen the one who forbids
96:10 a servant when he prays?
96:11 Have you seen: if he is upon guidance
96:12 or commands guarding,
96:13 have you seen: if he denies and turns away —
96:14 does he not know that Allah sees?
96:15 No! If he does not cease, We will seize him by the forelock,
96:16 a lying, erring forelock.
96:17 Let him call his people then —
96:18 We will call the ones who drive away.
96:19 No! Do not obey him — bow down and draw near.
97:1 Indeed We sent it down in the night of the decree.
97:2 And what tells you what the night of the decree is?
97:3 The night of the decree is better than 1,000 months.
97:4 The malakat and the spirit descend therein by permission of their Rabb with every command.
97:5 Peace it is until the rising of the dawn.
98:1 The kafirin among the people of the Kitabten sheets and the associators were not free to break away until the clear proof came to them.
98:2 A Rasul from Allah, reciting purified written-sheets,
98:3 in which are lasting writings.
98:4 Those given the Kitabten sheets did not split except after the clear proof had come to them.
98:5 They were commanded only to worship Allah, devoting the religion to Allah alone, upright, and to establish the prayer and give the alms; and that is the lasting religion.
98:6 Truly, the kafirin among the people of the Kitabten sheets and the associators are in the fire of Jahannam, abiding therein forever; those are the worst of creation.
98:7 Truly, those who believed and did salih deeds — those are the best of creation.
98:8 Their reward with their Rabb is gardens of Eden, rivers flowing beneath them, abiding therein forever. Allah is pleased with them, and they are pleased with Allah. That is for whoever holds reverent awe of their Rabb.
99:1 When the earth quakes with its quaking
99:2 and the earth casts out its burdens
99:3 and the human says: What is the matter with it?
99:4 That day it tells its account
99:5 because your Rabb has inspired it.
99:6 That day the people go forth in diverse groups, to see their deeds.
99:7 So whoever does a mote's weight of good shall see it,
99:8 and whoever does a mote's weight of evil shall see it.
100:1 By the chargers breathing hard,
100:2 and those striking sparks of fire,
100:3 and those raiding at dawn,
100:4 and they stirred up dust with it,
100:5 and they penetrated with it into the midst of the gathering —
100:6 indeed, man is ungrateful to the Rabb.
100:7 And man is a witness to that.
100:8 And man is intense in the love of wealth.
100:9 Does man not know that, when what is in the graves is scattered abroad,
100:10 and what is in the hearts is brought to light,
100:11 their Rabb is well aware of them that day?
101:1 The striking.
101:2 What is the striking?
101:3 And what tells you what the striking is?
101:4 On the day when people become like the scattered spread,
101:5 and the mountains become like fluffed wool.
101:6 Then as for the one whose scales weigh heavy,
101:7 then he is in a pleasing life.
101:8 And as for the one whose scales weigh light,
101:9 then his mother is a plunge.
101:10 And what tells you what it is?
101:11 A blazing fire.
102:1 The race to gain more has distracted you
102:2 until you swerved aside to the graves
102:3 By no means! You will soon know
102:4 Then by no means — you will soon know
102:5 By no means! If you knew with the knowledge of certainty
102:6 you would surely see the Blaze
102:7 Then you will surely see it with the eye of certainty
102:8 Then you will surely be asked that day about the comfort
103:1 By the press!
103:2 Indeed, humankind is surely in loss.
103:3 Except those who believed and did the salihin deeds and exhorted one another to the truth and exhorted one another to patience.
104:1 Woe to every slanderer and backbiter.
104:2 The one who gathers wealth and counts it.
104:3 He thinks his wealth will make him immortal.
104:4 No indeed! He will surely be cast into the Crushing Fire.
104:5 And what can tell you what the Crushing Fire is?
104:6 It is the fire of Allah, ever kindled.
104:7 It is the fire that rises over the hearts.
104:8 Indeed it is sealed over them.
104:9 In outstretched pillars.
105:1 Did you not see how your Rabb dealt with the companions of the elephant?
105:2 Did your Rabb not make their scheme go to ruin?
105:3 And sent upon them birds in flocks,
105:4 hurling at them stones of sijjil,
105:5 and made them like husk eaten up.
106:1 For the uniting of Quraysh
106:2 their uniting for the winter and summer journeys
106:3 So let them serve the Rabb of this House
106:4 who fed them from hunger and secured them from fear
107:1 Have you seen the one who denies the religion?
107:2 That is the one who drives the orphan away,
107:3 and does not urge anyone to feed the poor.
107:4 So woe to those who pray,
107:5 those who are heedless of their prayer,
107:6 those who act only to be seen,
107:7 and withhold even the smallest help.
108:1 Indeed We gave you the Kawthar
108:2 So pray for your Rabb and sacrifice
108:3 Indeed the one who hates you is the one cut off
109:1 Say, O kafirin:
109:2 I do not worship what you worship,
109:3 nor do you worship what I worship,
109:4 nor do I worship what you have worshipped,
109:5 nor do you worship what I worship.
109:6 For you your religion, and for me my religion.
110:1 When the help of Allah comes, and the triumph,
110:2 and you see people entering the religion of Allah in companies,
110:3 then glorify your Rabb with praise, and seek forgiveness from Allah — indeed, Allah is ever forgiving.
111:1 Perished be the two hands of Abu Lahab, and perished is he.
111:2 Neither his wealth nor what he earned was of any avail to him.
111:3 Soon he will enter a fire of flame.
111:4 And his imra'a, the carrier of firewood —
111:5 around her neck is a rope of twisted fiber.
112:1 Say: Allah is one.
112:2 Allah, the unique.
112:3 Allah did not beget, nor was begotten.
112:4 And there has never been anyone equal to Allah.
113:1 Say: I seek refuge in the Rabb of the dawn
113:2 From the evil of what has been created
113:3 And from the evil of the darkness when it closes in
113:4 And from the evil of those who blow on knots
113:5 And from the evil of the envier when he envies
114:1 Say: I seek refuge with the Rabb of people
114:2 the King of people
114:3 the ilah of people
114:4 from the evil of the whisperer who slinks away
114:5 who whispers into the chests of people
114:6 from among the jinn and people
